# Deuteronomy

דברים

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy)

נאומי משה האחרונים, חזרה על אירועי המדבר, סקירה של חוקים שנאמרו בספרים הקודמים, הצגת חוקים חדשים וקריאה לנאמנות לאלוהים.

Moses’ final speeches, recalling events of the desert, reviewing old laws, introducing new ones, and calling for faithfulness to God.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Torah

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 956, License: CC-BY-SA)

Versions (En):
  - Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009 (Segments: 956, License: CC-BY)
  - The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 956, License: CC-BY-NC)
  - The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein (Segments: 956, License: CC-BY)


## Chapter 1

###### Deuteronomy 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:1)


אֵ֣לֶּה הַדְּבָרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֤ר מֹשֶׁה֙ אֶל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בְּעֵ֖בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּ֑ן בַּמִּדְבָּ֡ר בָּֽעֲרָבָה֩ מ֨וֹל ס֜וּף בֵּֽין־פָּארָ֧ן וּבֵֽין־תֹּ֛פֶל וְלָבָ֥ן וַחֲצֵרֹ֖ת וְדִ֥י זָהָֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the words that Moshe addressed to all of Yisroel across the Yardein; [as he had] in the wilderness— on the Arava plain facing Suf; [and as he had] between Poron and Tofel and Lovon and Chatzeiros and Di Zohov.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
THESE are the words which Moshe spoke to all Yisra᾽el on the other side of the Yarden, in the wilderness over against Suf, between Paran, and Tofel, and Lavan, and Ḥażerot, and Di-zahav.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the words which Moses spoke [(Because they are words of reproof and he enumerated here all of the places where they angered the L-rd, he speaks only by way of allusion out of regard for the honor of Israel)] to all of Israel [(Had he spoken only to some of them, the others might have contended that were they there they could have countered the rebuke)] across the Jordan, concerning [their angering the L-rd in] the desert [(viz. Exodus 16:3)], concerning the plain [(their sinning through Ba'al Peor in Shittim, in the plains of Moav)], against Suf [i.e., concerning their rebelling against the L-rd at Suf (the Red Sea), (Exodus 14:11)], concerning [what they had done in the desert of] Paran [through the spies], and concerning [their] calumniating [the manna, saying that it was] white [i.e., "flimsy" (see Numbers 21:5)], and [concerning] Chatzeroth [the (encampment of the) Korach insurrection], and [concerning the golden calf that they made because of their] abundance of gold.


###### Deuteronomy 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:2)


אַחַ֨ד עָשָׂ֥ר יוֹם֙ מֵֽחֹרֵ֔ב דֶּ֖רֶךְ הַר־שֵׂעִ֑יר עַ֖ד קָדֵ֥שׁ בַּרְנֵֽעַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
eleven days [traveling time] from Choreiv by the Mount Seir road, to Kadeish Barnei’a.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is eleven days’ journey from Ḥorev by the way of mount Se῾ir to Qadesh-barnea.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[There is no shorter route from Chorev to Kadesh Barnea than that by way of Mount Seir, it taking] eleven days from Chorev by way of Mount Seir until Kadesh Barnea [and the L-rd, in His desire to hasten your entry into Eretz Yisrael, caused you to traverse it in three days; but because of your backsliding He led you around Mount Seir for forty years.]


###### Deuteronomy 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:3)


וַֽיְהִי֙ בְּאַרְבָּעִ֣ים שָׁנָ֔ה בְּעַשְׁתֵּֽי־עָשָׂ֥ר חֹ֖דֶשׁ בְּאֶחָ֣ד לַחֹ֑דֶשׁ דִּבֶּ֤ר מֹשֶׁה֙ אֶל־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל כְּ֠כֹ֠ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר צִוָּ֧ה יְהֹוָ֛ה אֹת֖וֹ אֲלֵהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[Beginning] in the fortieth year, in the eleventh month, on the first of the month, Moshe addressed Bnei Yisroel with every detail that Adonoy had commanded him [to say] to them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass in the fortieth year, in the eleventh month, on the first day of the month, that Moshe spoke to the children of Yisra᾽el, according to all that the Lord had given him in commandment to them;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, in the fortieth year, in the eleventh month, on the first of the month, that Moses spoke to the children of Israel according to all that the L-rd commanded him concerning them, [Moses reproving them only shortly before his death (taking Jacob as his precedent, who reproved his sons only shortly before his death)],


###### Deuteronomy 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:4)


אַחֲרֵ֣י הַכֹּת֗וֹ אֵ֚ת סִיחֹן֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הָֽאֱמֹרִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר יוֹשֵׁ֖ב בְּחֶשְׁבּ֑וֹן וְאֵ֗ת ע֚וֹג מֶ֣לֶךְ הַבָּשָׁ֔ן אֲשֶׁר־יוֹשֵׁ֥ב בְּעַשְׁתָּרֹ֖ת בְּאֶדְרֶֽעִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
After he had smitten Sichon, King of the Emorites, who was living in Cheshbon; and Og, King of the Boshon, who was living in Ashtoros, in Edrei.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
after he had slain Siĥon the king of the Emori, who dwelt in Ḥeshbon, and ῾Og the king of Bashan, who dwelt at ῾Ashtarot in Edre῾i:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Moses reproving them only] after he had smitten Sichon, king of the Emori, [(reasoning that if he reproved them before entering (at least) part of the land they might regard the reproof as an excuse for his inability to take it)], who dwelt in Cheshbon ["a hard king living in a hard place" — in spite of which Moses defeated him!], and Og, the king of Bashan, who dwelt in Ashtaroth [lit., "hardness" (again, "a hard king in a hard place")], in Edrei [(the name of the kingdom)].


###### Deuteronomy 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:5)


בְּעֵ֥בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּ֖ן בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מוֹאָ֑ב הוֹאִ֣יל מֹשֶׁ֔ה בֵּאֵ֛ר אֶת־הַתּוֹרָ֥ה הַזֹּ֖את לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Across the Yardein in the land of Moav, Moshe began to elucidate this Torah as follows:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
beyond the Yarden, in the land of Mo᾽av, Moshe began to declare this Tora, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Across the Jordan, in the land of Moav, Moses began to explain this Torah [in seventy languages], saying:


###### Deuteronomy 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:6)


יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵ֛ינוּ דִּבֶּ֥ר אֵלֵ֖ינוּ בְּחֹרֵ֣ב לֵאמֹ֑ר רַב־לָכֶ֥ם שֶׁ֖בֶת בָּהָ֥ר הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, our God, addressed us at Choreiv, saying, ‘‘Enough of your living at this mountain!


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord our God spoke to us in Ḥorev, saying, You have dwelt long enough in this mountain:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd our G-d spoke to us in Chorev, saying: You have dwelt in this mountain long enough.


###### Deuteronomy 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:7)


פְּנ֣וּ**׀** וּסְע֣וּ לָכֶ֗ם וּבֹ֨אוּ הַ֥ר הָֽאֱמֹרִי֮ וְאֶל־כׇּל־שְׁכֵנָיו֒ בָּעֲרָבָ֥ה בָהָ֛ר וּבַשְּׁפֵלָ֥ה וּבַנֶּ֖גֶב וּבְח֣וֹף הַיָּ֑ם אֶ֤רֶץ הַֽכְּנַעֲנִי֙ וְהַלְּבָנ֔וֹן עַד־הַנָּהָ֥ר הַגָּדֹ֖ל נְהַר־פְּרָֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Turn and travel for yourselves and arrive at the Emorite hill [region] and at all its neighbors—on the plain, on the mountain, and in the lowland, and in the Negev, and on the seacoast; the land of the Canaanites and the Lebanon, up to the great river, the Euphrates River.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
turn, and take your journey, and go to the mountain of the Emori, and to all the places near it, in the plain, in the hills, and in the lowland, and in the Negev, and by the sea side, to the land of the Kena῾ani, and the Levanon, as far as the great river, the river Perat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Turn and journey [the way to Arad and Charmah] and come to the mountain of the Emori and to all of its neighbors [Ammon, Moav, and Mount Seir], in the plain [the forest plain], in the mountain [the "King's Mountain" (see Gittin 57a)], in the lowland [of the south], in the south and by the seacoast [Ashkelon, Azzah, Caesarea, etc.], the land of the Canaani and the Levanon, until the Great River, the Euphrates [(called the "Great River" because of its being mentioned in conjunction with Eretz Yisrael)].


###### Deuteronomy 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:8)


רְאֵ֛ה נָתַ֥תִּי לִפְנֵיכֶ֖ם אֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ בֹּ֚אוּ וּרְשׁ֣וּ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁבַּ֣ע יְ֠הֹוָ֠ה לַאֲבֹ֨תֵיכֶ֜ם לְאַבְרָהָ֨ם לְיִצְחָ֤ק וּֽלְיַעֲקֹב֙ לָתֵ֣ת לָהֶ֔ם וּלְזַרְעָ֖ם אַחֲרֵיהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
See, I have set the land before you; come and inherit the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers, to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov, to give them and their descendants after them.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I have set the land before you: go in and possess the land which the Lord swore to your fathers, Avraham, Yiżĥaq, and Ya῾aqov, to give to them and to their seed after them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
See, [with your own eyes], I have set the land before you. Come and possess the land [No one will contest your doing so; you will need no recourse to war (Had they not sent the spies, they would not have needed weapons)], which the L-rd swore to your fathers: to Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob [(Each was worthy of it in himself)], to give to them and to their seed after them.


###### Deuteronomy 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:9)


וָאֹמַ֣ר אֲלֵכֶ֔ם בָּעֵ֥ת הַהִ֖וא לֵאמֹ֑ר לֹא־אוּכַ֥ל לְבַדִּ֖י שְׂאֵ֥ת אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I said to you at that time saying, ‘‘I cannot carry you by myself.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I spoke to you at that time, saying, I am not able to bear you myself alone:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to you at that time, saying [at the behest of the L-rd]: I shall not be able to bear you [i.e., to judge you] alone,


###### Deuteronomy 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:10)


יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם הִרְבָּ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֑ם וְהִנְּכֶ֣ם הַיּ֔וֹם כְּכוֹכְבֵ֥י הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם לָרֹֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, has multiplied you, and you are today as numerous the stars in the sky.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the Lord your God has multiplied you, and, behold, you are this day like the stars of heaven for multitude.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[For] the L-rd your G-d has elevated you [over your judges (making them accountable for the punishment they impose upon you)], and, behold, you are [like] the day, [i.e., enduring forever like the sun and the constellations], like the stars of the heavens in multitude.


###### Deuteronomy 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:11)


יְהֹוָ֞ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֽוֹתֵכֶ֗ם יֹסֵ֧ף עֲלֵיכֶ֛ם כָּכֶ֖ם אֶ֣לֶף פְּעָמִ֑ים וִיבָרֵ֣ךְ אֶתְכֶ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּ֥ר לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, God of your forefathers, will add to you one thousandfold, and will bless you as He spoke about you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(The Lord God of your fathers make you a thousand times so many more as you are, and bless you, as he has promised you!)


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
May the L-rd, the G-d of your fathers, add to you, such as you, a thousand times! [(At this point, Israel objects: "Would you limit our blessing when the L-rd has blessed us boundlessly (Genesis 13:16)!" Moses replies: "That was my blessing")], and let Him bless you [boundlessly] as He spoke to you!


###### Deuteronomy 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:12)


אֵיכָ֥ה אֶשָּׂ֖א לְבַדִּ֑י טׇרְחֲכֶ֥ם וּמַֽשַּׂאֲכֶ֖ם וְרִֽיבְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
How can I carry alone your troublesomeness, your burdensomeness, and your contentiousness ?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How can I myself alone bear your care, and your burden, and your strife?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How can I bear alone [see 9] your contentiousness, your heresy [against your judges], and your caviling!


###### Deuteronomy 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:13)


הָב֣וּ לָ֠כֶ֠ם אֲנָשִׁ֨ים חֲכָמִ֧ים וּנְבֹנִ֛ים וִידֻעִ֖ים לְשִׁבְטֵיכֶ֑ם וַאֲשִׂימֵ֖ם בְּרָאשֵׁיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Provide yourselves men, wise and understanding and renowned to your tribes, and I will designate them your leaders.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take wise men, and understanding, and known among your tribes, and I will make them rulers over you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Ready yourselves (to appoint) [upright, desirable] men: wise, understanding [one thing from another], and known [i.e., familiar to you] among your tribes, and I shall set them at your heads [to be honored, respected, and feared by you.]


###### Deuteronomy 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:14)


וַֽתַּעֲנ֖וּ אֹתִ֑י וַתֹּ֣אמְר֔וּ טֽוֹב־הַדָּבָ֥ר אֲשֶׁר־דִּבַּ֖רְתָּ לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You replied to me and said, ‘‘[It is a] good thing that you said to do.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you answered me, and said, The thing which thou hast spoken is good for us to do.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you answered me, and you said: Good is the thing that you have spoken to do [(for if we have many judges, we can use a gift to "get on the good side" of those who don't know us!) — this, instead of beseeching me to judge you!]


###### Deuteronomy 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:15)


וָאֶקַּ֞ח אֶת־רָאשֵׁ֣י שִׁבְטֵיכֶ֗ם אֲנָשִׁ֤ים חֲכָמִים֙ וִֽידֻעִ֔ים וָאֶתֵּ֥ן אוֹתָ֛ם רָאשִׁ֖ים עֲלֵיכֶ֑ם שָׂרֵ֨י אֲלָפִ֜ים וְשָׂרֵ֣י מֵא֗וֹת וְשָׂרֵ֤י חֲמִשִּׁים֙ וְשָׂרֵ֣י עֲשָׂרֹ֔ת וְשֹׁטְרִ֖ים לְשִׁבְטֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I took the leaders of your tribes, men wise and renowned, and designated them leaders over you: masters of thousands and masters of hundreds, and masters of fifties and masters of tens, and court officers for your tribes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So I took the chief of your tribes, wise men, and known, and made them heads over you, captains over thousands, and captains over hundreds, and captains over fifties, and captains over tens, and officers among your tribes.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I "took" [with words of suasion] the heads of your tribes, men, wise and known [(but I could not find "understanding" ones)], and I made them heads over you [that you honor them and give them precedence in all matters]: officers of thousands, [one for a thousand], officers of hundreds, [one for a hundred], officers of fifties, and officers of tens, and [I appointed] bailiffs [over you, to enforce the decisions of the judges] for your tribes.


###### Deuteronomy 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:16)


וָאֲצַוֶּה֙ אֶת־שֹׁ֣פְטֵיכֶ֔ם בָּעֵ֥ת הַהִ֖וא לֵאמֹ֑ר שָׁמֹ֤עַ בֵּין־אֲחֵיכֶם֙ וּשְׁפַטְתֶּ֣ם צֶ֔דֶק בֵּֽין־אִ֥ישׁ וּבֵין־אָחִ֖יו וּבֵ֥ין גֵּרֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I commanded your judges at that time, to say, ‘‘Listen between your brothers and you are to judge right between a man and his brother and between his complainant.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I charged your judges at that time, saying, Hear the causes between your brethren, and judge righteously between every man and his brother, and the stranger that is with him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I charged your judges [to be deliberate in judgment (regardless of how many times an identical case comes up before them)] at that time, [impressing upon them that they were no longer "free agents," but subservient to the public]: Hear among your brothers, and judge righteously between a man and his brother and his opponent.


###### Deuteronomy 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:17)


לֹֽא־תַכִּ֨ירוּ פָנִ֜ים בַּמִּשְׁפָּ֗ט כַּקָּטֹ֤ן כַּגָּדֹל֙ תִּשְׁמָע֔וּן לֹ֤א תָג֙וּרוּ֙ מִפְּנֵי־אִ֔ישׁ כִּ֥י הַמִּשְׁפָּ֖ט לֵאלֹהִ֣ים ה֑וּא וְהַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִקְשֶׁ֣ה מִכֶּ֔ם תַּקְרִב֥וּן אֵלַ֖י וּשְׁמַעְתִּֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not show favor in judgement; small and great alike will you hear out; do not fear any man; for justice belongs to God. And the matter that will be too difficult for you, present to me and I will hear it.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not respect persons in judgment; but hear the small as well as the great; do not be afraid of the face of any man; for the judgment is God’s: and the cause that is too hard for you, bring it to me, and I will hear it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not play favorites in judgment [i.e., in appointing judges]. You shall hear [i.e., you shall attach as much importance to] a small [case] as [to] a large one. Do not fear any man, for the judgment is G-d's [(You "burden" Him with returning what you take from one unlawfully in judgment.)] And the thing that is too difficult for you, you shall bring near to me and I shall hear it [(— for which "presumptuousness" the judgment in the instance of the daughters of Tzelafchad (Numbers 27:5) evaded Moses)].


###### Deuteronomy 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:18)


וָאֲצַוֶּ֥ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֑וא אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּעֲשֽׂוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I commanded you at that time all the things that you are to do.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I commanded you at that time all the things which you should do.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I charged you at that time with all the things that you shall do [(regarding the ten differences between monetary and capital litigations (see Sanhedrin 32a)].


###### Deuteronomy 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:19)


וַנִּסַּ֣ע מֵחֹרֵ֗ב וַנֵּ֡לֶךְ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַמִּדְבָּ֣ר הַגָּדוֹל֩ וְהַנּוֹרָ֨א הַה֜וּא אֲשֶׁ֣ר רְאִיתֶ֗ם דֶּ֚רֶךְ הַ֣ר הָֽאֱמֹרִ֔י כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוָּ֛ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ אֹתָ֑נוּ וַנָּבֹ֕א עַ֖ד קָדֵ֥שׁ בַּרְנֵֽעַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
We traveled from Choreiv and walked the entire great wilderness, that awesome one that you saw, in the direction of the Emorite hill [region] as Adonoy, our God, commanded us; and we arrived at Kadeish Barnei’a.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when we departed from Ḥorev, we went through all that great and terrible wilderness, which you saw; by the way of the mountain of the Emori, as the Lord our God commanded us; and we came to Qadesh-barnea.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we journeyed from Chorev and we went through all the great and fearful desert [(containing gigantic snakes and scorpions)] that you saw, by way of the mountain of the Emori, as the L-rd our G-d commanded us, and we came to Kadesh Barnea.


###### Deuteronomy 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:20)


וָאֹמַ֖ר אֲלֵכֶ֑ם בָּאתֶם֙ עַד־הַ֣ר הָאֱמֹרִ֔י אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I said to you, ‘‘You have arrived at the Emorite hill [region] that Adonoy, our God, is giving us.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I said to you, You are come to the mountain of the Emori, which the Lord our God gives to us.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to you: You have come to the mountain of the Emori, which the L-rd our G-d gives to us.


###### Deuteronomy 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:21)


רְ֠אֵ֠ה נָתַ֨ן יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ לְפָנֶ֖יךָ אֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ עֲלֵ֣ה רֵ֗שׁ כַּאֲשֶׁר֩ דִּבֶּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֱלֹהֵ֤י אֲבֹתֶ֙יךָ֙ לָ֔ךְ אַל־תִּירָ֖א וְאַל־תֵּחָֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
See! Adonoy, our God, has set the land before you. Go up, inherit [it] as promised by Adonoy, the God of your forefathers, about you; do not fear and do not tremble.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, the Lord thy God has set the land before thee: go up and possess it, as the Lord God of thy fathers has said to thee; fear not, nor be discouraged.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
See, the L-rd your G-d has set the land before you; go up and possess it, as the L-rd, the G-d of your fathers, has spoken to you. Do not fear and do not be broken.


###### Deuteronomy 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:22)


וַתִּקְרְב֣וּן אֵלַי֮ כֻּלְּכֶם֒ וַתֹּאמְר֗וּ נִשְׁלְחָ֤ה אֲנָשִׁים֙ לְפָנֵ֔ינוּ וְיַחְפְּרוּ־לָ֖נוּ אֶת־הָאָ֑רֶץ וְיָשִׁ֤בוּ אֹתָ֙נוּ֙ דָּבָ֔ר אֶת־הַדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נַעֲלֶה־בָּ֔הּ וְאֵת֙ הֶֽעָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָבֹ֖א אֲלֵיהֶֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You approached me, all of you, and said, ‘‘Let us send men ahead of us to spy out the land for us, and let them bring back word to us: the route we are to go up on and the cities we will be coming to.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you came near to me every one of you, and said, We will send men before us, and they shall search us out the land, and bring us back word by what way we must go up, and into what cities we shall come.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of you drew near to me ["promiscuously" (as opposed to (5:20): "And you approached me [deferentially], all the heads of your tribes and your elders, etc.")], and you said: Let us send out men before us and let them spy out the land for us. And let them return to us word [concerning the languages that they speak], the [straightest] way by which we should go up, and the cities to which we will come [first, to conquer].


###### Deuteronomy 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:23)


וַיִּיטַ֥ב בְּעֵינַ֖י הַדָּבָ֑ר וָאֶקַּ֤ח מִכֶּם֙ שְׁנֵ֣ים עָשָׂ֣ר אֲנָשִׁ֔ים אִ֥ישׁ אֶחָ֖ד לַשָּֽׁבֶט׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The idea pleased me, so I took from you twelve men, one man per tribe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the saying pleased me well: and I took twelve men of you, one for a tribe:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the thing was good in my eyes [i.e., I acceded to your request in the hope that my doing so would enhearten you to waive the sending of the spies — but it did not], and I took of you [(from the most select of you)] twelve men, one man to a tribe [(the tribe of Levi not being with them)].


###### Deuteronomy 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:24)


וַיִּפְנוּ֙ וַיַּעֲל֣וּ הָהָ֔רָה וַיָּבֹ֖אוּ עַד־נַ֣חַל אֶשְׁכֹּ֑ל וַֽיְרַגְּל֖וּ אֹתָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They turned and went uphill and arrived at Wadi Eshkol, and they spied it out.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and they turned and went up to the mountain, and came to the wadi of Eshkol, and searched it out.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they turned and they went up to the mountain, and they came to the river-bed of Eshkol [so called by reason of the cluster (eshkol) of grapes they were to take from there], and they spied it out, [traversing its boundaries, length and width (see Numbers 13:21)].

וַיִּקְח֤וּ בְיָדָם֙ מִפְּרִ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ וַיּוֹרִ֖דוּ אֵלֵ֑ינוּ וַיָּשִׁ֨בוּ אֹתָ֤נוּ דָבָר֙ וַיֹּ֣אמְר֔וּ טוֹבָ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They took in their hand some fruit of the land and brought [it] down to us. They brought back word to us and said, ‘‘Good is the land that Adonoy, our God, is giving us.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And they took of the fruit of the land in their hands, and brought it down to us, and brought us back word, and said, It is a good land which the Lord our God gives us.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they took in their hand from the fruit of the land and they brought it down to us [(whence it is seen that Eretz Yisrael is higher than all of the other lands)], and they returned word to us, and they [Joshua and Calev] said: Good is the land that the L-rd our G-d gives us.

וְלֹ֥א אֲבִיתֶ֖ם לַעֲלֹ֑ת וַתַּמְר֕וּ אֶת־פִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But you were unwilling to go up, and you defied Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet you would not go up, but rebelled against the commandment of the Lord your God:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you did not desire to go up, and you opposed the word of the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:27)


וַתֵּרָגְנ֤וּ בְאׇהֳלֵיכֶם֙ וַתֹּ֣אמְר֔וּ בְּשִׂנְאַ֤ת יְהֹוָה֙ אֹתָ֔נוּ הוֹצִיאָ֖נוּ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם לָתֵ֥ת אֹתָ֛נוּ בְּיַ֥ד הָאֱמֹרִ֖י לְהַשְׁמִידֵֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You grumbled in your tents and said, ‘‘Because of Adonoy’s hatred of us, He took us out of the land of Egypt to put us in the hand of the Emorite to destroy us.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and you murmured in your tents, and said, Because the Lord hated us, he has brought us forth out of the land of Miżrayim, to deliver us into the hand of the Emori, to destroy us.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you caviled in your tents, and you said: In the L-rd's hatred of us [("As you are disposed to another, you think he is disposed to you!")] He took us out of the land of Egypt [(from a fertile land to an arid land)] to deliver us into the hands of the Emori to destroy us.


###### Deuteronomy 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:28)


אָנָ֣ה**׀** אֲנַ֣חְנוּ עֹלִ֗ים אַחֵ֩ינוּ֩ הֵמַ֨סּוּ אֶת־לְבָבֵ֜נוּ לֵאמֹ֗ר עַ֣ם גָּד֤וֹל וָרָם֙ מִמֶּ֔נּוּ עָרִ֛ים גְּדֹלֹ֥ת וּבְצוּרֹ֖ת בַּשָּׁמָ֑יִם וְגַם־בְּנֵ֥י עֲנָקִ֖ים רָאִ֥ינוּ שָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Where are we going up to? Our brothers have shattered our hopes by saying, ‘A people greater and more powerful than we, cities great and fortified sky-high, and also descendants of the Anakim—did we see there.’’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Whither shall we go up? our brethren have made our heart faint, saying, The people are greater and taller than we; the cities are great and fortified up to heaven; and moreover we have seen the sons of the ῾Anaqim there.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Whither are we going up? Our brothers have melted our hearts, saying: A people greater and higher than we; cities great and fortified in heaven [(Scripture speaks hyperbolically here)], and also giants did we see there.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:29)


וָאֹמַ֖ר אֲלֵכֶ֑ם לֹא־תַעַרְצ֥וּן וְלֹא־תִֽירְא֖וּן מֵהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I said to you, ‘‘Do not crumble and do not fear them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then I said to you, Dread not, neither be afraid of them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I said to them: Do not break and do not fear them.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:30)


יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֙ הַהֹלֵ֣ךְ לִפְנֵיכֶ֔ם ה֖וּא יִלָּחֵ֣ם לָכֶ֑ם כְּ֠כֹ֠ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֧ה אִתְּכֶ֛ם בְּמִצְרַ֖יִם לְעֵינֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, who goes before you, He will wage war for you, as in everything that He did with you in Egypt before your eyes;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord your God who goes before you, he shall fight for you, according to all that he did for you in Miżrayim before your eyes;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd your G-d who goes before you — He will war for you, as all that He did with you in Egypt before your eyes.


###### Deuteronomy 1:31

וּבַמִּדְבָּר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר רָאִ֔יתָ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נְשָׂאֲךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר יִשָּׂא־אִ֖ישׁ אֶת־בְּנ֑וֹ בְּכׇל־הַדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֲלַכְתֶּ֔ם עַד־בֹּאֲכֶ֖ם עַד־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
and in the wilderness, as you have seen, (where) Adonoy, your God, carried you the way a man carries his son all along the road you went until you arrived at this place.

and in the wilderness, where thou hast seen how that the Lord thy God bore thee, as a man bears his son, in all the way that you went, until you came to this place.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [as all that He did with you] in the desert, where you saw how the L-rd your G-d bore you, as a man bears his son [(see Exodus 14:19-20)], in all the way that you went until you came to this place.

Yet in this matter, you do not trust Adonoy, your God,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Yet in this thing you did not believe the Lord your God,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in this thing [that He promises to bring you to the land] you do not believe in the L-rd your G-d!


###### Deuteronomy 1:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:33)


הַהֹלֵ֨ךְ לִפְנֵיכֶ֜ם בַּדֶּ֗רֶךְ לָת֥וּר לָכֶ֛ם מָק֖וֹם לַחֲנֹֽתְכֶ֑ם בָּאֵ֣שׁ**׀** לַ֗יְלָה לַרְאֹֽתְכֶם֙ בַּדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תֵּֽלְכוּ־בָ֔הּ וּבֶעָנָ֖ן יוֹמָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Who has been going ahead of you on the road to seek for you a place to encamp; with the fire at night to show you the way for you to go on, and with the cloud by day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who went in the way before you, to search you out a place to pitch your tents in, in fire by night, to show you by what way you should go, and in a cloud by day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who goes before you on the way, to seek out for you a place for your encampment — in fire by night to show you the way wherein you should go, and in cloud by day!


###### Deuteronomy 1:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:34)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֥ע יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־ק֣וֹל דִּבְרֵיכֶ֑ם וַיִּקְצֹ֖ף וַיִּשָּׁבַ֥ע לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy heard the intent of your words and angrily swore, to say:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord heard the voice of your words, and was angry, and swore, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd heard the voice of your words and He was wroth, and He swore, saying:


###### Deuteronomy 1:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:35)


אִם־יִרְאֶ֥ה אִישׁ֙ בָּאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה הַדּ֥וֹר הָרָ֖ע הַזֶּ֑ה אֵ֚ת הָאָ֣רֶץ הַטּוֹבָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁבַּ֔עְתִּי לָתֵ֖ת לַאֲבֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘That [no] man will see—of these men, this wicked generation—the good land that I swore to give to your forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Surely not one of these men of this evil generation shall see that good land, which I swore to give to your fathers,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
No man of these men, this evil generation, shall see the good land that I swore to give to their forefathers.


###### Deuteronomy 1:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:36)


זֽוּלָתִ֞י כָּלֵ֤ב בֶּן־יְפֻנֶּה֙ ה֣וּא יִרְאֶ֔נָּה וְלֽוֹ־אֶתֵּ֧ן אֶת־הָאָ֛רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר דָּֽרַךְ־בָּ֖הּ וּלְבָנָ֑יו יַ֕עַן אֲשֶׁ֥ר מִלֵּ֖א אַחֲרֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Except for Koleiv ben Yefuneh, who will see it, and to whom I will give the land that he trod upon and to his sons, because he perfected himself in fear of Adonoy.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
save Kalev the son of Yefunne; he shall see it, and to him will I give the land that he has trodden upon, and to his children, because he has wholly followed the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Except Calev the son of Yefuneh — he shall see it, and to him shall I give the land whereon he trod [i.e., Chevron (see Numbers 13:22)], and to his sons, because he filled [his heart to follow] after the L-rd.


###### Deuteronomy 1:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:37)


גַּם־בִּי֙ הִתְאַנַּ֣ף יְהֹוָ֔ה בִּגְלַלְכֶ֖ם לֵאמֹ֑ר גַּם־אַתָּ֖ה לֹא־תָבֹ֥א שָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Also at me was Adonoy angry because of you, to say, ‘‘You, too, will not arrive there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also the Lord was angry with me for your sakes, saying, Thou also shalt not go in there.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Against me, too, was the L-rd wroth because of you, saying: You, too, shall not come there.


###### Deuteronomy 1:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:38)


יְהוֹשֻׁ֤עַ בִּן־נוּן֙ הָעֹמֵ֣ד לְפָנֶ֔יךָ ה֖וּא יָ֣בֹא שָׁ֑מָּה אֹת֣וֹ חַזֵּ֔ק כִּי־ה֖וּא יַנְחִלֶ֥נָּה אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Yehoshua son of Nun, who attends you, he will arrive there. Encourage him, for he will apportion it to Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But Yehoshua the son of Nun, who stands before thee, he shall go in there: encourage him: for he shall cause Yisra᾽el to inherit it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Joshua the son of Nun, who stands before you — he shall come there; him shall you strengthen, for he shall cause Israel to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 1:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:39)


וְטַפְּכֶם֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲמַרְתֶּ֜ם לָבַ֣ז יִהְיֶ֗ה וּ֠בְנֵיכֶ֠ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹא־יָדְע֤וּ הַיּוֹם֙ ט֣וֹב וָרָ֔ע הֵ֖מָּה יָבֹ֣אוּ שָׁ֑מָּה וְלָהֶ֣ם אֶתְּנֶ֔נָּה וְהֵ֖ם יִירָשֽׁוּהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As to your infants, about whom you said, ‘They will become spoils,’ and your children who cannot distinguish today good from evil, they will arrive there; and to them will I give it and they will inherit it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And your little ones, concerning whom you said they should be a prey, and your children who in that day had no knowledge of good and evil, they shall go in there, and to them will I give it, and they shall possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your little ones, of whom you said that they would fall spoil, and your sons, who do not know this day good and evil — they will come there and to them will I give it, and they will inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 1:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:40)


וְאַתֶּ֖ם פְּנ֣וּ לָכֶ֑ם וּסְע֥וּ הַמִּדְבָּ֖רָה דֶּ֥רֶךְ יַם־סֽוּף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As for you, turn yourselves [around], and travel into the wilderness by the Yam Suf road.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you, turn you back, [instead of going directly north through the land of Edom as originally intended] and go through the desert in the direction of the Red Sea, [to the point of the exodus from Egypt, in the southwest corner (for the desert in which they were traveling was south of Mount Seir, between the Red Sea and Mount Seir. Now they were told to go back to the sea and to travel all around the south of Mount Seir from west to east.)]


###### Deuteronomy 1:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:41)


וַֽתַּעֲנ֣וּ**׀** וַתֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלַ֗י חָטָ֘אנוּ֮ לַיהֹוָה֒ אֲנַ֤חְנוּ נַעֲלֶה֙ וְנִלְחַ֔מְנוּ כְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֖נוּ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ וַֽתַּחְגְּר֗וּ אִ֚ישׁ אֶת־כְּלֵ֣י מִלְחַמְתּ֔וֹ וַתָּהִ֖ינוּ לַעֲלֹ֥ת הָהָֽרָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You replied and said to me, ‘‘We have sinned to Adonoy. We will go up and wage war in [fulfillment of] all that Adonoy, our God commanded us.’’ And each of you girded his weapons, and you got ready to go up the mountain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then you answered and said to me, We have sinned against the Lord, we will go up and fight, according to all that the Lord our God commanded us. And you girded on every man his weapons of war, and ventured to go up into the hill.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you answered and you said to me: We have sinned to the L-rd. We shall go up and do battle according to all that the L-rd our G-d commanded us, and you girded, each, his weapons of war, and you readied yourselves to go up to the mountain.


###### Deuteronomy 1:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:42)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י אֱמֹ֤ר לָהֶם֙ לֹ֤א תַֽעֲלוּ֙ וְלֹא־תִלָּ֣חֲמ֔וּ כִּ֥י אֵינֶ֖נִּי בְּקִרְבְּכֶ֑ם וְלֹא֙ תִּנָּ֣גְפ֔וּ לִפְנֵ֖י אֹיְבֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Say to them, ‘Do not go up and do not wage war, for I am not in your midst; so that you will not be shattered by your enemies.’’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to me, Say to them, Neither go up, nor fight; for I am not among you; lest you be smitten before your enemies.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me: Say to them: Do not go up and do not do battle, for I am not in your midst, and do not be smitten before your foes.


###### Deuteronomy 1:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:43)


וָאֲדַבֵּ֥ר אֲלֵיכֶ֖ם וְלֹ֣א שְׁמַעְתֶּ֑ם וַתַּמְרוּ֙ אֶת־פִּ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וַתָּזִ֖דוּ וַתַּעֲל֥וּ הָהָֽרָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I told you, but you did not obey. You defied Adonoy, and deliberately went up the hill.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So I spoke to you; and you would not hear, but rebelled against the commandment of the Lord, and went presumptuously up into the hill.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I spoke to you, but you did not hearken. And you opposed the word of the L-rd, and you ventured, and you went up to the mountain.


###### Deuteronomy 1:44

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:44](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:44)


וַיֵּצֵ֨א הָאֱמֹרִ֜י הַיֹּשֵׁ֨ב בָּהָ֤ר הַהוּא֙ לִקְרַאתְכֶ֔ם וַיִּרְדְּפ֣וּ אֶתְכֶ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּעֲשֶׂ֖ינָה הַדְּבֹרִ֑ים וַֽיַּכְּת֥וּ אֶתְכֶ֛ם בְּשֵׂעִ֖יר עַד־חׇרְמָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The Emorites came out—those who live in those hills—toward you, and chased you as the bees do; and they crushed you in Se’ir till Chorma.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Emori, who dwelt in that mountain, came out against you, and chased you, as bees do, and beat you down in Se῾ir, as far as Ḥorma.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Emori went out, who dwelt in that mountain, against you, and they pursued you, as the bees do [(dying when they smote you, just as a bee dies when it stings)], and they smote you from Seir until Charmah.


###### Deuteronomy 1:45

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:45](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/1:45)


וַתָּשֻׁ֥בוּ וַתִּבְכּ֖וּ לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹֽא־שָׁמַ֤ע יְהֹוָה֙ בְּקֹ֣לְכֶ֔ם וְלֹ֥א הֶאֱזִ֖ין אֲלֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You returned and wept before Adonoy, but Adonoy did not accept your prayer; He paid no attention to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you returned and wept before the Lord; but the Lord would not hearken to your voice, nor give ear to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you turned back and you cried before the L-rd; but the L-rd [Yod-Keh-Vav-Keh, the attribute of mercy] did not heed your voice [(You transformed, as it were, His attribute of mercy to one of cruelty)], and He did not give ear to you.

וַתֵּשְׁב֥וּ בְקָדֵ֖שׁ יָמִ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים כַּיָּמִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְשַׁבְתֶּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You lived in Kodeish many years, like the years you lived [elsewhere].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

## Chapter 2

###### Deuteronomy 2:1

וַנֵּ֜פֶן וַנִּסַּ֤ע הַמִּדְבָּ֙רָה֙ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יַם־ס֔וּף כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר דִּבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָ֑י וַנָּ֥סׇב אֶת־הַר־שֵׂעִ֖יר יָמִ֥ים רַבִּֽים׃{ס}

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Deuteronomy 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:2)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֥י לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me to say,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to me, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me:


###### Deuteronomy 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:3)


רַב־לָכֶ֕ם סֹ֖ב אֶת־הָהָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה פְּנ֥וּ לָכֶ֖ם צָפֹֽנָה׃

‘‘Enough of you circling this mountain! Turn yourselves northward.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You have compassed this mountain long enough: turn northwards.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You have gone around this mountain long enough — turn to the north [to the east of Moav].


###### Deuteronomy 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:4)


וְאֶת־הָעָם֮ צַ֣ו לֵאמֹר֒ אַתֶּ֣ם עֹֽבְרִ֗ים בִּגְבוּל֙ אֲחֵיכֶ֣ם בְּנֵי־עֵשָׂ֔ו הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בְּשֵׂעִ֑יר וְיִֽירְא֣וּ מִכֶּ֔ם וְנִשְׁמַרְתֶּ֖ם מְאֹֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And command the people as follows, ‘‘You are crossing into the border of your brothers, the descendants of Eisov who live in Seir. They will be afraid of you, so you must be extrememly cautious.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And command the people, saying, You are to pass through the border of your brethren the children of ῾Esav, who dwell in Se῾ir; and they shall be afraid of you: take good heed to yourselves therefore:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the people, charge, saying: You are to pass through the boundary of your brothers, the sons of Esav, who dwell in Seir. They shall fear you; and take great heed:


###### Deuteronomy 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:5)


אַל־תִּתְגָּר֣וּ בָ֔ם כִּ֠י לֹֽא־אֶתֵּ֤ן לָכֶם֙ מֵֽאַרְצָ֔ם עַ֖ד מִדְרַ֣ךְ כַּף־רָ֑גֶל כִּֽי־יְרֻשָּׁ֣ה לְעֵשָׂ֔ו נָתַ֖תִּי אֶת־הַ֥ר שֵׂעִֽיר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not incite them. For I will not give you of their land as much as a footstep, because for Eisov’s inheritance have I designated Mount Seir.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
meddle not with them; for I will not give you of their land, no, not so much as a foot breadth; because I have given mount Se῾ir to ῾Esav for a possession.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not incite them: For I shall not allow you [to pass through] their land [without their consent] as much as the space of a footstep, for as an inheritance to Esav have I given the mountain of Seir.


###### Deuteronomy 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:6)


אֹ֣כֶל תִּשְׁבְּר֧וּ מֵֽאִתָּ֛ם בַּכֶּ֖סֶף וַאֲכַלְתֶּ֑ם וְגַם־מַ֜יִם תִּכְר֧וּ מֵאִתָּ֛ם בַּכֶּ֖סֶף וּשְׁתִיתֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Food are you to buy from them with money and eat [it]; also water are you to purchase from them with money and drink [it.]


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall buy food of them for money, that you may eat; and you shall also buy water of them for money, that you may drink.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Food shall you buy of them with money, and you shall eat; and also water shall you buy of them with money, and you shall drink.


###### Deuteronomy 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:7)


כִּי֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ בֵּֽרַכְךָ֗ בְּכֹל֙ מַעֲשֵׂ֣ה יָדֶ֔ךָ יָדַ֣ע לֶכְתְּךָ֔ אֶת־הַמִּדְבָּ֥ר הַגָּדֹ֖ל הַזֶּ֑ה זֶ֣ה**׀** אַרְבָּעִ֣ים שָׁנָ֗ה יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ עִמָּ֔ךְ לֹ֥א חָסַ֖רְתָּ דָּבָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For Adonoy, your God, has blessed you in all your handiwork; He has been intimately concerned with your traversing this great wilderness. It is now forty years [that] Adonoy, your God, is accompanying you; you have lacked nothing.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord thy God has blessed thee in all the work of thy hand: he knows thy walking through this great wilderness: these forty years the Lord thy God has been with thee; thou hast lacked nothing.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the L-rd your G-d has blessed you in all the work of your hand [(Therefore, do not manifest ingratitude for His bounty by showing yourselves poor, but show yourselves rich)]. He knew (i.e., provided for) your going through this vast desert. These forty years the L-rd your G-d has been with you; you have lacked nothing.


###### Deuteronomy 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:8)


וַֽנַּעֲבֹ֞ר מֵאֵ֧ת אַחֵ֣ינוּ בְנֵי־עֵשָׂ֗ו הַיֹּֽשְׁבִים֙ בְּשֵׂעִ֔יר מִדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ הָֽעֲרָבָ֔ה מֵאֵילַ֖ת וּמֵעֶצְיֹ֣ן גָּ֑בֶר{ס}וַנֵּ֙פֶן֙ וַֽנַּעֲבֹ֔ר דֶּ֖רֶךְ מִדְבַּ֥ר מוֹאָֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
We passed over from our brothers, the descendants of Eisov, who live in Seir, from the Arava road, from Eilas and from Etzyon-Gover. We turned and passed over by way of the wilderness of Moav.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when we passed by from our brethren the children of ῾Esav, who dwelt in Se῾ir, through the way of the ῾Arava from Elat and from ῾Eżyon-gever, we turned and passed by the way of the wilderness of Mo᾽av.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we passed from our brothers, the sons of Esav, who dwelt in Seir, by way of the plain, from Eilath and from Etzyon Gaver. And we turned [north] and we passed by the desert of Moav, [proceeding on the eastern side].


###### Deuteronomy 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:9)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י אַל־תָּ֙צַר֙ אֶת־מוֹאָ֔ב וְאַל־תִּתְגָּ֥ר בָּ֖ם מִלְחָמָ֑ה כִּ֠י לֹֽא־אֶתֵּ֨ן לְךָ֤ מֵֽאַרְצוֹ֙ יְרֻשָּׁ֔ה כִּ֣י לִבְנֵי־ל֔וֹט נָתַ֥תִּי אֶת־עָ֖ר יְרֻשָּֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Do not besiege Moav and do not intimidate them with war. For I will not give you [any] of his land as inheritance, for to Lot’s descendants have I given Or as inheritance.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to me, Do not
				harass Mo᾽av, nor contend with them in battle: for I will not give thee of their land for a possession; because I have given ῾Ar to the children of Lot for a possession.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me: Do not besiege Moav and do not incite a war against them; for I shall not give to you of his land as an inheritance; for to the children of Lot have I given Ar [(the province)] as an inheritance.


###### Deuteronomy 2:10

כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר עָֽשׂוּ־לִ֜י בְּנֵ֣י עֵשָׂ֗ו הַיֹּֽשְׁבִים֙ בְּשֵׂעִ֔יר וְהַמּ֣וֹאָבִ֔ים הַיֹּשְׁבִ֖ים בְּעָ֑ר עַ֤ד אֲשֶֽׁר־אֶֽעֱבֹר֙ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן אֶל־הָאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
As the descendants of Eisov did for me who live in Seir and the Moavites who live in Or; until I will have crossed the Yardein into the land that Adonoy, our God, is giving us.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(as the children of ῾Esav who dwell in Se῾ir, and the Mo᾽avim who dwell in ῾Ar, did to me;) until I shall pass over the Yarden into the land which the Lord our God gives us.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As there did for me [re food and drink] the sons of Esav, who dwell in Seir, and the Moavites, who dwell in Ar, until I cross the Jordan to the land that the L-rd our G-d is giving us.


###### Deuteronomy 2:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:30)


וְלֹ֣א אָבָ֗ה סִיחֹן֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ חֶשְׁבּ֔וֹן הַעֲבִרֵ֖נוּ בּ֑וֹ כִּֽי־הִקְשָׁה֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ אֶת־רוּח֗וֹ וְאִמֵּץ֙ אֶת־לְבָב֔וֹ לְמַ֛עַן תִּתּ֥וֹ בְיָדְךָ֖ כַּיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But Sichon, King of Cheshbon, was not willing to let us traverse it, because Adonoy, your God, had hardened his spirit and emboldened his mind in order to put him in your hand this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But Siĥon king of Ḥeshbon would not let us pass by him: for the Lord thy God hardened his spirit, and made his heart obstinate, that he might deliver him into thy hand, as is apparent this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But Sichon the king of Cheshbon would not allow us to pass through him. For the L-rd your G-d hardened his spirit and fortified his heart, to deliver him into your hand as of this day.


###### Deuteronomy 2:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:31)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י רְאֵ֗ה הַֽחִלֹּ֙תִי֙ תֵּ֣ת לְפָנֶ֔יךָ אֶת־סִיחֹ֖ן וְאֶת־אַרְצ֑וֹ הָחֵ֣ל רָ֔שׁ לָרֶ֖שֶׁת אֶת־אַרְצֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me, ‘‘See, I have begun defeating before you Sichon and his land. Begin inheriting—to inherit his land.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to me, Behold, I have begun to give Siĥon and his land before thee: begin to possess, that thou mayst inherit his land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me: See, I have begun to give before you Sichon and his land [(He lowered the tutelary angel of the Emorites beneath Moses' feet and had them tread on his neck)]. Begin to drive (him) out, to inherit his land.


###### Deuteronomy 2:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:32)


וַיֵּצֵא֩ סִיחֹ֨ן לִקְרָאתֵ֜נוּ ה֧וּא וְכׇל־עַמּ֛וֹ לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה יָֽהְצָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Sichon went out toward us, he and his entire people, to wage war in Yahatz.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then Siĥon came out against us, he and all his people, to fight at Yahaż.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Sichon went out against us, [(not sending to Og for assistance, each being "self-sufficient")], he and all his people, to war, to Yahatz.


###### Deuteronomy 2:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:33)


וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֛הוּ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ לְפָנֵ֑ינוּ וַנַּ֥ךְ אֹת֛וֹ וְאֶת־בָּנָ֖ו וְאֶת־כׇּל־עַמּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, our God, defeated him before us, and we smote him and his sons and his entire people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord our God delivered him before us; and we smote him, and his sons, and all his people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd our G-d delivered him before us, and we smote him and his sons [(The written form is "son" — he had a son as mighty as he)], and all his people.


###### Deuteronomy 2:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:34)


וַנִּלְכֹּ֤ד אֶת־כׇּל־עָרָיו֙ בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֔וא וַֽנַּחֲרֵם֙ אֶת־כׇּל־עִ֣יר מְתִ֔ם וְהַנָּשִׁ֖ים וְהַטָּ֑ף לֹ֥א הִשְׁאַ֖רְנוּ שָׂרִֽיד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
We conquered all his cities at that time and annihilated every city’s men, woman, and children; we left no survivor.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And we took all his cities at that time, and devoted to destruction every city, the men, and the women, and the little ones; we left none remaining:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we conquered all of his cities at that time. And we laid waste the entire city: men, women, and children; we left no remnant.


###### Deuteronomy 2:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:35)


רַ֥ק הַבְּהֵמָ֖ה בָּזַ֣זְנוּ לָ֑נוּ וּשְׁלַ֥ל הֶעָרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לָכָֽדְנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Only the animals we plundered for ourselves, and the booty of the cities that we conquered.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
only the cattle we took for a prey to ourselves, and the spoil of the cities which we took;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only the beasts did we take for ourselves, and the spoil of the cities which we conquered.


###### Deuteronomy 2:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:36)


מֵֽעֲרֹעֵ֡ר אֲשֶׁר֩ עַל־שְׂפַת־נַ֨חַל אַרְנֹ֜ן וְהָעִ֨יר אֲשֶׁ֤ר בַּנַּ֙חַל֙ וְעַד־הַגִּלְעָ֔ד לֹ֤א הָֽיְתָה֙ קִרְיָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׂגְבָ֖ה מִמֶּ֑נּוּ אֶת־הַכֹּ֕ל נָתַ֛ן יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ לְפָנֵֽינוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From Aroer, which is on the bank of Wadi Arnon—and the city that is in the wadi—up to Gilad, there was no city that was stronger than we. All of it did Adonoy, our God, defeat before us.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
From ῾Aro῾er, which is by the edge of the wadi of Arnon, and from the city that is by the wadi, as far as Gil῾ad, there was not one city too strong for us: the Lord our God delivered all to us:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From Aroer, by the bank of the stream of Arnon and the city by the stream until Gilead. There was no city which was too formidable for us. All, the L-rd our G-d delivered before us.


###### Deuteronomy 2:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/2:37)


רַ֛ק אֶל־אֶ֥רֶץ בְּנֵי־עַמּ֖וֹן לֹ֣א קָרָ֑בְתָּ כׇּל־יַ֞ד נַ֤חַל יַבֹּק֙ וְעָרֵ֣י הָהָ֔ר וְכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־צִוָּ֖ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Only the land of the descendants of Ammon did you not attack—all along Wadi Yabok and the hill cities—everything Adonoy, our God, commanded.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
only to the land of the children of ῾Ammon thou didst not come, nor to any place of the torrent of Yabboq, nor to the cities in the mountains, nor to whatever place the Lord our God forbad us.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only to the land of the children of Ammon did you not come near. All [that is] alongside the stream of Yabbok and the cities of the mountain, and all that the L-rd our G-d commanded [not to conquer, we left alone.]



## Chapter 3

###### Deuteronomy 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:1)


וַנֵּ֣פֶן וַנַּ֔עַל דֶּ֖רֶךְ הַבָּשָׁ֑ן וַיֵּצֵ֣א עוֹג֩ מֶֽלֶךְ־הַבָּשָׁ֨ן לִקְרָאתֵ֜נוּ ה֧וּא וְכׇל־עַמּ֛וֹ לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה אֶדְרֶֽעִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
We turned and went up the Boshon road. Og, King of the Boshon, went out toward us, he and his entire people, to [wage] war in Edrei.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then we turned, and went up the way to Bashan: and ῾Og the king of Bashan came out against us, he and all his people, to battle at Edre῾i.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we turned and we went up [northwards, in an ascent] by way of the Bashan, and Og the king of Bashan went out against us, he and all of his people, to war in Edrei.


###### Deuteronomy 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:2)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֤ה אֵלַי֙ אַל־תִּירָ֣א אֹת֔וֹ כִּ֣י בְיָדְךָ֞ נָתַ֧תִּי אֹת֛וֹ וְאֶת־כׇּל־עַמּ֖וֹ וְאֶת־אַרְצ֑וֹ וְעָשִׂ֣יתָ לּ֔וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֗יתָ לְסִיחֹן֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הָֽאֱמֹרִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר יוֹשֵׁ֖ב בְּחֶשְׁבּֽוֹן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Do not fear him, for I have put him in your hand, with all his people and his land, and you will do to him as you did to Sichon, King of the Emorites who was living in Cheshbon.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to me, Fear him not: for I will deliver him, and all his people, and his land, into thy hand; and thou shalt do to him as thou didst to Siĥon king of the Emori, who dwelt at Ḥeshbon.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me: Do not fear him [(Moses needing this reassurance in view of Og's having abetted Abraham (see Genesis 14:13 and Numbers 21:34)], for in your hand have I given him and all of his people and his land. And you shall do to him as you did to Sichon king of the Emori who dwelt in Cheshbon.


###### Deuteronomy 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:3)


וַיִּתֵּן֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֵ֜ינוּ בְּיָדֵ֗נוּ גַּ֛ם אֶת־ע֥וֹג מֶֽלֶךְ־הַבָּשָׁ֖ן וְאֶת־כׇּל־עַמּ֑וֹ וַנַּכֵּ֕הוּ עַד־בִּלְתִּ֥י הִשְׁאִֽיר־ל֖וֹ שָׂרִֽיד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, our God, put into our hand also Og, King of the Boshon, and his entire people; and we smote him without leaving him a survivor.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So the Lord our God delivered into our hands ῾Og also, the king of Bashan, and all his people: and we smote him until none was left to him remaining.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd our G-d gave into our hands also Og the king of Bashan and all his people. And we smote him until there did not remain of him a remnant.


###### Deuteronomy 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:4)


וַנִּלְכֹּ֤ד אֶת־כׇּל־עָרָיו֙ בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֔וא לֹ֤א הָֽיְתָה֙ קִרְיָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־לָקַ֖חְנוּ מֵֽאִתָּ֑ם שִׁשִּׁ֥ים עִיר֙ כׇּל־חֶ֣בֶל אַרְגֹּ֔ב מַמְלֶ֥כֶת ע֖וֹג בַּבָּשָֽׁן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
We conquered all his cities at that time; there was no city that we did not take from them—sixty cities, the entire province of Argov, the kingdom of Og in the Boshon.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And we took all his cities at that time, there was not a city which we took not from them, sixty cities, all the region of Argov, the kingdom of ῾Og in Bashan.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we conquered all of his cities at that time. There was not a city which we did not take from them: sixty cities, the entire province of the palace, [the province being named after the palace], the kingdom of Og in the Bashan.


###### Deuteronomy 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:5)


כׇּל־אֵ֜לֶּה עָרִ֧ים בְּצֻרֹ֛ת חוֹמָ֥ה גְבֹהָ֖ה דְּלָתַ֣יִם וּבְרִ֑יחַ לְבַ֛ד מֵעָרֵ֥י הַפְּרָזִ֖י הַרְבֵּ֥ה מְאֹֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All of these were fortified cities with high walls, gates and bolts; besides very many unwalled cities.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All these cities were fortified with high walls, gates, and bars; besides unwalled towns a great many.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All these, cities fortified with a high wall, doors, and bolt, aside from very many unwalled cities.


###### Deuteronomy 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:6)


וַנַּחֲרֵ֣ם אוֹתָ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֔ינוּ לְסִיחֹ֖ן מֶ֣לֶךְ חֶשְׁבּ֑וֹן הַחֲרֵם֙ כׇּל־עִ֣יר מְתִ֔ם הַנָּשִׁ֖ים וְהַטָּֽף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
We annihilated them as we had done to Sichon, King of Cheshbon, annihilating every city’s men, women, and children.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And we devoted them to destruction, as we did to Siĥon king of Ḥeshbon, utterly destroying the men, women, and children, of every city.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we [progressively] laid them waste, as we did to Sichon king of Cheshbon, laying waste every city: men, women, and children.


###### Deuteronomy 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:7)


וְכׇל־הַבְּהֵמָ֛ה וּשְׁלַ֥ל הֶעָרִ֖ים בַּזּ֥וֹנוּ לָֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And all the animals and the booty of the cities we plundered for ourselves.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But all the cattle, and the spoil of the cities, we took for a prey to ourselves.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every beast and the spoil of the cities we scorned [to take] for ourselves, [being already sated with the plunder of Sichon (2:35)].


###### Deuteronomy 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:8)


וַנִּקַּ֞ח בָּעֵ֤ת הַהִוא֙ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ מִיַּ֗ד שְׁנֵי֙ מַלְכֵ֣י הָאֱמֹרִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּעֵ֣בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּ֑ן מִנַּ֥חַל אַרְנֹ֖ן עַד־הַ֥ר חֶרְמֽוֹן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
At that time we took the land from the possession of the two Emorite kings who were on the other side of the Yardein, from Wadi Arnon to Mount Chermon.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And we took at that time out of the hand of the two kings of the Emori the land that was on this side of the Yarden, from the wadi of Arnon to mount Ḥermon;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we took at that time the land from the rule of the two kings of the Emori across the Jordan, from the stream of Arnon until Mount Chermon.


###### Deuteronomy 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:9)


צִידֹנִ֛ים יִקְרְא֥וּ לְחֶרְמ֖וֹן שִׂרְיֹ֑ן וְהָ֣אֱמֹרִ֔י יִקְרְאוּ־ל֖וֹ שְׂנִֽיר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The Tzidonites call Chermon Siryon, and the Emorites call it Sneer.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(which Ḥermon the Żidonim call Siryon; and the Emori call it Senir;)


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Tziddonim called Chermon Siryon, and the Emori called it S'nir ["snow" (Chermon had four names, this betokening the excellence of Eretz Yisrael, each of the four kings desiring that it be associated with him)].


###### Deuteronomy 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:10)


כֹּ֣ל**׀** עָרֵ֣י הַמִּישֹׁ֗ר וְכׇל־הַגִּלְעָד֙ וְכׇל־הַבָּשָׁ֔ן עַד־סַלְכָ֖ה וְאֶדְרֶ֑עִי עָרֵ֛י מַמְלֶ֥כֶת ע֖וֹג בַּבָּשָֽׁן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All the cities of the plain and entire Gilod and the entire Boshon to Salcho and Edre’i; cities of Og’s kingdom in the Boshon.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
all the cities of the plain, and all Gil῾ad, and all Bashan, as far as Salkha and Edre῾i, cities of the kingdom of ῾Og in Bashan.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All the cities of the plain and all of the Gilead and all of the Bashan until Salchah and Edrei, cities of the kingdom of Og in the Bashan.


###### Deuteronomy 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:11)


כִּ֣י רַק־ע֞וֹג מֶ֣לֶךְ הַבָּשָׁ֗ן נִשְׁאַר֮ מִיֶּ֣תֶר הָרְפָאִים֒ הִנֵּ֤ה עַרְשׂוֹ֙ עֶ֣רֶשׂ בַּרְזֶ֔ל הֲלֹ֣ה הִ֔וא בְּרַבַּ֖ת בְּנֵ֣י עַמּ֑וֹן תֵּ֧שַׁע אַמּ֣וֹת אׇרְכָּ֗הּ וְאַרְבַּ֥ע אַמּ֛וֹת רׇחְבָּ֖הּ בְּאַמַּת־אִֽישׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For only Og, King of the Boshon, survived from the remainder of the giants. Look, his bed—an iron bed—isn’t it in Rabbas of the descendants of Ammon: nine *amohs* its length and four *amohs* its width, in a man’s *amoh.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For only ῾Og king of Bashan remained of the remnant of the Refa᾽im; behold, his bed is a bed of iron; is it not in Rabba of the children of ῾Ammon? nine cubits is the length of it, and four cubits the breadth of it, after the cubit of a man.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For only Og king of Bashan remained from the rest of the Refaim, [whom Amrafel and his allies killed in Ashteroth Karnayim, Og alone escaping (Genesis 14:5 and 14)]. Behold, his bedstead was a bedstead of iron. Is it not (to be seen) in Rabbath B'nei Ammon? Nine cubits ("forearms") its length and four cubits its breadth in the cubit of a man [i.e., Og].

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this land, which we possessed at that time, from ῾Aro῾er, which is by the wadi Arnon, and half mount Gil῾ad, and its cities, I gave to the Re᾽uveni and to the Gadi.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this land, [from the stream of Arnon until Mount Chermon (8)] we inherited at that time. From Aroer by the stream of Arnon and half the mountain of Gilead and its cities I gave to the Reuveni and the Gadi.

עַ֠ד אֲשֶׁר־יָנִ֨יחַ יְהֹוָ֥ה׀לַֽאֲחֵיכֶם֮ כָּכֶם֒ וְיָרְשׁ֣וּ גַם־הֵ֔ם אֶת־הָאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֛ם נֹתֵ֥ן לָהֶ֖ם בְּעֵ֣בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּ֑ן וְשַׁבְתֶּ֗ם אִ֚ישׁ לִֽירֻשָּׁת֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לָכֶֽם׃

אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אַתָּ֤ה הַֽחִלּ֙וֹתָ֙ לְהַרְא֣וֹת אֶֽת־עַבְדְּךָ֔ אֶ֨ת־גׇּדְלְךָ֔ וְאֶת־יָדְךָ֖ הַחֲזָקָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר מִי־אֵל֙ בַּשָּׁמַ֣יִם וּבָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה כְמַעֲשֶׂ֖יךָ וְכִגְבוּרֹתֶֽךָ׃

‘‘Adonoy, El him, You have begun to show Your servant Your greatness and Your powerful hand, that there is no power in heaven or on earth that can perform Your deeds and Your acts of power.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O Lord God, thou hast begun to show thy servant thy greatness, and thy mighty hand: for what god is there in heaven or in earth, that can do according to thy works, and according to thy might?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
O L-rd (Yod-Keh-Vav-Keh), G-d (Elokim) [Merciful in Judgment], You began to show Your servant Your greatness and Your strong hand [in the war of Sichon and Og]; for who is Almighty in the heavens and in the earth to do as Your deeds and as Your strength? [— therefore, show me the war of the thirty-one kings (of Canaan in the conquest of Eretz Yisrael proper)].

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Deuteronomy 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:26)


וַיִּתְעַבֵּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֥ה בִּי֙ לְמַ֣עַנְכֶ֔ם וְלֹ֥א שָׁמַ֖ע אֵלָ֑י וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֤ה אֵלַי֙ רַב־לָ֔ךְ אַל־תּ֗וֹסֶף דַּבֵּ֥ר אֵלַ֛י ע֖וֹד בַּדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּֽה׃
