Adonoy grew angry at me through your fault and did not heed me; Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Enough for you! Do not go on speaking to Me any more about this matter.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the Lord was angry with me for your sakes, and would not hear me: and the Lord said to me, Let it suffice thee; speak no more to me of this matter.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd was wroth with me because of you, and He did not heed me. And the L-rd said to me: Enough for you! Presume to speak to Me no more about this thing [(so that they not say: "How intransigent the Teacher and how importunate the disciple!")]


###### Deuteronomy 3:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:27)


עֲלֵ֣ה**׀** רֹ֣אשׁ הַפִּסְגָּ֗ה וְשָׂ֥א עֵינֶ֛יךָ יָ֧מָּה וְצָפֹ֛נָה וְתֵימָ֥נָה וּמִזְרָ֖חָה וּרְאֵ֣ה בְעֵינֶ֑יךָ כִּי־לֹ֥א תַעֲבֹ֖ר אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֥ן הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Go up to the peak of the Pisgah and look around—west, north, south, and east—see with your own eyes; for you will not cross this Yardein.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go up to the top of the Pisga, and lift up thy eyes westward, and northward, and southward, and eastward, and behold with thy eyes: for thou shalt not go over this Yarden.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Go up to the top of the height and lift your eyes west and north and south and east; and see with your eyes [(You asked to be shown only the good land (25) — I will show you all of it!)], for you shall not cross this Jordan.


###### Deuteronomy 3:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:28)


וְצַ֥ו אֶת־יְהוֹשֻׁ֖עַ וְחַזְּקֵ֣הוּ וְאַמְּצֵ֑הוּ כִּי־ה֣וּא יַעֲבֹ֗ר לִפְנֵי֙ הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה וְהוּא֙ יַנְחִ֣יל אוֹתָ֔ם אֶת־הָאָ֖רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּרְאֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And command Yehoshua, and encourage him and embolden him, because he will cross at the head of this people and he will apportion to them the land that you will see.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But charge Yehoshua, and encourage him, and strengthen him: for he shall go over before this people, and he shall cause them to inherit the land which thou shalt see.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And charge Joshua [concerning their contentiousness, their heresy, and their caviling (1:12)], and strengthen him and hearten him [with your words, that he not lose heart and say: Just as my master was punished because of them (and could not enter the land), so will it be with me], for [I warrant that] he will pass over before this people and he will cause them to inherit the land that you shall see [(If he passes over before them, they will inherit it; if he does not, they will not.)]


###### Deuteronomy 3:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/3:29)


וַנֵּ֣שֶׁב בַּגָּ֔יְא מ֖וּל בֵּ֥ית פְּעֽוֹר׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
We lived in the valley facing Beis Peor.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
So we abode in the valley over against Bet-pe῾or.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we abode in the valley opposite Beth Peor [and you succumbed to idolatry — in spite of which (below): "And now, O Israel, hearken to the statutes, etc." and all will be forgiven you, whereas I did not merit such forgiveness!]



## Chapter 4

###### Deuteronomy 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:1)


וְעַתָּ֣ה יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל שְׁמַ֤ע אֶל־הַֽחֻקִּים֙ וְאֶל־הַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָֽנֹכִ֛י מְלַמֵּ֥ד אֶתְכֶ֖ם לַעֲשׂ֑וֹת לְמַ֣עַן תִּֽחְי֗וּ וּבָאתֶם֙ וִֽירִשְׁתֶּ֣ם אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י אֲבֹתֵיכֶ֖ם נֹתֵ֥ן לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And now Yisroel, listen to the statutes and to the laws that I am teaching you to fulfill, in order that you will live, and will arrive in, and will inherit the land that Adonoy, the God of your fathers, is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore hearken, O Yisra᾽el, to the statutes and to the judgments, which I teach you, to do them, that you may live, and go in and possess the land which the Lord God of your fathers gives you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, O Israel, hearken to the statutes and to the judgments which I teach you to do, so that you may live and come and inherit the land which the L-rd, the G-d of your fathers, gives to you.


###### Deuteronomy 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:2)


לֹ֣א תֹסִ֗פוּ עַל־הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר אָנֹכִי֙ מְצַוֶּ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֔ם וְלֹ֥א תִגְרְע֖וּ מִמֶּ֑נּוּ לִשְׁמֹ֗ר אֶת־מִצְוֺת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י מְצַוֶּ֥ה אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not add to the matters that I am commanding you, and do not subtract from them; to guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, that I am commanding you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not add to the word which I command you, neither shall you diminish from it, that you may keep the commandments of the Lord your God which I command you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not add to the thing that I command you [e.g., placing five sections (instead of four) in the tefillin], and you shall not diminish therefrom, to heed the mitzvoth of the L-rd your G-d, which I command you.


###### Deuteronomy 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:3)


עֵֽינֵיכֶם֙ הָֽרֹא֔וֹת אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּבַ֣עַל פְּע֑וֹר כִּ֣י כׇל־הָאִ֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר הָלַךְ֙ אַחֲרֵ֣י בַֽעַל־פְּע֔וֹר הִשְׁמִיד֛וֹ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ מִקִּרְבֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Your eyes have witnessed what Adonoy did regarding Ba’al Peor, that every man who followed Ba’al Peor—Adonoy, your God, destroyed him from within you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Your eyes have seen what the Lord did because of Ba῾al-pe῾or: for all the men that followed Ba῾al-pe῾or, the Lord thy God destroyed them from among you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
(For it is) your (own) eyes that have seen what the L-rd did with (the worshippers of) Ba'al Peor. For every man that went after Ba'al Peor — the L-rd your G-d destroyed him from your midst.


###### Deuteronomy 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:4)


וְאַתֶּם֙ הַדְּבֵקִ֔ים בַּיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם חַיִּ֥ים כֻּלְּכֶ֖ם הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But you who cling to Adonoy, your God—all of you are alive today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But you that did cleave of the Lord your God are alive every one of you this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But you who cleaved to the L-rd your G-d are all alive this day.


###### Deuteronomy 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:5)


רְאֵ֣ה**׀** לִמַּ֣דְתִּי אֶתְכֶ֗ם חֻקִּים֙ וּמִשְׁפָּטִ֔ים כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוַּ֖נִי יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֑י לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת כֵּ֔ן בְּקֶ֣רֶב הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתֶּ֛ם בָּאִ֥ים שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
See, I have taught you statutes and laws as I was commanded by Adonoy, my God, to fulfill within the land that you are arriving there to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I have taught you statutes and judgments, even as the Lord my God commanded me, that you should act accordingly in the land whither you go to possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
See, I have taught you statutes and judgments as the L-rd my G-d commanded me, to do thus in the midst of the land whither you come to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:6)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם֮ וַעֲשִׂיתֶם֒ כִּ֣י הִ֤וא חׇכְמַתְכֶם֙ וּבִ֣ינַתְכֶ֔ם לְעֵינֵ֖י הָעַמִּ֑ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִשְׁמְע֗וּן אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הַחֻקִּ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה וְאָמְר֗וּ רַ֚ק עַם־חָכָ֣ם וְנָב֔וֹן הַגּ֥וֹי הַגָּד֖וֹל הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will preserve and you will fulfill [them], for that [displays] your wisdom and your understanding before the nations, who will hear all these statutes and will say, ‘‘The only wise and understanding people is this great nation.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Keep them therefore and do them; for this is your wisdom and your understanding in the sight of the nations, who shall hear all these statutes, and say, Surely this great nation is a wise and understanding people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall heed [This is mishnah (learning)], and you shall do [what you have learned]. For this is your wisdom and your understanding [i.e., You will thereby be accounted wise and understanding] in the eyes of the peoples, who will hear of all these statutes and who will say: Surely, a wise and understanding people is this great nation.


###### Deuteronomy 4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:7)


כִּ֚י מִי־ג֣וֹי גָּד֔וֹל אֲשֶׁר־ל֥וֹ אֱלֹהִ֖ים קְרֹבִ֣ים אֵלָ֑יו כַּיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ בְּכׇל־קׇרְאֵ֖נוּ אֵלָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For who is a great nation that has God close to it as [is] Adonoy, our God, whenever we call to Him?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For what nation is there so great, that has God so near to them, as the Lord our God is in all things that we call upon him for?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For who is a great nation which has a G-d near to it as the L-rd our G-d in all of our calling to Him?


###### Deuteronomy 4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:8)


וּמִי֙ גּ֣וֹי גָּד֔וֹל אֲשֶׁר־ל֛וֹ חֻקִּ֥ים וּמִשְׁפָּטִ֖ים צַדִּיקִ֑ם כְּכֹל֙ הַתּוֹרָ֣ה הַזֹּ֔את אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י נֹתֵ֥ן לִפְנֵיכֶ֖ם הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And who is a great nation that has upright statutes and laws, like this entire Torah that I am putting before you today?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And what nation is there so great, that has statutes and judgments so righteous as all this Tora, which I set before you this day?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And who is a great nation which has righteous statutes and judgments as all of this Torah which I place before you this day?


###### Deuteronomy 4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:9)


רַ֡ק הִשָּׁ֣מֶר לְךָ֩ וּשְׁמֹ֨ר נַפְשְׁךָ֜ מְאֹ֗ד פֶּן־תִּשְׁכַּ֨ח אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֜ים אֲשֶׁר־רָא֣וּ עֵינֶ֗יךָ וּפֶן־יָס֙וּרוּ֙ מִלְּבָ֣בְךָ֔ כֹּ֖ל יְמֵ֣י חַיֶּ֑יךָ וְהוֹדַעְתָּ֥ם לְבָנֶ֖יךָ וְלִבְנֵ֥י בָנֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look out for yourself and guard your life exceedingly, lest you forget the words your eyes witnessed, and lest they are removed from your mind, all the days of your life; you will make them known to your children and to your grandchildren.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only take heed to thyself, and keep thy soul diligently, lest thou forget the things which thy eyes have seen, and lest they depart from thy heart all the days of thy life: but teach them thy sons, and thy sons’ sons—


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only take heed to yourself, and heed your soul exceedingly, lest you forget the things which your eyes saw, and lest they depart from your heart all the days of your life. [Only if you do not forget them and do them as prescribed will you be thought wise and understanding; but if you distort them through forgetfulness, you will be accounted foolish.] And you shall impart them to your sons and to the sons of your sons.


###### Deuteronomy 4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:10)


י֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָמַ֜דְתָּ לִפְנֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֘יךָ֮ בְּחֹרֵב֒ בֶּאֱמֹ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י הַקְהֶל־לִי֙ אֶת־הָעָ֔ם וְאַשְׁמִעֵ֖ם אֶת־דְּבָרָ֑י אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִלְמְד֜וּן לְיִרְאָ֣ה אֹתִ֗י כׇּל־הַיָּמִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֵ֤ם חַיִּים֙ עַל־הָ֣אֲדָמָ֔ה וְאֶת־בְּנֵיהֶ֖ם יְלַמֵּדֽוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The day you stood before Adonoy, your God, at Choreiv, when Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Assemble for Me the people, and I will let them hear My statements, so that they will learn to fear Me all the years that they are living on the land, and will teach their sons.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the day that thou stoodest before the Lord thy God in Ḥorev, when the Lord said to me, Gather me the people together, and I will make them hear my words, that they may learn to fear me all the days that they shall live upon the earth, and that they may teach their children.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
["Lest you forget the things (the sounds and the lightnings) which your eyes saw" on] the day when you stood before the L-rd your G-d in Chorev, when the L-rd said to me: Assemble the people unto Me, and I shall make them hear My words, so that they learn to fear Me all the days that they live on the earth, and so that they teach their children.

וַתִּקְרְב֥וּן וַתַּֽעַמְד֖וּן תַּ֣חַת הָהָ֑ר וְהָהָ֞ר בֹּעֵ֤ר בָּאֵשׁ֙ עַד־לֵ֣ב הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם חֹ֖שֶׁךְ עָנָ֥ן וַעֲרָפֶֽל׃

You approached and stood beneath the mountain, while the mountain was burning with fire up to the heart of the heavens—darkness, cloud, and thick cloud.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you drew near and you stood at the foot of the mountain, and the mountain burned in fire until the heart of the heavens: darkness, cloud, and mist.


###### Deuteronomy 4:12

וַיְדַבֵּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֲלֵיכֶ֖ם מִתּ֣וֹךְ הָאֵ֑שׁ ק֤וֹל דְּבָרִים֙ אַתֶּ֣ם שֹׁמְעִ֔ים וּתְמוּנָ֛ה אֵינְכֶ֥ם רֹאִ֖ים זוּלָתִ֥י קֽוֹל׃

וְאֹתִ֞י צִוָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֔וא לְלַמֵּ֣ד אֶתְכֶ֔ם חֻקִּ֖ים וּמִשְׁפָּטִ֑ים לַעֲשֹׂתְכֶ֣ם אֹתָ֔ם בָּאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתֶּ֛ם עֹבְרִ֥ים שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃

###### Deuteronomy 4:15

וְנִשְׁמַרְתֶּ֥ם מְאֹ֖ד לְנַפְשֹׁתֵיכֶ֑ם כִּ֣י לֹ֤א רְאִיתֶם֙ כׇּל־תְּמוּנָ֔ה בְּי֗וֹם דִּבֶּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֧ה אֲלֵיכֶ֛ם בְּחֹרֵ֖ב מִתּ֥וֹךְ הָאֵֽשׁ׃

Be extremely cautious for your lives, for you did not see any image on the day Adonoy addressed you at Choreiv from within the fire.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

פֶּ֨ן־תַּשְׁחִת֔וּן וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֥ם לָכֶ֛ם פֶּ֖סֶל תְּמוּנַ֣ת כׇּל־סָ֑מֶל תַּבְנִ֥ית זָכָ֖ר א֥וֹ נְקֵבָֽה׃

Lest you destructively make for yourselves a statue in the image of any form, the representation of a male or a female;

lest you become corrupt, and make a carved idol, the similitude of any figure, the likeness of male or female,

###### Deuteronomy 4:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:17)


תַּבְנִ֕ית כׇּל־בְּהֵמָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּאָ֑רֶץ תַּבְנִית֙ כׇּל־צִפּ֣וֹר כָּנָ֔ף אֲשֶׁ֥ר תָּע֖וּף בַּשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[or] the representation of any animal that is on earth; [or] the representation of any winged bird that flies in the sky;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the likeness of any beast that is on the earth, the likeness of any winged bird that flies in the air,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The figure of any beast that is on the earth, the figure of any winged bird which flies in the heaven,


###### Deuteronomy 4:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:18)


תַּבְנִ֕ית כׇּל־רֹמֵ֖שׂ בָּאֲדָמָ֑ה תַּבְנִ֛ית כׇּל־דָּגָ֥ה אֲשֶׁר־בַּמַּ֖יִם מִתַּ֥חַת לָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
[or] the representation of anything that crawls on the ground; [or] the representation of any fish that is in the water beneath the earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the likeness of any thing that creeps on the ground, the likeness of any fish that is in the waters beneath the earth:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The figure of any thing that creeps on the ground, the figure of any fish that is in the waters beneath the earth.


###### Deuteronomy 4:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:19)


וּפֶן־תִּשָּׂ֨א עֵינֶ֜יךָ הַשָּׁמַ֗יְמָה וְֽ֠רָאִ֠יתָ אֶת־הַשֶּׁ֨מֶשׁ וְאֶת־הַיָּרֵ֜חַ וְאֶת־הַכּֽוֹכָבִ֗ים כֹּ֚ל צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְנִדַּחְתָּ֛ וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוִ֥יתָ לָהֶ֖ם וַעֲבַדְתָּ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר חָלַ֜ק יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ אֹתָ֔ם לְכֹל֙ הָֽעַמִּ֔ים תַּ֖חַת כׇּל־הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or lest you raise your eyes heavenward and see the sun or the moon or the stars—the entire host of the heavens—and you will err and bow to them and worship them, when Adonoy, your God, has made them available to all the peoples who are under the whole sky.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and lest thou lift up thy eyes to heaven, and when thou seest the sun, and the moon, and the stars, all the host of heaven, thou shouldst be misled to worship them, and serve them, which the Lord thy God has allotted to all the nations under the whole heaven.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And lest you lift your eyes to the heavens [to gaze upon the luminaries and to stray after them], and you see the sun and the moon and the stars — all the host of heaven — and you go astray and bow down to them and serve them, which the L-rd your G-d allotted to all the peoples under all of the heavens [to provide illumination for them].


###### Deuteronomy 4:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:20)


וְאֶתְכֶם֙ לָקַ֣ח יְהֹוָ֔ה וַיּוֹצִ֥א אֶתְכֶ֛ם מִכּ֥וּר הַבַּרְזֶ֖ל מִמִּצְרָ֑יִם לִהְי֥וֹת ל֛וֹ לְעַ֥ם נַחֲלָ֖ה כַּיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But Adonoy took you [for Himself] when He took you out of the iron crucible, from Egypt, to be for Him a people-territory like this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the Lord has taken you, and brought you out of the iron furnace, out of Miżrayim, to be to him a people of inheritance, as you are this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you did the L-rd take, and He brought you forth from the iron crucible, from Egypt, to be to Him a people of inheritance as of this day.


###### Deuteronomy 4:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:21)


וַֽיהֹוָ֥ה הִתְאַנַּף־בִּ֖י עַל־דִּבְרֵיכֶ֑ם וַיִּשָּׁבַ֗ע לְבִלְתִּ֤י עׇבְרִי֙ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן וּלְבִלְתִּי־בֹא֙ אֶל־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַטּוֹבָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ נַחֲלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Yet Adonoy grew angry at me on account of your statements, and He swore that I would not cross the Yardein and would not enter the good land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the Lord was angry with me for your sakes, and swore that I should not go over the Yarden, and that I should not go in to that good land, which the Lord thy God gives thee for an inheritance:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd was wroth with me because of your deeds, and He swore that I would not cross the Jordan and that I would not come to the good land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you as an inheritance.


###### Deuteronomy 4:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:22)


כִּ֣י אָנֹכִ֥י מֵת֙ בָּאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֔את אֵינֶ֥נִּי עֹבֵ֖ר אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֑ן וְאַתֶּם֙ עֹֽבְרִ֔ים וִֽירִשְׁתֶּ֕ם אֶת־הָאָ֥רֶץ הַטּוֹבָ֖ה הַזֹּֽאת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For I am to die in this land, I am not crossing the Yardein; but you are crossing, and will inherit this good land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but I must die in this land, I must not go over the Yarden: but you shall go over, and possess that good land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I shall die in this land; I [i.e., even my bones] shall not cross the Jordan. And you shall cross and inherit this good land.


###### Deuteronomy 4:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:23)


הִשָּׁמְר֣וּ לָכֶ֗ם פֶּֽן־תִּשְׁכְּחוּ֙ אֶת־בְּרִ֤ית יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹ֣הֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר כָּרַ֖ת עִמָּכֶ֑ם וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֨ם לָכֶ֥ם פֶּ֙סֶל֙ תְּמ֣וּנַת כֹּ֔ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוְּךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look out for yourselves lest you forget the covenant of Adonoy, your God, that He made with you, and you make for yourselves a statue—an image of anything—that Adonoy, your God, commanded you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take heed to yourselves, lest you forget the covenant of the Lord your God, which he made with you, and make you a carved idol, or the likeness of anything, which the Lord thy God has forbidden thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourselves lest you forget the covenant of the L-rd your G-d that He made with you and you make for yourselves a graven image of the likeness of any [thing] that the L-rd your G-d has commanded you [not to make].


###### Deuteronomy 4:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:24)


כִּ֚י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ אֵ֥שׁ אֹכְלָ֖ה ה֑וּא אֵ֖ל קַנָּֽא׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For Adonoy, your God, is a consuming fire, a jealous Almighty.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord thy God is a consuming fire, a jealous God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the L-rd your G-d is a consuming fire, a jealous G-d ["jealous" in vengeance to exact punishment of idolators].


###### Deuteronomy 4:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:25)


כִּֽי־תוֹלִ֤יד בָּנִים֙ וּבְנֵ֣י בָנִ֔ים וְנוֹשַׁנְתֶּ֖ם בָּאָ֑רֶץ וְהִשְׁחַתֶּ֗ם וַעֲשִׂ֤יתֶם פֶּ֙סֶל֙ תְּמ֣וּנַת כֹּ֔ל וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֥ם הָרַ֛ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָה־אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ לְהַכְעִיסֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you have children and grandchildren, and have grown old in the land; and you will be corrupt and make a statue—an image of anything—and do what is evil in the eyes of Adonoy, your God, to anger Him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou shalt beget children, and children’s children, and you shall have remained long in the land, and shall deal corruptly, and make a carved idol, the likeness of anything, and shall do evil in the sight of the Lord thy God, to provoke him to anger:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you bear sons and sons of sons and grow old (venoshantem) in the land [(an intimation that they would be exiled at the end of 852 years (this being the gematria of "venoshantem"). But He exiled them two years before, so that they not be subject to "you will go utterly lost" (26)], and you corrupt yourselves and you make a graven image of the likeness of any [thing], and you do what is evil in the eyes of the L-rd your G-d to anger Him,


###### Deuteronomy 4:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:26)


הַעִידֹ֩תִי֩ בָכֶ֨ם הַיּ֜וֹם אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם וְאֶת־הָאָ֗רֶץ כִּֽי־אָבֹ֣ד תֹּאבֵדוּן֮ מַהֵר֒ מֵעַ֣ל הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אַתֶּ֜ם עֹבְרִ֧ים אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֛ן שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּ֑הּ לֹֽא־תַאֲרִיכֻ֤ן יָמִים֙ עָלֶ֔יהָ כִּ֥י הִשָּׁמֵ֖ד תִּשָּׁמֵדֽוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I bring as witness against you today heaven and earth, that you will be completely and swiftly removed from upon the land, that you are crossing the Yardein there to inherit; you will not live long upon it, but will be completely destroyed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I call heaven and earth to witness against you this day, that you shall soon utterly perish from off the land into which you go over the Yarden to possess it; you shall not prolong your days upon it, but shall utterly be destroyed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I call heaven and earth to witness against you this day [that I have warned you] that you will go utterly lost, quickly, from the land whither you are crossing the Jordan to inherit it. You will not prolong days upon it, for you will be utterly destroyed.


###### Deuteronomy 4:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:27)


וְהֵפִ֧יץ יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם בָּעַמִּ֑ים וְנִשְׁאַרְתֶּם֙ מְתֵ֣י מִסְפָּ֔ר בַּגּוֹיִ֕ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְנַהֵ֧ג יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם שָֽׁמָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will disperse you among the peoples, and you will remain few in number among the nations that Adonoy leads you to.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord shall scatter you among the nations, and you shall be left few in number among the nations, where the Lord shall lead you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd will scatter you among the peoples, and you will remain few in number among the nations whither the L-rd will lead you.


###### Deuteronomy 4:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:28)


וַעֲבַדְתֶּם־שָׁ֣ם אֱלֹהִ֔ים מַעֲשֵׂ֖ה יְדֵ֣י אָדָ֑ם עֵ֣ץ וָאֶ֔בֶן אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־יִרְאוּן֙ וְלֹ֣א יִשְׁמְע֔וּן וְלֹ֥א יֹֽאכְל֖וּן וְלֹ֥א יְרִיחֻֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
There you will serve man-made gods of wood and stone that do not see, do not hear, do not eat, and do not smell.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there you shall serve gods, the work of men’s hands, wood and stone, which neither see, nor hear, nor eat, nor smell.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will serve gods there [(By serving their worshippers, it is as if you serve them)], the work of the hands of man, wood and stone, which do not see and do not hear and do not eat and do not smell.


###### Deuteronomy 4:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:29)


וּבִקַּשְׁתֶּ֥ם מִשָּׁ֛ם אֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ וּמָצָ֑אתָ כִּ֣י תִדְרְשֶׁ֔נּוּ בְּכׇל־לְבָבְךָ֖ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will seek from there Adonoy, your God, and you will find [Him], when you seek Him wholeheartedly and with your whole being.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if from there thou shalt seek the Lord thy God, thou shalt find him, if thou seek him with all thy heart and with all thy soul.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will seek from there the L-rd your G-d, and you will find Him if you seek Him with all your heart and with all your soul.


###### Deuteronomy 4:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:30)


בַּצַּ֣ר לְךָ֔ וּמְצָא֕וּךָ כֹּ֖ל הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה בְּאַחֲרִית֙ הַיָּמִ֔ים וְשַׁבְתָּ֙ עַד־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וְשָׁמַעְתָּ֖ בְּקֹלֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you are in distress and all these things happen to you, at the end of time, you will return to Adonoy, your God, and will obey Him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou art in distress, and all these things are come upon thee, in the latter days, if thou turn to the Lord thy God, and art obedient to his voice;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In your affliction, when all these things come upon you, in the end of days, then you shall return to the L-rd your G-d and you shall hearken to His voice.


###### Deuteronomy 4:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:31)


כִּ֣י אֵ֤ל רַחוּם֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לֹ֥א יַרְפְּךָ֖ וְלֹ֣א יַשְׁחִיתֶ֑ךָ וְלֹ֤א יִשְׁכַּח֙ אֶת־בְּרִ֣ית אֲבֹתֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִשְׁבַּ֖ע לָהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For a merciful Almighty is Adonoy, your God; He will not forsake you nor destroy you; nor will He forget the covenant of your fathers that He swore to them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(for the Lord thy God is a merciful God;) he will not forsake thee, nor will he destroy thee, nor forget the covenant of thy fathers which he swore to them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a G-d of mercy is the L-rd your G-d. He will not let go of you and He will not destroy you, and He will not forget the covenant of your fathers, which He swore to them.


###### Deuteronomy 4:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:32)


כִּ֣י שְׁאַל־נָא֩ לְיָמִ֨ים רִֽאשֹׁנִ֜ים אֲשֶׁר־הָי֣וּ לְפָנֶ֗יךָ לְמִן־הַיּוֹם֙ אֲשֶׁר֩ בָּרָ֨א אֱלֹהִ֤ים׀אָדָם֙ עַל־הָאָ֔רֶץ וּלְמִקְצֵ֥ה הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וְעַד־קְצֵ֣ה הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם הֲנִֽהְיָ֗ה כַּדָּבָ֤ר הַגָּדוֹל֙ הַזֶּ֔ה א֖וֹ הֲנִשְׁמַ֥ע כָּמֹֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Inquire now about the early years that preceded you, from the day that God created Adam on earth and from one end of the heavens to the other end of the heavens: Did anything ever happen comparable to this great event, or did anyone ever hear of such a thing?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For ask now of the days that are past, which were before thee, since the day that God created man upon the earth, and from the one side of heaven to the other, whether there has been any such thing as this great thing is, or whether aught has been heard like it?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For ask, now, concerning the first days which were before you, from the day G-d created man upon the earth, [and ask also of all the creations] from one end of the heavens to the other end: Has there ever been the like of this great thing or has its like ever been heard?


###### Deuteronomy 4:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:33)


הֲשָׁ֣מַֽע עָם֩ ק֨וֹל אֱלֹהִ֜ים מְדַבֵּ֧ר מִתּוֹךְ־הָאֵ֛שׁ כַּאֲשֶׁר־שָׁמַ֥עְתָּ אַתָּ֖ה וַיֶּֽחִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Did any people [ever] hear God’s voice speaking from within the fire as you heard and survive?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Did ever people hear the voice of God speaking out of the midst of the fire, as thou hast heard, and live?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[the "great thing":] Has a people ever heard the voice of G-d speaking from the midst of the fire as you have and lived?


###### Deuteronomy 4:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:34)


א֣וֹ**׀** הֲנִסָּ֣ה אֱלֹהִ֗ים לָ֠ב֠וֹא לָקַ֨חַת ל֣וֹ גוֹי֮ מִקֶּ֣רֶב גּוֹי֒ בְּמַסֹּת֩ בְּאֹתֹ֨ת וּבְמוֹפְתִ֜ים וּבְמִלְחָמָ֗ה וּבְיָ֤ד חֲזָקָה֙ וּבִזְר֣וֹעַ נְטוּיָ֔ה וּבְמוֹרָאִ֖ים גְּדֹלִ֑ים כְּ֠כֹ֠ל אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֨ה לָכֶ֜ם יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֛ם בְּמִצְרַ֖יִם לְעֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or did any god ever miraculously come and take for himself a nation from within a nation through tests, with signs and with wonders, and through warfare, and with a strong hand and with an extended arm, and with great displays; entirely as Adonoy, your God, did for you in Egypt as you watched?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Or has God ventured to go and take him a nation from the midst of another nation, by trials, by signs, and by wonders, and by war, and by a mighty hand, and by a stretched out arm, and by great terrors, according to all that the Lord your God did for you in Miżrayim before your eyes?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Or has a god ever done miracles to come and take for himself a nation from the midst of a nation with [self-imposed] trials, [by which He manifested His strength, e.g., (Exodus 8:5): "Presume to test me, etc." to see whether I can do so], with signs, [confirming Moses as G-d's messenger, e.g., (Ibid. 4:2): "What is this in your hand, etc."], and with wonders, [such as the "wondrous" plagues], and with war, [at the Red Sea, viz. (Ibid. 14:25): "For the L-rd wars for them"], and with a strong hand, and with an outstretched arm, and with great terrors, as all that the L-rd your G-d did for you in Egypt before your eyes?


###### Deuteronomy 4:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:35)


אַתָּה֙ הׇרְאֵ֣תָ לָדַ֔עַת כִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה ה֣וּא הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים אֵ֥ין ע֖וֹד מִלְּבַדּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You have been shown that you might know that Adonoy, He is *the* God; there is no [power] other than He.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To thee it was shown, that thou mightest know that the Lord he is God; there is none else beside him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You have been shown to know that the L-rd, He is G-d; there is none beside Him. [When the Holy One Blessed be He gave the Torah, He opened the seven heavens and the lower regions, and they saw that He was One].


###### Deuteronomy 4:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:36)


מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֛יִם הִשְׁמִֽיעֲךָ֥ אֶת־קֹל֖וֹ לְיַסְּרֶ֑ךָּ וְעַל־הָאָ֗רֶץ הֶרְאֲךָ֙ אֶת־אִשּׁ֣וֹ הַגְּדוֹלָ֔ה וּדְבָרָ֥יו שָׁמַ֖עְתָּ מִתּ֥וֹךְ הָאֵֽשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From the sky He made audible to you His voice to teach you, and on the earth He showed you His great fire, and you heard His words form within the fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Out of heaven he made thee hear his voice, that he might instruct thee: and upon earth he showed thee his great fire; and thou didst hear his words out of the midst of the fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From the heavens He made you hear His voice to exhort you, and on the earth He showed you His great fire, and His words you heard from the midst of the fire.


###### Deuteronomy 4:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:37)


וְתַ֗חַת כִּ֤י אָהַב֙ אֶת־אֲבֹתֶ֔יךָ וַיִּבְחַ֥ר בְּזַרְע֖וֹ אַחֲרָ֑יו וַיּוֹצִֽאֲךָ֧ בְּפָנָ֛יו בְּכֹח֥וֹ הַגָּדֹ֖ל מִמִּצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Because He loved your forefathers, He chose his descendants after him; and He took you out before Him with His great strength from Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And because he loved thy fathers, therefore, he chose their seed after them, and brought thee out, he himself being present, with his mighty power out of Miżrayim;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And [all this] because He loved your fathers and He chose their seed after them; and He took you out before Him [as a father leads his son before him (see Exodus 14:19)] with His great strength from Egypt.


###### Deuteronomy 4:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:38)


לְהוֹרִ֗ישׁ גּוֹיִ֛ם גְּדֹלִ֧ים וַעֲצֻמִ֛ים מִמְּךָ֖ מִפָּנֶ֑יךָ לַהֲבִֽיאֲךָ֗ לָֽתֶת־לְךָ֧ אֶת־אַרְצָ֛ם נַחֲלָ֖ה כַּיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃

To expel nations greater and stronger than you from before you; to bring you in [and] to give you their land as terrritory, like this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to drive out nations from before thee greater and mightier than thou art, to bring thee in, to give thee their land for an inheritance, as it is this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To drive out before you nations greater and mightier than you, to bring you, to give to you their land as an inheritance as [you see] this day.


###### Deuteronomy 4:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:39)


וְיָדַעְתָּ֣ הַיּ֗וֹם וַהֲשֵׁבֹתָ֮ אֶל־לְבָבֶ֒ךָ֒ כִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ ה֣וּא הָֽאֱלֹהִ֔ים בַּשָּׁמַ֣יִם מִמַּ֔עַל וְעַל־הָאָ֖רֶץ מִתָּ֑חַת אֵ֖ין עֽוֹד׃

You will know today, and will restore to your perception that Adonoy is *the* God in heaven above and on earth below; there is no other.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Know therefore this day, and consider it in thy heart, that the Lord he is God in heaven above, and upon the earth beneath: there is no other.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall know this day, and you shall set it to your heart, that the L-rd, He is G-d, in the heavens above and on the earth below; there is none else.


###### Deuteronomy 4:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:40)


וְשָׁמַרְתָּ֞ אֶת־חֻקָּ֣יו וְאֶת־מִצְוֺתָ֗יו אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֤י מְצַוְּךָ֙ הַיּ֔וֹם אֲשֶׁר֙ יִיטַ֣ב לְךָ֔ וּלְבָנֶ֖יךָ אַחֲרֶ֑יךָ וּלְמַ֨עַן תַּאֲרִ֤יךְ יָמִים֙ עַל־הָ֣אֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will guard His statutes and His commandments, which I am commanding you today, so that you have it good and your children after you, and in order that you live long on the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you for all time.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt keep therefore his statutes, and his commandments, which I command thee this day, that it may go well with thee, and with thy children after thee, and that thou mayst prolong thy days upon the earth, which the Lord thy God gives thee, for ever.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall keep His statutes and His mitzvoth which I command you this day, which will confer good upon you and your children after you, and so that you prolong days upon the land that the L-rd your G-d gives you all of the days.


###### Deuteronomy 4:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/4:41)


אָ֣ז יַבְדִּ֤יל מֹשֶׁה֙ שָׁלֹ֣שׁ עָרִ֔ים בְּעֵ֖בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּ֑ן מִזְרְחָ֖ה שָֽׁמֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Then Moshe set aside three cities across the Yardein, where the sun rises,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

###### Deuteronomy 4:42

לָנֻ֨ס שָׁ֜מָּה רוֹצֵ֗חַ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִרְצַ֤ח אֶת־רֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ בִּבְלִי־דַ֔עַת וְה֛וּא לֹא־שֹׂנֵ֥א ל֖וֹ מִתְּמֹ֣ל שִׁלְשֹׁ֑ם וְנָ֗ס אֶל־אַחַ֛ת מִן־הֶעָרִ֥ים הָאֵ֖ל וָחָֽי׃

for a murderer to escape there, who has murdered his neighbor without intent, and he had not been his enemy yesterday [or] the day before. He is to flee to one of these cities to survive:

that the slayer might flee there, who should kill his neighbour unawares, and hated him not in times past; and that fleeing unto one of these cities he might live:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To flee there a slayer who slays his neighbor unwittingly, not having hated him heretofore; and he shall flee to one of these cities and he shall live.

Betzer in the wilderness in the plains of the Reuvenites, Romos in the Gil’od of the Gaddites, and Golon in the Boshon of the Menashians.

Beżer in the wilderness, in the plain country, for the Re᾽uveni; and Ramot of Gil῾ad, for the Gadi; and Golan in Bashan, for the Menashshi.

בְּעֵ֨בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּ֜ן בַּגַּ֗יְא מ֚וּל בֵּ֣ית פְּע֔וֹר בְּאֶ֗רֶץ סִיחֹן֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ הָֽאֱמֹרִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר יוֹשֵׁ֖ב בְּחֶשְׁבּ֑וֹן אֲשֶׁ֨ר הִכָּ֤ה מֹשֶׁה֙ וּבְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בְּצֵאתָ֖ם מִמִּצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Across the Yardein, in the valley facing Beis Peor, in the land of Sichon, King of the Emorites, who lived in Cheshbon, whom Moshe and Bnei Yisrael smote when they came out of Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Beyond the Yarden, in the valley over against Bet-pe῾or, in the land of Siĥon king of the Emori, who dwelt at Ḥeshbon, whom Moshe and the children of Yisra᾽el smote, after they were come forth out of Miżrayim:

They inherited his land and the land of Og, King of the Boshon—the two Emorite kings who were across the Yardein, where the sun rises.

and they possessed his land, and the land of ῾Og king of Bashan, two kings of the Emori, which were beyond the Yarden toward the sun rising;

מֵעֲרֹעֵ֞ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר עַל־שְׂפַת־נַ֧חַל אַרְנֹ֛ן וְעַד־הַ֥ר שִׂיאֹ֖ן ה֥וּא חֶרְמֽוֹן׃

From Aroer, which is on the bank of Wadi Arnon, up to Mount Sion which is Chermon.

from ῾Aro῾er, which is by the bank of the wadi of Arnon, to mount Si’on, which is Ḥermon,

וְכׇל־הָ֨עֲרָבָ֜ה עֵ֤בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן֙ מִזְרָ֔חָה וְעַ֖ד יָ֣ם הָעֲרָבָ֑ה תַּ֖חַת אַשְׁדֹּ֥ת הַפִּסְגָּֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the entire Arava across the Yardein to the east, to Arava Lake, beneath the slopes of the Pisgah.

## Chapter 5

###### Deuteronomy 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:1)


וַיִּקְרָ֣א מֹשֶׁה֮ אֶל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֒ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם שְׁמַ֤ע יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ אֶת־הַחֻקִּ֣ים וְאֶת־הַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י דֹּבֵ֥ר בְּאׇזְנֵיכֶ֖ם הַיּ֑וֹם וּלְמַדְתֶּ֣ם אֹתָ֔ם וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֖ם לַעֲשֹׂתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe called all of Yisroel and said to them, ‘‘Hear, Yisroel, the statutes and the laws that I am relating in your presence today, for you are to study them and to be careful to fulfill them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe called all Yisra᾽el, and said to them, Hear, O Yisra᾽el, the statutes and judgments which I speak in your ears this day, that you may learn them, and keep, and do them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses called to all of Israel and he said to them: Hear, O Israel, the statutes and the judgments which I speak in your ears this day, and learn and observe them to do them.


###### Deuteronomy 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:2)


יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֗ינוּ כָּרַ֥ת עִמָּ֛נוּ בְּרִ֖ית בְּחֹרֵֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, our God, made a covenant with us at Choreiv.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord our God made a covenant with us in Ḥorev.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd our G-d made a covenant with us in Chorev.


###### Deuteronomy 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:3)


לֹ֣א אֶת־אֲבֹתֵ֔ינוּ כָּרַ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־הַבְּרִ֣ית הַזֹּ֑את כִּ֣י אִתָּ֔נוּ אֲנַ֨חְנוּ אֵ֥לֶּה פֹ֛ה הַיּ֖וֹם כֻּלָּ֥נוּ חַיִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Not [only] with our fathers did Adonoy make this covenant, but with us, we who are here today, all of us alive.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord made not this covenant with our fathers, but with us, even us, who are all of us here alive this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Not with our fathers [alone] did the L-rd make this covenant, but with us — we — these who are here today, all of us living.


###### Deuteronomy 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:4)


פָּנִ֣ים**׀** בְּפָנִ֗ים דִּבֶּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֧ה עִמָּכֶ֛ם בָּהָ֖ר מִתּ֥וֹךְ הָאֵֽשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Face to face Adonoy spoke with you at the mountain from within the fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord talked with you face to face in the mountain out of the midst of the fire,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Face to face [and not through an intermediary (whom you might accuse of dissimulation)] did the L-rd speak with you in the mountain from the midst of the fire.


###### Deuteronomy 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:5)


אָ֠נֹכִ֠י עֹמֵ֨ד בֵּין־יְהֹוָ֤ה וּבֵֽינֵיכֶם֙ בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֔וא לְהַגִּ֥יד לָכֶ֖ם אֶת־דְּבַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּ֤י יְרֵאתֶם֙ מִפְּנֵ֣י הָאֵ֔שׁ וְלֹֽא־עֲלִיתֶ֥ם בָּהָ֖ר לֵאמֹֽר׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I was standing between Adonoy and you at that time, to tell you Adonoy’s word, because you were afraid of the fire and did not go up on the mountain, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(I stood between the Lord and you at that time, to show you the word of the Lord: for you were afraid by reason of the fire, and went not up to the mountain;) saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I stood between the L-rd and you at that time to relate to you the word of the L-rd, for you were awed before the fire and you did not ascend the mountain. [(4): "…did the L-rd speak with you"], saying:


###### Deuteronomy 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:6)


אָֽנֹכִ֖י֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֧ר הוֹצֵאתִ֛יךָ מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרַ֖יִם מִבֵּ֣֥ית עֲבָדִ֑͏ֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘I am Adonoy, your God, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, the house of slavery.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I am the Lord thy God, who brought thee out of the land of Miżrayim, from the house of bondage.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[see Exodus 20:1-14] I am the L-rd your G-d who took you out of the land of Egypt from the house of bondage.


###### Deuteronomy 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:7)


לֹ֣א־יִהְיֶ֥͏ֽה־לְךָ֛֩ אֱלֹהִ֥֨ים אֲחֵרִ֖֜ים עַל־פָּנָֽ͏ַ֗י׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will not have any other gods in My Presence.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt have no other gods beside me.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There shall not be unto you gods of others before My presence [i.e., wherever I am — the entire world].


###### Deuteronomy 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:8)


לֹֽ֣א־תַעֲשֶֽׂ֨ה־לְךָ֥֣ פֶ֣֙סֶל֙׀כׇּל־תְּמוּנָ֔֡ה אֲשֶׁ֤֣ר בַּשָּׁמַ֣֙יִם֙׀מִמַּ֔֡עַל וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר֩ בָּאָ֖֨רֶץ מִתָּ֑͏ַ֜חַת וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר בַּמַּ֖֣יִם**׀** מִתַּ֥֣חַת לָאָֽ֗רֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not make for yourself a statue of any representation that is in the skies above or that is on the earth below, or that is in the water beneath the earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not make for thyself any carved idol, or any likeness of anything that is in heaven above, or that is in the earth beneath, or that is in the waters beneath the earth:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not make for yourself a graven image or the likeness of anything which is in the heavens above or in the earth below, or in the waters beneath the earth.


###### Deuteronomy 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:9)


לֹא־תִשְׁתַּחֲוֶ֥֣ה לָהֶ֖ם֮ וְלֹ֣א תׇעׇבְדֵ֑ם֒ כִּ֣י אָנֹכִ֞י יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ אֵ֣ל קַנָּ֔א פֹּ֠קֵ֠ד עֲוֺ֨ן אָב֧וֹת עַל־בָּנִ֛ים וְעַל־שִׁלֵּשִׁ֥ים וְעַל־רִבֵּעִ֖ים לְשֹׂנְאָֽ֑י׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not bow to them, and do not serve them, for I am Adonoy, your God, jealous Almighty, Who reckons the sin of parents for children and for the third generation and for the fourth generation of My enemies,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou shalt not bow down to them, nor serve them: for I the Lord thy God am a jealous God, punishing the iniquity of the fathers upon the children to the third and fourth generation of those who hate me,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not bow down to them and you shall not serve them, for I am the L-rd your G-d, a jealous G-d, visiting the sins of the fathers upon the children, and for the third and the fourth generations to those who hate Me,


###### Deuteronomy 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:10)


וְעֹ֥֤שֶׂה חֶ֖֙סֶד֙ לַֽאֲלָפִ֑֔ים לְאֹהֲבַ֖י וּלְשֹׁמְרֵ֥י (מצותו) [מִצְוֺתָֽי]׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
and performs kindliness for thousands [of generations] for those who love Me and for those who guard My commandments.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and showing mercy to the thousandth generation of those who love me and keep my commandments.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And doing lovingkindness for thousands, for My lovers and the keepers of My mitzvoth.


###### Deuteronomy 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:11)


לֹ֥א תִשָּׂ֛א אֶת־שֵֽׁם־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ לַשָּׁ֑וְא כִּ֣י לֹ֤א יְנַקֶּה֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־יִשָּׂ֥א אֶת־שְׁמ֖וֹ לַשָּֽׁוְא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not swear using the Name of Adonoy, your God, in vain; for Adonoy will not hold innocent whoever swears by His Name in vain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not take the name of the Lord thy God in vain: for the Lord will not hold him guiltless that takes his name in vain.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not take the name of the L-rd your G-d in vain, for the L-rd will not absolve him who takes His name in vain.


###### Deuteronomy 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:12)


שָׁמ֛֣וֹר אֶת־י֥וֹם֩ הַשַּׁבָּ֖֨ת לְקַדְּשׁ֑֜וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוְּךָ֖֣**׀** יְהֹוָ֥֣ה אֱלֹהֶֽ֗יךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Preserve the day of Shabbos to sanctify it, as Adonoy, your God, commanded you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Keep the sabbath day to sanctify it, as the Lord thy God has commanded thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Keep the Sabbath day to set it apart ["Remember" (Exodus 20:8) and "Keep" were stated in one pronouncement, spoken in one word, and heard in one hearing)], as the L-rd your G-d commanded you [before the giving of the Torah, in Marah].


###### Deuteronomy 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:13)


שֵׁ֤֣שֶׁת יָמִ֣ים֙ תַּֽעֲבֹ֔ד֮ וְעָשִׂ֖֣יתָ כׇּֿל־מְלַאכְתֶּֽךָ֒׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Six days will you work and perform all your labor,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Six days thou shalt labour, and do all thy work:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Six days shall you work, and you shall do all of your labor.


###### Deuteronomy 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:14)


וְי֨וֹם֙ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֔֜י שַׁבָּ֖֣ת**׀** לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑֗יךָ לֹ֣א תַעֲשֶׂ֣ה כׇל־מְלָאכָ֡ה אַתָּ֣ה וּבִנְךָֽ־וּבִתֶּ֣ךָ וְעַבְדְּךָֽ־וַ֠אֲמָתֶ֠ךָ וְשׁוֹרְךָ֨ וַחֲמֹֽרְךָ֜ וְכׇל־בְּהֶמְתֶּ֗ךָ וְגֵֽרְךָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בִּשְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ לְמַ֗עַן יָנ֛וּחַ עַבְדְּךָ֥ וַאֲמָתְךָ֖ כָּמֽ֑וֹךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
but the seventh day, Shabbos for Adonoy, your God, do not perform any labor—you, your son and your daughter, your male slave and your female slave, your ox and your donkey and all your animals, and the non-Jew who dwells in your cities—in order that your male slave will rest—and your female slave—like you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but the seventh day is the sabbath of the Lord thy God: on it thou shalt not do any work, thou, nor thy son, nor thy daughter, nor thy manservant, nor thy maidservant, nor thy ox, nor thy ass, nor any of thy cattle, nor thy stranger that is within thy gates; that thy manservant and thy maidservant may rest as well as thou.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the seventh day is Sabbath to the L-rd your G-d. You shall not perform any labor: you, and your son, and your daughter, and your man-servant, and your maid-servant, and your ox, and your ass, and all of your beasts, and your sojourner in your gates, so that your man-servant and your maid-servant rest like you.


###### Deuteronomy 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:15)


וְזָכַרְתָּ֗֞ כִּ֣י־עֶ֤֥בֶד הָיִ֣֙יתָ֙**׀** בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔֗יִם וַיֹּצִ֨אֲךָ֜֩ יְהֹוָ֤֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֤֙יךָ֙ מִשָּׁ֔ם֙ בְּיָ֥֤ד חֲזָקָ֖ה֙ וּבִזְרֹ֣עַ נְטוּיָ֑֔ה עַל־כֵּ֗ן צִוְּךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת אֶת־י֥וֹם הַשַּׁבָּֽת׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt, and Adonoy, your God, took you out of there with a strong hand and an extended arm. That is why Adonoy, your God, commanded you to celebrate the Shabbos day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And remember that thou wast a servant in the land of Miżrayim, and that the Lord thy God brought thee out from there with a mighty hand and a stretched out arm: therefore the Lord thy God commanded thee to keep the sabbath day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt, and that the L-rd your G-d took you out of there with a strong hand and with an outstretched arm [so that you would be His servants and keep His mitzvoth]; therefore, the L-rd your G-d commanded you to observe the Sabbath day.


###### Deuteronomy 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:16)


כַּבֵּ֤ד אֶת־אָבִ֙יךָ֙ וְאֶת־אִמֶּ֔ךָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוְּךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ לְמַ֣עַן**׀** יַאֲרִיכֻ֣ן יָמֶ֗יךָ וּלְמַ֙עַן֙ יִ֣יטַב לָ֔ךְ עַ֚ל הָֽאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽךְ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Honor your father and your mother in the way Adonoy, your God, commanded you; in order that you live long and in order that you have it good on the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Honour thy father and thy mother, as the Lord thy God has commanded thee; that thy days may be prolonged, and that it may go well with thee, in the land which the Lord thy God gives thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Honor your father and your mother, as the L-rd your G-d commanded you [in Marah (see Exodus 15:25)], so that your days be prolonged and so that it be good for you on the land which the L-rd your G-d gives to you.


###### Deuteronomy 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:17)


לֹ֥֖א תִּֿרְצָ֖͏ֽח׃{ס}וְלֹ֣֖א תִּֿנְאָ֑͏ֽף׃{ס}וְלֹ֣֖א תִּֿגְנֹֽ֔ב׃{ס}וְלֹֽא־תַעֲנֶ֥ה בְרֵֽעֲךָ֖ עֵ֥ד שָֽׁוְא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not murder. Do not commit adultery. And do not kidnap. And do not testify against your neighbor in vain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not murder. Neither shalt thou commit adultery. Neither shalt thou steal. Neither shalt thou bear false witness against thy neighbour.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not kill. And you shall not commit adultery. And you shall not steal. And you shall not testify against your neighbor false testimony.


###### Deuteronomy 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:18)


וְלֹ֥א תַחְמֹ֖ד אֵ֣שֶׁת רֵעֶ֑ךָ{ס}וְלֹ֨א תִתְאַוֶּ֜ה בֵּ֣ית רֵעֶ֗ךָ שָׂדֵ֜הוּ וְעַבְדּ֤וֹ וַאֲמָתוֹ֙ שׁוֹר֣וֹ וַחֲמֹר֔וֹ וְכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר לְרֵעֶֽךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And do not covet your neighbor’s wife. And do not desire your neighbor’s house, his field, or his male slave or his female slave, his ox or his donkey, or anything else that belongs to your neighbor.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shalt thou covet thy neighbour’s wife, neither shalt thou desire thy neighbour’s house, his field, or his manservant, or his maidservant, his ox, or his ass, or anything that is thy neighbour’s.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not covet your neighbor's wife. And you shall not desire your neighbor's house, his field, and his man-servant, and his maid-servant, his ox, and his ass, and all that belongs to your neighbor.


###### Deuteronomy 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:19)


אֶֽת־הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֡לֶּה דִּבֶּר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֶל־כׇּל־קְהַלְכֶ֜ם בָּהָ֗ר מִתּ֤וֹךְ הָאֵשׁ֙ הֶֽעָנָ֣ן וְהָֽעֲרָפֶ֔ל ק֥וֹל גָּד֖וֹל וְלֹ֣א יָסָ֑ף וַֽיִּכְתְּבֵ֗ם עַל־שְׁנֵי֙ לֻחֹ֣ת אֲבָנִ֔ים וַֽיִּתְּנֵ֖ם אֵלָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These words did Adonoy address to your entire assemblage at the mountain from within the fire, the cloud, and the thick cloud—a voice great and without cessation. He wrote them on two tablets of stone, and He gave them to me.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These words the Lord spoke to all your assembly in the mountain out of the midst of the fire, the cloud, and the thick darkness, with a great voice which was not heard again. And he wrote them on two tablets of stone, and delivered them to me.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These things the L-rd spoke to all of your congregation on the mountain from the midst of the fire, the cloud, and the thick darkness — a great voice, and it did not cease, [His voice being strong and everlasting]. And He wrote them on two tablets of stone and He gave them to me.


###### Deuteronomy 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:20)


וַיְהִ֗י כְּשׇׁמְעֲכֶ֤ם אֶת־הַקּוֹל֙ מִתּ֣וֹךְ הַחֹ֔שֶׁךְ וְהָהָ֖ר בֹּעֵ֣ר בָּאֵ֑שׁ וַתִּקְרְב֣וּן אֵלַ֔י כׇּל־רָאשֵׁ֥י שִׁבְטֵיכֶ֖ם וְזִקְנֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you heard the voice from within the darkness, as the mountain burned with fire, you approached me—all the leaders of your tribes and your elders.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when you heard the voice out of the midst of the darkness, (for the mountain did burn with fire,) that you came near to me, all the heads of your tribes, and your elders;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when you heard the voice from the midst of the darkness, and the mountain burning in fire, that you drew near to me, all the heads of your tribes and your elders.


###### Deuteronomy 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:21)


וַתֹּאמְר֗וּ הֵ֣ן הֶרְאָ֜נוּ יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֵ֙ינוּ֙ אֶת־כְּבֹד֣וֹ וְאֶת־גׇּדְל֔וֹ וְאֶת־קֹל֥וֹ שָׁמַ֖עְנוּ מִתּ֣וֹךְ הָאֵ֑שׁ הַיּ֤וֹם הַזֶּה֙ רָאִ֔ינוּ כִּֽי־יְדַבֵּ֧ר אֱלֹהִ֛ים אֶת־הָֽאָדָ֖ם וָחָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You said, ‘‘Look! Adonoy, our God, showed us His glory and His greatness, and His voice we heard from within the fire; today we saw that God can address man and he will survive.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and you said, Behold, the Lord our God has shown us his glory and his greatness, and we have heard his voice out of the midst of the fire: we have seen this day that God does talk with man, and he lives.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you said: Behold, the L-rd our G-d has shown us His glory and His greatness, and we heard His voice from the midst of the fire. This day we saw that G-d may speak with a man and he live!


###### Deuteronomy 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:22)


וְעַתָּה֙ לָ֣מָּה נָמ֔וּת כִּ֣י תֹֽאכְלֵ֔נוּ הָאֵ֥שׁ הַגְּדֹלָ֖ה הַזֹּ֑את אִם־יֹסְפִ֣ים**׀** אֲנַ֗חְנוּ לִ֠שְׁמֹ֠עַ אֶת־ק֨וֹל יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵ֛ינוּ ע֖וֹד וָמָֽתְנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Now, why should we die when this great fire will consume us? If we continue to hear the voice of Adonoy, our God, any longer we will die.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now therefore why should we die? for this great fire will consume us: if we hear the voice of the Lord our God any more, then we shall die.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, why should we die? For this great fire will consume us. If we continue hearing the voice of the L-rd our G-d we shall die.


###### Deuteronomy 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:23)


כִּ֣י מִ֣י כׇל־בָּשָׂ֡ר אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׁמַ֣ע קוֹל֩ אֱלֹהִ֨ים חַיִּ֜ים מְדַבֵּ֧ר מִתּוֹךְ־הָאֵ֛שׁ כָּמֹ֖נוּ וַיֶּֽחִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For who of all flesh has heard the voice of the living God speaking from within the fire like us, and survived?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For who is there of all flesh, that has heard the voice of the living God speaking out of the midst of the fire, as we have, and lived?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For who is there of all flesh who heard the voice of the living G-d speaking from the midst of the fire, as we have, and lived?


###### Deuteronomy 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:24)


קְרַ֤ב אַתָּה֙ וּֽשְׁמָ֔ע אֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר יֹאמַ֖ר יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ וְאַ֣תְּ**׀** תְּדַבֵּ֣ר אֵלֵ֗ינוּ אֵת֩ כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְדַבֵּ֜ר יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵ֛ינוּ אֵלֶ֖יךָ וְשָׁמַ֥עְנוּ וְעָשִֽׂינוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Approach—yourself—and listen to everything that Adonoy, our God, says; and you will tell us everything that Adonoy, our God, tells you and we will accept and fulfill.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go thou near, and hear all that the Lord our God shall say: and speak to us all that the Lord our God shall speak to thee; and we will hear it, and
				do it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You draw near and hear all that the L-rd our G-d says, and you ["at," the feminine form (Moses saying: You rendered me weak (as a woman) by saying this. Were it not better to draw near to Him in love and hear it from Him rather than from me!)], speak to us all that the L-rd our G-d will speak to you, and we shall hear and we shall do.


###### Deuteronomy 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:25)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ע יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־ק֣וֹל דִּבְרֵיכֶ֔ם בְּדַבֶּרְכֶ֖ם אֵלָ֑י וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י שָׁ֠מַ֠עְתִּי אֶת־ק֨וֹל דִּבְרֵ֜י הָעָ֤ם הַזֶּה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבְּר֣וּ אֵלֶ֔יךָ הֵיטִ֖יבוּ כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֵּֽרוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy heard the intent of your words when you spoke to me; and Adonoy said to me, ‘‘ I have heard the intent of this people’s words that they spoke to you—everything they said is excellent.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord heard the voice of your words, when you spoke to me; and the Lord said to me, I have heard the voice of the words of this people, which they have spoken to thee: they have well said all that they have spoken.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd heard the voice of your words in speaking to me. And the L-rd said to me: I have heard the voice of the words of this people that they spoke to you. They have done well in all that they have spoken.


###### Deuteronomy 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:26)


מִֽי־יִתֵּ֡ן וְהָיָה֩ לְבָבָ֨ם זֶ֜ה לָהֶ֗ם לְיִרְאָ֥ה אֹתִ֛י וְלִשְׁמֹ֥ר אֶת־כׇּל־מִצְוֺתַ֖י כׇּל־הַיָּמִ֑ים לְמַ֨עַן יִיטַ֥ב לָהֶ֛ם וְלִבְנֵיהֶ֖ם לְעֹלָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I wish they would retain their present attitude to fear Me and to preserve all My commandments always; so that they and their children benefit forever.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O that there were such a heart in them, that they would fear me, and keep all my commandments always, that it might be well with them, and with their children for ever!


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would that this heart of theirs were in them to fear Me and to keep all of My mitzvoth all of the days so that it be good for them and for their children forever!


###### Deuteronomy 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:27)


לֵ֖ךְ אֱמֹ֣ר לָהֶ֑ם שׁ֥וּבוּ לָכֶ֖ם לְאׇהֳלֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Go say to them, ‘Return for yourselves to your tents.’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Go say to them, Return again to your tents.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Go, say to them: Return you to your tents.


###### Deuteronomy 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:28)


וְאַתָּ֗ה פֹּה֮ עֲמֹ֣ד עִמָּדִי֒ וַאֲדַבְּרָ֣ה אֵלֶ֗יךָ אֵ֧ת כׇּל־הַמִּצְוָ֛ה וְהַחֻקִּ֥ים וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר תְּלַמְּדֵ֑ם וְעָשׂ֣וּ בָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י נֹתֵ֥ן לָהֶ֖ם לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You, however, remain here with Me, and I will tell you all the *mitzvohs* and the statutes and the laws that you will teach them; and they will fulfill [them] in the land that I am giving them to inherit.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But as for thee, stand here by me, and I will speak to thee all the commandments, and the statutes, and the judgments, which thou shalt teach them, that they may do them in the land which I gave them to possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you, stand here with Me, and I shall speak to you all the mitzvah and the statutes and the judgments that you shall teach them and that they shall do in the land that I give them to inherit.


###### Deuteronomy 5:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:29)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֣ם לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוָּ֛ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם אֶתְכֶ֑ם לֹ֥א תָסֻ֖רוּ יָמִ֥ין וּשְׂמֹֽאל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will be careful to fulfill as Adonoy, your God, has commanded you; do not deviate right or left.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall observe to do therefore as the Lord your God has commanded you: you shall not turn aside to the right hand or to the left.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall observe to do as the L-rd your G-d commanded you; you shall not turn right or left.


###### Deuteronomy 5:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/5:30)


בְּכׇל־הַדֶּ֗רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֨ר צִוָּ֜ה יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֛ם אֶתְכֶ֖ם תֵּלֵ֑כוּ לְמַ֤עַן תִּֽחְיוּן֙ וְט֣וֹב לָכֶ֔ם וְהַאֲרַכְתֶּ֣ם יָמִ֔ים בָּאָ֖רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּֽירָשֽׁוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Along the entire way that Adonoy, your God, commanded you, shall you go; in order that you live and enjoy good, and you live long in the land that you are inheriting.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall walk in all the ways which the Lord your God has commanded you, that you may live, and that it may be well with you, and that you may prolong your days in the land which you shall possess.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In all the way that the L-rd your G-d commanded you shall you go, so that you live and it be good for you, and you prolong days in the land that you shall inherit.



## Chapter 6

###### Deuteronomy 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:1)


וְזֹ֣את הַמִּצְוָ֗ה הַֽחֻקִּים֙ וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוָּ֛ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם לְלַמֵּ֣ד אֶתְכֶ֑ם לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת בָּאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתֶּ֛ם עֹבְרִ֥ים שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This is the *mitzvah,* the statutes and the laws that Adonoy, your God, commanded to teach you to fulfill in the land that you are crossing over there to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Now this is the commandment, the statutes, and the judgments, which the Lord your God commanded to teach you, that you might do them in the land into which you go to possess it:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is the mitzvah, the statutes and the judgments that the L‑rd your G‑d commanded to teach you to do in the land whither you are crossing over to inherit it —


###### Deuteronomy 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:2)


לְמַ֨עַן תִּירָ֜א אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ לִ֠שְׁמֹ֠ר אֶת־כׇּל־חֻקֹּתָ֣יו וּמִצְוֺתָיו֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָנֹכִ֣י מְצַוֶּ֒ךָ֒ אַתָּה֙ וּבִנְךָ֣ וּבֶן־בִּנְךָ֔ כֹּ֖ל יְמֵ֣י חַיֶּ֑יךָ וּלְמַ֖עַן יַאֲרִכֻ֥ן יָמֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In order that you will fear Adonoy, your God, to preserve all His statutes and commandments that I am commanding you—you and your son and your grandson—all the days of your life, and in order that you live long.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou mightest fear the Lord thy God, to keep all his statutes and his commandments, which I command thee, thou, and thy son, and thy son’s son, all the days of thy life; and that thy days may be prolonged.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that you fear the L‑rd your G‑d to keep all of His statutes and His mitzvoth that I command you — you, and your son, and your son's son, all the days of your life, and so that your days are prolonged.


###### Deuteronomy 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:3)


וְשָׁמַעְתָּ֤ יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וְשָׁמַרְתָּ֣ לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת אֲשֶׁר֙ יִיטַ֣ב לְךָ֔ וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּרְבּ֖וּן מְאֹ֑ד כַּאֲשֶׁר֩ דִּבֶּ֨ר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֱלֹהֵ֤י אֲבֹתֶ֙יךָ֙ לָ֔ךְ אֶ֛רֶץ זָבַ֥ת חָלָ֖ב וּדְבָֽשׁ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will heed, Yisroel, and you will be careful to fulfill, so that you will benefit and so that you will multiply exceedingly, as Adonoy, God of your forefathers, spoke about you—a land flowing milk and honey.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear therefore, O Yisra᾽el, and take care to do it; that it may be well with thee, and that you may increase mightily, as the Lord God of thy fathers has promised thee, in the land that flows with milk and honey.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall hear, Israel, and you shall observe to do, so that it be good for you and so that you increase greatly, as the L‑rd, the G‑d of your fathers, spoke to you — ("to inherit it":) a land flowing milk and honey.


###### Deuteronomy 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:4)


שְׁמַ֖ע יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ יְהֹוָ֥ה׀אֶחָֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Listen, Yisroel! Adonoy is our God, Adonoy is one.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, O Yisra᾽el: The Lord our God; the Lord is one.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, O Israel, the L‑rd [who is] our G‑d [now, and not the G‑d of the peoples — He is destined to be] the one L‑rd.


###### Deuteronomy 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:5)


וְאָ֣הַבְתָּ֔ אֵ֖ת יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ בְּכׇל־לְבָבְךָ֥ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשְׁךָ֖ וּבְכׇל־מְאֹדֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to love Adonoy, your God, with all your heart, with all your soul, and with all your possessions.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt love the Lord thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall love the L‑rd your G‑d [(Do His mitzvoth out of love)], with all your heart [(with both of your inclinations)] and with all your soul [(even if He takes your soul)] and with all your might [(with all your wealth — this, for one whose wealth is more beloved of him than his soul)].


###### Deuteronomy 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:6)


וְהָי֞וּ הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֗לֶּה אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֧י מְצַוְּךָ֛ הַיּ֖וֹם עַל־לְבָבֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And these words that I command you today shall be upon your heart.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be in thy heart:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
["And you shall love, etc." What is this love?] And these things that I command you this day [(They should be like a "new edict" to you)] shall be upon your heart [(for you thereby come to know the Holy One Blessed be He and to cleave to His ways)].


###### Deuteronomy 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:7)


וְשִׁנַּנְתָּ֣ם לְבָנֶ֔יךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ֖ בָּ֑ם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ֤ בְּבֵיתֶ֙ךָ֙ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ֣ בַדֶּ֔רֶךְ וּֽבְשׇׁכְבְּךָ֖ וּבְקוּמֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to teach them to your children and you are to discuss them, when you sit at home, and when you journey on the road, and when you go to sleep, and when you rise.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt teach them diligently to thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall teach them (veshinantam) to your sons [lit., "and they (words of Torah) shall be sharp" (in your mouth), so that if one questions you concerning them you will not stammer but answer him forthwith ("sons" here are disciples, and teachers are called "fathers")]. And you shall speak [primarily] in them [words of Torah] in your sitting in your house and in your walking upon the way and in your lying down [i.e., the time of lying down] and in your rising [i.e., the time of rising].


###### Deuteronomy 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:8)


וּקְשַׁרְתָּ֥ם לְא֖וֹת עַל־יָדֶ֑ךָ וְהָי֥וּ לְטֹטָפֹ֖ת בֵּ֥ין עֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to tie them as a sign on your arm and they are to be *totafos* between your eyes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt bind them for a sign upon thy arm, and they shall be as frontlets between thy eyes.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall bind them as a sign upon your hand [(the arm phylactery)], and they shall be frontlets (totafoth) [denoting "four" (Scriptural sections)] between your eyes [(the head phylactery)].


###### Deuteronomy 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:9)


וּכְתַבְתָּ֛ם עַל־מְזֻז֥וֹת בֵּיתֶ֖ךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to write them on the doorposts of your house, and on your gateposts.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt write them upon the doorposts of thy house, and on thy gates.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall write them on the doorposts (mezuzoth) of your house ["mezuzoth" is written defective (without the vav after the first zayin), indicating that only one mezuzah (per doorpost) is needed], and on your gates [(including the gates of courtyards, provinces, and cities)].


###### Deuteronomy 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:10)


וְהָיָ֞ה כִּ֥י יְבִיאֲךָ֣**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ אֶל־הָאָ֜רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִשְׁבַּ֧ע לַאֲבֹתֶ֛יךָ לְאַבְרָהָ֛ם לְיִצְחָ֥ק וּֽלְיַעֲקֹ֖ב לָ֣תֶת לָ֑ךְ עָרִ֛ים גְּדֹלֹ֥ת וְטֹבֹ֖ת אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־בָנִֽיתָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy your God will have brought you to the land that He swore to your forefathers—to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov—to give you: great and good cities that you did not build.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, when the Lord thy God shall bring thee to the land of which he swore to thy fathers, to Avraham, to Yiżĥaq, and to Ya῾aqov, to give thee great and goodly cities, which thou didst not build,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when the L‑rd your G‑d brings you to the land that He swore to your fathers, to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob, to give to you, great and good cities that you did not build,


###### Deuteronomy 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:11)


וּבָ֨תִּ֜ים מְלֵאִ֣ים כׇּל־טוּב֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־מִלֵּ֒אתָ֒ וּבֹרֹ֤ת חֲצוּבִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־חָצַ֔בְתָּ כְּרָמִ֥ים וְזֵיתִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־נָטָ֑עְתָּ וְאָכַלְתָּ֖ וְשָׂבָֽעְתָּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And houses full of all bounty that you did not fill, and hewn cisterns that you did not hew, vineyards and olive stands that you did not plant; and you will eat and be full.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and houses full of all good things, which thou didst not fill, and hewn out wells, which thou didst not dig, vineyards and olive trees, which thou didst not plant; when thou shalt eat and be replete;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And houses full of all good that you did not fill, hewn cisterns [(the ground being rocky)], which you did not hew, and vineyards and olive groves, which you did not plant, and you shall eat and be sated —


###### Deuteronomy 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:12)


הִשָּׁ֣מֶר לְךָ֔ פֶּן־תִּשְׁכַּ֖ח אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר הוֹצִֽיאֲךָ֛ מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרַ֖יִם מִבֵּ֥ית עֲבָדִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look out for yourself, lest you forget Adonoy, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of slavery.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
beware lest thou forget the Lord, who
				brought thee out of the land of Miżrayim, from the house of bondage.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourselves lest you forget the L‑rd, who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of bondage.


###### Deuteronomy 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:13)


אֶת־יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ תִּירָ֖א וְאֹת֣וֹ תַעֲבֹ֑ד וּבִשְׁמ֖וֹ תִּשָּׁבֵֽעַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Fear Adonoy, your God, and serve Him, and swear by His name.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt fear the Lord thy God, and serve him, and shalt swear by his name.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L‑rd your G‑d shall you fear, and Him shall you serve, and [only then (if you fear and serve Him)] in His name may you swear [(for you will then be circumspect in your oath; but otherwise, do not swear in His name)].


###### Deuteronomy 6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:14)


לֹ֣א תֵֽלְכ֔וּן אַחֲרֵ֖י אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֑ים מֵאֱלֹהֵי֙ הָֽעַמִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר סְבִיבוֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not follow other gods, from the gods of the peoples around you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not go after other gods, of the gods of the people who are round about you;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
