Do not follow after other gods, of the gods of the peoples that are around you [i.e., Be especially heedful of the latter in that their proximity presents an immediate danger.]


###### Deuteronomy 6:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:15)


כִּ֣י אֵ֥ל קַנָּ֛א יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בְּקִרְבֶּ֑ךָ פֶּן־יֶ֠חֱרֶ֠ה אַף־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ בָּ֔ךְ וְהִשְׁמִ֣ידְךָ֔ מֵעַ֖ל פְּנֵ֥י הָאֲדָמָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For a jealous Almighty is Adonoy, your God, within you; lest there be aroused the anger of Adonoy, your God, against you and He destroy you from upon the surface of the land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(for the Lord thy God is a jealous God among you) lest the anger of the Lord thy God be inflamed against thee, and he destroy thee from off the face of the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a jealous G‑d is the L‑rd your G‑d in your midst. Lest the wrath of the L‑rd your G‑d burn against you, and He destroy you from off the face of the ground.


###### Deuteronomy 6:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:16)


לֹ֣א תְנַסּ֔וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר נִסִּיתֶ֖ם בַּמַּסָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not test Adonoy, your God, as you tested at Massoh.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do not tempt the Lord your God, as you tempted him in Massa.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not try the L‑rd your G‑d as you tried Him in Massah [when they went out of Egypt and they tried Him with water (Exodus 17:7)].


###### Deuteronomy 6:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:17)


שָׁמ֣וֹר תִּשְׁמְר֔וּן אֶת־מִצְוֺ֖ת יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם וְעֵדֹתָ֥יו וְחֻקָּ֖יו אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Carefully guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, and His testimonies and His statutes that He commanded you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall diligently keep the commandments of the Lord your God, and his testimonies, and his statutes, which he has commanded thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Heed diligently the mitzvoth of the L‑rd your G‑d, and His testimonies and statutes which He commanded you.


###### Deuteronomy 6:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:18)


וְעָשִׂ֛יתָ הַיָּשָׁ֥ר וְהַטּ֖וֹב בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה לְמַ֙עַן֙ יִ֣יטַב לָ֔ךְ וּבָ֗אתָ וְיָֽרַשְׁתָּ֙ אֶת־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַטֹּבָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁבַּ֥ע יְהֹוָ֖ה לַאֲבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Perform the upright and the good in Adonoy’s eyes, in order that you benefit and arrive in, and inherit the good land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt do that which is right and good in the sight of the Lord: that it may be well with thee, and that thou mayst go in and possess the good land which the Lord swore to thy fathers,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall do what is just and what is good in the eyes of the L‑rd [i.e., compromise, beyond the strict letter of the law], so that He do good to you, and you come and inherit the good land that the L‑rd swore to your forefathers.


###### Deuteronomy 6:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:19)


לַהֲדֹ֥ף אֶת־כׇּל־אֹיְבֶ֖יךָ מִפָּנֶ֑יךָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To smash all your enemies before you, as Adonoy declared.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
to cast out all thy enemies from before thee, as the Lord has spoken.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To thrust out all of your foes before you, as the L‑rd spoke [(Exodus 23:27): "And I shall confound all the people, etc."]


###### Deuteronomy 6:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:20)


כִּֽי־יִשְׁאָלְךָ֥ בִנְךָ֛ מָחָ֖ר לֵאמֹ֑ר מָ֣ה הָעֵדֹ֗ת וְהַֽחֻקִּים֙ וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוָּ֛ה יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When your son asks you tomorrow, saying, ‘‘What are the testimonies and the statues and the laws that Adonoy, our God, commanded you?’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thy son asks thee in time to come, saying, What mean the testimonies, and the statutes, and the judgments, which the Lord our God has commanded you?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If your son ask you in time to come, saying: What are the testimonies and the statutes and the judgments which the L‑rd our G‑d commanded you?


###### Deuteronomy 6:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:21)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ לְבִנְךָ֔ עֲבָדִ֛ים הָיִ֥ינוּ לְפַרְעֹ֖ה בְּמִצְרָ֑יִם וַיֹּצִיאֵ֧נוּ יְהֹוָ֛ה מִמִּצְרַ֖יִם בְּיָ֥ד חֲזָקָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will tell your son, ‘‘We were slaves to Pharaoh in Egypt, and Adonoy took us out of Egypt with a strong hand.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then thou shalt say to thy son, We were the bondmen of Par῾o in Miżrayim; and the Lord brought us out of Miżrayim with a mighty hand:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall say to your son: We were slaves to Pharaoh in Egypt, and the L‑rd took us out of Egypt with a strong hand.


###### Deuteronomy 6:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:22)


וַיִּתֵּ֣ן יְהֹוָ֡ה אוֹתֹ֣ת וּ֠מֹפְתִ֠ים גְּדֹלִ֨ים וְרָעִ֧ים׀בְּמִצְרַ֛יִם בְּפַרְעֹ֥ה וּבְכׇל־בֵּית֖וֹ לְעֵינֵֽינוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy set signs and wonders [that were] great and harmful, upon Egypt, upon Pharaoh, and upon his entire household as we watched.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord showed signs and wonders, great and sore, upon Miżrayim, upon Par῾o, and upon all his household, before our eyes:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L‑rd wrought signs and wonders, great and sore, against Egypt, against Pharaoh, and against his whole household, before our eyes.


###### Deuteronomy 6:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:23)


וְאוֹתָ֖נוּ הוֹצִ֣יא מִשָּׁ֑ם לְמַ֙עַן֙ הָבִ֣יא אֹתָ֔נוּ לָ֤תֶת לָ֙נוּ֙ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִשְׁבַּ֖ע לַאֲבֹתֵֽינוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And us He took out of there, in order to bring us into, and to give us the land He swore to our forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he brought us out from there, that he might bring us in, to give us the land which he swore to our fathers.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he took us thence in order to bring us to give to us the land that He swore to our fathers.


###### Deuteronomy 6:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:24)


וַיְצַוֵּ֣נוּ יְהֹוָ֗ה לַעֲשׂוֹת֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הַחֻקִּ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה לְיִרְאָ֖ה אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ לְט֥וֹב לָ֙נוּ֙ כׇּל־הַיָּמִ֔ים לְחַיֹּתֵ֖נוּ כְּהַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy commanded us to perform all these statutes to [show that we] fear Adonoy, our God; to benefit us for all time, to keep us alive like this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord commanded us to do all these statutes, to fear the Lord our God, for our good always, that he might preserve us alive, as it is at this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L‑rd commanded us to do all of these statutes to fear the L‑rd our G‑d so that it be good for us all of the days, to keep us in life, as this day.


###### Deuteronomy 6:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/6:25)


וּצְדָקָ֖ה תִּֽהְיֶה־לָּ֑נוּ כִּֽי־נִשְׁמֹ֨ר לַעֲשׂ֜וֹת אֶת־כׇּל־הַמִּצְוָ֣ה הַזֹּ֗את לִפְנֵ֛י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוָּֽנוּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And it will be to our credit if we are careful to fulfill this entire *mitzvah* before Adonoy, our God, as He commanded us.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be accounted virtue in us, if we take care to do all these commandments before the Lord our God, as he has commanded us.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be [accounted] righteousness to us if we observe to do all of this mitzvah before the L‑rd our G‑d as He commanded us.



## Chapter 7

###### Deuteronomy 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:1)


כִּ֤י יְבִֽיאֲךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ אֶל־הָאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה בָא־שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּ֑הּ וְנָשַׁ֣ל גּֽוֹיִם־רַבִּ֣ים׀מִפָּנֶ֡יךָ הַֽחִתִּי֩ וְהַגִּרְגָּשִׁ֨י וְהָאֱמֹרִ֜י וְהַכְּנַעֲנִ֣י וְהַפְּרִזִּ֗י וְהַֽחִוִּי֙ וְהַיְבוּסִ֔י שִׁבְעָ֣ה גוֹיִ֔ם רַבִּ֥ים וַעֲצוּמִ֖ים מִמֶּֽךָּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy, your God, brings you into the land that you are coming there to inherit, He will cast out many nations from before you—the Chittites, the Girgashites and the Emorites, the Canaanites and the Perizites, the Chivites and the Yevusites—seven nations more numerous and more powerful than you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the Lord thy God shall bring thee to the land into which thou goest to possess it, and shall cast out many nations before thee, the Ḥitti, and the Girgashi, and the Emori, and the Kena῾ani, and the Perizzi, and the Ḥivvi, and the Yevusi, seven nations greater and mightier than thou;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the L-rd your G-d brings you to the land whither you come to inherit it, and He casts out many nations before you: the Chitti, the Girgashi, the Emori, the Canaani, the Perizi, the Chivi, and the Yevussi — seven nations more numerous and mightier than you.


###### Deuteronomy 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:2)


וּנְתָנָ֞ם יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ לְפָנֶ֖יךָ וְהִכִּיתָ֑ם הַחֲרֵ֤ם תַּחֲרִים֙ אֹתָ֔ם לֹא־תִכְרֹ֥ת לָהֶ֛ם בְּרִ֖ית וְלֹ֥א תְחׇנֵּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, will defeat them before you, and you will smite them. You must surely annihilate them. Do not make a treaty with them, and do not favor them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and when the Lord thy God shall deliver them before thee, and thou shalt smite them, then thou shalt devote them to utter destruction; thou shalt make no covenant with them, nor show mercy to them:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d delivers them before you and you smite them — lay them waste. You shall not make a covenant with them and you shall not grant them grace [(It is forbidden to say: "How beautiful is this heathen!")].


###### Deuteronomy 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:3)


וְלֹ֥א תִתְחַתֵּ֖ן בָּ֑ם בִּתְּךָ֙ לֹא־תִתֵּ֣ן לִבְנ֔וֹ וּבִתּ֖וֹ לֹא־תִקַּ֥ח לִבְנֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not marry them; do not give your daughter to his son, nor take his daughter for your son.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
neither shalt thou make marriages with them; thy daughter thou shalt not give to his son, nor shalt thou take his daughter to thy son.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not intermarry with them: Your daughter you shall not give to his son, and his daughter you shall not take for your son.


###### Deuteronomy 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:4)


כִּֽי־יָסִ֤יר אֶת־בִּנְךָ֙ מֵֽאַחֲרַ֔י וְעָבְד֖וּ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֑ים וְחָרָ֤ה אַף־יְהֹוָה֙ בָּכֶ֔ם וְהִשְׁמִידְךָ֖ מַהֵֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For he will turn your son away from Me and they will serve other gods; and Adonoy’s anger will be aroused against you and He will destroy you swiftly.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they will turn away thy son from following me, that they may serve other gods: so will the anger of the Lord be inflamed against you, and he will destroy thee speedily.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For he [your heathen son-in-law] will turn your son [i.e., your grandson] away from Me, [(whence it is seen that your daughter's son by a gentile is called your son (but not your son's son born to a gentile woman, it not being written "For she will turn, etc.")], and they will serve other gods, and the wrath of the L-rd will burn in you, and He will destroy you quickly.


###### Deuteronomy 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:5)


כִּֽי־אִם־כֹּ֤ה תַעֲשׂוּ֙ לָהֶ֔ם מִזְבְּחֹתֵיהֶ֣ם תִּתֹּ֔צוּ וּמַצֵּבֹתָ֖ם תְּשַׁבֵּ֑רוּ וַאֲשֵֽׁירֵהֶם֙ תְּגַדֵּע֔וּן וּפְסִילֵיהֶ֖ם תִּשְׂרְפ֥וּן בָּאֵֽשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather, thus shall you do to them: raze their altars and smash their monuments, cut down their worship-trees and burn their statues in fire.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But thus shall you deal with them: you shall destroy their altars, and break down their images, and cut down their asherim, and burn their carved idols with fire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But thus shall you do to them: Their [built-up] altars shall you destroy, and their monuments [of one stone] shall you break, and their asheirah trees, [which they worship] shall you cut down, and their graven images shall you burn with fire.


###### Deuteronomy 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:6)


כִּ֣י עַ֤ם קָדוֹשׁ֙ אַתָּ֔ה לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ בְּךָ֞ בָּחַ֣ר**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ לִהְי֥וֹת לוֹ֙ לְעַ֣ם סְגֻלָּ֔ה מִכֹּל֙ הָֽעַמִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הָאֲדָמָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For you are a holy people to Adonoy, your God; Adonoy, your God, chose you to be for Him a treasured people from all the peoples that are on the face of the earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thou art a holy people to the Lord thy God: the Lord thy God has chosen thee to be a special people to himself, above all peoples that are upon the face of the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a holy nation are you to the L-rd your G-d. In you has the L-rd your G-d chosen to be unto Him a select people of all the peoples on the face of the earth.


###### Deuteronomy 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:7)


לֹ֣א מֵֽרֻבְּכֶ֞ם מִכׇּל־הָֽעַמִּ֗ים חָשַׁ֧ק יְהֹוָ֛ה בָּכֶ֖ם וַיִּבְחַ֣ר בָּכֶ֑ם כִּֽי־אַתֶּ֥ם הַמְעַ֖ט מִכׇּל־הָעַמִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Not because of the multitude your population beyond all peoples did Adonoy desire you and choose you; for you are the least of all the peoples.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord did not set his love upon you, or choose you, because you were more in number than any people; for you were the fewest of all peoples:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Not because you are more numerous than all the peoples did the L-rd desire you and choose you, for you are the smallest of all the peoples.


###### Deuteronomy 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:8)


כִּי֩ מֵאַהֲבַ֨ת יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶתְכֶ֗ם וּמִשׇּׁמְר֤וֹ אֶת־הַשְּׁבֻעָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נִשְׁבַּע֙ לַאֲבֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם הוֹצִ֧יא יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם בְּיָ֣ד חֲזָקָ֑ה וַֽיִּפְדְּךָ֙ מִבֵּ֣ית עֲבָדִ֔ים מִיַּ֖ד פַּרְעֹ֥ה מֶֽלֶךְ־מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather, out of Adonoy’s love for you and out of His keeping the oath that He swore to your forefathers did Adonoy take you out with a strong hand, and did He redeem you from the house of slavery, from the hand of Pharaoh, king of Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but because the Lord loved you, and because he would keep the oath which he had sworn to your fathers, has the Lord brought you out with a mighty hand, and redeemed you out of the house of bondmen, from the hand of Par῾o king of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But out of the L-rd's love for you and in keeping with His oath that He swore to your fathers did the L-rd take you out with a strong hand, and He redeemed you from the house of bondage, from the hand of Pharaoh, king of Egypt.


###### Deuteronomy 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:9)


וְיָ֣דַעְתָּ֔ כִּֽי־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ ה֣וּא הָֽאֱלֹהִ֑ים הָאֵל֙ הַֽנֶּאֱמָ֔ן שֹׁמֵ֧ר הַבְּרִ֣ית וְהַחֶ֗סֶד לְאֹהֲבָ֛יו וּלְשֹׁמְרֵ֥י מִצְוֺתָ֖ו לְאֶ֥לֶף דּֽוֹר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will know that Adonoy, your God, is *the* God, the trusted Almighty, who keeps the covenant and the kindliness for those who love Him and for those who keep His commandments, for a thousand generations.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Know therefore that the Lord thy God, he is God, the faithful God, who keeps covenant and troth with those who love him and keep his commandments to a thousand generations;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall know that the L-rd your G-d is the god, the faithful G-d, guarding the covenant and the lovingkindness for His lovers [those who do the mitzvoth out of love], and for the keepers of His mitzvoth [out of fear] for a thousand generations, [(but for "His lovers," for "thousands" (5:10)].


###### Deuteronomy 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:10)


וּמְשַׁלֵּ֧ם לְשֹׂנְאָ֛יו אֶל־פָּנָ֖יו לְהַאֲבִיד֑וֹ לֹ֤א יְאַחֵר֙ לְשֹׂ֣נְא֔וֹ אֶל־פָּנָ֖יו יְשַׁלֶּם־לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And He pays His enemy to his face to destroy him. He does not delay for His enemy—to his face does He pay him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and repays them that hate him to their face, to destroy them: he will not be slack to him that hates him, he will repay him to his face.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He pays His haters to his face [He pays him the recompense for his good, in his lifetime] to make him go lost [from the world to come]. He shall not delay to [pay] His hater; to his face shall He pay him.


###### Deuteronomy 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:11)


וְשָׁמַרְתָּ֨ אֶת־הַמִּצְוָ֜ה וְאֶת־הַֽחֻקִּ֣ים וְאֶת־הַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֧י מְצַוְּךָ֛ הַיּ֖וֹם לַעֲשׂוֹתָֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to guard the commandments and the statutes and the laws that I am commanding you today, to fulfill.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt therefore keep the commandments, and the statutes, and the judgments, which I command thee this day, to do them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall keep the mitzvah and the statutes and the judgments which I command you today to do them; [and, tomorrow, in the world to come, you will receive their reward.]


###### Deuteronomy 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:12)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** עֵ֣קֶב תִּשְׁמְע֗וּן אֵ֤ת הַמִּשְׁפָּטִים֙ הָאֵ֔לֶּה וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֥ם וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם וְשָׁמַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ לְךָ֗ אֶֽת־הַבְּרִית֙ וְאֶת־הַחֶ֔סֶד אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִשְׁבַּ֖ע לַאֲבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In the future, as a consequence of your heeding these laws, and your guarding and fulfilling them; Adonoy, your God, will guard for you the covenant and the kindliness that He swore to your forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Wherefore it shall come to pass, if you hearken to these judgments, and keep, and do them, that the Lord thy God shall keep unto thee the covenant and the troth which he swore to thy fathers:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, if (ekev) you hearken to these judgments, and heed them and do them [i.e., if you observe the relatively slight mitzvoth, which men are wont to tread under their heel (ekev)], then the L-rd your G-d will keep for you [His oath re] the covenant and the lovingkindness which He swore to your fathers.


###### Deuteronomy 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:13)


וַאֲהֵ֣בְךָ֔ וּבֵרַכְךָ֖ וְהִרְבֶּ֑ךָ וּבֵרַ֣ךְ פְּרִֽי־בִטְנְךָ֣ וּפְרִֽי־אַ֠דְמָתֶ֠ךָ דְּגָ֨נְךָ֜ וְתִירֹשְׁךָ֣ וְיִצְהָרֶ֗ךָ שְׁגַר־אֲלָפֶ֙יךָ֙ וְעַשְׁתְּרֹ֣ת צֹאנֶ֔ךָ עַ֚ל הָֽאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁבַּ֥ע לַאֲבֹתֶ֖יךָ לָ֥תֶת לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He will love you, and bless you, and multiply you; and He will bless the fruit of your belly and the fruit of your soil—your grain, your wine, and your olive oil—the offspring of your cattle, and the herds of your sheep, upon the soil that He swore to your forefathers to give you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he will love thee, and bless thee, and multiply thee: and will bless the fruit of thy womb, and the fruit of thy land, thy corn, and thy wine, and thy oil, the increase of thy cattle, and the flocks of thy sheep, in the land which he swore to thy fathers to give thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He will love you and bless you and multiply you, and He will bless the fruit of your belly and the fruit of your earth: your corn and your wine and your oil, your kine sprung [from the womb] and your choice ones of the flock on the earth which He swore to your fathers to give to you.


###### Deuteronomy 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:14)


בָּר֥וּךְ תִּֽהְיֶ֖ה מִכׇּל־הָעַמִּ֑ים לֹא־יִהְיֶ֥ה בְךָ֛ עָקָ֥ר וַֽעֲקָרָ֖ה וּבִבְהֶמְתֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will be blessed more than all the peoples; there will not be among you a sterile male or a barren female, nor among your animals.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt be blessed above all peoples: there shall not be male or female barren among you, or among your cattle.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessed shall you be of all the peoples. There shall not be among you a barren male or female, or among your beasts.


###### Deuteronomy 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:15)


וְהֵסִ֧יר יְהֹוָ֛ה מִמְּךָ֖ כׇּל־חֹ֑לִי וְכׇל־מַדְוֵי֩ מִצְרַ֨יִם הָרָעִ֜ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָדַ֗עְתָּ לֹ֤א יְשִׂימָם֙ בָּ֔ךְ וּנְתָנָ֖ם בְּכׇל־שֹׂנְאֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will remove from you all illness; and all the harmful diseases of Egypt that you know, He will not place upon you, but will give them to all your enemies.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord will take away from thee all sickness, and will put none of the evil diseases of Miżrayim, which thou knowst, upon thee; but will lay them upon all those who hate thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd shall remove from you every ill. And all the sore travails of Egypt which you knew, He shall not place in you, but He shall place them in all of your haters.


###### Deuteronomy 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:16)


וְאָכַלְתָּ֣ אֶת־כׇּל־הָֽעַמִּ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ נֹתֵ֣ן לָ֔ךְ לֹא־תָח֥וֹס עֵֽינְךָ֖ עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם וְלֹ֤א תַעֲבֹד֙ אֶת־אֱלֹ֣הֵיהֶ֔ם כִּֽי־מוֹקֵ֥שׁ ה֖וּא לָֽךְ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will consume all the peoples that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; do not take pity on them. Do not serve their gods, because they are a snare for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt consume all the peoples which the Lord thy God shall deliver to thee; thy eye shall have no pity upon them: neither shalt thou serve their gods; for that will be a snare to thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall devour all the peoples that the L-rd your G-d delivers unto you. Let your eye have no mercy upon them and do not serve their gods; for it is a snare unto you.


###### Deuteronomy 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:17)


כִּ֤י תֹאמַר֙ בִּלְבָ֣בְךָ֔ רַבִּ֛ים הַגּוֹיִ֥ם הָאֵ֖לֶּה מִמֶּ֑נִּי אֵיכָ֥ה אוּכַ֖ל לְהוֹרִישָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Perhaps you may think to yourself, ‘‘These nations are more numerous than I, how can I inherit them?’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou shalt say in thy heart, These nations are more than I; how can I dispossess them?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest you say in your heart: (Many) more are these nations than I — How will I be able to drive them out!


###### Deuteronomy 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:18)


לֹ֥א תִירָ֖א מֵהֶ֑ם זָכֹ֣ר תִּזְכֹּ֗ר אֵ֤ת אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂה֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לְפַרְעֹ֖ה וּלְכׇל־מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not fear them. Recall well what Adonoy, your God, did to Pharaoh and to all the Egyptians.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not be afraid of them: but shalt well remember what the Lord thy God did to Par῾o, and to all Miżrayim;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
— You shall not fear them. Remember well what the L-rd your G-d did to Pharaoh and to all of Egypt.


###### Deuteronomy 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:19)


הַמַּסֹּ֨ת הַגְּדֹלֹ֜ת אֲשֶׁר־רָא֣וּ עֵינֶ֗יךָ וְהָאֹתֹ֤ת וְהַמֹּֽפְתִים֙ וְהַיָּ֤ד הַחֲזָקָה֙ וְהַזְּרֹ֣עַ הַנְּטוּיָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר הוֹצִֽאֲךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ כֵּֽן־יַעֲשֶׂ֞ה יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ לְכׇל־הָ֣עַמִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה יָרֵ֖א מִפְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The great tests you witnessed, and the signs and the wonders and the strong hand and the extended arm with which Adonoy, your God, took you out; so will Adonoy, your God, do to all the peoples that you are afraid of.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the great trials which thy eyes saw, and the signs, and the wonders, and the mighty hand, and the stretched out arm, whereby the Lord thy God brought thee out: so shall the Lord thy God do to all the people of whom thou art afraid.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The great trials that your eyes saw, and the signs, and the wonders (see 4:34), and the strong hand [the (plague of) pestilence], and the outstretched arm [the "sword" of the plague of the first-born] wherewith the L-rd your G-d took you out. Thus shall the L-rd your G-d do to all of the peoples whom you fear.


###### Deuteronomy 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:20)


וְגַם֙ אֶת־הַצִּרְעָ֔ה יְשַׁלַּ֛ח יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בָּ֑ם עַד־אֲבֹ֗ד הַנִּשְׁאָרִ֛ים וְהַנִּסְתָּרִ֖ים מִפָּנֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Also the *tzir’oh* will Adonoy, your God, send against them, until the destruction of those who survive and hide from you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover the Lord thy God will send the hornet among them, until they that are left, and hide themselves from thee, be destroyed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And also the tzirah [a kind of flying insect, which poisoned them, and castrated them, and blinded their eyes wherever they were hiding] will the L-rd your G-d send against them, until there shall perish the remnant and the hidden from before you.


###### Deuteronomy 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:21)


לֹ֥א תַעֲרֹ֖ץ מִפְּנֵיהֶ֑ם כִּֽי־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ בְּקִרְבֶּ֔ךָ אֵ֥ל גָּד֖וֹל וְנוֹרָֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not be intimidated by them, for Adonoy, your God, is amid you, the great and fearsome Almighty.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not be terrified by them: for the Lord thy God is among you, a mighty God and terrible.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not break before them, for the L-rd your G-d is in your midst, a great and awesome G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:22)


וְנָשַׁל֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ אֶת־הַגּוֹיִ֥ם הָאֵ֛ל מִפָּנֶ֖יךָ מְעַ֣ט מְעָ֑ט לֹ֤א תוּכַל֙ כַּלֹּתָ֣ם מַהֵ֔ר פֶּן־תִּרְבֶּ֥ה עָלֶ֖יךָ חַיַּ֥ת הַשָּׂדֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, will expel these nations before you, gradually; you will be unable to exterminate them rapidly, lest the wild animals multiply against you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord thy God will put out those nations before thee by little and little: thou mayst not consume them at once, lest the wild beasts of the field increase upon thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d will cast out these nations before you little by little. You will not be able to consume them quickly, lest there increase against you the animals of the field.


###### Deuteronomy 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:23)


וּנְתָנָ֛ם יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ לְפָנֶ֑יךָ וְהָמָם֙ מְהוּמָ֣ה גְדֹלָ֔ה עַ֖ד הִשָּׁמְדָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, will defeat them before you, and will confuse them with great confusion until they are destroyed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the Lord thy God shall deliver them to thee, and shall destroy them with a mighty destruction, until they are destroyed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d shall deliver them before you. And He shall confound them with a great confusion until they are destroyed.


###### Deuteronomy 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:24)


וְנָתַ֤ן מַלְכֵיהֶם֙ בְּיָדֶ֔ךָ וְהַאֲבַדְתָּ֣ אֶת־שְׁמָ֔ם מִתַּ֖חַת הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם לֹֽא־יִתְיַצֵּ֥ב אִישׁ֙ בְּפָנֶ֔יךָ עַ֥ד הִשְׁמִֽדְךָ֖ אֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He will put their kings in your hand, and you will eliminate their name from beneath the heavens; no man will stand up to you until you have destroyed them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall deliver their kings into thy hand, and thou shalt destroy their name from under heaven: there shall no man be able to stand before thee, until thou hast destroyed them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He will deliver their kings into your hand, and you shall cause their name to go lost from under the heavens. A man will not stand up against you until you have destroyed them.


###### Deuteronomy 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:25)


פְּסִילֵ֥י אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֖ם תִּשְׂרְפ֣וּן בָּאֵ֑שׁ לֹֽא־תַחְמֹד֩ כֶּ֨סֶף וְזָהָ֤ב עֲלֵיהֶם֙ וְלָקַחְתָּ֣ לָ֔ךְ פֶּ֚ן תִּוָּקֵ֣שׁ בּ֔וֹ כִּ֧י תוֹעֲבַ֛ת יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Burn the statues of their gods in fire; do not covet the silver and gold upon them and take it for yourself, lest you be ensnared by it, because it is abominated by Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The carvings of their gods shall you burn with fire: thou shalt not desire the silver or gold that is on them, or take it to thee, lest thou be snared with it: for it is an abomination to the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The graven images of their gods shall you burn in fire. You shall not covet the silver and the gold upon them and take it to you lest you be ensnared by it. For it is the abomination of the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 7:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/7:26)


וְלֹא־תָבִ֤יא תֽוֹעֵבָה֙ אֶל־בֵּיתֶ֔ךָ וְהָיִ֥יתָ חֵ֖רֶם כָּמֹ֑הוּ שַׁקֵּ֧ץ׀תְּשַׁקְּצֶ֛נּוּ וְתַעֵ֥ב׀תְּֽתַעֲבֶ֖נּוּ כִּי־חֵ֥רֶם הֽוּא׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not bring abomination into your house [lest] you become *cheirem* like it; thoroughly revile and abominate it, for it is *cheirem.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shalt thou bring an abomination into thy house, lest thou become accursed like it: but thou shalt utterly detest it, and thou shalt utterly abhor it; for it is a cursed thing.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not bring an abomination into your house, lest you be rejected as it is. Despise shall you despise it and abominate shall you abominate it; for it is rejected.



## Chapter 8

###### Deuteronomy 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:1)


כׇּל־הַמִּצְוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֧י מְצַוְּךָ֛ הַיּ֖וֹם תִּשְׁמְר֣וּן לַעֲשׂ֑וֹת לְמַ֨עַן תִּֽחְי֜וּן וּרְבִיתֶ֗ם וּבָאתֶם֙ וִֽירִשְׁתֶּ֣ם אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁבַּ֥ע יְהֹוָ֖ה לַאֲבֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today, are you to guard so as to fulfill; in order that you live and multiply and arrive to inherit the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the commandments which I command thee this day shall you observe to do, that you may live, and multiply, and go in and possess the land which the Lord swore to your fathers.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every mitzvah which I command to you this day shall you observe to do, that you may live and multiply, and come and inherit the land that the L-rd your G-d swore to your fathers.


###### Deuteronomy 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:2)


וְזָכַרְתָּ֣ אֶת־כׇּל־הַדֶּ֗רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֨ר הוֹלִֽיכְךָ֜ יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ זֶ֛ה אַרְבָּעִ֥ים שָׁנָ֖ה בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר לְמַ֨עַן עַנֹּֽתְךָ֜ לְנַסֹּֽתְךָ֗ לָדַ֜עַת אֶת־אֲשֶׁ֧ר בִּֽלְבָבְךָ֛ הֲתִשְׁמֹ֥ר מִצְוֺתָ֖ו אִם־לֹֽא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to recall the entire way on which Adonoy, your God, led you these forty years in the wilderness; in order to oppress you, to test you, to know what is in your heart—will you guard His commandments or not?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt remember all the way which the Lord thy God led thee these forty years in the wilderness, to humble thee, and to prove thee, to know what was in thy heart, whether thou wouldst keep his commandments, or no.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall remember all the way in which the L-rd your G-d led you this forty years in the desert, to afflict you to test you to know what was in your heart, whether you would keep His mitzvoth [without trying Him or "bethinking yourself" of Him], or not.


###### Deuteronomy 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:3)


וַֽיְעַנְּךָ֮ וַיַּרְעִבֶ֒ךָ֒ וַיַּאֲכִֽלְךָ֤ אֶת־הַמָּן֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־יָדַ֔עְתָּ וְלֹ֥א יָדְע֖וּן אֲבֹתֶ֑יךָ לְמַ֣עַן הוֹדִֽיעֲךָ֗ כִּ֠י לֹ֣א עַל־הַלֶּ֤חֶם לְבַדּוֹ֙ יִחְיֶ֣ה הָֽאָדָ֔ם כִּ֛י עַל־כׇּל־מוֹצָ֥א פִֽי־יְהֹוָ֖ה יִחְיֶ֥ה הָאָדָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He oppressed you and made you hungry, and He fed you the *mon* that neither you knew nor your forefathers knew, in order to inform you that not by bread alone does man live, rather by the entire expression of Adonoy’s word does man live.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he humbled thee, and suffered thee to hunger, and fed thee with manna, which thou knewest not, neither did thy fathers know; that he might make thee know that man does not live by bread only, but by every word that proceeds out of the mouth of the Lord does man live.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He afflicted you, and He made you hunger, and He fed you the manna, which you did not know, and which your fathers did not know, to make it known to you that not through bread alone shall a man live, but by all that issues from the mouth of the L-rd shall a man live.


###### Deuteronomy 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:4)


שִׂמְלָ֨תְךָ֜ לֹ֤א בָֽלְתָה֙ מֵֽעָלֶ֔יךָ וְרַגְלְךָ֖ לֹ֣א בָצֵ֑קָה זֶ֖ה אַרְבָּעִ֥ים שָׁנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Your garment did not wear out on you, nor did your foot swell these forty years.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy garment grew not old upon thee, nor did thy foot swell, these forty years.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your raiment did not wear out from upon you [The clouds of glory would rub their clothing clean, and the garments of children would grow to match their growth], and your feet did not swell [like dough (as occurs with those who go barefoot)] this forty years.


###### Deuteronomy 8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:5)


וְיָדַעְתָּ֖ עִם־לְבָבֶ֑ךָ כִּ֗י כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר יְיַסֵּ֥ר אִישׁ֙ אֶת־בְּנ֔וֹ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ מְיַסְּרֶֽךָּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will know with your heart that the way a man trains his son does Adonoy, your God, train you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt also consider in thy heart, that, as a man chastens his son, so the Lord thy God chastens thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall know within your heart that as a man afflicts his son, so does the L-rd your G-d afflict you.


###### Deuteronomy 8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:6)


וְשָׁ֣מַרְתָּ֔ אֶת־מִצְוֺ֖ת יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ לָלֶ֥כֶת בִּדְרָכָ֖יו וּלְיִרְאָ֥ה אֹתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, to go in His ways and to fear Him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore thou shalt keep the commandments of the Lord thy God, to walk in his ways, and to fear him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall keep the mitzvoth of the L-rd your G-d, to walk in His ways and fear Him.


###### Deuteronomy 8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:7)


כִּ֚י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ מְבִֽיאֲךָ֖ אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ טוֹבָ֑ה אֶ֚רֶץ נַ֣חֲלֵי מָ֔יִם עֲיָנֹת֙ וּתְהֹמֹ֔ת יֹצְאִ֥ים בַּבִּקְעָ֖ה וּבָהָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For Adonoy, your God, is bringing you to a good land, a land with streams of water, springs and groundwater that emerge in valley and hill.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord thy God brings thee into a good land, a land of water courses, of fountains and depths that spring out of valleys and hills;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the L-rd your G-d is bringing you to a good land, a land of streams of water, fountains and depths, issuing forth in valleys and in mountains.


###### Deuteronomy 8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:8)


אֶ֤רֶץ חִטָּה֙ וּשְׂעֹרָ֔ה וְגֶ֥פֶן וּתְאֵנָ֖ה וְרִמּ֑וֹן אֶֽרֶץ־זֵ֥ית שֶׁ֖מֶן וּדְבָֽשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A land of wheat and barley, and grapes and figs and pomegranates; a land of olives and honey.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a land of wheat, and barley, and vines, and fig trees, and pomegranates; a land of olive oil, and honey;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A land of wheat, barley, grapevine, fig, and pomegranate; a land of olive-oil and honey.


###### Deuteronomy 8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:9)


אֶ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹ֤א בְמִסְכֵּנֻת֙ תֹּֽאכַל־בָּ֣הּ לֶ֔חֶם לֹֽא־תֶחְסַ֥ר כֹּ֖ל בָּ֑הּ אֶ֚רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֲבָנֶ֣יהָ בַרְזֶ֔ל וּמֵהֲרָרֶ֖יהָ תַּחְצֹ֥ב נְחֹֽשֶׁת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A land where you will not eat bread in poverty, you will lack nothing there; a land whose stones are iron, and from whose hills you will mine copper.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a land in which thou shalt eat bread without scarceness, thou shalt not lack any thing in it; a land the stones of which are iron, and out of whose hills thou mayst dig brass.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A land where not in constraint shall you eat bread. You shall lack nothing in it; a land whose stones are iron and from whose mountains you shall hew copper.


###### Deuteronomy 8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:10)


וְאָכַלְתָּ֖ וְשָׂבָ֑עְתָּ וּבֵֽרַכְתָּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ עַל־הָאָ֥רֶץ הַטֹּבָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָֽתַן־לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will eat and be full, and you will bless Adonoy, your God, for the good land He has given you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou hast eaten and art replete, then thou shalt bless the Lord thy God for the good land which he has given thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall eat, and you shall be sated, and you shall bless the L-rd your G-d for the good land that He has given you.


###### Deuteronomy 8:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:11)


הִשָּׁ֣מֶר לְךָ֔ פֶּן־תִּשְׁכַּ֖ח אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ לְבִלְתִּ֨י שְׁמֹ֤ר מִצְוֺתָיו֙ וּמִשְׁפָּטָ֣יו וְחֻקֹּתָ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֛ר אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look out for yourself lest you forget Adonoy, your God, to not guard His commandments, His laws, and His statutes that I am commanding you today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Beware that thou forget not the Lord thy God, in not keeping his commandments, and his judgments, and his statutes, which I command thee this day:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourself lest you forget the L-rd your G-d, not to keep His mitzvoth and His judgments and His statutes that I command you this day.


###### Deuteronomy 8:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:12)


פֶּן־תֹּאכַ֖ל וְשָׂבָ֑עְתָּ וּבָתִּ֥ים טֹבִ֛ים תִּבְנֶ֖ה וְיָשָֽׁבְתָּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Lest you eat and be full, and build good houses and live [in them.]


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest
				when thou hast eaten and art replete, and hast built goodly houses, and dwelt in them;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest you eat and be sated, and you build and inhabit good houses,


###### Deuteronomy 8:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:13)


וּבְקָֽרְךָ֤ וְצֹֽאנְךָ֙ יִרְבְּיֻ֔ן וְכֶ֥סֶף וְזָהָ֖ב יִרְבֶּה־לָּ֑ךְ וְכֹ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־לְךָ֖ יִרְבֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And your cattle and your sheep multiply, and silver and gold multiply for you, and everything you own multiplies.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and when thy herds and thy flocks multiply, and thy silver and thy gold are multiplied, and all that thou hast is multiplied;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your herds and your flocks increase, and silver and gold increase for you, and whatever you have increase,


###### Deuteronomy 8:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:14)


וְרָ֖ם לְבָבֶ֑ךָ וְשָֽׁכַחְתָּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ הַמּוֹצִיאֲךָ֛ מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרַ֖יִם מִבֵּ֥ית עֲבָדִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And your pride increases, and you forget Adonoy, your God, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of slavery;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then thy heart be lifted up, and thou forget the Lord thy God, who brought thee out of the land of Miżrayim, from the house of bondage;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your heart be lifted and you forget the L-rd your G-d who took you out of the land of Egypt from the house of bondage,


###### Deuteronomy 8:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:15)


הַמּוֹלִ֨יכְךָ֜ בַּמִּדְבָּ֣ר**׀** הַגָּדֹ֣ל וְהַנּוֹרָ֗א נָחָ֤שׁ׀שָׂרָף֙ וְעַקְרָ֔ב וְצִמָּא֖וֹן אֲשֶׁ֣ר אֵֽין־מָ֑יִם הַמּוֹצִ֤יא לְךָ֙ מַ֔יִם מִצּ֖וּר הַֽחַלָּמִֽישׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Who led you through the wilderness—the great and awesome[one]— snake, serpent and scorpion, and thirst where there is no water; Who took water out for you from solid rock;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who led thee through that great and terrible wilderness, in which were venomous serpents, and scorpions, and drought, where there was no water; who brought forth water for thee out of the rock of flint;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who led you in the great, awesome desert of snake, serpent, scorpion and waterless drought, who brought forth water for you from the rock of flint,


###### Deuteronomy 8:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:16)


הַמַּאֲכִ֨לְךָ֥ מָן֙ בַּמִּדְבָּ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יָדְע֖וּן אֲבֹתֶ֑יךָ לְמַ֣עַן עַנֹּֽתְךָ֗ וּלְמַ֙עַן֙ נַסֹּתֶ֔ךָ לְהֵיטִֽבְךָ֖ בְּאַחֲרִיתֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Who fed you *mon* in the wilderness that your forefathers had not known, in order to oppress you and in order to test you, to ultimately benefit you—


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who fed thee in the wilderness with manna, which thy fathers knew not, that he might afflict thee, and that he might prove thee, to do thee good at thy latter end;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who fed you manna in the desert, which your fathers did not know, in order to afflict you and in order to try you, to benefit you in your latter end,


###### Deuteronomy 8:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:17)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֖ בִּלְבָבֶ֑ךָ כֹּחִי֙ וְעֹ֣צֶם יָדִ֔י עָ֥שָׂה לִ֖י אֶת־הַחַ֥יִל הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And you think, ‘‘My strength and the power of my hand have acquired this wealth for me.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou say in thy heart, My power and the might of my hand have gotten me this wealth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you say in your heart: My strength and the might of my hand have wrought this wealth for me —


###### Deuteronomy 8:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:18)


וְזָֽכַרְתָּ֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ כִּ֣י ה֗וּא הַנֹּתֵ֥ן לְךָ֛ כֹּ֖חַ לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת חָ֑יִל לְמַ֨עַן הָקִ֧ים אֶת־בְּרִית֛וֹ אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁבַּ֥ע לַאֲבֹתֶ֖יךָ כַּיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Recall Adonoy, your God, for it is He Who gives you counsel to acquire wealth, in order to establish His covenant that He swore to your forefathers this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But thou shalt remember the Lord thy God: for it is he who gives thee power to get wealth, that he may establish his covenant which he swore to thy fathers, as it is this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall remember the L-rd your G-d. For it is He who gives you strength to prosper, to establish His covenant that He swore to your fathers, as of this day.


###### Deuteronomy 8:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:19)


וְהָיָ֗ה אִם־שָׁכֹ֤חַ תִּשְׁכַּח֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וְהָֽלַכְתָּ֗ אַחֲרֵי֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וַעֲבַדְתָּ֖ם וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוִ֣יתָ לָהֶ֑ם הַעִדֹ֤תִי בָכֶם֙ הַיּ֔וֹם כִּ֥י אָבֹ֖ד תֹּאבֵדֽוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When and if you forget Adonoy, your God, and follow other gods, and serve them and bow to them, I testify about you today that you will surely be destroyed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, if thou do at all forget the Lord thy God, and walk after other gods, and serve them, and worship them, I testify against you this day that you shall surely perish.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, if you forget the L-rd your G-d and walk after other gods and serve them and bow down to them, I testify against you this day that you shall surely go lost.


###### Deuteronomy 8:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/8:20)


כַּגּוֹיִ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ מַאֲבִ֣יד מִפְּנֵיכֶ֔ם כֵּ֖ן תֹּאבֵד֑וּן עֵ֚קֶב לֹ֣א תִשְׁמְע֔וּן בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Like the nations that Adonoy is destroying before you, so will you be destroyed, as a consequence of your not heeding Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As the nations which the Lord destroys before your face, so shall you perish; because you would not be obedient to the voice of the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As the nations which the L-rd causes to go lost from before you, so will you go lost, for you did not hearken to the voice of the L-rd your G-d.



## Chapter 9

###### Deuteronomy 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:1)


שְׁמַ֣ע יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל אַתָּ֨ה עֹבֵ֤ר הַיּוֹם֙ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן לָבֹא֙ לָרֶ֣שֶׁת גּוֹיִ֔ם גְּדֹלִ֥ים וַעֲצֻמִ֖ים מִמֶּ֑ךָּ עָרִ֛ים גְּדֹלֹ֥ת וּבְצֻרֹ֖ת בַּשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Listen, Yisroel! You are today crossing the Yardein to come inherit nations greater and more powerful than you; cities great and fortified to the sky.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, O Yisra᾽el: Thou art to pass over the Yarden this day, to go in to possess nations greater and mightier than thyself, cities great and fortified up to heaven,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Hear, O Israel, you are crossing over the Jordan today to come to inherit nations who are greater and mightier than you [(You are mighty, but they are mightier than you)], great cities, and fortified in the heavens.


###### Deuteronomy 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:2)


עַֽם־גָּד֥וֹל וָרָ֖ם בְּנֵ֣י עֲנָקִ֑ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר אַתָּ֤ה יָדַ֙עְתָּ֙ וְאַתָּ֣ה שָׁמַ֔עְתָּ מִ֣י יִתְיַצֵּ֔ב לִפְנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֥י עֲנָֽק׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A great and powerful people, descendants of the Anokim, about whom you know and heard, ‘‘who can stand up to the descendants of Anok?’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a people great and tall, the children of the ῾Anaqim, whom thou knowst, and of whom thou hast heard say, Who can stand before the children of ῾Anaq!


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A people great and lofty, the sons of Anakim, whom you know, and of whom you have heard (saying): Who can stand up against the sons of Anak!


###### Deuteronomy 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:3)


וְיָדַעְתָּ֣ הַיּ֗וֹם כִּי֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ הֽוּא־הָעֹבֵ֤ר לְפָנֶ֙יךָ֙ אֵ֣שׁ אֹֽכְלָ֔ה ה֧וּא יַשְׁמִידֵ֛ם וְה֥וּא יַכְנִיעֵ֖ם לְפָנֶ֑יךָ וְהֽוֹרַשְׁתָּ֤ם וְהַֽאֲבַדְתָּם֙ מַהֵ֔ר כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר דִּבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You know today that Adonoy, your God, is the One crossing before you, a consuming fire, He will destroy them and He will subdue them before you; you will expel them and destroy them quickly, as Adonoy promised for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Understand therefore this day, that the Lord thy God is he who goes over before thee; as a consuming fire he shall destroy them, and he shall bring them down before thy face: so shalt thou drive them out, and destroy them quickly, as the Lord has said to thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall know this day that the L-rd your G-d — He crosses over before you, a consuming fire. He shall destroy them and He shall humble them before you. And you shall drive them out, and you shall cause them to go lost quickly, as the L-rd has spoken to you.


###### Deuteronomy 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:4)


אַל־תֹּאמַ֣ר בִּלְבָבְךָ֗ בַּהֲדֹ֣ף יְהֹוָה֩ אֱלֹהֶ֨יךָ אֹתָ֥ם׀מִלְּפָנֶ֘יךָ֮ לֵאמֹר֒ בְּצִדְקָתִי֙ הֱבִיאַ֣נִי יְהֹוָ֔ה לָרֶ֖שֶׁת אֶת־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֑את וּבְרִשְׁעַת֙ הַגּוֹיִ֣ם הָאֵ֔לֶּה יְהֹוָ֖ה מוֹרִישָׁ֥ם מִפָּנֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not think, when Adonoy, your God, smashes them before you, saying: ‘‘Because of my righteousness has Adonoy brought me to inherit this land,’’ and because of the wickedness of these nations is Adonoy expelling them before you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Speak not thou in thy heart, after the Lord thy God has cast them out from before thee, saying, For my righteousness the Lord has brought me in to possess this land: and for the wickedness of these nations the Lord does drive them out from before thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not say in your heart, when the L-rd your G-d thrusts them from before you: In my righteousness did the L-rd bring me to inherit this land, and that because of the wickedness of these nations [together with your righteousness] the L-rd drives them out before you.


###### Deuteronomy 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:5)


לֹ֣א בְצִדְקָתְךָ֗ וּבְיֹ֙שֶׁר֙ לְבָ֣בְךָ֔ אַתָּ֥ה בָ֖א לָרֶ֣שֶׁת אֶת־אַרְצָ֑ם כִּ֞י בְּרִשְׁעַ֣ת**׀** הַגּוֹיִ֣ם הָאֵ֗לֶּה יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ מוֹרִישָׁ֣ם מִפָּנֶ֔יךָ וּלְמַ֜עַן הָקִ֣ים אֶת־הַדָּבָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִשְׁבַּ֤ע יְהֹוָה֙ לַאֲבֹתֶ֔יךָ לְאַבְרָהָ֥ם לְיִצְחָ֖ק וּֽלְיַעֲקֹֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Not because of your righteousness and the uprightness of your heart are you coming to inherit their land. Rather, because of the wickedness of these nations is Adonoy, your God, expelling them before you, and in order to fulfill the matter that Adonoy swore to your forefathers—to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Not for thy righteousness, or for the uprightness of thine heart, dost thou go to possess their land: but on account of the wickedness of these nations the Lord thy God does drive them out from before thee, and that he may perform the word which the Lord swore to thy fathers, Avraham, Yiżĥaq, and Ya῾aqov.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Not in your righteousness and in the uprightness of your heart do you come to inherit their land, but in the wickedness of these nations does the L-rd your G-d drive them out before you, and to establish the thing that the L-rd swore to your fathers: to Abraham, to Isaac, and to Jacob.


###### Deuteronomy 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:6)


וְיָדַעְתָּ֗ כִּ֠י לֹ֤א בְצִדְקָֽתְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱ֠לֹהֶ֠יךָ נֹתֵ֨ן לְךָ֜ אֶת־הָאָ֧רֶץ הַטּוֹבָ֛ה הַזֹּ֖את לְרִשְׁתָּ֑הּ כִּ֥י עַם־קְשֵׁה־עֹ֖רֶף אָֽתָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Know that not because of your righteousness is Adonoy, your God, giving you this good land to inherit, for you are a stubborn people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Understand therefore, that the Lord thy God gives thee not this good land to possess it for thy righteousness; for thou art a stiffnecked people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall know that it is not in your righteousness that the L-rd your G-d gives you this good land to inherit it, for you are a stiff-necked people.


###### Deuteronomy 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:7)


זְכֹר֙ אַל־תִּשְׁכַּ֔ח אֵ֧ת אֲשֶׁר־הִקְצַ֛פְתָּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר לְמִן־הַיּ֞וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יָצָ֣אתָ**׀** מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֗יִם עַד־בֹּֽאֲכֶם֙ עַד־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֔ה מַמְרִ֥ים הֱיִיתֶ֖ם עִם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Recall, do not forget, how you angered Adonoy, your God, in the wilderness; from the day you left the land of Egypt until you arrived at this place you have been defiers with Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remember, and
				forget not, how thou didst provoke the Lord thy God to anger in the wilderness: from the day that thou didst depart out of the land of Miżrayim, until you came to this place, you have been rebellious against the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember, do not forget, your having angered the L-rd your G-d in the desert. From the day you left the land of Egypt until your coming to this place, you have been rebellious against the L-rd.


###### Deuteronomy 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:8)


וּבְחֹרֵ֥ב הִקְצַפְתֶּ֖ם אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה וַיִּתְאַנַּ֧ף יְהֹוָ֛ה בָּכֶ֖ם לְהַשְׁמִ֥יד אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And in Choreiv you angered Adonoy, and Adonoy was infuriated with you to destroy you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also in Ḥorev you provoked the Lord to anger, so that the Lord was angry with you to have destroyed you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in Chorev you angered the L-rd, and the L-rd was wroth with you to destroy you.


###### Deuteronomy 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:9)


בַּעֲלֹתִ֣י הָהָ֗רָה לָקַ֜חַת לוּחֹ֤ת הָֽאֲבָנִים֙ לוּחֹ֣ת הַבְּרִ֔ית אֲשֶׁר־כָּרַ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה עִמָּכֶ֑ם וָאֵשֵׁ֣ב בָּהָ֗ר אַרְבָּעִ֥ים יוֹם֙ וְאַרְבָּעִ֣ים לַ֔יְלָה לֶ֚חֶם לֹ֣א אָכַ֔לְתִּי וּמַ֖יִם לֹ֥א שָׁתִֽיתִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When I went up the mountain to take the stone tablets, the tablets of the covenant that Adonoy made with you, I remained on the mountain forty days and forty nights; I ate no bread and drank no water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When I was gone up into the mountain to receive the tablets of stone, the tablets of the covenant which the Lord made with you, then I abode in the mountain forty days and forty nights, I neither did eat bread nor drink water:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I went up to the mountain to take the tablets of stone, the tablets of the covenant that the L-rd made with you. And I was kept in the mountain forty days and forty nights. Bread I did not eat, and water I did not drink.


###### Deuteronomy 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:10)


וַיִּתֵּ֨ן יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י אֶת־שְׁנֵי֙ לוּחֹ֣ת הָֽאֲבָנִ֔ים כְּתֻבִ֖ים בְּאֶצְבַּ֣ע אֱלֹהִ֑ים וַעֲלֵיהֶ֗ם כְּֽכׇל־הַדְּבָרִ֡ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֶּר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה עִמָּכֶ֥ם בָּהָ֛ר מִתּ֥וֹךְ הָאֵ֖שׁ בְּי֥וֹם הַקָּהָֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy gave me the two stone tablets written with God’s finger and upon them all the statements that Adonoy addressed to you on the mountain from within the fire, on the day of assembly.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord delivered to me two tablets of stone written with the finger of God; and on them was written according to all the words which the Lord spoke with you in the mountain out of the midst of the fire on the day of the assembly.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd gave me the two tablets of stone, [(both were alike)], written with the finger of G-d; and on them as all the things that the L-rd spoke with you in the mountain from the midst of the fire on the day of the assembly.


###### Deuteronomy 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:11)


וַיְהִ֗י מִקֵּץ֙ אַרְבָּעִ֣ים י֔וֹם וְאַרְבָּעִ֖ים לָ֑יְלָה נָתַ֨ן יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י אֶת־שְׁנֵ֛י לֻחֹ֥ת הָאֲבָנִ֖ים לֻח֥וֹת הַבְּרִֽית׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When, at the end of forty days and forty nights, Adonoy gave me the two stone tablets, the covenant tablets,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass at the end of forty days and forty nights, that the Lord gave me the two tablets of stone, the tables of the covenant.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was at the end of forty days and forty nights that the L-rd gave me the two tablets of stone, the tablets of the covenant.


###### Deuteronomy 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:12)


וַיֹּ֨אמֶר יְהֹוָ֜ה אֵלַ֗י ק֣וּם רֵ֤ד מַהֵר֙ מִזֶּ֔ה כִּ֚י שִׁחֵ֣ת עַמְּךָ֔ אֲשֶׁ֥ר הוֹצֵ֖אתָ מִמִּצְרָ֑יִם סָ֣רוּ מַהֵ֗ר מִן־הַדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר צִוִּיתִ֔ם עָשׂ֥וּ לָהֶ֖ם מַסֵּכָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Get ready! Go down quickly from here, because your people has corrupted—whom you took out of Egypt; they have veered swiftly from the way that I commanded them; they made them a molten image.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to me, Arise, get thee down quickly from here; for thy people which thou hast brought forth out of Miżrayim have become corrupt; they are quickly turned aside out of the way which I commanded them; they have made them a molten image.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me: Arise, descend quickly hence. For the people that you took out of Egypt have corrupted themselves. They have turned quickly from the way that I commanded them; they have made a molten image.


###### Deuteronomy 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:13)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלַ֣י לֵאמֹ֑ר רָאִ֙יתִי֙ אֶת־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה וְהִנֵּ֥ה עַם־קְשֵׁה־עֹ֖רֶף הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me, to say, ‘‘I have seen this people, and look, it is a stubborn people!


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord spoke to me saying, I have seen this people, and, behold, it is a stiffnecked people:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me: I have seen this people, and, behold, it is a stiff-necked people.


###### Deuteronomy 9:14

הֶ֤רֶף מִמֶּ֙נִּי֙ וְאַשְׁמִידֵ֔ם וְאֶמְחֶ֣ה אֶת־שְׁמָ֔ם מִתַּ֖חַת הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וְאֶֽעֱשֶׂה֙ אֽוֹתְךָ֔ לְגוֹי־עָצ֥וּם וָרָ֖ב מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Leave Me alone and let Me destroy them and eliminate their name from beneath the sky, and let Me make you into a nation more powerful and numerous than they.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
let me alone, that I may destroy them, and blot out their name from under heaven: and I will make of thee a nation mightier and greater than they.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Quit Me and I shall destroy them, and I shall erase their name from beneath the heavens, and I shall make you a nation mightier and more numerous than they.

וָאֵ֗פֶן וָֽאֵרֵד֙ מִן־הָהָ֔ר וְהָהָ֖ר בֹּעֵ֣ר בָּאֵ֑שׁ וּשְׁנֵי֙ לוּחֹ֣ת הַבְּרִ֔ית עַ֖ל שְׁתֵּ֥י יָדָֽי׃

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I saw, and, behold, you had sinned against the L-rd your G-d; you had made for yourselves a molten calf. You had turned quickly from the way that the L-rd had commanded you.

וָֽאֶתְנַפַּל֩ לִפְנֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֜ה כָּרִאשֹׁנָ֗ה אַרְבָּעִ֥ים יוֹם֙ וְאַרְבָּעִ֣ים לַ֔יְלָה לֶ֚חֶם לֹ֣א אָכַ֔לְתִּי וּמַ֖יִם לֹ֣א שָׁתִ֑יתִי עַ֤ל כׇּל־חַטַּאתְכֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חֲטָאתֶ֔ם לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת הָרַ֛ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהַכְעִיסֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I prostrated myself before Adonoy as before, forty days and forty nights, bread I did not eat nor water did I drink; for all of your sin that you sinned by doing what is evil in Adonoy’s eyes to anger Him.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I prostrated myself before the L-rd as at first (see Exodus 33:30), forty days and forty nights. Bread I did not eat, and water I did not drink, because of all your sins wherein you had sinned, to do the evil in the eyes of the L-rd to anger Him.

כִּ֣י יָגֹ֗רְתִּי מִפְּנֵ֤י הָאַף֙ וְהַ֣חֵמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר קָצַ֧ף יְהֹוָ֛ה עֲלֵיכֶ֖ם לְהַשְׁמִ֣יד אֶתְכֶ֑ם וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ע יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י גַּ֖ם בַּפַּ֥עַם הַהִֽוא׃

###### Deuteronomy 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:21)


וְֽאֶת־חַטַּאתְכֶ֞ם אֲשֶׁר־עֲשִׂיתֶ֣ם אֶת־הָעֵ֗גֶל לָקַ֘חְתִּי֮ וָאֶשְׂרֹ֣ף אֹת֣וֹ׀בָּאֵשׁ֒ וָאֶכֹּ֨ת אֹת֤וֹ טָחוֹן֙ הֵיטֵ֔ב עַ֥ד אֲשֶׁר־דַּ֖ק לְעָפָ֑ר וָֽאַשְׁלִךְ֙ אֶת־עֲפָר֔וֹ אֶל־הַנַּ֖חַל הַיֹּרֵ֥ד מִן־הָהָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the sin[-object] that you made, the calf, I took and burned it in fire. I crushed it—thoroughly grinding it—until it was fine—to dust; and I threw its dust into the stream that was flowing down from the mountain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I took your sin, the calf which you had made, and burnt it with fire, and stamped it, and ground it very small, until it was as fine as dust: and I cast the dust of it into the stream that descended out of the mountain.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your sin that you wrought — the calf — I took it and I burnt it in fire, and I crushed it, grinding it down, until it was thin as dust. And I cast its dust into the stream that ran down from the mountain.

###### Deuteronomy 9:23

וּבִשְׁלֹ֨חַ יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶתְכֶ֗ם מִקָּדֵ֤שׁ בַּרְנֵ֙עַ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר עֲלוּ֙ וּרְשׁ֣וּ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לָכֶ֑ם וַתַּמְר֗וּ אֶת־פִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹ֣הֵיכֶ֔ם וְלֹ֤א הֶֽאֱמַנְתֶּם֙ ל֔וֹ וְלֹ֥א שְׁמַעְתֶּ֖ם בְּקֹלֽוֹ׃

###### Deuteronomy 9:25

וָֽאֶתְנַפַּ֞ל לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה אֵ֣ת אַרְבָּעִ֥ים הַיּ֛וֹם וְאֶת־אַרְבָּעִ֥ים הַלַּ֖יְלָה אֲשֶׁ֣ר הִתְנַפָּ֑לְתִּי כִּֽי־אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהַשְׁמִ֥יד אֶתְכֶֽם׃

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Deuteronomy 9:26

וָאֶתְפַּלֵּ֣ל אֶל־יְהֹוָה֮ וָאֹמַר֒ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה אַל־תַּשְׁחֵ֤ת עַמְּךָ֙ וְנַחֲלָ֣תְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁ֥ר פָּדִ֖יתָ בְּגׇדְלֶ֑ךָ אֲשֶׁר־הוֹצֵ֥אתָ מִמִּצְרַ֖יִם בְּיָ֥ד חֲזָקָֽה׃

And I prayed to the L-rd and I said: O L-rd G-d, do not destroy Your people and Your inheritance that You redeemed in Your greatness, that You took out of Egypt with a strong hand.

Remember thy servants, Avraham, Yiżĥaq, and Ya῾aqov; look not to the stubbornness of this people, nor to their wickedness, nor to their sin:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember Your servants Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob. Do not turn to the hardness of this people, and to its wickedness, and to its sin.


###### Deuteronomy 9:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/9:28)


פֶּן־יֹאמְר֗וּ הָאָ֘רֶץ֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר הוֹצֵאתָ֣נוּ מִשָּׁם֒ מִבְּלִי֙ יְכֹ֣לֶת יְהֹוָ֔ה לַהֲבִיאָ֕ם אֶל־הָאָ֖רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֣ר לָהֶ֑ם וּמִשִּׂנְאָת֣וֹ אוֹתָ֔ם הוֹצִיאָ֖ם לַהֲמִתָ֥ם בַּמִּדְבָּֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Lest [the inhabitants] say—[of] the land that you took us out of—‘Because of Adonony’s inability to bring them to the land that He promised for them, and because of His hatred of them, He took them out to kill them in the wilderness.’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest the land from which thou didst bring us out say, Because the Lord was not able to bring them into the land which he promised them, and because he hated them, he has brought them out to slay them in the wilderness.

Lest they [the people of] the land whence you brought us forth say: Because the L-rd was not able to bring them to the land whereof He spoke to them, and in His hatred of them, He took them out to put them to death in the desert —

But they are Your people, Your territory, whom You took out with Your great power and with Your extended arm.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

## Chapter 10

###### Deuteronomy 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:1)


בָּעֵ֨ת הַהִ֜וא אָמַ֧ר יְהֹוָ֣ה אֵלַ֗י פְּסׇל־לְךָ֞ שְׁנֵֽי־לוּחֹ֤ת אֲבָנִים֙ כָּרִ֣אשֹׁנִ֔ים וַעֲלֵ֥ה אֵלַ֖י הָהָ֑רָה וְעָשִׂ֥יתָ לְּךָ֖ אֲר֥וֹן עֵֽץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
At that time, Adonoy said to me, ‘‘Hew for yourself two stone tablets like the originals, and ascend to Me on the mountain; and make for yourself a wooden case.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At that time the Lord said to me, Hew for thyself two tablets of stone like the first, and come up to me into the mountain, and make for thyself an ark of wood.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At that time [(at the end of forty days)] the L-rd said to me: Hew for yourself two tablets of stone like the first and ascend to Me to the mountain and make for yourself an ark of wood [(This was not the ark which Bezalel made, but a different one, which went out with them to war.)]


###### Deuteronomy 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:2)


וְאֶכְתֹּב֙ עַל־הַלֻּחֹ֔ת אֶ֨ת־הַדְּבָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר הָי֛וּ עַל־הַלֻּחֹ֥ת הָרִאשֹׁנִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר שִׁבַּ֑רְתָּ וְשַׂמְתָּ֖ם בָּאָרֽוֹן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And I will write on the tablets the words that were on the original tablets that you smashed, and you will place them in the case.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will write on the tablets the words that were on the first tablets which thou didst break, and thou shalt put them in the ark.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I shall write upon the tablets the words which were on the first tablets, which you broke, and you shall place them in the ark.


###### Deuteronomy 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:3)


וָאַ֤עַשׂ אֲרוֹן֙ עֲצֵ֣י שִׁטִּ֔ים וָאֶפְסֹ֛ל שְׁנֵי־לֻחֹ֥ת אֲבָנִ֖ים כָּרִאשֹׁנִ֑ים וָאַ֣עַל הָהָ֔רָה וּשְׁנֵ֥י הַלֻּחֹ֖ת בְּיָדִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
So I made a case of *shittim* wood, and I hewed two stone tablets like the originals, and I ascended the mountain with the two tablets in my hand.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I made an ark of shittim wood, and hewed two tablets of stone like the first, and went up to the mountain, having the two tablets in my hand.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I made an ark of acacia wood [(before hewing the tablets so that it would be ready to receive them)], and I hewed two tablets of stone like the first, and I ascended the mountain with the two tablets in my hand.

וַיִּכְתֹּ֨ב עַֽל־הַלֻּחֹ֜ת כַּמִּכְתָּ֣ב הָרִאשׁ֗וֹן אֵ֚ת עֲשֶׂ֣רֶת הַדְּבָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֶּר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֲלֵיכֶ֥ם בָּהָ֛ר מִתּ֥וֹךְ הָאֵ֖שׁ בְּי֣וֹם הַקָּהָ֑ל וַיִּתְּנֵ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָֽי׃

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he wrote on the tablets, according to the first writing, the ten Words, which the Lord spoke to you in the mountain out of the midst of the fire in the day of the assembly: and the Lord gave them to me.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He wrote on the tablets like the first writing, the ten commandments which the L-rd spoke to you from the midst of the fire on the day of the assembly, and the L-rd gave them to me.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I turned and I descended the mountain, and I placed the tablets in the ark that I had made, and they were (contained) there, as the L-rd had commanded me.


###### Deuteronomy 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:6)


וּבְנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל נָ֥סְע֛וּ מִבְּאֵרֹ֥ת בְּנֵי־יַעֲקָ֖ן מוֹסֵרָ֑ה שָׁ֣ם מֵ֤ת אַהֲרֹן֙ וַיִּקָּבֵ֣ר שָׁ֔ם וַיְכַהֵ֛ן אֶלְעָזָ֥ר בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Bnei Yisroel traveled from Be’eiros Bnei Ya’akon to Moseiroh. There Aharon died and was buried there; and his son El’ozor served in his stead.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the children of Yisra᾽el took their journey from Be᾽erot-bene-ya῾aqan to Mosera: there Aharon died, and there he was buried; and El῾azar his son ministered in the priest’s office in his place.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the children of Israel journeyed from Be'eroth B'nai Ya'akan to Moserah. [This, too, is part of the reproof, viz.: This, too, did you do. When Aaron died in Hor Hahar at the end of forty days and the clouds of glory departed, you feared war with the king of Arad and you turned to go back to Egypt. You retraced eight journeys until B'nai Ya'akan, and thence to Moserah, where the sons of Levi warred with you (many being killed on both sides), until they forced you to retrace your retreat.] There [in Moserah] Aaron died [i.e., You made great mourning over Aaron, whose death had led to all this, so that it seemed to you as if he had died there,] and he was buried there [see Numbers 21:4]. And Elazar his son was Cohein in his place. [(This reproof is juxtaposed with that of the breaking of the tablets by way of showing that the death of the righteous is as grievous to the Holy One Blessed be He as the day of the breaking of the tablets, and that their saying "Let us make a head, etc." to abandon Him (see Numbers 14:4) is as grievous as the day on which they made the golden calf.)]

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
From thence they journeyed to Gudgoda; and from Gudgoda to Yotvata, a land of streaming wadis.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From there they journeyed [i.e., they returned] to Gudgodah [the same as Chor Hagidgad (Numbers 33:32)], and from Gudgodah to Yatvathah, a land of streams of water.

בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֗וא הִבְדִּ֤יל יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־שֵׁ֣בֶט הַלֵּוִ֔י לָשֵׂ֖את אֶת־אֲר֣וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֑ה לַעֲמֹד֩ לִפְנֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֤ה לְשָֽׁרְתוֹ֙ וּלְבָרֵ֣ךְ בִּשְׁמ֔וֹ עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
At that time Adonoy separated the tribe of Leivi to carry the case of Adonoy’s covenant, to stand before Adonoy to serve Him, and to bless in His name until this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At that time the Lord separated the tribe of Levi, to bear the ark of the covenant of the Lord, to stand before the Lord to minister to him, and to bless in his name, to this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
At that time [(a reversion to the beginning, i.e., In the first year of the exodus from Egypt, when you sinned with the golden calf, and the tribe of Levi did not sin)], the L-rd set apart the tribe of Levi to bear the ark of the covenant of the L-rd [(This is juxtaposed with the retreat to B'nai Ya'akan to show that there, too, the tribe of Levi did not waver but were staunch in their faith)], to stand before the L-rd [(This refers to the Cohanim)] to minister unto Him and to bless His name [(the priestly blessing)] until this day.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, [because they were set apart for the service of the altar and are not free to plow and to sow], Levi did not have a portion and an inheritance with his brothers; the L-rd is his inheritance [i.e., he has a "steady stipend" from the king's house], as the L-rd your G-d spoke to him.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only your fathers did the L-rd desire to love them; and He chose their seed after them — you, [as you see yourselves desired of all peoples], as of this day.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For Adonoy, your God, is God over judges and Lord over lords; the Almighty—the Great, the Powerful, and the Awesome [One]—Who neither exercises favoritism nor accepts bribes.

For the Lord your God is God of gods, and Lord of lords, a great God, a mighty, and a terrible, who favours no person, and takes no bribe:

For the L-rd your G-d — He is the G-d of gods and the L-rd of lords [No lord can rescue you from His hand], the great, mighty, awesome G-d, who will not forgive [if you cast off His yoke] and who will not take graft [i.e., He cannot be conciliated with money].

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Who performs justice for orphan and widow, and loves the convert to give him bread and a garment.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
he executes the judgment of the fatherless and widow, and loves the stranger, giving him food and raiment.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He does the judgment of orphan and widow ["Where you find His might, you find His humility"], and He loves the stranger, to give him bread and clothing, [the very essence of Jacob's supplication to the L-rd (Genesis 28:20)].

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to love the convert, because you were strangers in the land of Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Love therefore the stranger: for you were strangers in the land of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall love the stranger, for you were strangers in the land of Egypt [Do not ascribe your own blemish to others.]

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to fear Adonoy, your God, serve Him, cling to Him, and swear by His name.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He is your praise and He is your God, Who performed with you these great and fearsome things that your eyes witnessed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He is your glory and He is your G-d, who did with you these great and awesome things which your eyes have seen.


###### Deuteronomy 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/10:22)


בְּשִׁבְעִ֣ים נֶ֔פֶשׁ יָרְד֥וּ אֲבֹתֶ֖יךָ מִצְרָ֑יְמָה וְעַתָּ֗ה שָֽׂמְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ כְּכוֹכְבֵ֥י הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם לָרֹֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Numbering seventy people, your forefathers went down to Egypt; and now Adonoy, your God, has placed you like the stars of the sky, numerous.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy fathers went down to Miżrayim with seventy persons; and now the Lord thy God has made thee as the stars of heaven for multitude.
