**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With seventy souls did your fathers go down to Egypt, and now, the L-rd your G-d has made you as the stars of heaven in multitude.



## Chapter 11

###### Deuteronomy 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:1)


וְאָ֣הַבְתָּ֔ אֵ֖ת יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וְשָׁמַרְתָּ֣ מִשְׁמַרְתּ֗וֹ וְחֻקֹּתָ֧יו וּמִשְׁפָּטָ֛יו וּמִצְוֺתָ֖יו כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to love Adonoy, your God, and you are to guard His watch, and His statutes, and His laws, and His commandments, all the years.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore thou shalt love the Lord thy God, and keep his charge, and his statutes, and his judgments, and his commandments, always.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall love the L-rd your G-d, and you shall keep His keeping and His statutes and His judgments and His mitzvoth all of the days.


###### Deuteronomy 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:2)


וִֽידַעְתֶּם֮ הַיּוֹם֒ כִּ֣י**׀** לֹ֣א אֶת־בְּנֵיכֶ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־יָדְעוּ֙ וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־רָא֔וּ אֶת־מוּסַ֖ר יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם אֶת־גׇּדְל֕וֹ אֶת־יָדוֹ֙ הַחֲזָקָ֔ה וּזְרֹע֖וֹ הַנְּטוּיָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will know today that not with your children who have not known and who have not seen the teaching of Adonoy, your God—His greatness, His powerful hand and His extended arm,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And know this day: for I speak not with your children who have not known, and have not seen the chastisement of the Lord your God, his greatness, his mighty hand, and his stretched-out arm,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall know this day [Give heart to know and to understand and to accept my reproof], for it is not [with] your children [that I speak now], who did not know and who did not see the chastisement of the L-rd your G-d, His greatness, His strong hand, and His outstretched arm,


###### Deuteronomy 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:3)


וְאֶת־אֹֽתֹתָיו֙ וְאֶֽת־מַעֲשָׂ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֖ה בְּת֣וֹךְ מִצְרָ֑יִם לְפַרְעֹ֥ה מֶֽלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֖יִם וּלְכׇל־אַרְצֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
and His signs and His deeds that He performed within Egypt, to Pharaoh king of Egypt, and to his entire land;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and his miracles, and his acts, which he did in the midst of Miżrayim to Par῾o the king of Miżrayim, and to all his land;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And His signs and His deeds which He wrought in the midst of Egypt to Pharaoh king of Egypt and to all his land,


###### Deuteronomy 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:4)


וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂה֩ לְחֵ֨יל מִצְרַ֜יִם לְסוּסָ֣יו וּלְרִכְבּ֗וֹ אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֵצִ֜יף אֶת־מֵ֤י יַם־סוּף֙ עַל־פְּנֵיהֶ֔ם בְּרׇדְפָ֖ם אַחֲרֵיכֶ֑ם וַיְאַבְּדֵ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And what He did to the Egyptian army, to its horses and to its chariots—that He flooded the waters of Yam Suf over them when they were chasing you—Adonoy destroyed them until this day;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and what he did to the army of Miżrayim, to their horses, and to their chariots; how he made the water of the Sea of Suf overflow them as they pursued after you, and how the Lord destroyed them unto this day;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what He wrought to the host of Egypt, to his horses and to his chariots, flooding the waters of the Red Sea over their faces when they pursued you (and the L-rd caused them to go lost until this day),


###### Deuteronomy 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:5)


וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשָׂ֛ה לָכֶ֖ם בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר עַד־בֹּאֲכֶ֖ם עַד־הַמָּק֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And what He did for you in the wilderness, until you arrived at this place;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and what he did to you in the wilderness, until you came to this place;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what He wrought for you in the desert until you came to this place,


###### Deuteronomy 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:6)


וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֜ה לְדָתָ֣ן וְלַאֲבִירָ֗ם בְּנֵ֣י אֱלִיאָב֮ בֶּן־רְאוּבֵן֒ אֲשֶׁ֨ר פָּצְתָ֤ה הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ אֶת־פִּ֔יהָ וַתִּבְלָעֵ֥ם וְאֶת־בָּתֵּיהֶ֖ם וְאֶת־אׇהֳלֵיהֶ֑ם וְאֵ֤ת כׇּל־הַיְקוּם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּרַגְלֵיהֶ֔ם בְּקֶ֖רֶב כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And what He did to Dosson and to Avirom sons of Eli’ov son of Re’uven, that the earth opened its mouth and swallowed them and their families and their tents and all the sustenance at their feet amid all of Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and what he did to Datan and Aviram, the sons of Eli᾽av, the son of Re᾽uven: how the earth opened its mouth, and swallowed them up, and their households, and their tents, and all the living substance that followed them, in the midst of all Yisra᾽el:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And what He wrought against Dathan and Aviram the sons of Eliav the son of Reuven, the earth opening its mouth and swallowing them and their houses, and their tents, and all of the wealth at their feet in the midst of all of Israel [Wherever one of them would flee, the earth would split beneath him and swallow him] —


###### Deuteronomy 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:7)


כִּ֤י עֵֽינֵיכֶם֙ הָֽרֹאֹ֔ת אֶת־כׇּל־מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה הַגָּדֹ֑ל אֲשֶׁ֖ר עָשָֽׂה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather *your* eyes have been witnessing the entire overwhelming activity of Adonoy that He has performed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but your eyes have seen all the great acts of the Lord which he did.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
("It is not with your children, who did not know, etc." (2), with whom I speak now,) but [with you,] whose eyes have seen the entire great deed of the L-rd, that He has wrought.


###### Deuteronomy 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:8)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּם֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הַמִּצְוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֛ר אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּ֑וֹם לְמַ֣עַן תֶּחֶזְק֗וּ וּבָאתֶם֙ וִֽירִשְׁתֶּ֣ם אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתֶּ֛ם עֹבְרִ֥ים שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will guard the entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today, in order that you be encouraged and will arrive in, and inherit the land that you are crossing over there to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore shall you keep all the commandments which I command you this day, that you may be strong, and go in and possess the land, into which you go to possess it;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall keep the entire mitzvah that I command you this day, so that you be strengthened and come and inherit the land whither you are crossing to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:9)


וּלְמַ֨עַן תַּאֲרִ֤יכוּ יָמִים֙ עַל־הָ֣אֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר֩ נִשְׁבַּ֨ע יְהֹוָ֧ה לַאֲבֹתֵיכֶ֛ם לָתֵ֥ת לָהֶ֖ם וּלְזַרְעָ֑ם אֶ֛רֶץ זָבַ֥ת חָלָ֖ב וּדְבָֽשׁ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And in order that you will last long on the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers to give to them and to their descendants, a land flowing milk and honey.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that you may prolong your days in the land, which the Lord swore to your fathers to give to them and to their seed, a land flowing with milk and honey.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And so that you prolong days on the land that the L-rd swore to your fathers to give to them and to their seed, a land flowing milk and honey.


###### Deuteronomy 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:10)


כִּ֣י הָאָ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אַתָּ֤ה בָא־שָׁ֙מָּה֙ לְרִשְׁתָּ֔הּ לֹ֣א כְאֶ֤רֶץ מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ הִ֔וא אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְצָאתֶ֖ם מִשָּׁ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֤ר תִּזְרַע֙ אֶֽת־זַרְעֲךָ֔ וְהִשְׁקִ֥יתָ בְרַגְלְךָ֖ כְּגַ֥ן הַיָּרָֽק׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For the land where you are arriving to inherit is not like the land of Egypt from which you departed, where you planted your seed and watered [it] on foot like a vegetable garden.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the land, into which thou goest to possess it, is not as the land of Miżrayim, from whence you came out, where thou didst sow thy seed, and didst water it with thy foot, like a garden of vegetables:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For [(as they were assured when they left Egypt)] the land whither you are coming to inherit it is not like the land of Egypt [Ramses, the very best of the land of Egypt], from which you went forth, [but it is superior even to Ramses], where you sowed your seed and watered it with your foot [i.e., You had to go by foot to bring water from the Nile to water your land], as [it is necessary to do with] a garden of herbs, [rain alone not sufficing for it],


###### Deuteronomy 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:11)


וְהָאָ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אַתֶּ֜ם עֹבְרִ֥ים שָׁ֙מָּה֙ לְרִשְׁתָּ֔הּ אֶ֥רֶץ הָרִ֖ים וּבְקָעֹ֑ת לִמְטַ֥ר הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם תִּשְׁתֶּה־מָּֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather, the land where you are crossing to inherit is a land of mountains and plains—by the rain of the skies will you drink water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but the land, into which you go to possess it, is a land of hills and valleys, and drinks water of the rain of heaven:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the land whither you are crossing to inherit it is a land of mountains and plains, [(yet you may sleep soundly there. For unlike Egypt, where you had to carry water from the low places to the high, in Eretz Yisrael the Holy One Blessed be He waters everything, high and low, seen and unseen, alike)] — From the rain of the heavens you will drink water.


###### Deuteronomy 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:12)


אֶ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ דֹּרֵ֣שׁ אֹתָ֑הּ תָּמִ֗יד עֵינֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ בָּ֔הּ מֵֽרֵשִׁית֙ הַשָּׁנָ֔ה וְעַ֖ד אַחֲרִ֥ית שָׁנָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A land that Adonoy, your God, looks after; the eyes of Adonoy, your God, are always upon it, from the year’s beginning until the year’s end.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a land which the Lord thy God cares for: the eyes of the Lord thy God are always upon it, from the beginning of the year to the end of the year.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A land which the L-rd your G-d inquires after, [(the allotment for other lands depending upon this "inquiry")]. Always, the eyes of the L-rd your G-d are in it [for the ascertainment of its needs and for the making of decrees (sometimes for the good, sometimes otherwise)], from the beginning of the year until the end of the year. [On Rosh Hashanah the land is judged for what is to transpire in it until the end of the year.]


###### Deuteronomy 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:13)


וְהָיָ֗ה אִם־שָׁמֹ֤עַ תִּשְׁמְעוּ֙ אֶל־מִצְוֺתַ֔י אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י מְצַוֶּ֥ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם הַיּ֑וֹם לְאַהֲבָ֞ה אֶת־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֙ וּלְעׇבְד֔וֹ בְּכׇל־לְבַבְכֶ֖ם וּבְכׇל־נַפְשְׁכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Should you thoroughly heed My commandments that I am commanding you today—to love Adonoy, your God, and to serve Him with all your desires and with your entire beings,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall come to pass, if you hearken diligently to my commandments which I command you this day, to love the Lord your God, and to serve him with all your heart and with all your soul,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be [i.e., "From the rain of the heavens you will drink water" (11)] if you hearken, hearken [(i.e., If you hearken to the old, you will "hear" the new)] to My mitzvoth which I command you this day [(They should be "new" to you, as if you had heard them "this day")], to love the L-rd your G-d [(Do not study in order to be rich or honored or rewarded; but do all out of love, and the rest is bound to come)], and to serve Him with all your [(plural)] heart ["service of the heart," i.e., prayer], and with all your [(plural)] soul [(an exhortation to the community; verse 6:5 is an exhortation to the individual)],


###### Deuteronomy 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:14)


וְנָתַתִּ֧י מְטַֽר־אַרְצְכֶ֛ם בְּעִתּ֖וֹ יוֹרֶ֣ה וּמַלְק֑וֹשׁ וְאָסַפְתָּ֣ דְגָנֶ֔ךָ וְתִירֹֽשְׁךָ֖ וְיִצְהָרֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I will provide the rain of your land in its time, fall rain and spring rain, and you will harvest your grain and your wine and your olive oil.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that I will give you the rain of your land in its due season, the early rain and the late rain, that thou mayst gather in thy corn, and thy wine, and thy oil.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then I shall give the rain of your land [(You, doing what is yours, I shall do what is Mine)] in its (proper) time [(at night, so as not to incommode you)], the yoreh, [the rain which falls after sowing, which "saturates" (marveh) the ground], and the malkosh, [the "latter" rain, which falls before the harvest to ripen the grain], and you [and not your foes] will gather in your corn, and your wine and your oil.


###### Deuteronomy 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:15)


וְנָתַתִּ֛י עֵ֥שֶׂב בְּשָׂדְךָ֖ לִבְהֶמְתֶּ֑ךָ וְאָכַלְתָּ֖ וְשָׂבָֽעְתָּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And I will provide grass in your field for your animals, and you will eat and be full.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I will send grass in thy fields for thy cattle, that thou mayst eat and be full.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I shall give grass in your field to your beasts [(You will not need to lead them to other fields)]; and you will eat and you will be sated [(Your bread will be "blessed" in your stomach.)]


###### Deuteronomy 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:16)


הִשָּֽׁמְר֣וּ לָכֶ֔ם פֶּ֥ן יִפְתֶּ֖ה לְבַבְכֶ֑ם וְסַרְתֶּ֗ם וַעֲבַדְתֶּם֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוִיתֶ֖ם לָהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look out for yourselves lest your heart be misled and you turn away and serve other gods and bow to them,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take heed to yourselves, that your heart be not deceived, and you turn aside, and serve other gods, and worship them;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourselves lest [eating and being sated] your hearts be enticed, [one rebelling against the L-rd only out of satiety (see 8:12-14)], and you go astray, [separating yourselves from the Torah], and [as a result] you [come to] serve other gods, ["others," i.e., "strangers," to those who serve them — being cried out to and not answering], and you bow down to them.


###### Deuteronomy 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:17)


וְחָרָ֨ה אַף־יְהֹוָ֜ה בָּכֶ֗ם וְעָצַ֤ר אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ וְלֹֽא־יִהְיֶ֣ה מָטָ֔ר וְהָ֣אֲדָמָ֔ה לֹ֥א תִתֵּ֖ן אֶת־יְבוּלָ֑הּ וַאֲבַדְתֶּ֣ם מְהֵרָ֗ה מֵעַל֙ הָאָ֣רֶץ הַטֹּבָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה נֹתֵ֥ן לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy’s anger will be aroused against you, and He will restrain the skies and there will be no rain, and the soil will not yield its produce, and you will swiftly be removed from upon the good land that Adonoy is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and then the Lord’s anger be inflamed against you, and he shut up the heavens, that there be no rain, and that the land yield not its fruit; and you perish quickly from off the good land which the Lord gives you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the wrath of the L-rd will burn against you, and He will hold back the heavens and there will not be rain, and the ground will not yield [even] yevulah [what you bring (movil) to it], and [in addition to all of the other afflictions], you will go lost quickly [into exile] from the good land that the L-rd gives you, [the fatness of the land having caused you to sin. ("quickly"): unlike the generation of the flood, which was given a grace period; for you had from whom to learn, whereas they did not.]


###### Deuteronomy 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:18)


וְשַׂמְתֶּם֙ אֶת־דְּבָרַ֣י אֵ֔לֶּה עַל־לְבַבְכֶ֖ם וְעַֽל־נַפְשְׁכֶ֑ם וּקְשַׁרְתֶּ֨ם אֹתָ֤ם לְאוֹת֙ עַל־יֶדְכֶ֔ם וְהָי֥וּ לְטוֹטָפֹ֖ת בֵּ֥ין עֵינֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to place these words of Mine on your mind and on your being, and bind them as a sign on your hand and they will be *totafos* on your head;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall lay up these my words in your heart and in your soul, and bind them for a sign upon your hand, and they will be as frontlets between your eyes.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall place these, My words, upon your heart and upon your souls [(Even after you are exiled, be distinctive with mitzvoth: Put on tefillin, affix mezuzoth, so that they not be new to you when you return)]. And you shall bind them as a sign upon your hand and they shall be frontlets between your eyes.


###### Deuteronomy 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:19)


וְלִמַּדְתֶּ֥ם אֹתָ֛ם אֶת־בְּנֵיכֶ֖ם לְדַבֵּ֣ר בָּ֑ם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ֤ בְּבֵיתֶ֙ךָ֙ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ֣ בַדֶּ֔רֶךְ וּֽבְשׇׁכְבְּךָ֖ וּבְקוּמֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And you are to teach them to your sons to speak in them when you sit at home, and when you journey on the road, and when you go to sleep, and when you rise;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall teach them your children, speaking of them when thou dost sit in thy house, and when thou dost walk by the way, when thou liest down, and when thou risest up.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall teach them to your sons to speak through them [(As soon as your son is ready to speak, teach him (33:4): "Torah did Moses command us" to initiate him into speaking)], in your sitting in your house, and in your walking upon the way, and in your lying down, and in your arising.


###### Deuteronomy 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:20)


וּכְתַבְתָּ֛ם עַל־מְזוּז֥וֹת בֵּיתֶ֖ךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And you are to write them on the doorposts of your home and your gateways.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt write them upon the door posts of thy house, and upon thy gates:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall write them on the doorposts of your house and upon your gates.


###### Deuteronomy 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:21)


לְמַ֨עַן יִרְבּ֤וּ יְמֵיכֶם֙ וִימֵ֣י בְנֵיכֶ֔ם עַ֚ל הָֽאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִשְׁבַּ֧ע יְהֹוָ֛ה לַאֲבֹתֵיכֶ֖ם לָתֵ֣ת לָהֶ֑ם כִּימֵ֥י הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ¹׃{ס}

footnotes:
¹ (בספרי תימן הָאָֽרֶץ בצד״י גדולה)


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In order that they will be many—your years and the years of your children on the soil that Adonoy swore to your forefathers to give them, like the years of heaven on earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that your days may be multiplied, and the days of your children, in the land which the Lord swore to your fathers to give them, as the days of heaven upon the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that your days be prolonged and the days of your children [(But if not, they will not be prolonged)], on the land that the L-rd swore to your fathers to give to them [(a Scriptural allusion to the resurrection)], as the days of heaven upon the earth.


###### Deuteronomy 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:22)


כִּי֩ אִם־שָׁמֹ֨ר תִּשְׁמְר֜וּן אֶת־כׇּל־הַמִּצְוָ֣ה הַזֹּ֗את אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י מְצַוֶּ֥ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם לַעֲשֹׂתָ֑הּ לְאַהֲבָ֞ה אֶת־יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֛ם לָלֶ֥כֶת בְּכׇל־דְּרָכָ֖יו וּלְדׇבְקָה־בֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For if you truly guard this entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you to perform, to love Adonoy, your God, to walk in all His ways and to cling to Him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For if you shall diligently keep all these commandments which I command you, to do them, to love the Lord your God, to walk in all his ways, and to hold fast to him;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For if you keep, keep, all of this mitzvah that I command you to do [(an exhortation to many "keepings," that one take care not to forget his learning)], to love the L-rd your G-d, to walk in all His ways [i.e., As He is merciful, so you be merciful, etc.], and to cleave to Him [(Cleaving to Torah scholars is accounted cleaving to the L-rd)],


###### Deuteronomy 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:23)


וְהוֹרִ֧ישׁ יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֥ם הָאֵ֖לֶּה מִלִּפְנֵיכֶ֑ם וִֽירִשְׁתֶּ֣ם גּוֹיִ֔ם גְּדֹלִ֥ים וַעֲצֻמִ֖ים מִכֶּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will expel all these nations before you, and you will inherit nations greater and more powerful than you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then will the Lord drive out all these nations from before you, and you shall possess greater nations and mightier than yourselves.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the L-rd will drive out all these nations from before you [(You, doing what is yours, I shall do what is Mine)], and you will inherit nations greater and mightier than you [(You are mighty, but they are mightier than you)].


###### Deuteronomy 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:24)


כׇּל־הַמָּק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֨ר תִּדְרֹ֧ךְ כַּֽף־רַגְלְכֶ֛ם בּ֖וֹ לָכֶ֣ם יִהְיֶ֑ה מִן־הַמִּדְבָּ֨ר וְהַלְּבָנ֜וֹן מִן־הַנָּהָ֣ר נְהַר־פְּרָ֗ת וְעַד֙ הַיָּ֣ם הָאַֽחֲר֔וֹן יִהְיֶ֖ה גְּבֻלְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every place that the sole of your foot steps on will be yours, from the wilderness and the Lebanon, form the river—the Euphrates River—up to the ultimate sea, will be your boundary.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every place whereon the sole of your foot shall tread shall be yours: from the wilderness to the Levanon, from the river, the river Perat, to the uttermost sea shall be your border.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every place where the soles of your feet tread shall be yours. From the desert and the Levanon, from the river, the river Perath, until the western sea shall be your border.


###### Deuteronomy 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:25)


לֹא־יִתְיַצֵּ֥ב אִ֖ישׁ בִּפְנֵיכֶ֑ם פַּחְדְּכֶ֨ם וּמוֹרַֽאֲכֶ֜ם יִתֵּ֣ן**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֗ם עַל־פְּנֵ֤י כׇל־הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּדְרְכוּ־בָ֔הּ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּ֥ר לָכֶֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
No man will stand up to you; fear of you and awe of you will Adonoy, your God, place upon the surface of the entire land that you are stepping into, as He promised you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There shall no man be able to stand against you: for the Lord your God shall lay the fear of you and the dread of you upon all the land that you shall tread upon, as he has spoken to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
No man [— even one like Og, the king of Bashan —] will stand up against you. Fear of you [(on the part of those near you)] and dread of you [(on the part of those distant)] shall the L-rd G-d place on the face of all the land where you will tread, as He spoke to you [viz. Exodus 23:27].


###### Deuteronomy 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:26)


רְאֵ֗ה אָנֹכִ֛י נֹתֵ֥ן לִפְנֵיכֶ֖ם הַיּ֑וֹם בְּרָכָ֖ה וּקְלָלָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look, I place before you today, a blessing and a curse.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Behold, I set before you this day a blessing and a curse;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Behold, I set before you this day blessing and curse, [those (to be) pronounced in Mount Gerizim and Mount Eival].


###### Deuteronomy 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:27)


אֶֽת־הַבְּרָכָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּשְׁמְע֗וּ אֶל־מִצְוֺת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י מְצַוֶּ֥ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The blessing—that you heed the commandments of Adonoy, your God, that I am commanding you today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
a blessing, if you obey the commandments of the Lord your God, which I command you this day:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The blessing, so that you hearken to the mitzvoth of the L-rd your G-d, which I command you this day.


###### Deuteronomy 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:28)


וְהַקְּלָלָ֗ה אִם־לֹ֤א תִשְׁמְעוּ֙ אֶל־מִצְוֺת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם וְסַרְתֶּ֣ם מִן־הַדֶּ֔רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י מְצַוֶּ֥ה אֶתְכֶ֖ם הַיּ֑וֹם לָלֶ֗כֶת אַחֲרֵ֛י אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יְדַעְתֶּֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the curse—if you do not heed the commandments of Adonoy, your God, and you veer from the course which I command you this day, to follow other gods which you did not know.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and a curse, if you do not obey the commandments of the Lord your God, but turn aside out of the way which I command you this day, to go after other gods, which you have not known.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the curse, if you do not hearken to the mitzvoth of the L-rd your G-d and you turn away from the way that I command you this day, to go after other gods, which you did not know, [whence it is derived that serving idolatry is tantamount to denying the entire Torah ("the way")].


###### Deuteronomy 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:29)


וְהָיָ֗ה כִּ֤י יְבִֽיאֲךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ אֶל־הָאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה בָא־שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּ֑הּ וְנָתַתָּ֤ה אֶת־הַבְּרָכָה֙ עַל־הַ֣ר גְּרִזִ֔ים וְאֶת־הַקְּלָלָ֖ה עַל־הַ֥ר עֵיבָֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy, your God, has brought you to the land that you are coming to inherit, you shall place the blessers on Mount Gerizim and the cursers on Mount Eival.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall come to pass, when the Lord thy God has brought thee in to the land into which thou dost go to possess it, that thou shalt put the blessing upon mount Gerizzim, and the curse upon mount ῾Eval.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when the L-rd your G-d brings you to the land whither you come to inherit it, that you shall set the blessers towards Mount Gerizim and the cursers towards Mount Eival. [The blessers face Mount Gerizim and recite: "Blessed is the man who does not make a graven or a molten image," and then they turn to Mount Eival and recite the corresponding curse, viz. (27:15): "Cursed is the man who makes a graven or a molten image," etc.]


###### Deuteronomy 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:30)


הֲלֹא־הֵ֜מָּה בְּעֵ֣בֶר הַיַּרְדֵּ֗ן אַֽחֲרֵי֙ דֶּ֚רֶךְ מְב֣וֹא הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ בְּאֶ֙רֶץ֙ הַֽכְּנַעֲנִ֔י הַיֹּשֵׁ֖ב בָּעֲרָבָ֑ה מ֚וּל הַגִּלְגָּ֔ל אֵ֖צֶל אֵלוֹנֵ֥י מֹרֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Are they not across the Yardein and far beyond, along the route of the sunset? In the land of the Canaanites who dwell on the Arava Plain, opposite Gilgol, next to the Moreh Plain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Are they not on the other side of the Yarden, by the way where the sun goes down, in the land of the Kena῾ani, who dwell in the ῾Arava over against Gilgal, beside the terebinths of More?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Are they [the mountains] not beyond the passage of the Jordan towards the setting of the sun [the west], in the land of the Canaani, who dwells in the plain, distant from Gilgal, near Eilonei Moreh [Shchem]?


###### Deuteronomy 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:31)


כִּ֤י אַתֶּם֙ עֹבְרִ֣ים אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן לָבֹא֙ לָרֶ֣שֶׁת אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם נֹתֵ֣ן לָכֶ֑ם וִֽירִשְׁתֶּ֥ם אֹתָ֖הּ וִֽישַׁבְתֶּם־בָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For you shall pass across the Yardein to come to inherit the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; you will inherit it and will dwell in it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For you shall pass over the Yarden to go in to possess the land which the Lord your God gives you, and you shall possess it, and dwell in it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you cross the Jordan to come to inherit the land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you, [the miracles that you experience then will be a sign to you that] you will inherit it and you will dwell in it.


###### Deuteronomy 11:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/11:32)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֣ם לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הַֽחֻקִּ֖ים וְאֶת־הַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֑ים אֲשֶׁ֧ר אָנֹכִ֛י נֹתֵ֥ן לִפְנֵיכֶ֖ם הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will guard to fulfill all the statutes and the laws that I am setting before you today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall observe to do all the statutes and judgments which I set before you this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall observe to do all of the statutes and the judgments that I set before you this day.



## Chapter 12

###### Deuteronomy 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:1)


אֵ֠לֶּה הַֽחֻקִּ֣ים וְהַמִּשְׁפָּטִים֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּשְׁמְר֣וּן לַעֲשׂוֹת֒ בָּאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר֩ נָתַ֨ן יְהֹוָ֜ה אֱלֹהֵ֧י אֲבֹתֶ֛יךָ לְךָ֖ לְרִשְׁתָּ֑הּ כׇּ֨ל־הַיָּמִ֔ים אֲשֶׁר־אַתֶּ֥ם חַיִּ֖ים עַל־הָאֲדָמָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the statutes and the laws that you will guard to fulfill in the land that Adonoy, God of your forefathers, has given to inherit, all the years that you are alive on the land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are the statutes and judgments, which you shall observe to do in the land, which the Lord God of thy fathers gives thee to possess it, all the days that you live upon the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the statutes and the judgments which you shall observe to do in the land which the L-rd, the G-d of your fathers, gave to you to inherit, all the days that you live upon the earth.


###### Deuteronomy 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:2)


אַבֵּ֣ד תְּ֠אַבְּד֠וּן אֶֽת־כׇּל־הַמְּקֹמ֞וֹת אֲשֶׁ֧ר עָֽבְדוּ־שָׁ֣ם הַגּוֹיִ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתֶּ֛ם יֹרְשִׁ֥ים אֹתָ֖ם אֶת־אֱלֹהֵיהֶ֑ם עַל־הֶהָרִ֤ים הָֽרָמִים֙ וְעַל־הַגְּבָע֔וֹת וְתַ֖חַת כׇּל־עֵ֥ץ רַעֲנָֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must obliterate all the places where the nations worshipped, whom you are inheriting, their gods, on the high mountains, and on the hills, and under every evergreen tree.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall utterly destroy all the places, in which the nations whom you are to dispossess, served their gods, upon the high mountains, and upon the hills, and under every leafy tree:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Destroy shall you destroy [(whence it is derived that in destroying idolatry one must uproot it entirely)], from all of the places where the nations which you are inheriting worshipped, their gods [which are] on the high mountains and on the hills and under every leafy tree.


###### Deuteronomy 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:3)


וְנִתַּצְתֶּ֣ם אֶת־מִזְבְּחֹתָ֗ם וְשִׁבַּרְתֶּם֙ אֶת־מַצֵּ֣בֹתָ֔ם וַאֲשֵֽׁרֵיהֶם֙ תִּשְׂרְפ֣וּן בָּאֵ֔שׁ וּפְסִילֵ֥י אֱלֹֽהֵיהֶ֖ם תְּגַדֵּע֑וּן וְאִבַּדְתֶּ֣ם אֶת־שְׁמָ֔ם מִן־הַמָּק֖וֹם הַהֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to break apart their altars, and you are to smash their pillars, and their asherahs are you to burn in fire, and the statues of their gods are you to cut down; and you are to obliterate their name from that place.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and you shall overthrow their altars, and break their pillars, and burn their asherim with fire; and you shall hew down the carvings of their gods, and destroy the name of them out of that place.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall raze their altars [of many stones] and you shall break their monuments [of one stone], and their asheiroth [trees that they worship] shall you burn in fire, and the graven images of their gods shall you cut down, and you shall cause their name to go lost from that place [by giving it a new, demeaning name].


###### Deuteronomy 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:4)


לֹֽא־תַעֲשׂ֣וּן כֵּ֔ן לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not act this way to Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This you shall not do to the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not do thus [to offer sacrifices in all places] to the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:5)


כִּ֠י אִֽם־אֶל־הַמָּק֞וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֙ מִכׇּל־שִׁבְטֵיכֶ֔ם לָשׂ֥וּם אֶת־שְׁמ֖וֹ שָׁ֑ם לְשִׁכְנ֥וֹ תִדְרְשׁ֖וּ וּבָ֥אתָ שָּֽׁמָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather at the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses from all your tribes to set His Presence there, will you seek His Presence and come there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But to the place which the Lord your God shall choose out of all your tribes to put his name there, there shall you seek him, at his dwelling, and there shalt thou come:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But to the place that the L-rd your G-d will choose from all your tribes to place His name there; His dwelling [the sanctuary of Shiloh] shall you inquire after, and you shall come there.


###### Deuteronomy 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:6)


וַהֲבֵאתֶ֣ם שָׁ֗מָּה עֹלֹֽתֵיכֶם֙ וְזִבְחֵיכֶ֔ם וְאֵת֙ מַעְשְׂרֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם וְאֵ֖ת תְּרוּמַ֣ת יֶדְכֶ֑ם וְנִדְרֵיכֶם֙ וְנִדְבֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם וּבְכֹרֹ֥ת בְּקַרְכֶ֖ם וְצֹאנְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to bring there your burnt-offerings and your sacred-offerings, and your tithes and the elevation of your hands, and your pledges, and your donations, and the firstborn of your cattle and your flocks.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and there you shall bring your burnt offerings, and your sacrifices, and your tithes, and the offerings of your hand, and your vows, and your freewill offerings, and the firstlings of your herds and your flocks:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall bring there your burnt-offerings, and your sacrifices [obligatory peace-offerings] and your tithes [the beast-tithe and ma'aser sheni], and the offering of your hands [first-fruits], and your vows and your gifts, and the firstborn of your herds and your flocks [to give them to the Cohein to be sacrificed there].


###### Deuteronomy 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:7)


וַאֲכַלְתֶּם־שָׁ֗ם לִפְנֵי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם וּשְׂמַחְתֶּ֗ם בְּכֹל֙ מִשְׁלַ֣ח יֶדְכֶ֔ם אַתֶּ֖ם וּבָתֵּיכֶ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר בֵּֽרַכְךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to eat there before Adonoy, your God, and you will be happy with the sum of your handiwork, you and your households, as Adonoy, your God, has blessed you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and there you shall eat before the Lord your God, and you shall rejoice in all that to which you put your hand, you and your households, wherein the Lord thy God has blessed thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall eat there before the L-rd your G-d, and you shall rejoice in all to which you put your hand; you and your households. [Bring offerings in accordance with the blessing] wherewith the L-rd your G-d has blessed you.


###### Deuteronomy 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:8)


לֹ֣א תַעֲשׂ֔וּן כְּ֠כֹ֠ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲנַ֧חְנוּ עֹשִׂ֛ים פֹּ֖ה הַיּ֑וֹם אִ֖ישׁ כׇּל־הַיָּשָׁ֥ר בְּעֵינָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You may not do everything we do here today, each what is upright in his eyes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not do after all the things that we do here this day, every man whatever is right in his own eyes.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[(11:31): "When you cross the Jordan, etc." and you are permitted to sacrifice on bamoth (temporary altars) all the fourteen years of conquest and apportionment of the land], you shall not do [i.e., you shall not sacrifice upon the bamoth] according to all that we do here today [in the tabernacle (mishkan) that is with you and which is anointed and fit for sin-offerings, guilt-offerings, and vow and gift-offerings, but] a man [may offer on the bamoth, only] all that is fitting in his eyes [i.e., vow and gift-offerings].


###### Deuteronomy 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:9)


כִּ֥י לֹא־בָאתֶ֖ם עַד־עָ֑תָּה אֶל־הַמְּנוּחָה֙ וְאֶל־הַֽנַּחֲלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For you will not have come as yet to the peace and to the territory that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For you are not as yet come to the rest and to the inheritance, which the Lord your God gives you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For you will not yet have come [all those fourteen years] to the rest [Shiloh] and to the inheritance [Jerusalem] that the L-rd your G-d gives you.


###### Deuteronomy 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:10)


וַעֲבַרְתֶּם֮ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּן֒ וִֽישַׁבְתֶּ֣ם בָּאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֖ם מַנְחִ֣יל אֶתְכֶ֑ם וְהֵנִ֨יחַ לָכֶ֧ם מִכׇּל־אֹיְבֵיכֶ֛ם מִסָּבִ֖יב וִֽישַׁבְתֶּם־בֶּֽטַח׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you cross the Yardein and settle in the land that Adonoy, your God, is apportioning to you, and He has granted you peace from all your enemies around, and you will live secure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But when you traverse the Yarden, and dwell in the land which the Lord your God gives you to inherit, and when he gives you rest from all your enemies round about, so that you dwell in safety;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will cross the Jordan, and you will dwell in the land that the L-rd your G-d causes you to inherit. [After you apportion it and each one knows his portion and (that of) his tribe], and He gives you rest from all of your foes roundabout [which He left there to test you], and you dwell secure [(This did not transpire until the days of David)] —


###### Deuteronomy 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:11)


וְהָיָ֣ה הַמָּק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֥ם בּוֹ֙ לְשַׁכֵּ֤ן שְׁמוֹ֙ שָׁ֔ם שָׁ֣מָּה תָבִ֔יאוּ אֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י מְצַוֶּ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֑ם עוֹלֹתֵיכֶ֣ם וְזִבְחֵיכֶ֗ם מַעְשְׂרֹֽתֵיכֶם֙ וּתְרֻמַ֣ת יֶדְכֶ֔ם וְכֹל֙ מִבְחַ֣ר נִדְרֵיכֶ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּדְּר֖וּ לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Let it be that the place where Adonoy, your God, chooses to place His Presence therein—it is there you shall bring all that I am commanding you: your burnt-offerings and your sacred-offerings, your tithes and your hand-offerings, and your choice promissory offerings that you pledge to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then there shall be a place which the Lord your God shall choose to cause his name to dwell there; there shall you bring all that I command you; your burnt offerings, and your sacrifices, your tithes, and the offering of your hand, and all your choice vows which you vow to the Lord:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[Only then] the place the L-rd your G-d shall choose to repose His name therein — [(build the Temple in Jerusalem. All of this occurred as stated (see II Samuel 7:1-1)] — there [in Jerusalem (After the destruction of Shiloh, bamoth were permitted at intervals until they came to Jerusalem)] shall you bring all that I command you: your burnt-offerings, and your sacrifices, your tithes and the offering of your hands, and all your choice vows [(One should bring only of the choicest)] that you vow to the L-rd.


###### Deuteronomy 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:12)


וּשְׂמַחְתֶּ֗ם לִפְנֵי֮ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֒ אַתֶּ֗ם וּבְנֵיכֶם֙ וּבְנֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם וְעַבְדֵיכֶ֖ם וְאַמְהֹתֵיכֶ֑ם וְהַלֵּוִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּשַֽׁעֲרֵיכֶ֔ם כִּ֣י אֵ֥ין ל֛וֹ חֵ֥לֶק וְנַחֲלָ֖ה אִתְּכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to rejoice in the presence of Adonoy, your God, you and your sons and your daughters, your male slaves and your female slaves, and the Levite who is in your cities, for he has no portion or territory with you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and you shall rejoice before the Lord your God, you and your sons, and your daughters, and your menservants, and your maidservants, and the Levite who is within your gates; for he has no part or inheritance with you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall rejoice before the L-rd your G-d: you, your sons, your daughters, your men-servants, your maid-servants, and the Levite in your gates; for he has no portion or inheritance with you.


###### Deuteronomy 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:13)


הִשָּׁ֣מֶר לְךָ֔ פֶּֽן־תַּעֲלֶ֖ה עֹלֹתֶ֑יךָ בְּכׇל־מָק֖וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּרְאֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Take heed, lest you offer up your burnt-offerings anywhere you envision as proper.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take heed to thyself that thou offer not thy burnt offerings in every place that thou seest:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourselves [A negative commandment is superadded to the positive (11)] lest you offer up your burnt-offerings in every place that you see [fit (but you may do so at the behest of a prophet, viz. Eliyahu on Mount Carmel)].


###### Deuteronomy 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:14)


כִּ֣י אִם־בַּמָּק֞וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ בְּאַחַ֣ד שְׁבָטֶ֔יךָ שָׁ֖ם תַּעֲלֶ֣ה עֹלֹתֶ֑יךָ וְשָׁ֣ם תַּעֲשֶׂ֔ה כֹּ֛ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י מְצַוֶּֽךָּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Solely in the place that Adonoy chooses within one of your tribes, there are you to offer up your burnt-offerings, and there are you to do everything that I am commanding you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but only in the place which the Lord shall choose in one of thy tribes, there thou shalt offer thy burnt offerings, and there thou shalt do all that I command thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But only in the place that the L-rd shall choose, in (the portion of) one of your tribes [Benjamin] — there shall you offer up your burnt-offerings, and there shall you do all that I command you.


###### Deuteronomy 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:15)


רַק֩ בְּכׇל־אַוַּ֨ת נַפְשְׁךָ֜ תִּזְבַּ֣ח**׀** וְאָכַלְתָּ֣ בָשָׂ֗ר כְּבִרְכַּ֨ת יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָֽתַן־לְךָ֖ בְּכׇל־שְׁעָרֶ֑יךָ הַטָּמֵ֤א וְהַטָּהוֹר֙ יֹאכְלֶ֔נּוּ כַּצְּבִ֖י וְכָאַיָּֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Except with the fullness of your appetite you may slaughter and eat meat in accord with the blessing of Adonoy, your God, that He has given you in all your cities; the ritually unclean and the clean will eat it like the deer and the gazelle.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nonetheless, thou mayst slaughter animals and eat their flesh to thy heart’s desire, according to the blessing of the Lord thy God which he has bestowed on thee, throughout all thy gates, the unclean and the clean may eat of it as they do of the gazelle and the deer.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only [(This excludes from redemption consecrated animals with passing blemishes)], with all the desire of your soul may you slaughter [after redemption, consecrated animals which have sustained a (permanent) blemish], and you may eat [in any place] flesh, [(but you may not use their fleece and their milk)], according to the blessing of the L-rd your G-d that He has given you in all of your gates. The unclean one and the clean one may eat of it [from the same plate (even though it was once consecrated)], as the deer and as the hart [from which a sacrifice is never brought; and, like them, too, in that they are exempt from the priestly gift (shoulder, cheeks, and maw)].


###### Deuteronomy 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:16)


רַ֥ק הַדָּ֖ם לֹ֣א תֹאכֵ֑לוּ עַל־הָאָ֥רֶץ תִּשְׁפְּכֶ֖נּוּ כַּמָּֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, you may not eat the blood; on the earth are you to spill it like water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only you shall not eat the blood; you shall pour it upon the earth like water.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only the blood shall you not eat, [even though it is not to be sprinkled on the altar]; on the earth shall you spill it as water [It does not require covering].


###### Deuteronomy 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:17)


לֹֽא־תוּכַ֞ל לֶאֱכֹ֣ל בִּשְׁעָרֶ֗יךָ מַעְשַׂ֤ר דְּגָֽנְךָ֙ וְתִירֹשְׁךָ֣ וְיִצְהָרֶ֔ךָ וּבְכֹרֹ֥ת בְּקָרְךָ֖ וְצֹאנֶ֑ךָ וְכׇל־נְדָרֶ֙יךָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּדֹּ֔ר וְנִדְבֹתֶ֖יךָ וּתְרוּמַ֥ת יָדֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are not permitted to eat in your cities the tithe of your grain and your wine and your olive oil, and the first-born of your cattle and your flocks, and all your pledges that you pledge, and your donations and the terumah-separation of your hand-offerings.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou mayst not eat within thy gates the tithe of thy corn, or of thy wine, or of thy oil, or the firstlings of thy herds or of thy flock, or any of thy vows which thou vowest, or thy freewill offerings, or offering of thy hand:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not be able to eat in your gates [A negative commandment is superadded to the positive (11)] the tithe of your corn and your wine and your oil and the first-born of your herd or your flocks [(this, being an exhortation to the Cohanim)], and all your vows which you vow, and your gifts, and the offering of your hands [bikkurim].


###### Deuteronomy 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:18)


כִּ֡י אִם־לִפְנֵי֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ תֹּאכְלֶ֗נּוּ בַּמָּקוֹם֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִבְחַ֜ר יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֘יךָ֮ בּוֹ֒ אַתָּ֨ה וּבִנְךָ֤ וּבִתֶּ֙ךָ֙ וְעַבְדְּךָ֣ וַאֲמָתֶ֔ךָ וְהַלֵּוִ֖י אֲשֶׁ֣ר בִּשְׁעָרֶ֑יךָ וְשָׂמַחְתָּ֗ לִפְנֵי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בְּכֹ֖ל מִשְׁלַ֥ח יָדֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather, before Adonoy, your God, are you to eat it, in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses—you and your son and your daughter, and your male slave and your female slave, and the Levite in your cities; and you will be happy in the presence of Adonoy, your God, with the sum of your handiwork.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but thou must eat them before the Lord thy God in the place which the Lord thy God shall choose, thou, and thy son, and thy daughter, and thy manservant, and thy maidservant, and the Levite who is within thy gates: and thou shalt rejoice before the Lord thy God in all that to which thou puttest thy hand.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But only before the L-rd your G-d [within the wall] shall you eat it, in the place that the L-rd your G-d chooses — you, your son, your daughter, your man-servant, your maid-servant, and the Levite who is in your gates [(If you cannot give him of his portion, the first tithe, give him second tithe; if you have no second tithe, invite him to partake with you of your peace-offerings)], and you shall rejoice before the L-rd your G-d in all to which you put your hand.


###### Deuteronomy 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:19)


הִשָּׁ֣מֶר לְךָ֔ פֶּֽן־תַּעֲזֹ֖ב אֶת־הַלֵּוִ֑י כׇּל־יָמֶ֖יךָ עַל־אַדְמָתֶֽךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Watch yourself, lest you forsake the Levite, all your years on your land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take heed to thyself that thou forsake not the Levite as long as thou livest upon the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourself lest you forsake the Levite all of your days upon your land [(But in the exile, you are not exhorted more towards him than towards any of the poor of Israel.)]


###### Deuteronomy 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:20)


כִּֽי־יַרְחִיב֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֥יךָ אֶֽת־גְּבֻלְךָ֮ כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבֶּר־לָךְ֒ וְאָמַרְתָּ֙ אֹכְלָ֣ה בָשָׂ֔ר כִּֽי־תְאַוֶּ֥ה נַפְשְׁךָ֖ לֶאֱכֹ֣ל בָּשָׂ֑ר בְּכׇל־אַוַּ֥ת נַפְשְׁךָ֖ תֹּאכַ֥ל בָּשָֽׂר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy, your God, expands your border as He promised you, and you say, ‘‘I would like to eat meat’’ because you have an appetite to eat meat; to the full extent of your appetite eat meat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the Lord thy God shall enlarge thy border, as he has promised thee, and thou shalt say, I will eat meat, because thou longest to eat meat; thou mayst eat meat, to thy heart’s desire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the L-rd your G-d broadens your boundary as He spoke to you, and you say I shall eat flesh, for your soul shall desire to eat flesh [(The Torah hereby teaches proper conduct, that one should desire to eat flesh only out of "expansiveness" and wealth)], then with all the desire of your soul shall you eat flesh [(But in the desert, mundane (non-consecrated) flesh (chullin) was not permitted to them until the animal was consecrated and sacrificed as peace-offerings)].


###### Deuteronomy 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:21)


כִּֽי־יִרְחַ֨ק מִמְּךָ֜ הַמָּק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִבְחַ֜ר יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֘יךָ֮ לָשׂ֣וּם שְׁמ֣וֹ שָׁם֒ וְזָבַחְתָּ֞ מִבְּקָרְךָ֣ וּמִצֹּֽאנְךָ֗ אֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתַ֤ן יְהֹוָה֙ לְךָ֔ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר צִוִּיתִ֑ךָ וְאָֽכַלְתָּ֙ בִּשְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ בְּכֹ֖ל אַוַּ֥ת נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When the place is distant from you that Adonoy, your God, chooses to set His Presence there, you may slaughter some of your cattle or your flocks that Adonoy gave you, as I have commanded you; and you will eat in your cities with all your appetite.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If the place which the Lord thy God has chosen to put his name there be too far from thee, then thou shalt kill of thy herd and of thy flock, which the Lord has given thee, as I have commanded thee, and thou shalt eat in thy gates to thy heart’s desire.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If the place be distant from you in which the L-rd your G-d shall choose to place His name, [and you will not be able to come and bring peace-offerings every day, as you can now that the mishkan travels with you], then you shall slaughter of your herd and of your flock that the L-rd has given you, as He commanded you [(whence it is seen that there is a command as to the proper procedure for slaughtering, these being the laws of shechitah as stated to Moses on Sinai)], and you shall eat in your gates with all the desire of your soul.


###### Deuteronomy 12:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:22)


אַ֗ךְ כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר יֵאָכֵ֤ל אֶֽת־הַצְּבִי֙ וְאֶת־הָ֣אַיָּ֔ל כֵּ֖ן תֹּאכְלֶ֑נּוּ הַטָּמֵא֙ וְהַטָּה֔וֹר יַחְדָּ֖ו יֹאכְלֶֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Only, as the deer and the gazelle may be eaten, so may you eat it; the ritually unclean and the clean together may eat it


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Even as the gazelle and the deer is eaten, so thou shalt eat them: the unclean and the clean shall eat of them alike.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only [(This excludes permission to eat fats of a beast of chullin, though it is permitted to eat those of an animal, viz., deer and hart.)] as the deer and hart are eaten [i.e., even in a state of uncleanliness], so shall you eat it; the unclean one and the clean one together may eat it.


###### Deuteronomy 12:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:23)


רַ֣ק חֲזַ֗ק לְבִלְתִּי֙ אֲכֹ֣ל הַדָּ֔ם כִּ֥י הַדָּ֖ם ה֣וּא הַנָּ֑פֶשׁ וְלֹא־תֹאכַ֥ל הַנֶּ֖פֶשׁ עִם־הַבָּשָֽׂר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Only, exercise strength in not eating the blood, for the blood is the life; and do not eat the spirit with the meat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only be sure that thou eat not the blood: for the blood is the life; and thou mayst not eat the life with the meat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only strengthen yourself not to eat the blood [(whence it is seen that they were drawn to the eating of blood)]. For the blood is the life, and you shall not eat the life with the flesh [(an exhortation against (eating) ever min hechai (a limb torn from a living animal)].


###### Deuteronomy 12:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:24)


לֹ֖א תֹּאכְלֶ֑נּוּ עַל־הָאָ֥רֶץ תִּשְׁפְּכֶ֖נּוּ כַּמָּֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not eat it; onto the earth are you to spill it like water.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not eat it; thou shalt pour it upon the earth like water.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not eat it [an exhortation against (eating) dam hatamtzith (the residue of the blood flow)]; on the earth shall you spill it as water.


###### Deuteronomy 12:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:25)


לֹ֖א תֹּאכְלֶ֑נּוּ לְמַ֨עַן יִיטַ֤ב לְךָ֙ וּלְבָנֶ֣יךָ אַחֲרֶ֔יךָ כִּֽי־תַעֲשֶׂ֥ה הַיָּשָׁ֖ר בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not eat it, so that you may enjoy beneficience, and your children after you, when you do the upright in Adonoy’s eyes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not eat it; that it may go well with thee, and with thy children after thee, when thou shalt do that which is right in the sight of the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not eat it [an exhortation against (eating) the blood of the organs], so that it be good for you and your children after you for doing what is just in the eyes of the L-rd.


###### Deuteronomy 12:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:26)


רַ֧ק קׇֽדָשֶׁ֛יךָ אֲשֶׁר־יִהְי֥וּ לְךָ֖ וּנְדָרֶ֑יךָ תִּשָּׂ֣א וּבָ֔אתָ אֶל־הַמָּק֖וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, your sacred offerings that you will have, and your pledges, you are to bring when you come to the place that Adonoy chooses.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only thy holy things which thou hast, and thy vows, thou shalt take and go to the place which the Lord shall choose:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only your consecrated things which shall be to you, and your vows, shall you bear and you shall come to the place that the L-rd shall choose. [i.e., Though you are permitted to slaughter chullin, I did not permit you to slaughter consecrated animals and to eat them in your gates without offering them up; but bring them to the Temple.]


###### Deuteronomy 12:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:27)


וְעָשִׂ֤יתָ עֹלֹתֶ֙יךָ֙ הַבָּשָׂ֣ר וְהַדָּ֔ם עַל־מִזְבַּ֖ח יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וְדַם־זְבָחֶ֗יךָ יִשָּׁפֵךְ֙ עַל־מִזְבַּח֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וְהַבָּשָׂ֖ר תֹּאכֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to execute your burnt-offerings—the meat and the blood—on the altar of Adonoy, your God; and the blood of your peace -offerings will be poured on the altar of Adonoy, your God, then you will eat the meat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt offer thy burnt offerings, the meat and the blood, upon the altar of the Lord thy God: and the blood of thy sacrifices shall be poured out upon the altar of the Lord thy God, and thou shalt eat the meat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall offer your burnt-offerings, the flesh and the blood, on the altar of the L-rd your G-d [i.e., If they are burnt-offerings, place the flesh and the blood on the altar], and [if they are peace-offerings] the blood of your sacrifices shall be spilled on the altar of the L-rd your G-d [first], and [then] the flesh shall you eat.


###### Deuteronomy 12:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:28)


שְׁמֹ֣ר וְשָׁמַעְתָּ֗ אֵ֚ת כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה אֲשֶׁ֥ר אָנֹכִ֖י מְצַוֶּ֑ךָּ לְמַ֩עַן֩ יִיטַ֨ב לְךָ֜ וּלְבָנֶ֤יךָ אַחֲרֶ֙יךָ֙ עַד־עוֹלָ֔ם כִּ֤י תַעֲשֶׂה֙ הַטּ֣וֹב וְהַיָּשָׁ֔ר בְּעֵינֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Preserve and heed all these words that I am commanding you, in order that you have it good and your children after you forever, when you do that which is good and that which is upright in the eyes of Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Observe and hear all these words which I command thee, that it may go well with thee, and with thy children after thee for ever, when thou doest that which is good and right in the sight of the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Keep [mishnah (i.e., learning) within you] so that you will heed all of these things that I command you, [i.e., so that a "light" mitzvah be as beloved by you as a "weighty" one], so that it be good for you and for your children after you forever for doing what is good [in the eyes of Heaven] and just [in the eyes of man] in the eyes of the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 12:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:29)


כִּֽי־יַכְרִית֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ אֶת־הַגּוֹיִ֗ם אֲשֶׁ֨ר אַתָּ֥ה בָא־שָׁ֛מָּה לָרֶ֥שֶׁת אוֹתָ֖ם מִפָּנֶ֑יךָ וְיָרַשְׁתָּ֣ אֹתָ֔ם וְיָשַׁבְתָּ֖ בְּאַרְצָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy, your God, will have eliminated the nations whom you are coming there to inherit, from before you; and you inherit them and dwell in their land;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the Lord thy God shall cut off the nations from before thee, whither thou goest to dispossess them, and thou dost succeed them and dost dwell in their land;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the L-rd your G-d will cut off the nations whither you come to inherit them, from before you, and you inherit them and dwell in their land,


###### Deuteronomy 12:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:30)


הִשָּׁ֣מֶר לְךָ֗ פֶּן־תִּנָּקֵשׁ֙ אַחֲרֵיהֶ֔ם אַחֲרֵ֖י הִשָּׁמְדָ֣ם מִפָּנֶ֑יךָ וּפֶן־תִּדְרֹ֨שׁ לֵאלֹֽהֵיהֶ֜ם לֵאמֹ֗ר אֵיכָ֨ה יַעַבְד֜וּ הַגּוֹיִ֤ם הָאֵ֙לֶּה֙ אֶת־אֱלֹ֣הֵיהֶ֔ם וְאֶעֱשֶׂה־כֵּ֖ן גַּם־אָֽנִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look out for yourself, lest you are torn apart along with them, after they have been annihilated before you, and lest you inquire about their gods, saying, ‘‘How did these nations worship their gods, let me do so, too.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
take heed to thyself that thou be not ensnared into following them, after they are destroyed from before thee; and that thou inquire not after their gods, saying, How did these nations serve their gods? even so will I do likewise.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourselves lest you are drawn in after them after [i.e., in spite of having witnessed] their destruction before you [for their perverse deeds], and lest you inquire after their gods, saying: How did these nations serve their gods that I may do the same? [(We are hereby apprised that if the service of a particular god is not a conventional one, the one who serves him thus is nonetheless liable.)]


###### Deuteronomy 12:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/12:31)


לֹא־תַעֲשֶׂ֣ה כֵ֔ן לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ כִּי֩ כׇל־תּוֹעֲבַ֨ת יְהֹוָ֜ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׂנֵ֗א עָשׂוּ֙ לֵאלֹ֣הֵיהֶ֔ם כִּ֣י גַ֤ם אֶת־בְּנֵיהֶם֙ וְאֶת־בְּנֹ֣תֵיהֶ֔ם יִשְׂרְפ֥וּ בָאֵ֖שׁ לֵאלֹֽהֵיהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not do so to Adonoy, your God, because whatever is abominated by God, what He hates, they have done for their gods; for even their sons and their daughters do they burn in fire to their gods.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not do so to the Lord thy God: for every abomination to the Lord, which he hates, have they done to their gods; for even their sons and their daughters they have burnt in the fire to their gods.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not do thus to the L-rd your G-d. For every abomination of the L-rd, which He hates, did they do unto their gods. For even [(including their fathers and mothers)] their sons and their daughters did they burn in fire to their gods.



## Chapter 13

###### Deuteronomy 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:1)


אֵ֣ת כׇּל־הַדָּבָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֤ר אָנֹכִי֙ מְצַוֶּ֣ה אֶתְכֶ֔ם אֹת֥וֹ תִשְׁמְר֖וּ לַעֲשׂ֑וֹת לֹא־תֹסֵ֣ף עָלָ֔יו וְלֹ֥א תִגְרַ֖ע מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Everything that I am commanding you—be careful to fulfill it; do not add to it and do not subtract from it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Every matter which I command you, observe to do it: thou shalt not add thereto, nor diminish from it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The entire thing that I command you ["light" as well as "weighty"], shall you take heed to do [A negative commandment is hereby superadded to each of the positive commandments mentioned above (but stripes do not obtain here, there being no stripes for the "take heed" of a positive commandment)]. You shall not add to it and you shall not diminish therefrom [See 4:2]


###### Deuteronomy 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:2)


כִּֽי־יָק֤וּם בְּקִרְבְּךָ֙ נָבִ֔יא א֖וֹ חֹלֵ֣ם חֲל֑וֹם וְנָתַ֥ן אֵלֶ֛יךָ א֖וֹת א֥וֹ מוֹפֵֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a prophet arises among you or a dreamer of a dream, and he gives you an omen or a miracle,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If there arise among you a prophet, or a dreamer of dreams, and he give thee a sign or a wonder,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there arise in your midst a prophet or a dreamer of dreams, and he give to you a sign [in the heavens] or a wonder [on earth],


###### Deuteronomy 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:3)


וּבָ֤א הָאוֹת֙ וְהַמּוֹפֵ֔ת אֲשֶׁר־דִּבֶּ֥ר אֵלֶ֖יךָ לֵאמֹ֑ר נֵֽלְכָ֞ה אַחֲרֵ֨י אֱלֹהִ֧ים אֲחֵרִ֛ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יְדַעְתָּ֖ם וְנׇֽעׇבְדֵֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the omen or the miracle happens—the one he told you about—saying, ‘‘Let us go after other gods, that you do not know, and let us serve them.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the sign or the wonder come to pass, of which he spoke to thee, saying, Let us go after other gods, which thou hast not known, and let us serve them;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there shall materialize the sign and the wonder which he spoke to you, saying: Let us go after other gods, that you did not know, and let us serve them,


###### Deuteronomy 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:4)


לֹ֣א תִשְׁמַ֗ע אֶל־דִּבְרֵי֙ הַנָּבִ֣יא הַה֔וּא א֛וֹ אֶל־חוֹלֵ֥ם הַחֲל֖וֹם הַה֑וּא כִּ֣י מְנַסֶּ֞ה יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֙ אֶתְכֶ֔ם לָדַ֗עַת הֲיִשְׁכֶ֤ם אֹֽהֲבִים֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֔ם בְּכׇל־לְבַבְכֶ֖ם וּבְכׇל־נַפְשְׁכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not listen to the words of that prophet or to that dreamer of a dream, because Adonoy, your God, is testing you to know whether you love Adonoy, your God, wholeheartedly and with your entire beings.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou shalt not hearken to the words of that prophet, or that dreamer of dreams: for the Lord your God puts you to the proof, to know whether you love the Lord your God with all your heart and with all your soul.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Do not pay heed to the words of that prophet or that dreamer of dreams. For the L-rd your G-d is trying you to know whether you love the L-rd your G-d with all your heart and with all your soul.


###### Deuteronomy 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:5)


אַחֲרֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֛ם תֵּלֵ֖כוּ וְאֹת֣וֹ תִירָ֑אוּ וְאֶת־מִצְוֺתָ֤יו תִּשְׁמֹ֙רוּ֙ וּבְקֹל֣וֹ תִשְׁמָ֔עוּ וְאֹת֥וֹ תַעֲבֹ֖דוּ וּב֥וֹ תִדְבָּקֽוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
After Adonoy, your God, are you to go, fear Him, keep His commandments, heed His voice, serve Him, and cleave to Him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall walk after the Lord your God, and fear him, and keep his commandments, and obey his voice, and you shall serve him, and hold fast to him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
After the L-rd your G-d shall you go, and Him shall you fear, and His mitzvoth [the Torah of Moses], shall you keep, and His voice [the voice of the prophets] shall you heed, and Him shall you serve [in His Temple], and to Him [i.e., to His ways] shall you cleave [(Be kind, bury the dead, visit the sick, as He did)].


###### Deuteronomy 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:6)


וְהַנָּבִ֣יא הַה֡וּא א֣וֹ חֹלֵם֩ הַחֲל֨וֹם הַה֜וּא יוּמָ֗ת כִּ֣י דִבֶּר־סָ֠רָ֠ה עַל־יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֜ם הַמּוֹצִ֥יא אֶתְכֶ֣ם**׀** מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֗יִם וְהַפֹּֽדְךָ֙ מִבֵּ֣ית עֲבָדִ֔ים לְהַדִּֽיחֲךָ֙ מִן־הַדֶּ֔רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֧ר צִוְּךָ֛ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ לָלֶ֣כֶת בָּ֑הּ וּבִֽעַרְתָּ֥ הָרָ֖ע מִקִּרְבֶּֽךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And that prophet or that dreamer of a dream is to be executed, for he has uttered a fabrication about Adonoy, your God—Who took you out of the land of Egypt, and Who redeemed you from the house of bondage—to mislead you from the way that Adonoy, your God, commanded you to go upon, and you will eliminate the evil from within you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And that prophet, or that dreamer of dreams, shall be put to death; because he has spoken to turn you away from the Lord your God, who brought you out of the land of Miżrayim and redeemed you out of the house of bondage, to thrust thee out of the way which the Lord thy God commanded thee to walk in. So shalt thou put the evil away from the midst of thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And that prophet or that dreamer of dreams shall be put to death. For of what is "removed" [from the world, it never having been created, and His never having been commanded by Me to speak thus] has he spoken of the L-rd your G-d — who took you out of the land of Egypt, and who redeemed you from the house of bondage. [Had He done only that, it should have been sufficient!] — to turn you from the way on which the L-rd your G-d commanded you to go. And you shall remove the evil from your midst.


###### Deuteronomy 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:7)


כִּ֣י יְסִֽיתְךָ֡ אָחִ֣יךָ בֶן־אִ֠מֶּ֠ךָ אֽוֹ־בִנְךָ֨ אֽוֹ־בִתְּךָ֜ א֣וֹ**׀** אֵ֣שֶׁת חֵיקֶ֗ךָ א֧וֹ רֵֽעֲךָ֛ אֲשֶׁ֥ר כְּנַפְשְׁךָ֖ בַּסֵּ֣תֶר לֵאמֹ֑ר נֵֽלְכָ֗ה וְנַֽעַבְדָה֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁר֙ לֹ֣א יָדַ֔עְתָּ אַתָּ֖ה וַאֲבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If one shall incite you, your brother—son of your mother—or your son or your daughter, or the wife of your bosom, or your neighbor who is your soul mate, clandestinely, saying, ‘‘Let us go and serve other gods ’’ whom you never knew—neither you nor your forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thy brother, the son of thy mother, or thy son, or thy daughter, or the wife of thy bosom, or thy friend, who is as thy own soul, entice thee secretly, saying, Let us go and serve other gods, which thou hast not known, thou, nor thy fathers;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there incite you your brother [from your father] or the son of your mother, or your son, or your daughter, or the wife of your bosom, or your friend who is as your soul [i.e., your father (Scripture adduces those who are most beloved by you, how much more so others (who incite you thus!)], in secret [(this being the common mode for such incitement)], saying: Let us go and serve other gods, which you did not know, neither you nor your fathers [(Even the heathens do not forsake what their fathers have bequeathed them — and this one bids you to do so!)]


###### Deuteronomy 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:8)


מֵאֱלֹהֵ֣י הָֽעַמִּ֗ים אֲשֶׁר֙ סְבִיבֹ֣תֵיכֶ֔ם הַקְּרֹבִ֣ים אֵלֶ֔יךָ א֖וֹ הָרְחֹקִ֣ים מִמֶּ֑ךָּ מִקְצֵ֥ה הָאָ֖רֶץ וְעַד־קְצֵ֥ה הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From the gods of the nations that surround you, who are near you or at a distance from you, from the ends of the earth to the ends of the earth,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
of the gods of the peoples who are round about you, either near to thee, or far off from thee, from the one end of the earth even to the other end of the earth;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Of the gods of the peoples around you, who are near to you or who are far from you [(Just as there is (manifestly) nothing to those near you, so there is nothing to those distant from you)], from one end of the earth [the sun, the moon, and the hosts of heaven (which go from one end of the world)] until the other —


###### Deuteronomy 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:9)


לֹא־תֹאבֶ֣ה ל֔וֹ וְלֹ֥א תִשְׁמַ֖ע אֵלָ֑יו וְלֹא־תָח֤וֹס עֵֽינְךָ֙ עָלָ֔יו וְלֹֽא־תַחְמֹ֥ל וְלֹֽא־תְכַסֶּ֖ה עָלָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not be favorbly inclined towards him, and do not listen to him; and do not view him compassionately, and do not take pity and do not cover for him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou shalt not consent to him, nor hearken to him; nor shall thy eye pity him, nor shalt thou spare, nor shalt thou conceal him:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall feel no affection for him, and you shall not hearken to him [when he pleads for his life], and your eye shall not be compassionate towards him, and you shall not pity [i.e., You shall not seek an "out" for him], and you shall not cover up for him [if you know something against him].


###### Deuteronomy 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:10)


כִּ֤י הָרֹג֙ תַּֽהַרְגֶ֔נּוּ יָ֥דְךָ֛ תִּֽהְיֶה־בּ֥וֹ בָרִֽאשׁוֹנָ֖ה לַהֲמִית֑וֹ וְיַ֥ד כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם בָּאַחֲרֹנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For you must surely execute him. Let your hand strike him first to execute him, and the hand of the entire people afterward.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but thou shalt surely kill him; thy hand shall be first upon him to put him to death, and afterwards the hand of all the people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But kill shall you kill him [If he has left beth-din acquitted, return him for conviction; if he has left beth-din convicted, do not return him for acquittal.] Your hand shall be against him first to put him to death, and the hand of all the people afterwards. [It is a mitzvah for the one incited to put him to death; if he does not die by his hand, he is to die by the hand of others.]


###### Deuteronomy 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:11)


וּסְקַלְתּ֥וֹ בָאֲבָנִ֖ים וָמֵ֑ת כִּ֣י בִקֵּ֗שׁ לְהַדִּֽיחֲךָ֙ מֵעַל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ הַמּוֹצִיאֲךָ֛ מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרַ֖יִם מִבֵּ֥ית עֲבָדִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And you are to stone him with stones so that he dies, for he sought to mislead you from Adonoy, your God, Who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of slavery.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt stone him with stones, that he die; because he has sought to draw thee away from the Lord thy God, who brought thee out of the land of Miżrayim, from the house of bondage.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall stone him with stones and he shall die; for he sought to turn you from the L-rd your G-d, who took you out of the land of Egypt, from the house of bondage.


###### Deuteronomy 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:12)


וְכׇ֨ל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל יִשְׁמְע֖וּ וְיִֽרָא֑וּן וְלֹֽא־יוֹסִ֣פוּ לַעֲשׂ֗וֹת כַּדָּבָ֥ר הָרָ֛ע הַזֶּ֖ה בְּקִרְבֶּֽךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And all of Yisroel will hear and will fear, and they will not proceed to do so evil a thing as this, within you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all Yisra᾽el shall hear, and fear, and shall do no more any such wickedness as this is among you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of Israel shall hear and see, and they shall not do again according to this evil thing in your midst.


###### Deuteronomy 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:13)


כִּֽי־תִשְׁמַ֞ע בְּאַחַ֣ת עָרֶ֗יךָ אֲשֶׁר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֛ לָשֶׁ֥בֶת שָׁ֖ם לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you hear in one of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you in which to settle, the following:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou shalt hear say in one of thy cities, which the Lord thy God has given thee to dwell there, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you hear in one of your cities which the L-rd your G-d gives you to dwell there [(This excludes Jerusalem, which was not intended as a dwelling (for a particular tribe)], saying:


###### Deuteronomy 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:14)


יָצְא֞וּ אֲנָשִׁ֤ים בְּנֵֽי־בְלִיַּ֙עַל֙ מִקִּרְבֶּ֔ךָ וַיַּדִּ֛יחוּ אֶת־יֹשְׁבֵ֥י עִירָ֖ם לֵאמֹ֑ר נֵלְכָ֗ה וְנַעַבְדָ֛ה אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יְדַעְתֶּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘Men have gone out—unscrupulous —from among you, and they have misled the inhabitants of their city by saying, ‘Let us go and serve other gods ’ that you do not know.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Certain men, wicked persons, are gone out from among you, and have drawn away the inhabitants of their city, saying, Let us go and serve other gods, which you know not;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There have gone out men [(Women are excluded)], who have cast off the yoke [of Heaven] from your midst, and they have turned astray the dwellers of their city [(but not the dwellers of a different city)], saying: Let us go and serve other gods that you did not know,


###### Deuteronomy 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:15)


וְדָרַשְׁתָּ֧ וְחָקַרְתָּ֛ וְשָׁאַלְתָּ֖ הֵיטֵ֑ב וְהִנֵּ֤ה אֱמֶת֙ נָכ֣וֹן הַדָּבָ֔ר נֶעֶשְׂתָ֛ה הַתּוֹעֵבָ֥ה הַזֹּ֖את בְּקִרְבֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
