You must investigate, inquire, and interrogate thoroughly; and if in fact the report is true and accurate—this abomination was committed among you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then shalt thou inquire, and make search, and ask diligently; and, behold, if it be truth, and the thing certain, that such abomination was done among you;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall inquire, and you shall search out, and you shall ask well, and, behold, true and correct is the thing; this abomination has been done in your midst,


###### Deuteronomy 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:16)


הַכֵּ֣ה תַכֶּ֗ה אֶת־יֹ֥שְׁבֵ֛י הָעִ֥יר הַהִ֖וא לְפִי־חָ֑רֶב הַחֲרֵ֨ם אֹתָ֧הּ וְאֶת־כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר־בָּ֛הּ וְאֶת־בְּהֶמְתָּ֖הּ לְפִי־חָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must surely strike down the inhabitants of that city by the sword; annihilate it and everything that is in it, and its livestock by the sword.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou shalt surely smite the inhabitants of that city with the edge of the sword, destroying it utterly, and all that is in it, and its cattle with the edge of the sword.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Smite shall you smite [(If you cannot administer the prescribed death, administer another)] the inhabitants of that city by the sword. Lay it waste, and all that is in it, and its beasts, by the sword.


###### Deuteronomy 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:17)


וְאֶת־כׇּל־שְׁלָלָ֗הּ תִּקְבֹּץ֮ אֶל־תּ֣וֹךְ רְחֹבָהּ֒ וְשָׂרַפְתָּ֨ בָאֵ֜שׁ אֶת־הָעִ֤יר וְאֶת־כׇּל־שְׁלָלָהּ֙ כָּלִ֔יל לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וְהָיְתָה֙ תֵּ֣ל עוֹלָ֔ם לֹ֥א תִבָּנֶ֖ה עֽוֹד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And all its booty are you to collect within its plaza, and you are to burn in fire the city and all its booty—totally—for Adonoy, your God; and it will be a ruin forever, it is never to be rebuilt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt gather all the spoil of it into the midst of the open place of the city, and shalt burn with fire both the city and the entire plunder taken in it, for the Lord thy God: and it shall be a heap for ever; it shall not be built again.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all of its spoil shall you gather into the midst of its square, and you shall burn with fire the city and all its spoil, entire, to the L-rd your G-d [i.e., in His name and for His sake]. And it shall be a heap forever; it shall not be built again.


###### Deuteronomy 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:18)


וְלֹֽא־יִדְבַּ֧ק בְּיָדְךָ֛ מְא֖וּמָה מִן־הַחֵ֑רֶם לְמַ֩עַן֩ יָשׁ֨וּב יְהֹוָ֜ה מֵחֲר֣וֹן אַפּ֗וֹ וְנָֽתַן־לְךָ֤ רַחֲמִים֙ וְרִֽחַמְךָ֣ וְהִרְבֶּ֔ךָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר נִשְׁבַּ֖ע לַאֲבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Let nothing of the *cherem* cling to your hand; so that Adonoy withdraws His raising fury and grant you mercy, and He will be merciful toward you and multiply you as He swore to your forefathers


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And nothing of that which was devoted to destruction shall remain in your hand: that the Lord may turn from the fierceness of his anger, and show thee mercy, and have compassion upon thee, and multiply thee, as he has sworn to thy fathers;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And let naught from the spoil adhere to your hand, so that the L-rd turn from the burning of His wrath [(For so long as idolatry is in the world, burning wrath is in the world)] and He will grant you mercy and He will be merciful to you and He will multiply you as He swore to your fathers,


###### Deuteronomy 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/13:19)


כִּ֣י תִשְׁמַ֗ע בְּקוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לִשְׁמֹר֙ אֶת־כׇּל־מִצְוֺתָ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֛ר אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּ֑וֹם לַעֲשׂוֹת֙ הַיָּשָׁ֔ר בְּעֵינֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you heed the voice of Adonoy, your God, to guard all His commandments that I am commanding you today, to do the upright in the eyes of Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
when thou shalt hearken to the voice of the Lord thy God, to keep all his commandments which I command thee this day, to do that which is right in the eyes of the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you hearken to the voice of the L-rd your G-d to keep all of His mitzvoth that I command you this day, to do what is just in the eyes of the L-rd your G-d.



## Chapter 14

###### Deuteronomy 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:1)


בָּנִ֣ים אַתֶּ֔ם לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם לֹ֣א תִתְגֹּֽדְד֗וּ וְלֹֽא־תָשִׂ֧ימוּ קׇרְחָ֛ה בֵּ֥ין עֵינֵיכֶ֖ם לָמֵֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are sons to Adonoy, your God; do not lacerate yourselves and do not make yourselves bald between your eyes for a dead person.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You are the children of the Lord your God: you shall not gash yourselves, nor make any baldness between your eyes for the dead.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Children are you to the L-rd your G-d. Do not lacerate yourselves [as the Emorites do (for you are children of the L-rd, and it befits you to be comely and not lacerated and bald)], and do not make baldness between your eyes [opposite the forehead (see Leviticus 21:5)] for the dead.


###### Deuteronomy 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:2)


כִּ֣י עַ֤ם קָדוֹשׁ֙ אַתָּ֔ה לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וּבְךָ֞ בָּחַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה לִֽהְי֥וֹת לוֹ֙ לְעַ֣ם סְגֻלָּ֔ה מִכֹּל֙ הָֽעַמִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הָאֲדָמָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For you are a sacred people to Adonoy, your God, and Adonoy has chosen you to be for Him a treasured people from all the peoples who are on the surface of the earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For thou art a holy people to the Lord thy God, and the Lord has chosen thee to be a special possession to himself, out of all the nations that are upon the earth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For you are a holy people to the L-rd your G-d [by virtue of your fathers], and in you [in your own right] has the L-rd chosen to be unto Him a chosen people from all the peoples that are on the face of the earth.


###### Deuteronomy 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:3)


לֹ֥א תֹאכַ֖ל כׇּל־תּוֹעֵבָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not eat any abomination.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not eat any abominable thing.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not eat any abomination [i.e., anything that I rendered an abomination to you, e.g., cooking milk and meat together. (If you have performed this abomination, you may not eat it)].


###### Deuteronomy 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:4)


זֹ֥את הַבְּהֵמָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר תֹּאכֵ֑לוּ שׁ֕וֹר שֵׂ֥ה כְשָׂבִ֖ים וְשֵׂ֥ה עִזִּֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the animals that you are to eat: the bovine, the sheep and the goat;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are the beasts which you may eat: the ox, the sheep, and the goat,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This is the beast that you may eat: the ox, the lamb of sheep, and the kid of goats.


###### Deuteronomy 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:5)


אַיָּ֥ל וּצְבִ֖י וְיַחְמ֑וּר וְאַקּ֥וֹ וְדִישֹׁ֖ן וּתְא֥וֹ וָזָֽמֶר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The gazelle, the deer and the fallow deer; and the ibex and the adax; and the wild ox and the wild sheep.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the deer, and the gazelle, and the fallow deer, and the wild goat, and the adax, and the bison, and the wild sheep.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The hart, the gazelle, and the fallow deer [(whence it is derived that an animal is in the category of "beast" (4), and, also, that the unclean beasts and animals are more numerous than the clean, the lesser number always being specified)], and the wild goat, and the dyshon, and the wild ox, and the chamois.


###### Deuteronomy 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:6)


וְכׇל־בְּהֵמָ֞ה מַפְרֶ֣סֶת פַּרְסָ֗ה וְשֹׁסַ֤עַת שֶׁ֙סַע֙ שְׁתֵּ֣י פְרָס֔וֹת מַעֲלַ֥ת גֵּרָ֖ה בַּבְּהֵמָ֑ה אֹתָ֖הּ תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And every animal whose sole is cloven, whose hoof is truly split in two, regurgitating [its] cud within the animals, it may you eat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And every beast that parts the hoof, forming thereby two entirely cloven hoofs, and chews the cud among the beasts, that may you eat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every beast that has split hooves, entirely cloven in two [(for there are those whose hooves are split but not entirely cloven)], and which chews the cud in the beast [(The implication is that what is found in the beast after slaughtering, i.e., a fetus, may be eaten without slaughtering)] — it shall you eat, [(but not an unclean beast (a positive commandment being superadded to the negative, in which instance the transgressor is liable for violation of both a positive and a negative commandment (See 20)].


###### Deuteronomy 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:7)


אַ֣ךְ אֶת־זֶ֞ה לֹ֤א תֹֽאכְלוּ֙ מִמַּֽעֲלֵ֣י הַגֵּרָ֔ה וּמִמַּפְרִיסֵ֥י הַפַּרְסָ֖ה הַשְּׁסוּעָ֑ה אֶֽת־הַ֠גָּמָ֠ל וְאֶת־הָאַרְנֶ֨בֶת וְאֶת־הַשָּׁפָ֜ן כִּֽי־מַעֲלֵ֧ה גֵרָ֣ה הֵ֗מָּה וּפַרְסָה֙ לֹ֣א הִפְרִ֔יסוּ טְמֵאִ֥ים הֵ֖ם לָכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These, however, do not eat from the regurgitators of cud and from the cloven-soled : the dromedary, the camel, and the hare and the hyrax; for regurgitators of cud are they, but have no cloven sole; they are ritually unclean for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nevertheless these you shall not eat of those which chew the cud, or of those which divide the cloven hoof; the camel, and the hare, and the coney: for they chew the cud, but do not divide the hoof; therefore they are unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only this may you not eat of those which chew the cud and of those which have cloven hooves: the shesuah [a creature with two backs and two spines], the camel, the hare, and the coney, for they chew the cud but do not have cloven hooves. They are unclean to you. [The enumeration is repeated though given previously (Leviticus 11) — the beasts, because of the shesuah; and the birds, because of the ra'ah (13), both of which are not mentioned there.]


###### Deuteronomy 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:8)


וְאֶת־הַ֠חֲזִ֠יר כִּֽי־מַפְרִ֨יס פַּרְסָ֥ה הוּא֙ וְלֹ֣א גֵרָ֔ה טָמֵ֥א ה֖וּא לָכֶ֑ם מִבְּשָׂרָם֙ לֹ֣א תֹאכֵ֔לוּ וּבְנִבְלָתָ֖ם לֹ֥א תִגָּֽעוּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Also the pig, because it is cloven-soled but does not [regurgitate its] cud; it is ritually unclean for you. From their flesh do not eat, and do not come in contact with their carcasses.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the swine, because it divides the hoof, yet chews not the cud, is unclean to you: you shall not eat of their meat, nor touch their carcasses.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the swine, for its hooves are cloven, but it does not chew the cud. It is unclean to you. Of their flesh you shall not eat, and their carcass you shall not touch [(on the festival, one being obliged to cleanse himself on a festival)].


###### Deuteronomy 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:9)


אֶת־זֶה֙ תֹּֽאכְל֔וּ מִכֹּ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר בַּמָּ֑יִם כֹּ֧ל אֲשֶׁר־ל֛וֹ סְנַפִּ֥יר וְקַשְׂקֶ֖שֶׂת תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are you to eat from whatever is in the water: whatever has fins and scales may you eat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These you may eat of all that are in the waters: all that have fins and scales shall you eat:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This may you eat of all that is in the water: all that have fins and scales may you eat.


###### Deuteronomy 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:10)


וְכֹ֨ל אֲשֶׁ֧ר אֵֽין־ל֛וֹ סְנַפִּ֥יר וְקַשְׂקֶ֖שֶׂת לֹ֣א תֹאכֵ֑לוּ טָמֵ֥א ה֖וּא לָכֶֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And whatever does not have fins and scales do not eat; they are ritually unclean for you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and whatever has no fins and scales you may not eat; it is unclean to you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And whatever does not have fins and scales you may not eat; it is unclean to you.


###### Deuteronomy 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:11)


כׇּל־צִפּ֥וֹר טְהֹרָ֖ה תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You may eat any pure bird.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All clean birds you may eat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every clean bird you may eat [including that sent out by the leper (Leviticus 14:7)].


###### Deuteronomy 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:12)


וְזֶ֕ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־תֹאכְל֖וּ מֵהֶ֑ם הַנֶּ֥שֶׁר וְהַפֶּ֖רֶס וְהָֽעׇזְנִיָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And this is what you may not eat: the griffon vulture and the bearded vulture, and the black vulture;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But these are they of which you shall not eat: the griffon vulture, and the bearded vulture, and the black vulture,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is what you may not eat of them [(including the slaughtered bird of the leper)]: the great vulture, the bearded vulture, and the osprey.


###### Deuteronomy 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:13)


וְהָרָאָה֙ וְאֶת־הָ֣אַיָּ֔ה וְהַדַּיָּ֖ה לְמִינָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The ra’ah and the *ayah,* and the kite according to its kind;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the buzzard, and the kite, and the hawk after its kind,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the ra'ah, and the ayah, and the dayah after its kind. [Ra'ah, ayah, and dayah are one and the same. It is called "ra'ah," for it "sees" (roeh) very clearly. All of its names are given so as not to provide an opening for "objectors," one who forbids it calling it "ra'ah," and one who wishes to permit it calling it "dayah" or "ayah," saying that Scripture did not interdict the latter. (The unclean birds are enumerated, being fewer than the clean. See 5)]

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:14)


וְאֵ֥ת כׇּל־עֹרֵ֖ב לְמִינֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And every raven according to its kind;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and every raven after its kind,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every raven after its kind.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:15)


וְאֵת֙ בַּ֣ת הַֽיַּעֲנָ֔ה וְאֶת־הַתַּחְמָ֖ס וְאֶת־הַשָּׁ֑חַף וְאֶת־הַנֵּ֖ץ לְמִינֵֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the dark desert eagle owl and the kestrel, and the gull, and the sparrow hawk according to its kind;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the dark desert owl, and the kestrel, and the gull, and the sparrow hawk after its kind,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the ostrich, and the night-hawk, and the sea-mew, and the hawk after its kind.


###### Deuteronomy 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:16)


אֶת־הַכּ֥וֹס וְאֶת־הַיַּנְשׁ֖וּף וְהַתִּנְשָֽׁמֶת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The dark little owl and the long-eared owl, and the *tinshames*;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the little owl, and the long eared owl, and the barn screech owl,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The little owl, and the great owl, and the tinshameth.


###### Deuteronomy 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:17)


וְהַקָּאָ֥ת וְאֶֽת־הָרָחָ֖מָה וְאֶת־הַשָּׁלָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the light little owl and the Egyptian vulture, and the shalach;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the night prowler, and the gier eagle, and the fish owl,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the pelican, and the carrion-vulture, and the cormorant.


###### Deuteronomy 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:18)


וְהַ֣חֲסִידָ֔ה וְהָאֲנָפָ֖ה לְמִינָ֑הּ וְהַדּוּכִיפַ֖ת וְהָעֲטַלֵּֽף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the stork, and the heron according to its kind, and the duchifas, and the bat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the stork, and the heron after its kind, and the hoopoe, and the bat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the stork, and the heron after its kind, and the lapwing, and the bat.


###### Deuteronomy 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:19)


וְכֹל֙ שֶׁ֣רֶץ הָע֔וֹף טָמֵ֥א ה֖וּא לָכֶ֑ם לֹ֖א יֵאָכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And all flying creeping creatures are ritually unclean for you; they may not be eaten.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And every creeping thing that flies is unclean to you: they shall not be eaten.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And every creeping thing (sheretz) of the bird, [which moves on the ground, e.g., flies, hornets, and unclean hoppers] is unclean to you; they shall not be eaten.


###### Deuteronomy 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:20)


כׇּל־ע֥וֹף טָה֖וֹר תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every ritually clean bird you may eat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But of all clean fowls you may eat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
All clean birds may you eat, [but not the unclean — a positive commandment superadded to the negative, in which instance the transgressor is liable for violation of both a positive and a negative commandment (see 6)].


###### Deuteronomy 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:21)


לֹ֣א תֹאכְל֣וּ כׇל־נְ֠בֵלָ֠ה לַגֵּ֨ר אֲשֶׁר־בִּשְׁעָרֶ֜יךָ תִּתְּנֶ֣נָּה וַאֲכָלָ֗הּ א֤וֹ מָכֹר֙ לְנׇכְרִ֔י כִּ֣י עַ֤ם קָדוֹשׁ֙ אַתָּ֔ה לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ לֹֽא־תְבַשֵּׁ֥ל גְּדִ֖י בַּחֲלֵ֥ב אִמּֽוֹ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not eat any carcass; to the alien in your cities give it and he will eat it or sell it to a non-Jew, for you are a people sanctified to Adonoy, your God; do not cook a kid in its mother’s milk.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You shall not eat of any thing that dies of itself: thou shalt give it to the stranger who is in thy gates, that he may eat it: or thou mayst sell it to an alien: for thou art a holy people to the Lord thy God. Thou shalt not boil a kid in its mother’s milk.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not eat any carcass. To the stranger in your gates, [a ger toshav (a "resident stranger"), one who has taken it upon himself not to serve idolatry, but who eats carcass] shall you give it, and he shall eat it; or sell it to the gentile. For you are a holy people to the L-rd your G-d [(Things which are permitted to you, and which others forbid to themselves, you are forbidden to permit in their presence.)] You shall not cook a kid in its mother's milk. [This is stated three times (here, Exodus 23:19, and 34:26), ("a kid"): excluding animals, birds, and unclean beasts)].


###### Deuteronomy 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:22)


עַשֵּׂ֣ר תְּעַשֵּׂ֔ר אֵ֖ת כׇּל־תְּבוּאַ֣ת זַרְעֶ֑ךָ הַיֹּצֵ֥א הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה שָׁנָ֥ה שָׁנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must surely tithe all the produce of your planting, that your field yields on a yearly basis.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt truly tithe all the increase of thy seed, that the field brings forth year by year.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Tithe shall you tithe all the produce of your seed which goes forth from the field year by year [(whence it is derived that tithe is not taken from the new crop for the old)] [Re the juxtaposition with verse 21: The Holy One Blessed be He says, as it were, to Israel: If you do not give the tithe as prescribed, when it is close to ripening, you shall compel Me to blast the "kids" (i.e., the kernels) of the grain while they are yet in their mother's womb (i.e., the husks)].


###### Deuteronomy 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:23)


וְאָכַלְתָּ֞ לִפְנֵ֣י**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ בַּמָּק֣וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַר֮ לְשַׁכֵּ֣ן שְׁמ֣וֹ שָׁם֒ מַעְשַׂ֤ר דְּגָֽנְךָ֙ תִּירֹשְׁךָ֣ וְיִצְהָרֶ֔ךָ וּבְכֹרֹ֥ת בְּקָרְךָ֖ וְצֹאנֶ֑ךָ לְמַ֣עַן תִּלְמַ֗ד לְיִרְאָ֛ה אֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall eat in the presence of Adonoy, your God, in the place He chooses to house His Presence there—the tithe of your grain, your wine, and your olive oil, and the firstborn of your cattle and flocks; in order that you learn to fear Adonoy, your God, all the years.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt eat before the Lord thy God, in the place which he shall choose to place his name there, the tithe of thy corn, of thy wine, and of thy oil, and the firstlings of thy herds, and of thy flocks; that thou mayst learn to fear the Lord thy God always.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall eat [the second tithe (ma'aser sheni)] before the L-rd your G-d, in the place that He shall choose to repose His name there, the tithe of your corn, your wine, and your oil, and the firstlings of your herd and your flock, so that you learn to fear the L-rd your G-d all of the days.


###### Deuteronomy 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:24)


וְכִֽי־יִרְבֶּ֨ה מִמְּךָ֜ הַדֶּ֗רֶךְ כִּ֣י לֹ֣א תוּכַל֮ שְׂאֵתוֹ֒ כִּֽי־יִרְחַ֤ק מִמְּךָ֙ הַמָּק֔וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִבְחַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לָשׂ֥וּם שְׁמ֖וֹ שָׁ֑ם כִּ֥י יְבָרֶכְךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If the journey will be beyond you; if you will be unable to carry it because the place is distant for you where Adonoy, your God, chooses to set His Presence there, for Adonoy, your God, has blessed you—


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the way be too long for thee, so that thou art not able to carry it; because the place is too far from thee, which the Lord thy God shall choose to set his name there, when the Lord thy God has blessed thee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the way be too long for you, so that you not be able to carry it, for the place be too far from you that the L-rd your G-d will choose to repose His name there, for the L-rd your G-d will bless you [that the produce be too much to carry] —


###### Deuteronomy 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:25)


וְנָתַתָּ֖ה בַּכָּ֑סֶף וְצַרְתָּ֤ הַכֶּ֙סֶף֙ בְּיָ֣דְךָ֔ וְהָֽלַכְתָּ֙ אֶל־הַמָּק֔וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִבְחַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
you will substitute coins. You will bundle the coins in your hand, and will go to the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then shalt thou turn it into money, and bind up the money in thy hand, and shalt go to the place which the Lord thy God shall choose:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall change it to money, and you shall bind the money in your hand, and you shall go to the place that the L-rd your G-d chooses.


###### Deuteronomy 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:26)


וְנָתַתָּ֣ה הַכֶּ֡סֶף בְּכֹל֩ אֲשֶׁר־תְּאַוֶּ֨ה נַפְשְׁךָ֜ בַּבָּקָ֣ר וּבַצֹּ֗אן וּבַיַּ֙יִן֙ וּבַשֵּׁכָ֔ר וּבְכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּֽשְׁאָלְךָ֖ נַפְשֶׁ֑ךָ וְאָכַ֣לְתָּ שָּׁ֗ם לִפְנֵי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וְשָׂמַחְתָּ֖ אַתָּ֥ה וּבֵיתֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will spend the money for anything you desire—for cattle, sheep, wine, intoxicating liquor—and for anything that you wish; and you will eat there in the presence of Adonoy, your God, and will rejoice, you and your household.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt bestow that money on all that thy heart desires, on oxen, or sheep, or wine, or strong drink, or whatever thy soul requires: and thou shalt eat there before the Lord thy God, and thou shalt rejoice, thou, and thy household,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall give the money for all that your soul desires ["general"], of herd, flock, wine, and strong drink ["particular"], and for all that your soul asks ["reversion to the general" — general-particular-general. The rule is in accordance with the particular, viz.: Just as the particular is: one fruit from another, originating from the earth (including animals, which derive their sustenance from the earth), so, everything of this nature (is purchasable with ma'aser money (Eruvin 27b)]. And you shall eat there before the L-rd your G-d, and you shall rejoice, you and your household.


###### Deuteronomy 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:27)


וְהַלֵּוִ֥י אֲשֶׁר־בִּשְׁעָרֶ֖יךָ לֹ֣א תַֽעַזְבֶ֑נּוּ כִּ֣י אֵ֥ין ל֛וֹ חֵ֥לֶק וְנַחֲלָ֖ה עִמָּֽךְ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the Levite who is in your city, you must not abandon; since he has no portion or inheritance with you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Levite who is within thy gates; thou shalt not forsake him; for he has no part or inheritance with thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levite who is in your gate, you shall not forsake him [by not giving him first tithe (ma'aser rishon)], for he does not have a portion and an inheritance with you [(This excludes from the tithe: leket, shikchah, peah (see Leviticus 19) and hefker (ownerless produce); for he has a portion in them just as you do.)]


###### Deuteronomy 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:28)


מִקְצֵ֣ה**׀** שָׁלֹ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֗ים תּוֹצִיא֙ אֶת־כׇּל־מַעְשַׂר֙ תְּבוּאָ֣תְךָ֔ בַּשָּׁנָ֖ה הַהִ֑וא וְהִנַּחְתָּ֖ בִּשְׁעָרֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
At the end of three years, separate all the tithes of your produce of that year and set them aside in your city.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At the end of three years thou shalt bring forth in that year, all the tithe of thy produce, and shalt lay it up within thy gates:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From the end of three years you shall remove all the tithe of your produce in that year, and you shall place it in your gates. [If one delayed giving his tithes of the first and second years of the shemitah period, he must remove them from the house in the third year.]


###### Deuteronomy 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/14:29)


וּבָ֣א הַלֵּוִ֡י כִּ֣י אֵֽין־לוֹ֩ חֵ֨לֶק וְנַחֲלָ֜ה עִמָּ֗ךְ וְ֠הַגֵּ֠ר וְהַיָּת֤וֹם וְהָֽאַלְמָנָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בִּשְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ וְאָכְל֖וּ וְשָׂבֵ֑עוּ לְמַ֤עַן יְבָרֶכְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בְּכׇל־מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יָדְךָ֖ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּעֲשֶֽׂה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The Levite shall come, for he has no portion or inheritance with you, and the proselyte and the orphan and the widow who are in your city, let them eat their fill; in order that Adonoy, your God, bless you in all the endeavors that you make.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Levite, (because he has no part or inheritance with thee,) and the stranger, and the fatherless, and the widow, who are within thy gates, shall come, and shall eat and be satisfied; that the Lord thy God may bless thee in all the work of thy hand which thou doest.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Levite shall come [and take ma'aser rishon], for he has no portion and inheritance with you, and the stranger and the orphan and the widow who are in your gates [They take the second tithe, which reverts to the poor in that year], and they shall eat and be sated [Give them as much as they need to be sated (and you go to Jerusalem with the tithe of the first and second years that you delayed and make confession: "I have removed the holy thing from the house" (Deuteronomy 26:13)], so that the L-rd your G-d bless you in all to which you put your hand.



## Chapter 15

###### Deuteronomy 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:1)


מִקֵּ֥ץ שֶֽׁבַע־שָׁנִ֖ים תַּעֲשֶׂ֥ה שְׁמִטָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
At the end of seven years, you are to make *shemitah.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At the end of every seven years thou shalt make a release.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
From the end of seven years [of the reckoning of the shemitah period], you shall make shemitah (a release).


###### Deuteronomy 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:2)


וְזֶה֮ דְּבַ֣ר הַשְּׁמִטָּה֒ שָׁמ֗וֹט כׇּל־בַּ֙עַל֙ מַשֵּׁ֣ה יָד֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַשֶּׁ֖ה בְּרֵעֵ֑הוּ לֹֽא־יִגֹּ֤שׂ אֶת־רֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ וְאֶת־אָחִ֔יו כִּֽי־קָרָ֥א שְׁמִטָּ֖ה לַיהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And this is the statement of *shemitah:* suspend every creditor’s hand from his loan to his neighbor; he will not claim [it] from his neighbor or his brother because it was proclaimed *shemitah* to Adonoy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this is the manner of the release: every creditor that lends anything to his neighbour shall release it: he shall not exact it of his neighbour, or of his brother; because he has proclaimed a release to the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is the word of the shemitah: Every creditor shall release his claim against his neighbor. He shall not exact it of his neighbor and of his brother because it has been declared shemitah to the L-rd.


###### Deuteronomy 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:3)


אֶת־הַנׇּכְרִ֖י תִּגֹּ֑שׂ וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר יִהְיֶ֥ה לְךָ֛ אֶת־אָחִ֖יךָ תַּשְׁמֵ֥ט יָדֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You should demand payment from the stranger, but what you have with your brother your hand will relinquish.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Of a foreigner thou mayst exact it again: but that which is thine with thy brother thy hand shall release.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Of the gentile shall you exact [(This is a positive commandment)], but what you have with your brother your hand shall release.


###### Deuteronomy 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:4)


אֶ֕פֶס כִּ֛י לֹ֥א יִֽהְיֶה־בְּךָ֖ אֶבְי֑וֹן כִּֽי־בָרֵ֤ךְ יְבָֽרֶכְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה בָּאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹֽתֵן־לְךָ֥ נַחֲלָ֖ה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
End it [poverty], so that there will be no one, destitute among you when Adonoy will surely bless you in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But there shall be practically no poor among you; for the Lord shall greatly bless thee in the land which the Lord thy God gives thee for an inheritance to possess it:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But there shall not be in you a pauper, [lacking everything. (If you do the will of the L-rd, the paupers shall be in others but not in you; and if not, they shall be in you)]. For the L-rd will bless you in the land which the L-rd your G-d gives you as an inheritance to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:5)


רַ֚ק אִם־שָׁמ֣וֹעַ תִּשְׁמַ֔ע בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ לִשְׁמֹ֤ר לַעֲשׂוֹת֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הַמִּצְוָ֣ה הַזֹּ֔את אֲשֶׁ֛ר אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Only if you will listen to the voice of Adonoy, your God, to guard to fulfill this entire *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
only if thou carefully hearken to the voice of the Lord thy God, to observe to do all these commandments which I command thee this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[But there shall be no paupers in you] only if you hearken, hearken [(If you hearken somewhat, you will be enabled to hearken greatly)] to the voice of the L-rd your G-d, to observe to do this entire mitzvah that I command you this day.


###### Deuteronomy 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:6)


כִּֽי־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ בֵּֽרַכְךָ֔ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּר־לָ֑ךְ וְהַֽעֲבַטְתָּ֞ גּוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֗ים וְאַתָּה֙ לֹ֣א תַעֲבֹ֔ט וּמָֽשַׁלְתָּ֙ בְּגוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֔ים וּבְךָ֖ לֹ֥א יִמְשֹֽׁלוּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy, your God, blesses you just as He told you, you will grant loans to nations but you will not borrow; and you will rule over many nations, but over you they will not rule.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord thy God blesses thee, as he promised thee: and thou shalt lend to many nations, but thou shalt not borrow; and thou shalt reign over many nations, but they shall not reign over thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the L-rd your G-d has blessed you, as He spoke to you [viz. 28:3-12]. And you will lend to many nations, and you will not borrow [from one nation to pay the other], and you will rule many nations, and they [other nations] will not rule you.


###### Deuteronomy 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:7)


כִּֽי־יִהְיֶה֩ בְךָ֨ אֶבְי֜וֹן מֵאַחַ֤ד אַחֶ֙יךָ֙ בְּאַחַ֣ד שְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ בְּאַ֨רְצְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֣ן לָ֑ךְ לֹ֧א תְאַמֵּ֣ץ אֶת־לְבָבְךָ֗ וְלֹ֤א תִקְפֹּץ֙ אֶת־יָ֣דְךָ֔ מֵאָחִ֖יךָ הָאֶבְיֽוֹן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If there should be someone destitute in your midst among one of your brothers in one of your cities, in your land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; do not harden your heart and do not close your hand against your destitute brother.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If there be among you a poor man, one of thy brethren within any of thy gates in thy land which the Lord thy God gives thee, thou shalt not harden thy heart, nor shut thy hand from thy poor brother:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there be in you a pauper [(the neediest takes precedence)], of one of your brothers [(Your paternal brother takes precedence to your maternal brother)], in one of your gates [(The poor of your city take precedence to those of another)], in your land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you, do not harden your heart [in irresolution], and do not close your hand [after stretching it forth], to your brother the pauper [(If you do not give, in the end you will be a brother to the pauper)].


###### Deuteronomy 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:8)


כִּֽי־פָתֹ֧חַ תִּפְתַּ֛ח אֶת־יָדְךָ֖ ל֑וֹ וְהַעֲבֵט֙ תַּעֲבִיטֶ֔נּוּ דֵּ֚י מַחְסֹר֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יֶחְסַ֖ר לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather, you must surely open your hand generously to him, and you must surely extend a loan to him to cover the wants which he lacks.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but thou shalt open thy hand wide to
				him, and shalt surely lend him sufficient for his need, in that which he lacks.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But open shall you open your hand to him [even many times], and lend shall you lend him [(If he refuses a gift, give it to him as a loan)] what suffices for his needs [(but you are not required to enrich him)] that are lacking [(even a horse to ride upon and a servant to run before him)] unto him [(even) a wife].


###### Deuteronomy 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:9)


הִשָּׁ֣מֶר לְךָ֡ פֶּן־יִהְיֶ֣ה דָבָר֩ עִם־לְבָבְךָ֨ בְלִיַּ֜עַל לֵאמֹ֗ר קָֽרְבָ֣ה שְׁנַֽת־הַשֶּׁ֘בַע֮ שְׁנַ֣ת הַשְּׁמִטָּה֒ וְרָעָ֣ה עֵֽינְךָ֗ בְּאָחִ֙יךָ֙ הָֽאֶבְי֔וֹן וְלֹ֥א תִתֵּ֖ן ל֑וֹ וְקָרָ֤א עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְהָיָ֥ה בְךָ֖ חֵֽטְא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Look out for yourself lest there be an evil thought in your mind, saying, ‘‘The seventh year is approaching, the *shemitah* year,’’ and you will look askance at your destitute brother and will not give him; and if he cries out concerning you to Adonoy, you will be regarded as sinful.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Beware that there be not an unworthy thought in thy heart, saying, The seventh year, the year of release, is at hand; and thy eye be evil against thy poor brother, and thou give him nothing; and he cry to the Lord against thee; for it shall be reckoned to you as sin.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take heed unto yourself lest there be in your heart a thing of wantonness, to say: The seventh year has drawn near, the year of shemitah, and your eye be evil against your brother the pauper, and you not give him, and he cry out against you to the L-rd [(G-d makes haste to exact payment when he "cries out")], there will be in you a sin [(even if he does not cry out)].


###### Deuteronomy 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:10)


נָת֤וֹן תִּתֵּן֙ ל֔וֹ וְלֹא־יֵרַ֥ע לְבָבְךָ֖ בְּתִתְּךָ֣ ל֑וֹ כִּ֞י בִּגְלַ֣ל**׀** הַדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֗ה יְבָרֶכְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בְּכׇֽל־מַעֲשֶׂ֔ךָ וּבְכֹ֖ל מִשְׁלַ֥ח יָדֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must surely give him, and let your heart not hurt when you give him; for, as a consequence of this thing, Adonoy, your God, will bless you in all your work and in all your commerce.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt surely give him, and thy heart shall not be grieved when thou givest to him: because for this thing the Lord thy God shall bless thee in all thy works, and in all that to which thou puttest thy hand.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Give shall you give [(even a hundred times)] to him [privately], and let your heart not take it ill in giving to him, for because of this word [(Even if you say that you will give, you receive reward for saying along with that for giving)], the L-rd your G-d will bless you in all your deeds and in all to which you put your hand.


###### Deuteronomy 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:11)


כִּ֛י לֹא־יֶחְדַּ֥ל אֶבְי֖וֹן מִקֶּ֣רֶב הָאָ֑רֶץ עַל־כֵּ֞ן אָנֹכִ֤י מְצַוְּךָ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר פָּ֠תֹ֠חַ תִּפְתַּ֨ח אֶת־יָדְךָ֜ לְאָחִ֧יךָ לַעֲנִיֶּ֛ךָ וּלְאֶבְיֹנְךָ֖ בְּאַרְצֶֽךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For the destitute will not cease to exist within the land; therefore I am commanding you saying, ‘‘Open your hand generously to your brother, to your indigent, and to your destitute in your land.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the poor shall never cease out of the land: therefore I command thee, saying, Thou shalt open thy hand wide to thy brother, to thy poor, and to thy needy, in thy land.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a pauper will not cease from the midst of the land, wherefore I charge you [in good counsel, for your benefit]: Open shall you open your hand to your brother — your poor one and your pauper in your land.


###### Deuteronomy 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:12)


כִּֽי־יִמָּכֵ֨ר לְךָ֜ אָחִ֣יךָ הָֽעִבְרִ֗י א֚וֹ הָֽעִבְרִיָּ֔ה וַעֲבָֽדְךָ֖ שֵׁ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֑ים וּבַשָּׁנָה֙ הַשְּׁבִיעִ֔ת תְּשַׁלְּחֶ֥נּוּ חׇפְשִׁ֖י מֵעִמָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If your brother—Jew or Jewess—is sold to you and he serves you six years; then in the seventh year you are to send him free from you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thy brother, a Hebrew man, or a Hebrew woman, be sold to thee, he shall serve thee six years; and in the seventh year thou shalt let him go free from thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there be sold to you [(by beth-din, for his theft)], your brother, the Hebrew man, or [(if there be sold to you by her father, when she is a minor)] the Hebrew woman, then he shall serve you for six years; and on the seventh year you shall send him free from you [(and if the woman's six years expire before she shows pubertal signs, she leaves at that time)].


###### Deuteronomy 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:13)


וְכִֽי־תְשַׁלְּחֶ֥נּוּ חׇפְשִׁ֖י מֵֽעִמָּ֑ךְ לֹ֥א תְשַׁלְּחֶ֖נּוּ רֵיקָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you send him free from you, do not send him away empty-handed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when thou sendest him out free from thee, thou shalt not let him go away empty:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you send him free from you, do not send him empty-handed.


###### Deuteronomy 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:14)


הַעֲנֵ֤יק תַּעֲנִיק֙ ל֔וֹ מִצֹּ֣אנְךָ֔ וּמִֽגׇּרְנְךָ֖ וּמִיִּקְבֶ֑ךָ אֲשֶׁ֧ר בֵּרַכְךָ֛ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ תִּתֶּן־לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You must surely present him with a severance bonus from your flocks, and from your threshing area, and your wine cellar, whatever Adonoy, your God, has blessed you with are you to give him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou shalt furnish him liberally out of thy flock, and out of thy threshing floor, and out of thy winepress: of that with which the Lord thy God has blessed thee thou shalt give him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Bestow shall you bestow upon him [so that your "bestowal" is clearly visible], of your flock, of your threshing floor, and of your winepress — [Of all] that the L-rd your G-d has blessed you with shall you give him [Just as these ("flock, etc.") are characterized by "blessing" (i.e., fruitfulness), so (bestow upon him) all that are thus characterized — excluding mules (which are sterile)].


###### Deuteronomy 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:15)


וְזָכַרְתָּ֗ כִּ֣י עֶ֤בֶד הָיִ֙יתָ֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם וַֽיִּפְדְּךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ עַל־כֵּ֞ן אָנֹכִ֧י מְצַוְּךָ֛ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּ֖ה הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt and Adonoy, your God, redeemed you; that is why I am commanding you this matter today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt remember that thou wast a bondman in the land of Miżrayim and the Lord thy God redeemed thee: therefore I command thee this thing today.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt, and the L-rd your G-d redeemed you [and "bestowed" upon you, twice — from the spoils of Egypt and from the spoils of the sea]. Therefore, I command you in respect to this thing [(to bestow, likewise,)] this day.


###### Deuteronomy 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:16)


וְהָיָה֙ כִּֽי־יֹאמַ֣ר אֵלֶ֔יךָ לֹ֥א אֵצֵ֖א מֵעִמָּ֑ךְ כִּ֤י אֲהֵֽבְךָ֙ וְאֶת־בֵּיתֶ֔ךָ כִּי־ט֥וֹב ל֖וֹ עִמָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Should he say to you, ‘‘I am not leaving you;’’ because he loves you and your household, for he has it good with you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, if he say to thee, I will not go away from thee; because he loves thee and thy house, because he is happy with thee;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, if he say to you: I shall not leave you, because he loves you and your household, because it is good for him with you.


###### Deuteronomy 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:17)


וְלָקַחְתָּ֣ אֶת־הַמַּרְצֵ֗עַ וְנָתַתָּ֤ה בְאׇזְנוֹ֙ וּבַדֶּ֔לֶת וְהָיָ֥ה לְךָ֖ עֶ֣בֶד עוֹלָ֑ם וְאַ֥ף לַאֲמָתְךָ֖ תַּעֲשֶׂה־כֵּֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Take the awl and put it through his ear and the door, and he will become your perpetual slave. And do the same to your maid-servant as well.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then thou shalt take an awl, and thrust it through his ear to the door, and he shall be thy servant for ever. And also to thy maidservant thou shalt do likewise.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall take the awl and place it in his ear and in the door, and he shall be unto you a servant forever [i.e., until the Jubilee year]. And to your maid-servant, too, shall you do this [i.e., you shall "bestow" unto her (but she cannot become a servant forever)].


###### Deuteronomy 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:18)


לֹא־יִקְשֶׁ֣ה בְעֵינֶ֗ךָ בְּשַׁלֵּֽחֲךָ֨ אֹת֤וֹ חׇפְשִׁי֙ מֵֽעִמָּ֔ךְ כִּ֗י מִשְׁנֶה֙ שְׂכַ֣ר שָׂכִ֔יר עֲבָֽדְךָ֖ שֵׁ֣שׁ שָׁנִ֑ים וּבֵֽרַכְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בְּכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּעֲשֶֽׂה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Let it not seem difficult to you when you send him free from you, for double the profit produced by the wage earner has he worked for you six years; and Adonoy, your God, will bless you in everything you do.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It shall not seem hard to thee, when thou sendest him away free from thee; for he has been worth double a hired servant to thee, in serving thee six years: and the Lord thy God shall bless thee in all that thou doest.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let it not be difficult in your eyes when you send him away free from you, for double the hire of a hired man [day and night] has he served you for six years [(Aside from his day labor, his master gives him a Canaanite maid-servant to bear slaves for him)]. And the L-rd your G-d will bless you in all that you do.


###### Deuteronomy 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:19)


כׇּֽל־הַבְּכ֡וֹר אֲשֶׁר֩ יִוָּלֵ֨ד בִּבְקָרְךָ֤ וּבְצֹֽאנְךָ֙ הַזָּכָ֔ר תַּקְדִּ֖ישׁ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ לֹ֤א תַעֲבֹד֙ בִּבְכֹ֣ר שׁוֹרֶ֔ךָ וְלֹ֥א תָגֹ֖ז בְּכ֥וֹר צֹאנֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Every firstborn that is born in your cattle, and in your flocks—a male—you must consecrate to Adonoy, your God; you may not work with your first-born ox, or shear the first-born of your flocks.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
All the firstling males that come of thy herd and of thy flock thou shalt sanctify to the Lord thy God: thou shalt do no work with the firstling of thy bullock, nor shear the firstling of thy sheep.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Every firstling which is born in your herd and in your flock, the male, you shall consecrate to the L-rd your G-d [It is a mitzvah to say: "You are sanctified for the bechorah" (the mitzvah of the first-born)]. You shall not work with the firstling of your bullock and you shall not shear the firstling of your flock [(The converse is also forbidden, but the common instance is given)].


###### Deuteronomy 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:20)


לִפְנֵי֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֤יךָ תֹאכְלֶ֙נּוּ֙ שָׁנָ֣ה בְשָׁנָ֔ה בַּמָּק֖וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה אַתָּ֖ה וּבֵיתֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Before Adonoy, your God, you must eat it, each year, in the place that Adonoy chooses, you and your household.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt eat it before the Lord thy God year by year in the place which the Lord shall choose, thou and thy household.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Before the L-rd your G-d shall you [the Cohein] eat it, year in year [It is a mitzvah to sacrifice and eat it in its first year. Also, if it were slaughtered at the end of the year, it may be eaten that day and one day of the next (whence it is derived that it may be eaten for two days and one night)], in the place that the L-rd shall choose, you and your household.


###### Deuteronomy 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:21)


וְכִֽי־יִהְיֶ֨ה ב֜וֹ מ֗וּם פִּסֵּ֙חַ֙ א֣וֹ עִוֵּ֔ר כֹּ֖ל מ֣וּם רָ֑ע לֹ֣א תִזְבָּחֶ֔נּוּ לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And if it has a blemish—if it is crippled or blind—or has any severe blemish, do not slaughter it to Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if there be any blemish in it, as if it be lame, or blind, or have any ill blemish, thou shalt not sacrifice it to the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if there be in it a blemish ["general"], lame or blind ["particular"], any sore blemish ["reversion to the general" — Just as the particular specifies a visible blemish which never passes, so (all animals with) visible blemishes that never pass (are unfit for sacrifice)], you shall not sacrifice it to the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 15:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:22)


בִּשְׁעָרֶ֖יךָ תֹּאכְלֶ֑נּוּ הַטָּמֵ֤א וְהַטָּהוֹר֙ יַחְדָּ֔ו כַּצְּבִ֖י וְכָאַיָּֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In your cities may you eat it; the ritually unclean and the clean together, like the deer and the gazelle.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt eat it within thy gates: the unclean and the clean person alike shall eat it, as the gazelle and the deer.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In your gates may you eat it, the unclean and the clean together, as the deer and as the hart.


###### Deuteronomy 15:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/15:23)


רַ֥ק אֶת־דָּמ֖וֹ לֹ֣א תֹאכֵ֑ל עַל־הָאָ֥רֶץ תִּשְׁפְּכֶ֖נּוּ כַּמָּֽיִם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Only, do not eat its blood; spill it like water on the ground.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only thou shalt not eat its blood; thou shalt pour it upon the ground like water.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But its blood you shall not eat [(Even though it was originally forbidden and is now permitted, its blood remains forbidden)]; on the earth shall you spill it as water.



## Chapter 16

###### Deuteronomy 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:1)


שָׁמוֹר֙ אֶת־חֹ֣דֶשׁ הָאָבִ֔יב וְעָשִׂ֣יתָ פֶּ֔סַח לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ כִּ֞י בְּחֹ֣דֶשׁ הָֽאָבִ֗יב הוֹצִ֨יאֲךָ֜ יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ מִמִּצְרַ֖יִם לָֽיְלָה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Take heed of the month of spring, when you will celebrate *Pesach* for Adonoy your God; for in the month of spring Adonoy, your God, took you out from Egypt at night.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Observe the month of Aviv, and keep the passover to the Lord thy God: for in the month of Aviv the Lord thy God brought thee forth out of Miżrayim by night.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Observe the month of aviv [(Before its coming, observe that it be fit for aviv (spring), so that the omer meal-offering may be brought upon it; and if not, intercalate the year)], and make Pesach to the L-rd your G-d. For in the month of aviv the L-rd your G-d took you out of Egypt, [Pharaoh having acceded] at night [to your going].


###### Deuteronomy 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:2)


וְזָבַ֥חְתָּ פֶּ֛סַח לַיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ צֹ֣אן וּבָקָ֑ר בַּמָּקוֹם֙ אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה לְשַׁכֵּ֥ן שְׁמ֖וֹ שָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall slaughter the *pesach*-offering to Adonoy, your God, flocks of ruminants and cattle in the place that Adonoy chooses to house His Presence there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt therefore sacrifice the passover to the Lord thy God, of the flock and the herd, in the place which
				the Lord shall choose to place his name there.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall sacrifice a Pesach offering to the L-rd your G-d, sheep [viz. (Exodus 12:5)] and cattle [shall you slaughter as a festival offering (chagigah), so that if a very large company is numbered over the (eating of the) pesach, a chagigah is brought so that it (the pesach) may be eaten in satiety)], in the place where the L-rd shall choose to repose His name.


###### Deuteronomy 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:3)


לֹא־תֹאכַ֤ל עָלָיו֙ חָמֵ֔ץ שִׁבְעַ֥ת יָמִ֛ים תֹּֽאכַל־עָלָ֥יו מַצּ֖וֹת לֶ֣חֶם עֹ֑נִי כִּ֣י בְחִפָּז֗וֹן יָצָ֙אתָ֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם לְמַ֣עַן תִּזְכֹּ֗ר אֶת־י֤וֹם צֵֽאתְךָ֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם כֹּ֖ל יְמֵ֥י חַיֶּֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not eat *chometz* on it; seven days are you to eat on it *matzos,* bread of anguish; since in haste you left the land of Egypt, so that you remember the day of your exodus from the land of Egypt all the days of your life.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt eat no leavened bread with it; seven days shalt thou eat unleavened bread with it, the bread of affliction; for thou camest forth out of the land of Miżrayim in haste: that thou mayst remember the day when thou camest out of the land of Miżrayim all the days of thy life.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not eat upon it chametz; seven days shall you eat upon it matzoth, the bread [which reminds you] of [the] affliction [that you underwent in Egypt], for in haste [the haste of the Egyptians] did you leave the land of Egypt, [and the dough had not time to leaven, and this (the matzoh) will serve as a reminder], so that you remember [by eating the pesach and the matzoh] the day of your leaving the land of Egypt all the days of your life.


###### Deuteronomy 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:4)


וְלֹֽא־יֵרָאֶ֨ה לְךָ֥ שְׂאֹ֛ר בְּכׇל־גְּבֻלְךָ֖ שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֑ים וְלֹא־יָלִ֣ין מִן־הַבָּשָׂ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר תִּזְבַּ֥ח בָּעֶ֛רֶב בַּיּ֥וֹם הָרִאשׁ֖וֹן לַבֹּֽקֶר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And no sourdough of yours may be seen in all of your boundary seven days; and none of the flesh may remain overnight which you slaughtered towards the evening of the first day—until morning.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there shall be no leaven seen with thee in all thy border for seven days; nor shall any thing of the meat, which thou dost sacrifice on the first day at evening, remain all night until the morning.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there shall not be seen unto you leaven in all of your border for seven days, and there shall not remain of the flesh [of the Pesach offering] that you sacrifice towards evening on the first day [the fourteenth of Nissan] until the morning.


###### Deuteronomy 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:5)


לֹ֥א תוּכַ֖ל לִזְבֹּ֣חַ אֶת־הַפָּ֑סַח בְּאַחַ֣ד שְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are forbidden to slaughter the *pesach* in any of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou mayst not sacrifice the passover within any of thy gates, which the Lord thy God gives thee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You may not sacrifice the Pesach offering in one of your gates that the L-rd your G-d gives to you.


###### Deuteronomy 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:6)


כִּ֠י אִֽם־אֶל־הַמָּק֞וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ לְשַׁכֵּ֣ן שְׁמ֔וֹ שָׁ֛ם תִּזְבַּ֥ח אֶת־הַפֶּ֖סַח בָּעָ֑רֶב כְּב֣וֹא הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ מוֹעֵ֖ד צֵֽאתְךָ֥ מִמִּצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Solely in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses to house His Presence, there will you slaughter the *pesach* towards the afternoon, at sunset, at the time you left Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but only at the place which the Lord thy God shall choose to place his name in, there thou shalt sacrifice the passover at evening, at the going down of the sun, at the season when thou camest out of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But in the place where the L-rd your G-d chooses to repose His name, there shall you sacrifice the Pesach offering in the evening [from the sixth hour on]; as the sun sets [shall you eat it, and] at the time of your going out of Egypt (i.e., in the morning) [if there is any flesh left over, you shall burn it (i.e., take it to the burning site for burning after the festival)].


###### Deuteronomy 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:7)


וּבִשַּׁלְתָּ֙ וְאָ֣כַלְתָּ֔ בַּמָּק֕וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִבְחַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בּ֑וֹ וּפָנִ֣יתָ בַבֹּ֔קֶר וְהָלַכְתָּ֖ לְאֹהָלֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall cook [it] and eat [it] in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses; and you may depart in the morning and go to your residence.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt roast and eat it in the place which the Lord thy God shall choose: and thou shalt turn in the morning, and go to thy tents.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall cook [i.e., roast it] and eat it in the place that the L-rd your G-d has chosen, and you shall betake yourselves in the morning [of the second day (whence it is seen that they must spend the night there)], and you shall go to your tents.


###### Deuteronomy 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:8)


שֵׁ֥שֶׁת יָמִ֖ים תֹּאכַ֣ל מַצּ֑וֹת וּבַיּ֣וֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִ֗י עֲצֶ֙רֶת֙ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לֹ֥א תַעֲשֶׂ֖ה מְלָאכָֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For six days you shall eat *matzos,* and on the seventh day, is one of withdrawal for the sake of Adonoy, your God, do not do work.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Six days thou shalt eat unleavened bread, and on the seventh day shall be a solemn assembly to the Lord thy God: thou shalt do no work.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Six days shall you eat matzoth [i.e., You may eat matzoth of the new grain (after the omer offering on the second day) and you may eat it seven days from the old grain], and on the seventh day is a holding back [from labor] for the L-rd your G-d; you shall do no work.


###### Deuteronomy 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:9)


שִׁבְעָ֥ה שָׁבֻעֹ֖ת תִּסְפׇּר־לָ֑ךְ מֵהָחֵ֤ל חֶרְמֵשׁ֙ בַּקָּמָ֔ה תָּחֵ֣ל לִסְפֹּ֔ר שִׁבְעָ֖ה שָׁבֻעֽוֹת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Seven weeks count for yourself; from the time the sickle begins felling the standing grain, begin to count seven weeks.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seven weeks shalt thou number to thee: from such time as thou beginnest to put the sickle to the corn shalt thou commence to number seven weeks.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Seven weeks shall you count for yourself; from the beginning of the sickle in the standing corn [i.e., from the cutting of the omer, which is the beginning of the harvest] shall you begin to count seven weeks.


###### Deuteronomy 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:10)


וְעָשִׂ֜יתָ חַ֤ג שָׁבֻעוֹת֙ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ מִסַּ֛ת נִדְבַ֥ת יָדְךָ֖ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּתֵּ֑ן כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר יְבָרֶכְךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to celebrate the festival of *Shovuos* for Adonoy, your God, to the fullness of your open-handed gift that you can give, as Adonoy, your God, has blessed you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt keep the feast of weeks to the Lord thy God with a tribute of a freewill offering of thy hand, which thou shalt give, according as the Lord thy God has blessed thee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall make a festival of weeks (shavuoth) to the L-rd your G-d, the sufficiency of the offering of your hand, which you shall give as the L-rd your G-d shall bless you [i.e., In accordance with the amplitude of His blessing, bring peace-offerings of joy and invite guests for the repast.]


###### Deuteronomy 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:11)


וְשָׂמַחְתָּ֞ לִפְנֵ֣י**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ אַתָּ֨ה וּבִנְךָ֣ וּבִתֶּ֘ךָ֮ וְעַבְדְּךָ֣ וַאֲמָתֶ֒ךָ֒ וְהַלֵּוִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר בִּשְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ וְהַגֵּ֛ר וְהַיָּת֥וֹם וְהָאַלְמָנָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּקִרְבֶּ֑ךָ בַּמָּק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִבְחַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לְשַׁכֵּ֥ן שְׁמ֖וֹ שָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to rejoice in the presence of Adonoy, your God—you, and your son and your daughter, and your male slave and your female slave, and the Levite who is in your city, and the proselyte, and the orphan and the widow who are among you—in the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses to house His Presence there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt rejoice before the Lord thy God, thou, and thy son, and thy daughter, and thy manservant, and thy maidservant, and the Levite who is within thy gates, and the stranger, and the fatherless, and the widow, that are among you, in the place which the Lord thy God has chosen as the residence of his name.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall rejoice before the L-rd your G-d: you — your son, your daughter, your man-servant, your maid-servant — the Levite that is in your gates, the stranger, the orphan, and the widow who are in your midst [(If you cause My four to rejoice, I shall cause your four to rejoice)], in the place where the L-rd your G-d shall choose to repose His name.


###### Deuteronomy 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:12)


וְזָ֣כַרְתָּ֔ כִּי־עֶ֥בֶד הָיִ֖יתָ בְּמִצְרָ֑יִם וְשָׁמַרְתָּ֣ וְעָשִׂ֔יתָ אֶת־הַֽחֻקִּ֖ים הָאֵֽלֶּה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Remember that you were a slave in Egypt; you are to guard and celebrate these statutes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt remember that thou wast a bondman in Miżrayim: therefore thou shalt observe and do these statutes.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall remember that you were a slave in Egypt, and you shall keep and observe these statutes [i.e., it is to this end that I redeemed you].


###### Deuteronomy 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:13)


חַ֧ג הַסֻּכֹּ֛ת תַּעֲשֶׂ֥ה לְךָ֖ שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֑ים בְּאׇ֨סְפְּךָ֔ מִֽגׇּרְנְךָ֖ וּמִיִּקְבֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The festival of *Sukkos* celebrate for yourself seven days, when you harvest your threshing-floor and your wine-press.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt observe the feast of booths seven days, after thou hast gathered in thy corn and thy wine:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The festival of succoth (booths) shall you make for yourself, seven days, when [i.e., at the time that] you gather in [to your house the summer fruits] from your threshing floor and from your winepress.


###### Deuteronomy 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:14)


וְשָׂמַחְתָּ֖ בְּחַגֶּ֑ךָ אַתָּ֨ה וּבִנְךָ֤ וּבִתֶּ֙ךָ֙ וְעַבְדְּךָ֣ וַאֲמָתֶ֔ךָ וְהַלֵּוִ֗י וְהַגֵּ֛ר וְהַיָּת֥וֹם וְהָאַלְמָנָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר בִּשְׁעָרֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to rejoice during your festival—you and your son and your daughter, and your male slave and your female slave, and the Levite and the proselyte, and the orphan and the widow who are in your city.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt rejoice in thy feast, thou, and thy son, and thy daughter, and thy manservant, and thy maidservant, and the Levite, the stranger, and the fatherless, and the widow, that are within thy gates.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall rejoice in your festival: you — your son, your daughter, your man-servant, your maid-servant — the Levite, the stranger, the orphan, and the widow, who are in your gates.


###### Deuteronomy 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:15)


שִׁבְעַ֣ת יָמִ֗ים תָּחֹג֙ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בַּמָּק֖וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה כִּ֣י יְבָרֶכְךָ֞ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ בְּכֹ֤ל תְּבוּאָֽתְךָ֙ וּבְכֹל֙ מַעֲשֵׂ֣ה יָדֶ֔יךָ וְהָיִ֖יתָ אַ֥ךְ שָׂמֵֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Seven days are you to be festive for Adonoy, your God, in the place Adonoy chooses, for Adonoy, your God, will bless you in all your produce and in all your endeavors; and you shall experience pure joy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Seven days shalt thou keep a solemn feast to the Lord thy God in the place which the Lord shall choose: because the Lord thy God shall bless thee in all thy produce, and in all the work of thy hands, therefore thou shalt surely rejoice.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Seven days shall you rejoice before the L-rd your G-d in the place that the L-rd chooses. For the L-rd your G-d will bless you in all of your produce and in all to which you put your hand, and you will be only happy.


###### Deuteronomy 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:16)


שָׁל֣וֹשׁ פְּעָמִ֣ים׀בַּשָּׁנָ֡ה יֵרָאֶ֨ה כׇל־זְכוּרְךָ֜ אֶת־פְּנֵ֣י**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ בַּמָּקוֹם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִבְחָ֔ר בְּחַ֧ג הַמַּצּ֛וֹת וּבְחַ֥ג הַשָּׁבֻע֖וֹת וּבְחַ֣ג הַסֻּכּ֑וֹת וְלֹ֧א יֵרָאֶ֛ה אֶת־פְּנֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה רֵיקָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Three times a year are all your males to be seen in the presence of Adonoy, your God, in the place that He chooses—in the festival of Matzos, and on the festival of Shovuos, and on the festival of Sukkos—and he shall not appear in the presence of Adonoy empty-handed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Three times a year shall all thy males appear before the Lord thy God in the place which he shall choose; in the feast of unleavened bread, and in the feast of weeks, and in the feast of booths: and they shall not appear before the Lord empty:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Three times a year shall appear each of your males in the presence of the L-rd your G-d in the place that He chooses: on the festival of matzoth, and on the festival of Shavuoth, and on the festival of Succoth. And he shall not appear in the presence of the L-rd empty-handed, [but he shall bring burnt-offerings of "appearance" and the peace-offerings of the festival].


###### Deuteronomy 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:17)


אִ֖ישׁ כְּמַתְּנַ֣ת יָד֑וֹ כְּבִרְכַּ֛ת יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָֽתַן־לָֽךְ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Everyone according to the gift appropriate to his means, according to the blessing of Adonoy, your God, that He gave you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
every man shall give as he is able, according to the blessing of
				the Lord thy God which he has given thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Each according to the gift of his hand [(If one has much food and much property, he brings many burnt-offerings and peace-offerings)], according to the blessing of the L-rd your G-d that He has given you.


###### Deuteronomy 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:18)


שֹׁפְטִ֣ים וְשֹֽׁטְרִ֗ים תִּֽתֶּן־לְךָ֙ בְּכׇל־שְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ לִשְׁבָטֶ֑יךָ וְשָׁפְט֥וּ אֶת־הָעָ֖ם מִשְׁפַּט־צֶֽדֶק׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Judges and police officers you shall appoint for yourself in all of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you for your tribes; who will judge the people righteous justice.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Judges and officers shalt thou make thee in all thy gates, which the Lord thy God gives thee, throughout thy tribes: and they shall judge the people with righteous judgment.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Judges and officers, [who enforce the rulings of the judges with "rod and strap"] shall you appoint for yourselves in all of your gates [i.e., in every city] that the L-rd your G-d gives you for your tribes [(Judges are appointed for each tribe and in each city)], and they shall judge the people a righteous judgment [i.e., Appoint expert, righteous judges, who will judge righteously.]


###### Deuteronomy 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:19)


לֹא־תַטֶּ֣ה מִשְׁפָּ֔ט לֹ֥א תַכִּ֖יר פָּנִ֑ים וְלֹא־תִקַּ֣ח שֹׁ֔חַד כִּ֣י הַשֹּׁ֗חַד יְעַוֵּר֙ עֵינֵ֣י חֲכָמִ֔ים וִֽיסַלֵּ֖ף דִּבְרֵ֥י צַדִּיקִֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not pervert justice; do not display favoritism; and do not accept bribery, for bribery blinds the eyes of the wise and distorts words that are just.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not wrest judgment; thou shalt not respect persons, neither take a bribe: for a bribe blinds the eyes of the wise, and perverts the words of the righteous.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not pervert judgment, and you shall not play favorites, [even during the pleading — an exhortation to the judge not to be firm with one and lenient with the other, e.g., making one stand and allowing the other to sit], and you shall not take a bribe [even to judge righteously]; for a bribe blinds the eyes of the sages [(Once the judge takes a bribe from someone it is impossible for him not to seek arguments in his favor)], and it distorts righteous words [i.e., true judgments].


###### Deuteronomy 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:20)


צֶ֥דֶק צֶ֖דֶק תִּרְדֹּ֑ף לְמַ֤עַן תִּֽחְיֶה֙ וְיָרַשְׁתָּ֣ אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽךְ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Pursue absolute justice so that you may live and inherit the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Justice, only justice shalt thou pursue, that thou mayst live, and inherit the land which the Lord thy God gives thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Righteousness, righteousness shall you pursue [Seek out the finest beth-din], so that you live and inherit the land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you. [The appointment of fit judges serves to sustain Israel and to settle them in their land.]


###### Deuteronomy 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:21)


לֹֽא־תִטַּ֥ע לְךָ֛ אֲשֵׁרָ֖ה כׇּל־עֵ֑ץ אֵ֗צֶל מִזְבַּ֛ח יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּעֲשֶׂה־לָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not plant an Asherah for yourself [or] any tree near the altar of Adonoy, your God, that you will make for yourself.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not plant thee an ashera of any tree near the altar of the Lord thy God, which thou shalt make thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not plant for yourself an asheirah (a tree devoted to idolatry) [(One is liable from the time of planting)], any tree near the altar of the L-rd your G-d that you shall make for yourself [(an exhortation against planting a tree or building a house on the Temple Mount)].


###### Deuteronomy 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/16:22)


וְלֹֽא־תָקִ֥ים לְךָ֖ מַצֵּבָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׂנֵ֖א יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And do not erect for yourself a monument that Adonoy, your God, detests.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shalt thou set thee up any pillar; which the Lord thy God hates.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not raise up for yourself a monument [one stone, on which to sacrifice (even to the L-rd)], which the L-rd your G-d hates, [it having become an institution of idolatry].



## Chapter 17

###### Deuteronomy 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:1)


לֹא־תִזְבַּח֩ לַיהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ שׁ֣וֹר וָשֶׂ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִהְיֶ֥ה בוֹ֙ מ֔וּם כֹּ֖ל דָּבָ֣ר רָ֑ע כִּ֧י תוֹעֲבַ֛ת יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ הֽוּא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not sacrifice to Adonoy, your God, an ox or a lamb that has a blemish, any bad thing; for it is abominated by Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not sacrifice to the Lord thy God any bullock, or sheep, in which is a blemish, or anything evil: for that is an abomination to the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not sacrifice to the L-rd your G-d an ox or a lamb in which there is a blemish, any evil thing [(davar ra) an exhortation against invalidating sacrifices through evil speech (dibbur ra, see Leviticus 7:18)], for it is the abomination of the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:2)


כִּֽי־יִמָּצֵ֤א בְקִרְבְּךָ֙ בְּאַחַ֣ד שְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֣ן לָ֑ךְ אִ֣ישׁ אוֹ־אִשָּׁ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַעֲשֶׂ֧ה אֶת־הָרַ֛ע בְּעֵינֵ֥י יְהֹוָה־אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ לַעֲבֹ֥ר בְּרִיתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If there is found among you, in one of your cities that Adonoy, your God, is giving you, a man or a woman who does what is evil in the eyes of Adonoy, your God, by violating His covenant.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If there be found among you, within any of thy gates which the Lord thy God gives thee, a man or woman, who has perpetrated wickedness in the sight of the Lord thy God, in transgressing his covenant,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there be found in your midst, in one of your cities which the L-rd your G-d gives you, a man or a woman who would do what is evil in the eyes of the L-rd your G-d to transgress His covenant [that He made with you — not to serve idolatry],


###### Deuteronomy 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:3)


וַיֵּ֗לֶךְ וַֽיַּעֲבֹד֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וַיִּשְׁתַּ֖חוּ לָהֶ֑ם וְלַשֶּׁ֣מֶשׁ**׀** א֣וֹ לַיָּרֵ֗חַ א֛וֹ לְכׇל־צְבָ֥א הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־צִוִּֽיתִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He goes and serves other gods and bows to them—whether to the sun or to the moon or to any of the host of heaven that I did not command.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and has gone and served other gods, and worshipped them, either the sun, or moon, or any of the host of heaven, which I have not commanded;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he go and serve other gods and he bow down to them, or to the sun, or to the moon, or to the whole host of heaven, which I did not command [(that they be served)],


###### Deuteronomy 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:4)


וְהֻֽגַּד־לְךָ֖ וְשָׁמָ֑עְתָּ וְדָרַשְׁתָּ֣ הֵיטֵ֔ב וְהִנֵּ֤ה אֱמֶת֙ נָכ֣וֹן הַדָּבָ֔ר נֶעֶשְׂתָ֛ה הַתּוֹעֵבָ֥ה הַזֹּ֖את בְּיִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you are told and you understand, you are to inquire thoroughly. If in fact the report is authenticated and accurate—this abomination was committed within Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and it be told thee, and thou hast heard of it, and inquired diligently, and, behold, it be true, and the thing certain, that such abomination has been perpetrated in Yisra᾽el:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it be told to you, and you hear, and you search it out well, and, behold, the thing is true and correct [i.e., the testimony bears it out] — this abomination has been done in Israel,


###### Deuteronomy 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:5)


וְהֽוֹצֵאתָ֣ אֶת־הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֡וּא אוֹ֩ אֶת־הָאִשָּׁ֨ה הַהִ֜וא אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָ֠שׂ֠וּ אֶת־הַדָּבָ֨ר הָרָ֤ע הַזֶּה֙ אֶל־שְׁעָרֶ֔יךָ אֶת־הָאִ֕ישׁ א֖וֹ אֶת־הָאִשָּׁ֑ה וּסְקַלְתָּ֥ם בָּאֲבָנִ֖ים וָמֵֽתוּ׃
