**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to take out that man or that woman who did this evil thing to your city—the man or the woman—and you are to stone them with stones so that they die.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then shalt thou bring forth that man or that woman, who has committed that wicked thing, unto thy gates, and shalt stone that man or that woman with stones till they die.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall take out that man or that woman, who did this evil thing, to your cities [where they committed the act], the man or the woman, and you shall stone them with stones and they shall die.


###### Deuteronomy 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:6)


עַל־פִּ֣י**׀** שְׁנַ֣יִם עֵדִ֗ים א֛וֹ שְׁלֹשָׁ֥ה עֵדִ֖ים יוּמַ֣ת הַמֵּ֑ת לֹ֣א יוּמַ֔ת עַל־פִּ֖י עֵ֥ד אֶחָֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
By the speech of two witnesses or three witnesses is the guilty one to be executed; he is not to be executed by the oral testimonoy of one witness.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At the mouth of two witnesses, or three witnesses, shall he that is worthy of death be put to death; but at the mouth of one witness he shall not be put to death.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
By the word of two witnesses or three witnesses shall the dead one be put to death [(Just as two constitute one testimony, so do three, and they do not become "scheming witnesses" (eidim zomemim) until all are shown to be so.)] He shall not be put to death by the word of one witness.


###### Deuteronomy 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:7)


יַ֣ד הָעֵדִ֞ים תִּֽהְיֶה־בּ֤וֹ בָרִאשֹׁנָה֙ לַהֲמִית֔וֹ וְיַ֥ד כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם בָּאַחֲרֹנָ֑ה וּבִֽעַרְתָּ֥ הָרָ֖ע מִקִּרְבֶּֽךָ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Let the hand of the witnesses be against him first to execute him, and the hand of the entire people afterward; and you will eliminate the evil from within you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The hands of the witnesses shall be first upon him to put him to death, and afterwards the hands of all the people. So thou shalt put the evil away from among you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The hand of the witnesses shall be against him first to put him to death, and the hand of all the people afterwards, and you shall remove the evil from your midst.


###### Deuteronomy 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:8)


כִּ֣י יִפָּלֵא֩ מִמְּךָ֨ דָבָ֜ר לַמִּשְׁפָּ֗ט בֵּֽין־דָּ֨ם׀לְדָ֜ם בֵּֽין־דִּ֣ין לְדִ֗ין וּבֵ֥ין נֶ֙גַע֙ לָנֶ֔גַע דִּבְרֵ֥י רִיבֹ֖ת בִּשְׁעָרֶ֑יךָ וְקַמְתָּ֣ וְעָלִ֔יתָ אֶ֨ל־הַמָּק֔וֹם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִבְחַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a matter of law is too abstruse for you—between blood and blood, between decision and decision, or between leprosy and leprosy, matters under dispute in your city; you shall rise and ascend to the place that Adonoy, your God, will have chosen.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If there arise a matter too hard for thee in judgment, between blood and blood, between plea and plea, and between plague and plague, matters of controversy within thy gates: then shalt thou arise, and go up to the place which the Lord thy God shall choose;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there be hidden from you a thing of judgment — between blood [that is unclean] and blood [that is clean], between ruling [of acquittal] and ruling [of liability], between plague-spot [that is unclean] and plague-spot [that is clean], words of contention in your gates, [the sages of the city disagreeing with each other], then you shall arise and go up to the place that the L-rd your G-d shall choose [(whence it is seen that the Temple is the highest of all the places)].


###### Deuteronomy 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:9)


וּבָאתָ֗ אֶל־הַכֹּהֲנִים֙ הַלְוִיִּ֔ם וְאֶ֨ל־הַשֹּׁפֵ֔ט אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִהְיֶ֖ה בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֑ם וְדָרַשְׁתָּ֙ וְהִגִּ֣ידוּ לְךָ֔ אֵ֖ת דְּבַ֥ר הַמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to come before the *kohanim*-the Levites and the judge officiating during those days; you will inquire and they will tell you the legal decision.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt come to the priests the Levites, and to the judge that shall be in those days, and inquire; and they shall tell thee the sentence of judgment:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall come to the Cohanim, the Levites [i.e., from the tribe of Levi], and to the judge that shall be in those days [(Even if he is inferior to his predecessors, you must abide by his ruling)], and you shall inquire, and they shall tell you the word of the judgment.


###### Deuteronomy 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:10)


וְעָשִׂ֗יתָ עַל־פִּ֤י הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יַגִּ֣ידֽוּ לְךָ֔ מִן־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַה֔וּא אֲשֶׁ֖ר יִבְחַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה וְשָׁמַרְתָּ֣ לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת כְּכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר יוֹרֽוּךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to act according to the word that they tell you from that place that Adonoy will have chosen; and you are to be careful to fulfill exactly as they instruct you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt do according to the sentence, which they of that place which the Lord shall choose shall tell thee; and thou shalt observe to do according to all that they inform thee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall do according to the word that they shall tell you from that place which the L-rd shall choose, and you shall observe to do according to all that they teach you.


###### Deuteronomy 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:11)


עַל־פִּ֨י הַתּוֹרָ֜ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר יוֹר֗וּךָ וְעַל־הַמִּשְׁפָּ֛ט אֲשֶׁר־יֹאמְר֥וּ לְךָ֖ תַּעֲשֶׂ֑ה לֹ֣א תָס֗וּר מִן־הַדָּבָ֛ר אֲשֶׁר־יַגִּ֥ידֽוּ לְךָ֖ יָמִ֥ין וּשְׂמֹֽאל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In accord with the Torah that they instruct you and upon the law that they state to you, are you to act; do not deviate from the word they tell you, neither right or left.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
according to the sentence of the Tora which they shall teach thee, and according to the judgment which they shall tell thee, thou shalt do: thou shalt not deviate from the sentence which they shall tell thee, to the right hand, or to the left.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
According to the Torah that they teach you and the judgment that they tell you shall you do. You shall not turn aside from the thing that they tell you, right or left [(even if they tell you that right is left and left is right.)]


###### Deuteronomy 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:12)


וְהָאִ֞ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה בְזָד֗וֹן לְבִלְתִּ֨י שְׁמֹ֤עַ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵן֙ הָעֹמֵ֞ד לְשָׁ֤רֶת שָׁם֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ א֖וֹ אֶל־הַשֹּׁפֵ֑ט וּמֵת֙ הָאִ֣ישׁ הַה֔וּא וּבִֽעַרְתָּ֥ הָרָ֖ע מִיִּשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But the man who acts deliberately to not heed the *kohein* who stands to serve there Adonoy, your God, or the judge; that man is to be executed and you will eliminate the evil from Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the man that will act presumptuously, and will not hearken to the priest that stands to minister there before the Lord thy God, or to the judge, that man shall die: and thou shalt put away the evil from Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the man who shall do wilfully, not to listen to the Cohein who stands there to serve the L-rd your G-d, or to the judge, that man shall die; and you shall remove the evil from Israel.


###### Deuteronomy 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:13)


וְכׇל־הָעָ֖ם יִשְׁמְע֣וּ וְיִרָ֑אוּ וְלֹ֥א יְזִיד֖וּן עֽוֹד׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Let all the people hear and fear, and not sin deliberately again.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all the people shall hear, and fear, and do no more presumptuously.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the people shall hear and fear [— whence it is derived that they wait for him (i.e., they wait to execute the rebellious elder) until the (next) festival, and they execute him during the (period of the) festival], and they shall not act wilfully again.


###### Deuteronomy 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:14)


כִּֽי־תָבֹ֣א אֶל־הָאָ֗רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ נֹתֵ֣ן לָ֔ךְ וִֽירִשְׁתָּ֖הּ וְיָשַׁ֣בְתָּה בָּ֑הּ וְאָמַרְתָּ֗ אָשִׂ֤ימָה עָלַי֙ מֶ֔לֶךְ כְּכׇל־הַגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר סְבִיבֹתָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you arrive in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you and inherit it and live in it, and you say, ‘‘Let me appoint over me a king like all the nations around me;’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou art come to the land which the Lord thy God gives thee, and shalt possess it, and shalt dwell in it, and shalt say, I will set a king over me, like all the nations that are about me;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you come to the land which the L-rd your G-d gives to you and you inherit it and inhabit it, and you say: I shall place over myself a king, as all the nations that are around me,


###### Deuteronomy 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:15)


שׂ֣וֹם תָּשִׂ֤ים עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ מֶ֔לֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִבְחַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בּ֑וֹ מִקֶּ֣רֶב אַחֶ֗יךָ תָּשִׂ֤ים עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ מֶ֔לֶךְ לֹ֣א תוּכַ֗ל לָתֵ֤ת עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ אִ֣ישׁ נׇכְרִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־אָחִ֖יךָ הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Appoint are you to appoint over yourself a king whom Adonoy, your God, will choose. From among your brothers are you to appoint over yourself a king; you may not place over yourself a foreigner who is not your brother.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then thou mayst appoint a king over thee, whom the Lord thy God shall choose: one from among thy brethren shalt thou set as king over thee: thou mayst not set a stranger over thee, who is not thy brother.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Place shall you place over yourself a king whom the L-rd your G-d shall choose; from the midst of your brothers shall you place over yourself a king. You shall not be able to place over yourself a strange man, who is not your brother.


###### Deuteronomy 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:16)


רַק֮ לֹא־יַרְבֶּה־לּ֣וֹ סוּסִים֒ וְלֹֽא־יָשִׁ֤יב אֶת־הָעָם֙ מִצְרַ֔יְמָה לְמַ֖עַן הַרְבּ֣וֹת ס֑וּס וַֽיהֹוָה֙ אָמַ֣ר לָכֶ֔ם לֹ֣א תֹסִפ֗וּן לָשׁ֛וּב בַּדֶּ֥רֶךְ הַזֶּ֖ה עֽוֹד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, he must not acquire an abundance of horses for himself so that he will not return the people to Egypt in order to acquire an abundance of horses, because Adonoy told you, ‘‘You are not to proceed to return along this route again.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But he shall not multiply horses to himself, nor cause the people to return to Miżrayim, to the end that he should multiply horses: since the Lord has said to you, You shall henceforth return no more that way.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only, he shall not multiply for himself horses, [but only what he needs for his chariots], so that he not return the people to Egypt [the center of horses], to add a horse, the L-rd having said to you: You shall not venture to return upon this way anymore.


###### Deuteronomy 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:17)


וְלֹ֤א יַרְבֶּה־לּוֹ֙ נָשִׁ֔ים וְלֹ֥א יָס֖וּר לְבָב֑וֹ וְכֶ֣סֶף וְזָהָ֔ב לֹ֥א יַרְבֶּה־לּ֖וֹ מְאֹֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And he is not to acquire an abundance of wives for himself so that his heart will not veer; and silver and gold he may not accumulate for himself in great abundance.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither shall he multiply wives to himself, that his heart turn not away: neither shall he greatly multiply to himself silver and gold.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall not multiply for himself wives [(beyond eighteen)], that his heart not turn astray, and silver and gold he shall not multiply for himself overmuch, [but only as much as he needs to maintain his army].


###### Deuteronomy 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:18)


וְהָיָ֣ה כְשִׁבְתּ֔וֹ עַ֖ל כִּסֵּ֣א מַמְלַכְתּ֑וֹ וְכָ֨תַב ל֜וֹ אֶת־מִשְׁנֵ֨ה הַתּוֹרָ֤ה הַזֹּאת֙ עַל־סֵ֔פֶר מִלִּפְנֵ֖י הַכֹּהֲנִ֥ים הַלְוִיִּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It shall be, that when he occupies the throne of his kingdom, he must write for himself a duplicate of this Torah in a scroll form [the scroll] before the *kohanim*—the Levites.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, when he sits upon the throne of his kingdom, that he shall write for himself a copy of this Tora in a book out of that which is before the priests the Levites:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when, [as a reward for fulfilling the above,] he sits [secure] on his throne of kingdom, then he shall write for himself a double of this Torah [i.e., two Torah scrolls: one to be reposited in his treasury, and one to accompany him constantly] on a scroll from before the Cohanim, the Levites.


###### Deuteronomy 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:19)


וְהָיְתָ֣ה עִמּ֔וֹ וְקָ֥רָא ב֖וֹ כׇּל־יְמֵ֣י חַיָּ֑יו לְמַ֣עַן יִלְמַ֗ד לְיִרְאָה֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֔יו לִ֠שְׁמֹ֠ר אֶֽת־כׇּל־דִּבְרֵ֞י הַתּוֹרָ֥ה הַזֹּ֛את וְאֶת־הַחֻקִּ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה לַעֲשֹׂתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It is to accompany him and he is to read in it all the days of his life, in order that he learn to fear Adonoy, his God, to guard every word of this Torah and these statutes to fulfill them;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and it shall be with him, and he shall read therein all the days of his life: that he may learn to fear the Lord his God, to keep all the words of this Tora and these statutes, to do them:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be with him and he shall read in it all the days of his life, so that he learn to fear the L-rd his G-d, to heed all the words of this Torah and these statutes to do them.


###### Deuteronomy 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/17:20)


לְבִלְתִּ֤י רוּם־לְבָבוֹ֙ מֵֽאֶחָ֔יו וּלְבִלְתִּ֛י ס֥וּר מִן־הַמִּצְוָ֖ה יָמִ֣ין וּשְׂמֹ֑אול לְמַ֩עַן֩ יַאֲרִ֨יךְ יָמִ֧ים עַל־מַמְלַכְתּ֛וֹ ה֥וּא וּבָנָ֖יו בְּקֶ֥רֶב יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
That his pride not increase over his brothers and he does not stray from the commandment right or left; so that the days of his reign are lengthy over his kingdom, he and his sons within Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that his heart be not lifted up above his brethren, and that he turn not aside from the commandment, to the right hand, or to the left: to the end that he may prolong his days in his kingdom, he, and his children, in the midst of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that his heart not rise above his brothers, and so that he not depart from the mitzvah, [even a "light" mitzvah of a prophet], right or left, so that he prolong days in his kingdom [(But "departing," he shall not prolong days, e.g., I Samuel 10:8, 13:8 and 13-14)], he and his sons, in the midst of Israel [(If his son is fit to rule, he takes precedence to others.)]



## Chapter 18

###### Deuteronomy 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:1)


לֹֽא־יִ֠הְיֶ֠ה לַכֹּהֲנִ֨ים הַלְוִיִּ֜ם כׇּל־שֵׁ֧בֶט לֵוִ֛י חֵ֥לֶק וְנַחֲלָ֖ה עִם־יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אִשֵּׁ֧י יְהֹוָ֛ה וְנַחֲלָת֖וֹ יֹאכֵלֽוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They will not have—the *kohanim,* the Levites, the entire tribe of Levi—a portion or an inheritance with Yisroel; Adonoy’s fire-offerings and His inheritance will they eat.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The priests the Levites, all the tribe of Levi, shall have no part or inheritance with Yisra᾽el: the offerings of the Lord made by fire, and his dues shall they eat.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There shall not be to the Cohanim, the Levites, the entire tribe of Levi, [both the unblemished and the blemished], a portion [in the spoils] and an inheritance [in the land] with Israel. The fire-offerings of the L-rd [the Temple offerings] and His inheritance [the boundary offerings, i.e., terumoth and ma'asroth] shall they eat,


###### Deuteronomy 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:2)


וְנַחֲלָ֥ה לֹא־יִֽהְיֶה־לּ֖וֹ בְּקֶ֣רֶב אֶחָ֑יו יְהֹוָה֙ ה֣וּא נַחֲלָת֔וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּר־לֽוֹ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But he will have no territory among his brothers; Adonoy is his territory, as He said to him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore shall they have no inheritance among their brethren: the Lord is their inheritance, as he has said to them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But he shall not have an [absolute] inheritance in the midst of his brothers; the L-rd is his inheritance, as He spoke to him [(Numbers 18:20)].


###### Deuteronomy 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:3)


וְזֶ֡ה יִהְיֶה֩ מִשְׁפַּ֨ט הַכֹּהֲנִ֜ים מֵאֵ֣ת הָעָ֗ם מֵאֵ֛ת זֹבְחֵ֥י הַזֶּ֖בַח אִם־שׁ֣וֹר אִם־שֶׂ֑ה וְנָתַן֙ לַכֹּהֵ֔ן הַזְּרֹ֥עַ וְהַלְּחָיַ֖יִם וְהַקֵּבָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And this will be the stipend of the *kohanim* from the people from the slaughterers of [permitted] meat, whether an ox or a sheep; he will give the *kohein* the foreleg and the jaw and the [fourth] stomach.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this shall be the priest’s allotment from the people, from those who offer a sacrifice, whether it be ox or sheep; they shall give to the priest the shoulder, and the two cheeks, and the maw.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this shall be the judgment for the Cohanim from the people, [but not from the Cohanim (who slaughter for themselves)]. From the slaughterers of the slaughtering, whether ox or sheep [Animals (as opposed to beasts) are excluded], he shall give to the Cohein the shoulder [(from the knee-joint to the shoulder blade)], the cheeks [with the tongue], and the maw.


###### Deuteronomy 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:4)


רֵאשִׁ֨ית דְּגָֽנְךָ֜ תִּירֹשְׁךָ֣ וְיִצְהָרֶ֗ךָ וְרֵאשִׁ֛ית גֵּ֥ז צֹאנְךָ֖ תִּתֶּן־לֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The first portion of your grain, your wine, and your olive oil, and the first of the shearing of your sheep are you to give him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The firstfruit also of thy corn, of thy wine, and of thy oil, and the first of the fleece of thy sheep, shalt thou give him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The first [i.e., terumah] of your corn, your wine, and your oil, and the first of the [annual] shearing of your flock shall you give to him.


###### Deuteronomy 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:5)


כִּ֣י ב֗וֹ בָּחַ֛ר יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ מִכׇּל־שְׁבָטֶ֑יךָ לַעֲמֹ֨ד לְשָׁרֵ֧ת בְּשֵׁם־יְהֹוָ֛ה ה֥וּא וּבָנָ֖יו כׇּל־הַיָּמִֽים׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For him did Adonoy, your God, choose from all your tribes to stand and perform the service in the name of Adonoy, he and his sons for all time.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord thy God has chosen him out of all thy tribes, to stand to minister in the name of the Lord, him and his sons for ever.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For in him did the L-rd your G-d choose from all of your tribes to stand and minister [(whence it is derived that the ministrant must stand)] in the name of the L-rd, he and his sons all of the days.


###### Deuteronomy 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:6)


וְכִֽי־יָבֹ֨א הַלֵּוִ֜י מֵאַחַ֤ד שְׁעָרֶ֙יךָ֙ מִכׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁר־ה֖וּא גָּ֣ר שָׁ֑ם וּבָא֙ בְּכׇל־אַוַּ֣ת נַפְשׁ֔וֹ אֶל־הַמָּק֖וֹם אֲשֶׁר־יִבְחַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a Levite should come from one of your cities throughout Yisroel where he sojourns, coming will all his soul’s desire to the place that Adonoy chooses.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if a Levite come from any of thy gates out of all Yisra᾽el, where he sojourned, and come in all eagerness to the place which the Lord shall choose;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the Levite [i.e., a Cohein] come from one of your cities, from all of Israel, wherein he lives, and he come in all the desire of his soul [to sacrifice his gift or obligatory offerings] to the place that the L-rd shall choose,


###### Deuteronomy 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:7)


וְשֵׁרֵ֕ת בְּשֵׁ֖ם יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֑יו כְּכׇל־אֶחָיו֙ הַלְוִיִּ֔ם הָעֹמְדִ֥ים שָׁ֖ם לִפְנֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall perform the service in the name of Adonoy, his God, like all his brother Levites who stand there in Adonoy’s Presence.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then he shall minister in the name of the Lord his God, as all his brethren the Levites do, who stand there before the Lord.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
then, [even if his watch is not officiating], he shall minister in the name of the L-rd his G-d, as all of his brothers, the Levites, [i.e., the Cohanim] who stand there before the L-rd.


###### Deuteronomy 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:8)


חֵ֥לֶק כְּחֵ֖לֶק יֹאכֵ֑לוּ לְבַ֥ד מִמְכָּרָ֖יו עַל־הָאָבֽוֹת׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They shall eat equal portions, except for what the families sold [to one another].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall have like portions to eat, besides that which comes of the sale of his patrimony.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Portion [of the hides (of the burnt-offerings) and the flesh of the he-goat sin-offerings] as portion shall they eat [along with the Cohanim of the watch], except for the sale of the fathers [i.e., those sacrifices offered not by virtue of the festival, e.g., the temidin, the mussafin of Shabbath, and gift and vow offerings, the rights to which were sold by their fathers in the days of David and Samuel, when the rotation of priestly watches was instituted].


###### Deuteronomy 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:9)


כִּ֤י אַתָּה֙ בָּ֣א אֶל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֣ן לָ֑ךְ לֹֽא־תִלְמַ֣ד לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת כְּתוֹעֲבֹ֖ת הַגּוֹיִ֥ם הָהֵֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Because you are entering the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you; do not learn to perpetrate the abominations of those nations.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou art come to the land which the Lord thy God gives thee, thou shalt not learn to do after the abominations of those nations.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you come to the land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you, do not learn to do as the abominations of those nations [(but you may learn to understand the falsity of their ways and to teach your children, by identifying them as heathen practices, not to emulate them.)]


###### Deuteronomy 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:10)


לֹֽא־יִמָּצֵ֣א בְךָ֔ מַעֲבִ֥יר בְּנֽוֹ־וּבִתּ֖וֹ בָּאֵ֑שׁ קֹסֵ֣ם קְסָמִ֔ים מְעוֹנֵ֥ן וּמְנַחֵ֖שׁ וּמְכַשֵּֽׁף׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Let there not exist among you anyone who passes his son or daughter through fire, who practices the kosem-occult; who practices time-frame-occult or who divines portentuous events or a sorcerer.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
There must not be found among you anyone that makes his son or his daughter to pass through the fire, or that uses divination, a soothsayer, or an enchanter, or a witch,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
There shall not be found among you one who passes his son or daughter through fire [This is the service of the Molech (see Leviticus 18:21)], a diviner of divinations [one who "divines" by holding his stick, whether to go or not to go], a me'onein [one who assigns an auspicious "time" (onah) for a particular undertaking], a necromancer [one who interprets natural happenings as signs and portents], and a sorcerer.


###### Deuteronomy 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:11)


וְחֹבֵ֖ר חָ֑בֶר וְשֹׁאֵ֥ל אוֹב֙ וְיִדְּעֹנִ֔י וְדֹרֵ֖שׁ אֶל־הַמֵּתִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Or a snake charmer, or one who invokes the spirit of Ov or *yidoni,* or communicates with the dead.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
or a charmer, or a medium, or a wizard, or a necromancer.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A chover chaver [one who "congregates" snakes or scorpions and the like into one spot], a shoel ov [one who raises the spirit of the dead, which speaks from his arm-pit], a yidoni [(see Leviticus 19:31)], and one who inquires of the dead [e.g., via a skull].


###### Deuteronomy 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:12)


כִּֽי־תוֹעֲבַ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה כׇּל־עֹ֣שֵׂה אֵ֑לֶּה וּבִגְלַל֙ הַתּוֹעֵבֹ֣ת הָאֵ֔לֶּה יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ מוֹרִ֥ישׁ אוֹתָ֖ם מִפָּנֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For Adonoy’s abomination is anyone perpetrating this and because of these abominations, Adonoy, your God, is expelling them from before you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For all that do these things are an abomination to the Lord: and because of these abominations the Lord thy God drives them out from before thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the abomination of the L-rd are all who do [(even one of)] these, and because of these abominations the L-rd your G-d drives them out before you.


###### Deuteronomy 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:13)


תָּמִ֣ים תִּֽהְיֶ֔ה עִ֖ם יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Walk in perfect trust with Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt be perfect with the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Whole shall you be with the L-rd your G-d [i.e., If you repose all of your trust in Him, without probing the future, but accepting wholeheartedly all that befalls you, then you shall be "with the L-rd your G-d," and His portion.]


###### Deuteronomy 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:14)


כִּ֣י**׀** הַגּוֹיִ֣ם הָאֵ֗לֶּה אֲשֶׁ֤ר אַתָּה֙ יוֹרֵ֣שׁ אוֹתָ֔ם אֶל־מְעֹנְנִ֥ים וְאֶל־קֹסְמִ֖ים יִשְׁמָ֑עוּ וְאַתָּ֕ה לֹ֣א כֵ֔ן נָ֥תַן לְךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Although these nations whom you are inheriting heed augurers and sorcerers, but as to you—Adonoy, your God, has not given you their status.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For these nations, which thou shalt dispossess, hearken to soothsayers, and to diviners: but as for thee, the Lord thy God has not permitted thee so to do.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For these nations that you inherit hearken to me'onenim (see 10) and to diviners; but you, not thus [to hearken to them] did the L-rd your G-d relegate you, [for He reposed His shechinah on the prophets and the urim vethumim.]


###### Deuteronomy 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:15)


נָבִ֨יא מִקִּרְבְּךָ֤ מֵאַחֶ֙יךָ֙ כָּמֹ֔נִי יָקִ֥ים לְךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ אֵלָ֖יו תִּשְׁמָעֽוּן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A prophet from your midst, of your brethren, like me, will Adonoy, your God, establish for you; heed him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord thy God will raise up to thee a prophet from the midst of thee, of thy brethren, like me; to him you shall hearken;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A prophet from your midst, from your brothers, such as I [i.e., just as I am from your midst and from your brothers] shall the L-rd your G-d raise up for you [in my place, and so, from prophet to prophet]; to him shall you hearken.


###### Deuteronomy 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:16)


כְּכֹ֨ל אֲשֶׁר־שָׁאַ֜לְתָּ מֵעִ֨ם יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ בְּחֹרֵ֔ב בְּי֥וֹם הַקָּהָ֖ל לֵאמֹ֑ר לֹ֣א אֹסֵ֗ף לִשְׁמֹ֙עַ֙ אֶת־קוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֔י וְאֶת־הָאֵ֨שׁ הַגְּדֹלָ֥ה הַזֹּ֛את לֹֽא־אֶרְאֶ֥ה ע֖וֹד וְלֹ֥א אָמֽוּת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Exactly as you requested from Adonoy, your God, at Choreiv, on the day of assembly, saying, ‘‘Let me not continue to hear the voice of Adonoy, my God, and this great fire let me not see any more as that I will not die.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
according to all that thou didst desire of the Lord thy God in Ḥorev in the day of the assembly, saying, Let me not hear again the voice of the Lord my God, neither let me see this great fire any more, that I die not.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
As all that you asked from the L-rd your G-d in Chorev, on the day of the assembly, saying: I shall not continue hearing the voice of the L-rd my G-d, and this great fire I shall see no more, that I not die.


###### Deuteronomy 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:17)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְהֹוָ֖ה אֵלָ֑י הֵיטִ֖יבוּ אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֵּֽרוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy said to me, ‘‘What they said is excellent.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord said to me, They have well spoken that which they have spoken.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd said to me: They have done well in speaking as they did.


###### Deuteronomy 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:18)


נָבִ֨יא אָקִ֥ים לָהֶ֛ם מִקֶּ֥רֶב אֲחֵיהֶ֖ם כָּמ֑וֹךָ וְנָתַתִּ֤י דְבָרַי֙ בְּפִ֔יו וְדִבֶּ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֔ם אֵ֖ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֲצַוֶּֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A prophet will I establish for them from among their brethren like you, and I will place My words in his mouth and he will tell them everything that I command him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will raise them up a prophet from among their brethren, like thee, and will put my words in his mouth; and he shall speak to them all that I shall command him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
A prophet shall I raise up for them from the midst of their brothers, such as you, and I shall place My words in his mouth, and he shall speak to them all that I command him.


###### Deuteronomy 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:19)


וְהָיָ֗ה הָאִישׁ֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־יִשְׁמַע֙ אֶל־דְּבָרַ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר יְדַבֵּ֖ר בִּשְׁמִ֑י אָנֹכִ֖י אֶדְרֹ֥שׁ מֵעִמּֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Now, the man who does not heed My words that he speaks in My Name; I will demand from him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall come to pass, that whoever will not hearken to my words which he shall speak in my name, I will require it of him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, the man who will not heed My words, which he shall speak in My name, I shall require it of him. [One who suppresses his prophecy, one who transgresses the words of a prophet, and a prophet who transgresses his own words incur death at the hands of Heaven.]


###### Deuteronomy 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:20)


אַ֣ךְ הַנָּבִ֡יא אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָזִיד֩ לְדַבֵּ֨ר דָּבָ֜ר בִּשְׁמִ֗י אֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־צִוִּיתִיו֙ לְדַבֵּ֔ר וַאֲשֶׁ֣ר יְדַבֵּ֔ר בְּשֵׁ֖ם אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֑ים וּמֵ֖ת הַנָּבִ֥יא הַהֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But the prophet who will malevolently make a statement in My Name, something that I did not instruct him to say, or which he says in the name of foreign gods, that prophet must die.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the prophet, who shall presume to speak a word in my name, which I have not commanded him to speak, or that shall speak in the name of other gods, that prophet shall die.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the prophet who shall presume to speak a thing in My name which I did not command him to speak [(but which I commanded his fellow prophet)], and one who speaks in the name of other gods, [even if he is in agreement with the halachah, forbidding what is forbidden, and permitting what is permitted], that prophet shall be put to death [by strangulation. Three incur death by strangulation: one who prophesies what he did not hear, one who prophesies what was told to his fellow, and one who prophesies in the name of idolatry.]


###### Deuteronomy 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:21)


וְכִ֥י תֹאמַ֖ר בִּלְבָבֶ֑ךָ אֵיכָה֙ נֵדַ֣ע אֶת־הַדָּבָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־דִבְּר֖וֹ יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you should say to yourself, ‘‘How can we know [which is] the statement that Adonoy did not speak?’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if thou say in thy heart, How shall we know the word which the Lord has not spoken?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And when you say in your heart, [as you will in the incident of Chananiah ben Azur and Jeremiah (see Jeremiah 27:16, 19-22)]: How shall we know the thing which the L-rd has not spoken?


###### Deuteronomy 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/18:22)


אֲשֶׁר֩ יְדַבֵּ֨ר הַנָּבִ֜יא בְּשֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֗ה וְלֹֽא־יִהְיֶ֤ה הַדָּבָר֙ וְלֹ֣א יָבֹ֔א ה֣וּא הַדָּבָ֔ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־דִבְּר֖וֹ יְהֹוָ֑ה בְּזָדוֹן֙ דִּבְּר֣וֹ הַנָּבִ֔יא לֹ֥א תָג֖וּר מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Should the prophet speak in Adonoy’s Name, and the matter does not happen and is not fulfilled, that is the statement that Adonoy did not speak. The prophet spoke it malevolently; do not fear him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Know that when a prophet speaks in the name of the Lord, if the thing follow not, nor come to pass, that is the thing which the Lord has not spoken, but the prophet has spoken it out of presumption; thou shalt not be afraid of him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What the prophet shall speak in the name of the L-rd [i.e., "This and this shall happen to you"] and the thing not be and not come to pass, that is the thing which the L-rd has not spoken, [and put him to death. (For an instance in which he says: "The L-rd has told me to tell you to do thus and thus," see 13:9 and 15 above)]. In presumption has the prophet spoken it; do not hold back from [incriminating] him [and do not fear incurring punishment because of him].



## Chapter 19

###### Deuteronomy 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:1)


כִּֽי־יַכְרִ֞ית יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶת־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ אֶת־אַרְצָ֑ם וִֽירִשְׁתָּ֕ם וְיָשַׁבְתָּ֥ בְעָרֵיהֶ֖ם וּבְבָתֵּיהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy, your God, annihilates the nations whose land Adonoy, your God is giving you; and you inherit them and live in their cities and in their houses;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the Lord thy God has cut off the nations, whose land the Lord thy God gives thee, and thou hast driven them out, and dost dwell in their cities, and in their houses;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the L-rd your G-d cuts off the nations whose land the L-rd your G-d gives to you, and you inherit them and you dwell in their cities and in their houses,


###### Deuteronomy 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:2)


שָׁל֥וֹשׁ עָרִ֖ים תַּבְדִּ֣יל לָ֑ךְ בְּת֣וֹךְ אַרְצְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Separate three cities for yourself, within your land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou shalt separate three cities for thee in the midst of thy land, which the Lord thy God gives thee to possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Three cities shall you set aside for yourself in the midst of your land which the L-rd your G-d gives you to inherit.


###### Deuteronomy 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:3)


תָּכִ֣ין לְךָ֮ הַדֶּ֒רֶךְ֒ וְשִׁלַּשְׁתָּ֙ אֶת־גְּב֣וּל אַרְצְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַנְחִֽילְךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וְהָיָ֕ה לָנ֥וּס שָׁ֖מָּה כׇּל־רֹצֵֽחַ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Ready the route for yourself and divide the borders of your land into three sections that Adonoy, your God, will allot you; this will serve for any murderer to flee there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt prepare the way, and divide the border of thy land, which
				the Lord thy God gives thee to inherit, into three parts, that every slayer may flee thither.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Prepare for yourself the way [("Refuge" signs were posted on the crossroads)], and "third" the border of your land that the L-rd your G-d will cause you to inherit, [so that there be equal distances from the beginning of the border to the first city of refuge, from there to the second city, from there to the third city, and from there to the second border of Eretz Yisrael], that every slayer may flee there.


###### Deuteronomy 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:4)


וְזֶה֙ דְּבַ֣ר הָרֹצֵ֔חַ אֲשֶׁר־יָנ֥וּס שָׁ֖מָּה וָחָ֑י אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַכֶּ֤ה אֶת־רֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ בִּבְלִי־דַ֔עַת וְה֛וּא לֹא־שֹׂנֵ֥א ל֖וֹ מִתְּמֹ֥ל שִׁלְשֹֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And this is the matter of the murderer who may flee there to survive: whoever smites his peer without intent, and he had not been his enemy yesterday [or] the day before;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this is the case of the slayer, who shall flee there, that he may live: whoever unwittingly kills his neighbour, whom he hated not in time past;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is the matter of the slayer who shall flee there and live: one who smites his neighbor unwittingly, not having hated him in the past,


###### Deuteronomy 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:5)


וַאֲשֶׁר֩ יָבֹ֨א אֶת־רֵעֵ֥הוּ בַיַּ֘עַר֮ לַחְטֹ֣ב עֵצִים֒ וְנִדְּחָ֨ה יָד֤וֹ בַגַּרְזֶן֙ לִכְרֹ֣ת הָעֵ֔ץ וְנָשַׁ֤ל הַבַּרְזֶל֙ מִן־הָעֵ֔ץ וּמָצָ֥א אֶת־רֵעֵ֖הוּ וָמֵ֑ת ה֗וּא יָנ֛וּס אֶל־אַחַ֥ת הֶעָרִים־הָאֵ֖לֶּה וָחָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And whoever comes with his peer into the woods to chop trees, and as his hand swung the axe downward to cut the wood the iron flew off the wooden handle and encounters his peer and he dies; he is to flee to one of these cities to survive.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
as when a man goes into the forest with his neighbour to hew wood, and his hand fetches a stroke with the axe to cut down the tree, and the head slips from the handle, and strikes his neighbour, that he die; he shall flee to one of those cities, and live:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And one who comes with his neighbor in the forest to chop wood, and his hand swing the axe to cut the tree, and the blade slip from the haft [or, "and the blade chip (aught) of the tree"], and it (the blade or the chip) find his neighbor and he die — he shall flee to one of these cities, and he shall live.


###### Deuteronomy 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:6)


פֶּן־יִרְדֹּף֩ גֹּאֵ֨ל הַדָּ֜ם אַחֲרֵ֣י הָרֹצֵ֗חַ כִּי־יֵחַם֮ לְבָבוֹ֒ וְהִשִּׂיג֛וֹ כִּֽי־יִרְבֶּ֥ה הַדֶּ֖רֶךְ וְהִכָּ֣הוּ נָ֑פֶשׁ וְלוֹ֙ אֵ֣ין מִשְׁפַּט־מָ֔וֶת כִּ֠י לֹ֣א שֹׂנֵ֥א ה֛וּא ל֖וֹ מִתְּמ֥וֹל שִׁלְשֽׁוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Lest the blood-redeemer pursue the murderer when his heart grows heated, and he catches up with him over the length of the road and he smite him dead when he has no death sentence because he had not been his enemy yesterday [or] the day before.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest the avenger of blood pursue the slayer, while his heart is hot, and overtake him, because the way is long, and slay him; though he was not worthy of death, since he hated him not in time past.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[I tell you to prepare the way (3) and numerous cities of refuge] lest the avenger of blood pursue the slayer, his heart being hot, and he overtake him, the way being long, and he smite him mortally, and he (the slayer) not deserve to die, not having hated him (the victim) in the past.


###### Deuteronomy 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:7)


עַל־כֵּ֛ן אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ לֵאמֹ֑ר שָׁלֹ֥שׁ עָרִ֖ים תַּבְדִּ֥יל לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Therefore am I commanding you the following: three cities shall you separate for yourself.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore I command thee, saying, Thou shalt separate three cities for thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Therefore, I command you, saying: Three cities shall you set aside for yourself.


###### Deuteronomy 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:8)


וְאִם־יַרְחִ֞יב יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶת־גְּבֻ֣לְךָ֔ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר נִשְׁבַּ֖ע לַאֲבֹתֶ֑יךָ וְנָ֤תַן לְךָ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֶּ֖ר לָתֵ֥ת לַאֲבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And when Adonoy, your God, expands your boundary, in accordance with His oath to your forefathers, and He give you the entire land that He promised to give to your forefathers,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the Lord thy God enlarge thy border, as he has sworn to thy fathers, and give thee all the land which he promised to give to thy fathers;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the L-rd your G-d broaden your borders, as He swore to your forefathers [to give you the land of the Keini, the Kenizi, and the Kadmoni (Genesis 15:9)], and He give you all the land that He said to give to your forefathers,


###### Deuteronomy 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:9)


כִּֽי־תִשְׁמֹר֩ אֶת־כׇּל־הַמִּצְוָ֨ה הַזֹּ֜את לַעֲשֹׂתָ֗הּ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֣י מְצַוְּךָ֮ הַיּוֹם֒ לְאַהֲבָ֞ה אֶת־יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ וְלָלֶ֥כֶת בִּדְרָכָ֖יו כׇּל־הַיָּמִ֑ים וְיָסַפְתָּ֨ לְךָ֥ עוֹד֙ שָׁלֹ֣שׁ עָרִ֔ים עַ֖ל הַשָּׁלֹ֥שׁ הָאֵֽלֶּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you will be guarding this entire *mitzvah* to fulfill it, that I am commanding you today, to love Adonoy, your God, and to go in His ways for all time; then you shall add three more cities to these three.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou shalt keep all these commandments to do them, which I command thee this day, to love the Lord thy God, and to walk ever in his ways: then shalt thou add three cities more for thee, beside these three:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you keep all of this mitzvah to do it, that I command you this day, to love the L-rd your G-d and to walk in His ways all of the days — then you shall add for yourself another three cities to these three [— nine in all: three across the Jordan, three in the land of Canaan, and three in time to come].


###### Deuteronomy 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:10)


וְלֹ֤א יִשָּׁפֵךְ֙ דָּ֣ם נָקִ֔י בְּקֶ֣רֶב אַרְצְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ נַחֲלָ֑ה וְהָיָ֥ה עָלֶ֖יךָ דָּמִֽים׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And let innocent blood not be shed within your land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory, [otherwise] you will bear liability for the blood.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that innocent blood be not shed in thy land, which the Lord thy God gives thee for an inheritance, and so blood be upon thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And innocent blood shall not be shed in the midst of your land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you as an inheritance, and there be blood upon you.


###### Deuteronomy 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:11)


וְכִֽי־יִהְיֶ֥ה אִישׁ֙ שֹׂנֵ֣א לְרֵעֵ֔הוּ וְאָ֤רַב לוֹ֙ וְקָ֣ם עָלָ֔יו וְהִכָּ֥הוּ נֶ֖פֶשׁ וָמֵ֑ת וְנָ֕ס אֶל־אַחַ֖ת הֶעָרִ֥ים הָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If there is a man who hates his neighbor, and will ambush him, arising against him and smiting him dead, and he will flee to one of these cities;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if any man hate his neighbour, and lie in wait for him, and rise up against him, and smite him mortally that he die, and then flees into one of these cities:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if a man hate his neighbor, [(transgressing Leviticus 19:17)], and he lie in wait for him [(transgression of a "light" commandment leading to transgression of a grave one)], and he rise up against him, and he smite him mortally, and he die, and he flees to one of these cities,


###### Deuteronomy 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:12)


וְשָֽׁלְחוּ֙ זִקְנֵ֣י עִיר֔וֹ וְלָקְח֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ מִשָּׁ֑ם וְנָתְנ֣וּ אֹת֗וֹ בְּיַ֛ד גֹּאֵ֥ל הַדָּ֖ם וָמֵֽת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The elders of his city will send for and take him from there, and will hand him over to the blood-redeemer, and he will be executed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then the elders of his city shall send and fetch him from there, and deliver him into the hand of the avenger of blood, that he may die.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the elders of his city shall send, and they shall take him from there, and they shall put him into the hand of the avenger of blood, and he shall die.


###### Deuteronomy 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:13)


לֹא־תָח֥וֹס עֵֽינְךָ֖ עָלָ֑יו וּבִֽעַרְתָּ֧ דַֽם־הַנָּקִ֛י מִיִּשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וְט֥וֹב לָֽךְ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not view him with compassion. You are to eliminate the [shedding of] innocent blood from Yisroel, and you will have it good.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thy eye shall not pity him, but thou shalt put away the guilt of innocent blood from Yisra᾽el, that it may go well with thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Let your eye not pity him [(Do not say: One has already been killed — why kill two Jews?], and remove the innocent blood from Israel, and it shall be good for you.


###### Deuteronomy 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:14)


לֹ֤א תַסִּיג֙ גְּב֣וּל רֵֽעֲךָ֔ אֲשֶׁ֥ר גָּבְל֖וּ רִאשֹׁנִ֑ים בְּנַחֲלָֽתְךָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּנְחַ֔ל בָּאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not move back the boundary of your neighbor that the first [settlers] determine in your territory that you will inherit in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not remove thy neighbour’s landmark, which they of old time have set in thy inheritance, which thou shalt inherit in the land that the Lord thy God gives thee to possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not move back [(into his land, thus broadening your own)], the boundary marker of your neighbor [(If you do so, you violate two negative commandments — this and (Leviticus 19:13): "You shall not rob")], which the first ones bounded, in your inheritance which you shall inherit in the land which the L-rd your G-d gives you to inherit. [(Outside of Eretz Yisrael one is in violation only of "You shall not rob")].


###### Deuteronomy 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:15)


לֹֽא־יָקוּם֩ עֵ֨ד אֶחָ֜ד בְּאִ֗ישׁ לְכׇל־עָוֺן֙ וּלְכׇל־חַטָּ֔את בְּכׇל־חֵ֖טְא אֲשֶׁ֣ר יֶֽחֱטָ֑א עַל־פִּ֣י**׀** שְׁנֵ֣י עֵדִ֗ים א֛וֹ עַל־פִּ֥י שְׁלֹשָֽׁה־עֵדִ֖ים יָק֥וּם דָּבָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
One witness may not arise against a man about any sin or for any transgression or transgression that he transgresses; by the word of two witnesses or by the word of three witnesses let a matter be established.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
One witness shall not rise up against a man for any iniquity, or for any sin, in any sin that he may commit: at the mouth of two witnesses, or at the mouth of three witnesses, shall the matter be established.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
One witness shall not arise against a man for every transgression and for every sin, in any sin that one commit. By the mouth of two witnesses, [and not by sending written testimony to beth-din, and not by interposing an interpreter between the witnesses and the judges], or by the mouth of three witnesses shall a thing be established.


###### Deuteronomy 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:16)


כִּֽי־יָק֥וּם עֵד־חָמָ֖ס בְּאִ֑ישׁ לַעֲנ֥וֹת בּ֖וֹ סָרָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If false witnesses arise against a man and bear fallacious testimony against him;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If a false witness rise up against any man to testify against him that which is wrong;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there arise a false witness [i.e., a false set of witnesses] against a man to testify against him, removed, [i.e., when they were completely removed from having witnessed the incident (as when other witnesses challenge them: But were you not with us at this and this place at that time!)],


###### Deuteronomy 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:17)


וְעָמְד֧וּ שְׁנֵֽי־הָאֲנָשִׁ֛ים אֲשֶׁר־לָהֶ֥ם הָרִ֖יב לִפְנֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה לִפְנֵ֤י הַכֹּֽהֲנִים֙ וְהַשֹּׁ֣פְטִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִהְי֖וּ בַּיָּמִ֥ים הָהֵֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The two men shall stand, who are involved in the dispute, before Adonoy, before the *kohanim* and the judges who are in those days.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then both the men, between whom is the controversy, shall stand before the Lord, before the priests and the judges, who shall be in those days;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the two men [the witnesses] shall stand [(Witnesses must be men and they must testify standing)], (and those men) who have the contention (must stand) before the L-rd [(It should be in their eyes as if they are standing "before the L-rd")], before the Cohanim and the judges who shall be in those days [("Yiftach in his generation is like Samuel in his generation.")]


###### Deuteronomy 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:18)


וְדָרְשׁ֥וּ הַשֹּׁפְטִ֖ים הֵיטֵ֑ב וְהִנֵּ֤ה עֵֽד־שֶׁ֙קֶר֙ הָעֵ֔ד שֶׁ֖קֶר עָנָ֥ה בְאָחִֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When the judges shall investigate thoroughly and behold, the witness testified falsely, they testified falsely against their brother;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the judges shall make diligent inquiry: and, behold, if the witness is a false witness, and has testified falsely against his brother;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the judges shall inquire well [by cross-examining those who come to discredit the witnesses], and, behold, a false witness is the witness [("witness" always connotes two)] — falsely did he testify against his brother,


###### Deuteronomy 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:19)


וַעֲשִׂ֣יתֶם ל֔וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר זָמַ֖ם לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת לְאָחִ֑יו וּבִֽעַרְתָּ֥ הָרָ֖ע מִקִּרְבֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to do to [each of] them as he conspired to do to his brother, and eliminate the evil from among you;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then shall you do to him, as he had thought to have done to his brother: so shalt thou put the evil away from among you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall do to him as he schemed to do [(but not "as he did")] to his brother, [but not to his sister (in an instance of their testifying that the married daughter of a Cohein was adulterous, in which instance they receive not the adulteress's penalty (burning), but the adulterer's (strangulation)], and you shall remove the evil from your midst.


###### Deuteronomy 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:20)


וְהַנִּשְׁאָרִ֖ים יִשְׁמְע֣וּ וְיִרָ֑אוּ וְלֹֽא־יֹסִ֨פוּ לַעֲשׂ֜וֹת ע֗וֹד כַּדָּבָ֥ר הָרָ֛ע הַזֶּ֖ה בְּקִרְבֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And the remainder will hear and be fearful, and they will not repeat this evil thing among you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And those who remain shall hear, and fear, and shall henceforth commit no more any such evil among you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the rest shall hear and fear, and they shall not venture to do again as this evil thing in your midst. [Scheming witnesses require "proclamation," viz.: "These men were executed for having been found 'scheming witnesses' in beth-din."]


###### Deuteronomy 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/19:21)


וְלֹ֥א תָח֖וֹס עֵינֶ֑ךָ נֶ֣פֶשׁ בְּנֶ֗פֶשׁ עַ֤יִן בְּעַ֙יִן֙ שֵׁ֣ן בְּשֵׁ֔ן יָ֥ד בְּיָ֖ד רֶ֥גֶל בְּרָֽגֶל׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are not to have compassion: life for life, eye for eye, tooth for tooth, hand for hand, foot for foot.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thy eye shall not pity; but life for life, eye for eye, tooth for tooth, hand for hand, foot for foot.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And let your eyes not pity. A soul for a soul, an eye for an eye [i.e., monetary compensation (and so with what follows)], a tooth for a tooth, a hand for a hand, a foot for a foot.



## Chapter 20

###### Deuteronomy 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:1)


כִּֽי־תֵצֵ֨א לַמִּלְחָמָ֜ה עַל־אֹיְבֶ֗ךָ וְֽרָאִ֜יתָ ס֤וּס וָרֶ֙כֶב֙ עַ֚ם רַ֣ב מִמְּךָ֔ לֹ֥א תִירָ֖א מֵהֶ֑ם כִּֽי־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ עִמָּ֔ךְ הַמַּֽעַלְךָ֖ מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you go to war against your enemy, and you see horse and chariot, people who outnumber you; do not be afraid of them, for Adonoy, your God, is with you, He Who brought you up from the land of Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou goest out to battle against thy enemies, and seest horses, and chariots, and a people more numerous than thou, be not afraid of them: for the Lord thy God is with thee, who brought thee up out of the land of Miżrayim.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you go out to war [(the rationale of the juxtaposition: one lacking a limb does not go out to war)], against your foes [(Let them be as foes in your eyes: Do not pity them, for they will not pity you)], and you see horse and chariot [(In My eyes they are all like one horse)], a nation greater 'from you' [i.e., from your vantage point, not from Mine], do not fear them; for the L-rd your G-d is with you, who brought you up from the land of Egypt.


###### Deuteronomy 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:2)


וְהָיָ֕ה כְּקָֽרׇבְכֶ֖ם אֶל־הַמִּלְחָמָ֑ה וְנִגַּ֥שׁ הַכֹּהֵ֖ן וְדִבֶּ֥ר אֶל־הָעָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Now, as you near the battle, the *kohein* shall approach and speak to the people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, when you are come near to the battle, that the priest shall approach and speak to the people, and shall say to them,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when you draw near to the battle [soon after leaving the boundary of your land], that the Cohein [the Cohein "anointed for war"] shall come forward, and he shall speak to the people [in the holy tongue].


###### Deuteronomy 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:3)


וְאָמַ֤ר אֲלֵהֶם֙ שְׁמַ֣ע יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אַתֶּ֨ם קְרֵבִ֥ים הַיּ֛וֹם לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה עַל־אֹיְבֵיכֶ֑ם אַל־יֵרַ֣ךְ לְבַבְכֶ֗ם אַל־תִּֽירְא֧וּ וְאַֽל־תַּחְפְּז֛וּ וְאַל־תַּֽעַרְצ֖וּ מִפְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He will say to them, ‘‘Hear, Yisroel! You are setting out today to battle against your enemies. Do not be faint hearted; or intimidated and do not panic, and do not be crushed before them;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Hear, O Yisra᾽el, you draw near today to do battle against your enemies: let not your hearts faint, fear not, and do not tremble, nor be terrified because of them;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he shall say to them: Hear, (Shema) O Israel [(Even if you had no other merit but that of reciting the Shema, it would suffice for your salvation)], you are drawing near today to do battle with your foes, [and not with your brethren, who would treat you with compassion (see II Chronicles 28:15)]. Let your hearts not faint [at the whinnying of the foes' horses], do not fear [the clashing of their shields], do not tremble [at the blaring of their trumpets], and do not break before them [i.e., before their battle cries].


###### Deuteronomy 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:4)


כִּ֚י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶ֔ם הַהֹלֵ֖ךְ עִמָּכֶ֑ם לְהִלָּחֵ֥ם לָכֶ֛ם עִם־אֹיְבֵיכֶ֖ם לְהוֹשִׁ֥יעַ אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Because Adonoy, your God, marches with you to do battle for you with your enemies to save you.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for the Lord your God is he that goes with you, to fight for you against your enemies, to save you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it is the L-rd your G-d [(They come with the triumph of flesh and blood, and you come with the triumph of the L-rd)] which [viz., the encampment of the ark] goes with you to war for you with your foes, to save you.


###### Deuteronomy 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:5)


וְדִבְּר֣וּ הַשֹּֽׁטְרִים֮ אֶל־הָעָ֣ם לֵאמֹר֒ מִֽי־הָאִ֞ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֨ר בָּנָ֤ה בַֽיִת־חָדָשׁ֙ וְלֹ֣א חֲנָכ֔וֹ יֵלֵ֖ךְ וְיָשֹׁ֣ב לְבֵית֑וֹ פֶּן־יָמוּת֙ בַּמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וְאִ֥ישׁ אַחֵ֖ר יַחְנְכֶֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The officers will address the people as follows, ‘‘Whichever man has built a new house and did not inaugurate it as a dwelling, let him go and return home lest he die in battle and another man will inaugurate it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the officers shall speak to the people, saying, What man is there that has built a new house, and has not dedicated it? let him go and return to his house, lest he die in the battle, and another man dedicate it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then [the Cohanim shall say to the officers and] the officers shall speak to the people, saying: Who is the man that has built a new house and has not inhabited it? Let him go and return to his house, lest he die in the war and another man inhabit it [and the thought of this happening cause him distress].


###### Deuteronomy 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:6)


וּמִֽי־הָאִ֞ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־נָטַ֥ע כֶּ֙רֶם֙ וְלֹ֣א חִלְּל֔וֹ יֵלֵ֖ךְ וְיָשֹׁ֣ב לְבֵית֑וֹ פֶּן־יָמוּת֙ בַּמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וְאִ֥ישׁ אַחֵ֖ר יְחַלְּלֶֽנּוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And whichever man has planted a vineyard and did not redeem it[s fruit,] let him go and return home, lest he die in battle and another man redeem it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And what man is he that has planted a vineyard, and has not yet eaten of it? let him also go and return to his house, lest he die in the battle, and another man eat of it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And who is the man that has planted a vineyard and not redeemed it [in the fourth year (the fruits to be eaten in Jerusalem or redeemed for money to be spent for eating in Jerusalem)]? Let him go and return to his house, lest he die in the war and another man redeem it.


###### Deuteronomy 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:7)


וּמִֽי־הָאִ֞ישׁ אֲשֶׁר־אֵרַ֤שׂ אִשָּׁה֙ וְלֹ֣א לְקָחָ֔הּ יֵלֵ֖ךְ וְיָשֹׁ֣ב לְבֵית֑וֹ פֶּן־יָמוּת֙ בַּמִּלְחָמָ֔ה וְאִ֥ישׁ אַחֵ֖ר יִקָּחֶֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And whichever man has betrothed a woman and not married her, let him go and return home, lest he die in battle and another man marry her.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And what man is there that has betrothed a wife, and has not taken her? let him go and return to his house, lest he die in the battle, and another man take her.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And who is the man who has betrothed a woman and not taken her? Let him go and return to his house, lest he die in the war [(If he does not heed the Cohein, he deserves to die)], and another man take her.


###### Deuteronomy 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:8)


וְיָסְפ֣וּ הַשֹּׁטְרִים֮ לְדַבֵּ֣ר אֶל־הָעָם֒ וְאָמְר֗וּ מִי־הָאִ֤ישׁ הַיָּרֵא֙ וְרַ֣ךְ הַלֵּבָ֔ב יֵלֵ֖ךְ וְיָשֹׁ֣ב לְבֵית֑וֹ וְלֹ֥א יִמַּ֛ס אֶת־לְבַ֥ב אֶחָ֖יו כִּלְבָבֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The officers will further address the people and say. ‘‘Whoever is afraid or faint hearted, let him go and return home, and let him not destroy the resolve of his brothers like his own resolve.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the officers shall speak further to the people, and they shall say, What man is there that is fearful and fainthearted? let him go and return to his house, lest his brethren’s heart melt like his heart.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the officers shall add [to the words of the Cohanim] to speak to the people, and they shall say: Who is the man that fears [According to one view, fear of combat; according to another, fear of (dying in battle because of) one's transgressions], and that is faint of heart? Let him go and return to his house and not melt the heart of his brothers as his own heart.


###### Deuteronomy 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:9)


וְהָיָ֛ה כְּכַלֹּ֥ת הַשֹּׁטְרִ֖ים לְדַבֵּ֣ר אֶל־הָעָ֑ם וּפָ֥קְד֛וּ שָׂרֵ֥י צְבָא֖וֹת בְּרֹ֥אשׁ הָעָֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When the officers finish addressing the people; they will appoint army commanders at the head of the people.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, when the officers have made an end of speaking to the people, that they shall make captains of the armies to lead the people.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when the officers finish speaking to the people, that they shall post sentinels at the head of the people, [before and behind, with iron staves in their hands, and empowered to "cripple" all who would flee, for the beginning of defeat is flight.]


###### Deuteronomy 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:10)


כִּֽי־תִקְרַ֣ב אֶל־עִ֔יר לְהִלָּחֵ֖ם עָלֶ֑יהָ וְקָרָ֥אתָ אֵלֶ֖יהָ לְשָׁלֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you near a city to do battle against it, you are to offer it peace.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou comest near to a city to fight against it, then proclaim peace to it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If [in an optional war (as opposed to a war of mitzvah)] you draw near to a city to do battle with it, then you shall call out to it for peace.


###### Deuteronomy 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:11)


וְהָיָה֙ אִם־שָׁל֣וֹם תַּֽעַנְךָ֔ וּפָתְחָ֖ה לָ֑ךְ וְהָיָ֞ה כׇּל־הָעָ֣ם הַנִּמְצָא־בָ֗הּ יִהְי֥וּ לְךָ֛ לָמַ֖ס וַעֲבָדֽוּךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Should it respond, ‘‘Peace!’’ and open for you, then all the people found in it will become your payers of tribute, and your servants.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, if it make thee answer of peace, and open to thee, then it shall be, that all the people to be found in it shall be tributaries to thee, and they shall serve thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, if it answers you for peace and opens itself to you, then all the people found in it [even those of the seven nations, whom you are (otherwise) commanded to destroy] shall be tribute to you and they shall serve you [They must accede to both.]


###### Deuteronomy 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:12)


וְאִם־לֹ֤א תַשְׁלִים֙ עִמָּ֔ךְ וְעָשְׂתָ֥ה עִמְּךָ֖ מִלְחָמָ֑ה וְצַרְתָּ֖ עָלֶֽיהָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And if it does not settle for peace with you but engages in war against you, you shall lay siege to it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if it will make no peace with thee, but will make war against thee, then thou shalt besiege it:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But if it does not make peace with you, and it makes war with you [(If it does not make peace with you and you let it be, in the end it will make war with you)], then you shall besiege it, [even to cause famine, thirst, and mortal disease],


###### Deuteronomy 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:13)


וּנְתָנָ֛הּ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ בְּיָדֶ֑ךָ וְהִכִּיתָ֥ אֶת־כׇּל־זְכוּרָ֖הּ לְפִי־חָֽרֶב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, will deliver it into your hand, and you will smite all its males by the sword.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and when the Lord thy God has delivered it into thy hands, thou shalt smite every male of it with the edge of the sword:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d will deliver it into your hands [if you do as you have been charged], and you shall smite all of its [adult] males by the sword.


###### Deuteronomy 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:14)


רַ֣ק הַ֠נָּשִׁ֠ים וְהַטַּ֨ף וְהַבְּהֵמָ֜ה וְכֹל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִהְיֶ֥ה בָעִ֛יר כׇּל־שְׁלָלָ֖הּ תָּבֹ֣ז לָ֑ךְ וְאָֽכַלְתָּ֙ אֶת־שְׁלַ֣ל אֹיְבֶ֔יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֛ן יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, the women and the children, and the animals, and everything that will be in the city—all its booty—are you to plunder for yourself; you will eat the booty of your enemies that Adonoy, your God, gave you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but the women, and the little ones, and the cattle, and all that is in the city, all the spoil of it, shalt thou take to thyself; and thou shalt eat the spoil of thy enemies, which the Lord thy God has given thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But the women, and the little ones, and the beasts, and all that is in the city, all of its spoil, shall you take for yourself, and you shall eat the spoil of your foes, which the L-rd your G-d has given you.


###### Deuteronomy 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:15)


כֵּ֤ן תַּעֲשֶׂה֙ לְכׇל־הֶ֣עָרִ֔ים הָרְחֹקֹ֥ת מִמְּךָ֖ מְאֹ֑ד אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹא־מֵעָרֵ֥י הַגּֽוֹיִם־הָאֵ֖לֶּה הֵֽנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
So will you do to all the cities, that are very distant from you, that are not among the cities of these nations.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thus shalt thou do to all the cities which are very far off from thee, which are not of the cities of these nations.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So shall you do to all the cities which are very far from you, which are not of the cities of these nations.


###### Deuteronomy 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:16)


רַ֗ק מֵעָרֵ֤י הָֽעַמִּים֙ הָאֵ֔לֶּה אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ נַחֲלָ֑ה לֹ֥א תְחַיֶּ֖ה כׇּל־נְשָׁמָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
However, from the cities of these peoples that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as inheritance, you are not to leave any person alive.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But of the cities of these peoples, which the Lord thy God gives thee for an inheritance, thou shalt save alive nothing that breathes:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But from the cities of these people which the L-rd your G-d gives you as an inheritance you shall not allow any soul to live.


###### Deuteronomy 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:17)


כִּֽי־הַחֲרֵ֣ם תַּחֲרִימֵ֗ם הַחִתִּ֤י וְהָאֱמֹרִי֙ הַכְּנַעֲנִ֣י וְהַפְּרִזִּ֔י הַחִוִּ֖י וְהַיְבוּסִ֑י כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוְּךָ֖ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Rather annihilate are you to annihilate them: the Chittites and the Emorites, the Canaanites and the Perizites, the Chivites and the Yevusites; as Adonoy, your God, commanded you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but thou
				shalt utterly destroy them; the Ḥitti, and the Emori, the Kena῾ani, and the Perizzi, the Ḥivvi, and the Yevusi; as the Lord thy God has commanded thee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But you shall utterly destroy them: the Chitti, the Emori, the Canaani, the Perizi, the Chivi, and the Yevussi, as the L-rd your G-d commanded you [(The Girgashi is hereby included)].


###### Deuteronomy 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:18)


לְמַ֗עַן אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־יְלַמְּד֤וּ אֶתְכֶם֙ לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת כְּכֹל֙ תּֽוֹעֲבֹתָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשׂ֖וּ לֵאלֹֽהֵיהֶ֑ם וַחֲטָאתֶ֖ם לַיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In order that they do not teach you to do any of their abominations that they did for their gods, and you will sin to Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that they teach you not to do after all their abominations, which they have done to their gods; so should you sin against the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that they not teach you to do according to all their abominations which they performed unto their gods and you sin to the L-rd your G-d [(but if they repent and convert, you may accept them)].


###### Deuteronomy 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:19)


כִּֽי־תָצ֣וּר אֶל־עִיר֩ יָמִ֨ים רַבִּ֜ים לְֽהִלָּחֵ֧ם עָלֶ֣יהָ לְתׇפְשָׂ֗הּ לֹֽא־תַשְׁחִ֤ית אֶת־עֵצָהּ֙ לִנְדֹּ֤חַ עָלָיו֙ גַּרְזֶ֔ן כִּ֚י מִמֶּ֣נּוּ תֹאכֵ֔ל וְאֹת֖וֹ לֹ֣א תִכְרֹ֑ת כִּ֤י הָֽאָדָם֙ עֵ֣ץ הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה לָבֹ֥א מִפָּנֶ֖יךָ בַּמָּצֽוֹר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you besiege a city many days to wage war against it, to capture it, do not harm [any of] its trees by chopping it with an ax, because you eat from it you are not to cut it down; For, is the tree in the field a man to join the besieged to escape you?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou shalt besiege a city a long time, in making war against it to take it, thou shalt not destroy its trees by forcing an axe against them: for thou mayst eat of them, and thou shalt not cut them down; for is the tree of the field a man, that it should be besieged by thee?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you besiege a city [(in an optional war)] many days [("days" = two; "many days" = three — whence it is derived that gentile cities are not besieged fewer than three days before the Sabbath and that the peace overture is repeated for two or three days)], to make war upon it, to capture it, do not destroy its tree by wielding an axe against it. For from it shall you eat, but it shall you not cut down. For is the tree of the field a man, to come before you into the siege, [there to be afflicted with hunger and thirst like the people of the city? Why should you destroy it?]


###### Deuteronomy 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/20:20)


רַ֞ק עֵ֣ץ אֲשֶׁר־תֵּדַ֗ע כִּֽי־לֹא־עֵ֤ץ מַאֲכָל֙ ה֔וּא אֹת֥וֹ תַשְׁחִ֖ית וְכָרָ֑תָּ וּבָנִ֣יתָ מָצ֗וֹר עַל־הָעִיר֙ אֲשֶׁר־הִ֨וא עֹשָׂ֧ה עִמְּךָ֛ מִלְחָמָ֖ה עַ֥ד רִדְתָּֽהּ׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Only a tree that you know that it is not a fruit tree may you harm or cut down; and you will build battlements against the city that is waging war against you until it is conquered.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Only the trees which thou knowst that they be not trees for food, thou shalt destroy and cut them down; and thou shalt build bulwarks against the city that makes war with thee, until it be subdued.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Only a tree that you know not to be a food tree, it may you destroy and cut down and build siegeworks against the city that makes war with you until it is subdued.



## Chapter 21

###### Deuteronomy 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:1)


כִּי־יִמָּצֵ֣א חָלָ֗ל בָּאֲדָמָה֙ אֲשֶׁר֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ נֹתֵ֤ן לְךָ֙ לְרִשְׁתָּ֔הּ נֹפֵ֖ל בַּשָּׂדֶ֑ה לֹ֥א נוֹדַ֖ע מִ֥י הִכָּֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a corpse is found in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you to inherit, fallen in the field, it is not known who smote him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If one be found slain in the land which the Lord thy God gives thee to possess it, lying in the field, and it be not known who has slain him:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there be found a slain body on the land that the L-rd your G-d gives you to inherit, fallen in the field, it not being known who killed him,


###### Deuteronomy 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:2)


וְיָצְא֥וּ זְקֵנֶ֖יךָ וְשֹׁפְטֶ֑יךָ וּמָדְדוּ֙ אֶל־הֶ֣עָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֖ר סְבִיבֹ֥ת הֶחָלָֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Your elders shall go out—and your judges—and measure in the direction of the cities around the corpse.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then thy elders and thy judges shall come out, and they shall measure to the cities which are round about him that is slain:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then there shall go out your elders [the Great Sanhedrin] and your judges, and they shall measure [from the body] to the cities that are around the body [to determine which is closest].


###### Deuteronomy 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:3)


וְהָיָ֣ה הָעִ֔יר הַקְּרֹבָ֖ה אֶל־הֶחָלָ֑ל וְלָֽקְח֡וּ זִקְנֵי֩ הָעִ֨יר הַהִ֜וא עֶגְלַ֣ת בָּקָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־עֻבַּד֙ בָּ֔הּ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־מָשְׁכָ֖ה בְּעֹֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Now, the city nearest the corpse—the elders of that city are to take a calf-heifer that has not been worked, that has not drawn [with] a yoke.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and it shall be, that as for the city which is nearest to the slain man, the elders of that city shall take a heifer, which has not been put to work, and which has not drawn in the yoke;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, the city closest to the body — the elders of that city shall take a heifer that has not been worked, that has not drawn under the yoke,


###### Deuteronomy 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/21:4)


וְהוֹרִ֡דוּ זִקְנֵי֩ הָעִ֨יר הַהִ֤וא אֶת־הָֽעֶגְלָה֙ אֶל־נַ֣חַל אֵיתָ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֛ר לֹא־יֵעָבֵ֥ד בּ֖וֹ וְלֹ֣א יִזָּרֵ֑עַ וְעָֽרְפוּ־שָׁ֥ם אֶת־הָעֶגְלָ֖ה בַּנָּֽחַל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
