Stand outside, and the man from whom you are claiming, will bring out the pledge to you outside.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt stand outside, and the man to whom thou dost lend shall bring out the pledge to thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Outside shall you stand, and the man who is indebted to you shall take out the pledge to you outside.


###### Deuteronomy 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:12)


וְאִם־אִ֥ישׁ עָנִ֖י ה֑וּא לֹ֥א תִשְׁכַּ֖ב בַּעֲבֹטֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And if he is a poor man, do not repose with his collateral [in your possession].


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the man be poor, thou shalt not sleep with his pledge:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if he is a poor man, you shall not lie down with his pledge [in your possession].


###### Deuteronomy 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:13)


הָשֵׁב֩ תָּשִׁ֨יב ל֤וֹ אֶֽת־הַעֲבוֹט֙ כְּב֣וֹא הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ וְשָׁכַ֥ב בְּשַׂלְמָת֖וֹ וּבֵֽרְכֶ֑ךָּ וּלְךָ֙ תִּהְיֶ֣ה צְדָקָ֔ה לִפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Return are you to return the pledge to him at sunset, and he will sleep with his garment and bless you; and it will be to your credit before Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
thou shalt surely deliver him the pledge again when the sun goes down, that he may sleep in his own garment, and bless thee: and it shall be as righteousness to thee before the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Return shall you return to him the pledge when the sun goes down [if it is a night garment (and see Exodus 22:25)], so that he may sleep in his garment, and he shall bless you; and [even if he does not bless you], it shall be (accounted) righteousness for you before the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:14)


לֹא־תַעֲשֹׁ֥ק שָׂכִ֖יר עָנִ֣י וְאֶבְי֑וֹן מֵאַחֶ֕יךָ א֧וֹ מִגֵּרְךָ֛ אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּאַרְצְךָ֖ בִּשְׁעָרֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not steal from the wage earner who is poor or destitute; from your brothers or your converts who are in your land in your cities.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not oppress a hired servant that is poor and needy, whether he be of thy brethren, or of thy strangers that are in thy land within thy gates.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not oppress a hired laborer who is poor and needy, of your brothers [(One who does so transgresses two negative commandments, this and Leviticus 19:13)], or of your stranger [a righteous convert] that is in your land [(Withholding the hire of animals and utensils is hereby included)], in your gates [(a resident stranger, one who eats carrion)].


###### Deuteronomy 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:15)


בְּיוֹמוֹ֩ תִתֵּ֨ן שְׂכָר֜וֹ וְֽלֹא־תָב֧וֹא עָלָ֣יו הַשֶּׁ֗מֶשׁ כִּ֤י עָנִי֙ ה֔וּא וְאֵלָ֕יו ה֥וּא נֹשֵׂ֖א אֶת־נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וְלֹֽא־יִקְרָ֤א עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְהָיָ֥ה בְךָ֖ חֵֽטְא׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
On its day give his wage and let the sun not set upon it, for he is a poor man, and he places his life in jeopardy for it; [so that] he should not complain about you to Adonoy, and you will have a sin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
At his day thou shalt give him his hire, neither shall the sun go down upon it; for he is poor, and sets his heart upon it: lest he cry against thee to the Lord, and it be sin in thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In his day shall you give his wage, and the sun shall not go down upon it; for he is poor and to it [his wage] he bears his soul [risking death in his work] — that he not call against you to the L-rd; and there will be in you a sin, [in any event, even if he does not call, (but calling hastens punishment)].


###### Deuteronomy 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:16)


לֹֽא־יוּמְת֤וּ אָבוֹת֙ עַל־בָּנִ֔ים וּבָנִ֖ים לֹא־יוּמְת֣וּ עַל־אָב֑וֹת אִ֥ישׁ בְּחֶטְא֖וֹ יוּמָֽתוּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Fathers shall not be executed through sons, and sons shall not be executed through fathers; each to be executed for his sin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Fathers shall not be put to death for children, neither shall children be put to death for fathers: every man shall be put to death for his own sin.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Fathers shall not be put to death by [the testimony of] sons; and sons shall not be put to death by [the testimony of] fathers. Every man for his own sin shall be put to death [(But a child, one who is not yet "a man," (sometimes) dies by the hands of Heaven for his father's sin.)]


###### Deuteronomy 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:17)


לֹ֣א תַטֶּ֔ה מִשְׁפַּ֖ט גֵּ֣ר יָת֑וֹם וְלֹ֣א תַחֲבֹ֔ל בֶּ֖גֶד אַלְמָנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Do not be devious in the judgment of the proselyte or orphan; and you may not secure as collateral, a widow’s garment.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not pervert the judgment of the stranger, or of the fatherless; nor take a widow’s garment as a pledge:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not pervert the judgment of a stranger and an orphan [One who does so transgresses two negative commandments, this and 16:19 (the interdict being repeated here in that it is "easier" to pervert the judgment of the poor)], and you shall not take as a pledge [(not at the time of the loan)] the garment of a widow.


###### Deuteronomy 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:18)


וְזָכַרְתָּ֗ כִּ֣י עֶ֤בֶד הָיִ֙יתָ֙ בְּמִצְרַ֔יִם וַֽיִּפְדְּךָ֛ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ מִשָּׁ֑ם עַל־כֵּ֞ן אָנֹכִ֤י מְצַוְּךָ֙ לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת אֶת־הַדָּבָ֖ר הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Remember that you were a slave in Egypt, and Adonoy, your God, redeemed you from there; that is why I am commanding you to do this thing.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but thou shalt remember that thou wast a bondman in Miżrayim, and the Lord thy God redeemed thee from there: therefore I command thee to do this thing.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall remember that you were a slave in Egypt, and the L-rd your G-d redeemed you from there [to observe His statutes (even if doing so involves monetary loss)]; therefore, I command you to do this thing.


###### Deuteronomy 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:19)


כִּ֣י תִקְצֹר֩ קְצִֽירְךָ֨ בְשָׂדֶ֜ךָ וְשָֽׁכַחְתָּ֧ עֹ֣מֶר בַּשָּׂדֶ֗ה לֹ֤א תָשׁוּב֙ לְקַחְתּ֔וֹ לַגֵּ֛ר לַיָּת֥וֹם וְלָאַלְמָנָ֖ה יִהְיֶ֑ה לְמַ֤עַן יְבָרֶכְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בְּכֹ֖ל מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יָדֶֽיךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you reap your reaping in your field, and you forget a sheaf in the field, you may not return to take it; for the proselyte, for the orphan, and for the widow let it be; in order that Adonoy, your God, will bless you in all your endeavors.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou reapest thy harvest in thy field, and hast forgotten a sheaf in the field, thou shalt not go back to fetch it: it shall be for the stranger, for the fatherless, and for the widow: that the Lord thy God may bless thee in all the work of thy hands.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you reap your harvest in your field, if you forget a sheaf, [but not a stack (a relatively large amount — whence it is derived that a sheaf containing two sa'ah is not considered shikchah ("forgetting")], in the field [including standing grain, part of which he forgot to cut], do not return to take it [(What is behind him is considered shikchah; what is before him, not.)] To the stranger, to the orphan, and to the widow shall it be, so that the L-rd your G-d will bless you in all the work of your hands, [though the shikchah comes to them without specific intent. How much more so will He bless you where there is specific intent!]


###### Deuteronomy 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:20)


כִּ֤י תַחְבֹּט֙ זֵֽיתְךָ֔ לֹ֥א תְפַאֵ֖ר אַחֲרֶ֑יךָ לַגֵּ֛ר לַיָּת֥וֹם וְלָאַלְמָנָ֖ה יִהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you harvest your olive tree, you may not strip it of its glory behind you. for the proselyte, for the orphan, and for the widow let it be.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou beatest thy olive tree, thou shalt not go over the boughs again: it shall be for the stranger, for the fatherless, and for the widow.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you beat your olive tree, you shall not cut off [its fruit (whence it is derived that peah is left from a tree)] after you. [This alludes to shikchah.] To the stranger, to the orphan, and to the widow shall it be.


###### Deuteronomy 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:21)


כִּ֤י תִבְצֹר֙ כַּרְמְךָ֔ לֹ֥א תְעוֹלֵ֖ל אַחֲרֶ֑יךָ לַגֵּ֛ר לַיָּת֥וֹם וְלָאַלְמָנָ֖ה יִהְיֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you harvest your vineyard you may not harvest pygmy vines behind you; for the proselyte, for the orphan, and for the widow let it be.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou gatherest the grapes of thy vineyard, thou shalt not glean it afterwards; it shall be for the stranger, for the fatherless, and for the widow.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you cut your vineyard, do not glean it after you [i.e., Do not take the tender grapes, those which have neither "arm" nor "dripping."] To the stranger, to the orphan, and to the widow shall it be.


###### Deuteronomy 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/24:22)


וְזָ֣כַרְתָּ֔ כִּי־עֶ֥בֶד הָיִ֖יתָ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם עַל־כֵּ֞ן אָנֹכִ֤י מְצַוְּךָ֙ לַעֲשׂ֔וֹת אֶת־הַדָּבָ֖ר הַזֶּֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt; that is why I am commanding you to do this thing.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt remember that thou wast a slave in the land of Miżrayim: therefore I command thee to do this thing.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall remember that you were a slave in the land of Egypt. Therefore, I command you to do this thing.



## Chapter 25

###### Deuteronomy 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:1)


כִּֽי־יִהְיֶ֥ה רִיב֙ בֵּ֣ין אֲנָשִׁ֔ים וְנִגְּשׁ֥וּ אֶל־הַמִּשְׁפָּ֖ט וּשְׁפָט֑וּם וְהִצְדִּ֙יקוּ֙ אֶת־הַצַּדִּ֔יק וְהִרְשִׁ֖יעוּ אֶת־הָרָשָֽׁע׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If a quarrel should occur among men and they bring it to court and they judge them and they determine who is righteous and they convict the villain.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If there be a controversy between men, and
				they come to judgment, that the judges may judge them; then they shall justify the righteous, and condemn the wicked.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If there be a quarrel among men, and they draw near to judgment, [as, in the end, they will be constrained to do, peace not resulting from a quarrel], and they judge them, and they vindicate the righteous one, and they incriminate the wicked one,


###### Deuteronomy 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:2)


וְהָיָ֛ה אִם־בִּ֥ן הַכּ֖וֹת הָרָשָׁ֑ע וְהִפִּיל֤וֹ הַשֹּׁפֵט֙ וְהִכָּ֣הוּ לְפָנָ֔יו כְּדֵ֥י רִשְׁעָת֖וֹ בְּמִסְפָּֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Should the wicked one deserve flogging, the judge shall incline him and have him flogged before him in the amount befitting his crime, with the number near.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, if the wicked man be worthy to be beaten, that the judge shall cause him to lie down, and he shall be beaten in his presence, according to his fault, by a certain number.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then it shall be, if the wicked one is liable to stripes [(as he is when he is in violation of a negative commandment which is unlinked to a (remedial) positive commandment, (like 4)], that the judge shall bend him down [(he receives stripes neither standing nor sitting, but bent over)] and he shall smite him on his front according to his (one) wickedness [i.e., one third of thirty-nine (and, on his back, two thirds)] in number (leading to)


###### Deuteronomy 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:3)


אַרְבָּעִ֥ים יַכֶּ֖נּוּ לֹ֣א יֹסִ֑יף פֶּן־יֹסִ֨יף לְהַכֹּת֤וֹ עַל־אֵ֙לֶּה֙ מַכָּ֣ה רַבָּ֔ה וְנִקְלָ֥ה אָחִ֖יךָ לְעֵינֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Forty is he to have him flogged, he may not add; lest he additionally flog him over these, a great flogging, when your brother will be slighted before you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Forty stripes he may give him, and not exceed: lest, if he should exceed, and beat him above these with many stripes, then thy brother shall be thus made vile before thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
forty [i.e., thirty-nine] shall he smite him, and not more [(an exhortation against striking one's neighbor)]; lest he smite him more than these, a great blow, and your brother be demeaned before your eyes. [All the day he is called "wicked"; once he has been smitten, he is called "your brother."]


###### Deuteronomy 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:4)


לֹא־תַחְסֹ֥ם שׁ֖וֹר בְּדִישֽׁוֹ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You may not muzzle an ox while it threshes.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not muzzle the ox when he treads out the corn.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not muzzle an ox [or any other beast, animal, or bird being worked where food obtains. (A man is not included in the interdict against muzzling)] in its threshing [(All like "threshing" is included, thus excluding foods which do not grow from the ground, etc.)]


###### Deuteronomy 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:5)


כִּֽי־יֵשְׁב֨וּ אַחִ֜ים יַחְדָּ֗ו וּמֵ֨ת אַחַ֤ד מֵהֶם֙ וּבֵ֣ן אֵֽין־ל֔וֹ לֹֽא־תִהְיֶ֧ה אֵֽשֶׁת־הַמֵּ֛ת הַח֖וּצָה לְאִ֣ישׁ זָ֑ר יְבָמָהּ֙ יָבֹ֣א עָלֶ֔יהָ וּלְקָחָ֥הּ ל֛וֹ לְאִשָּׁ֖ה וְיִבְּמָֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If brothers reside together, and one of them dies having no son, let the wife of the dead man not marry outside [the family] to a strange man; her brother-in-law will consummate with her thus marrying her to be his wife, and perform levirate marriage with her.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If brothers dwell together, and one of them die, and have no child, the wife of the dead shall not marry abroad to a stranger: her husband’s brother shall go in to her, and take her to him to wife, and perform the duty of a husband’s brother to her.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When brothers dwell together [i.e., when they had dwelt concurrently in the world (This excludes from yibum (levirate marriage) the wife of one's brother who had never been "in his world"). They must also be "together" vis-à-vis inheritance (This excludes from yibum the wife of one's maternal brother)], and one of them dies, and he has no son [or daughter, or son of a son or daughter of a son, or son of a daughter or daughter of a daughter], then the wife of the dead one shall not be outside to a strange man. Her levir (her husband's brother) shall come upon her and take her for himself as a wife, and he shall have her in levirate marriage.


###### Deuteronomy 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:6)


וְהָיָ֗ה הַבְּכוֹר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תֵּלֵ֔ד יָק֕וּם עַל־שֵׁ֥ם אָחִ֖יו הַמֵּ֑ת וְלֹֽא־יִמָּחֶ֥ה שְׁמ֖וֹ מִיִּשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It shall be that the firstborn, when she is capable of bearing children, shall be established in place of his deceased brother, so that his name may not be obliterated from Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, that the firstborn which she bears shall succeed in the name of his brother who is dead, that his name be not wiped out in Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, the first-born [i.e., the eldest of the brothers] (in an instance) where she can bear [(a woman who cannot bear is excluded from yibum)], he shall be invested in the name of his dead brother [with the latter's inheritance in his father's property], and his name shall not be wiped out of Israel. [(This excludes from yibum the wife of a saris (one who is impotent), whose name is already "wiped out.")


###### Deuteronomy 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:7)


וְאִם־לֹ֤א יַחְפֹּץ֙ הָאִ֔ישׁ לָקַ֖חַת אֶת־יְבִמְתּ֑וֹ וְעָלְתָה֩ יְבִמְתּ֨וֹ הַשַּׁ֜עְרָה אֶל־הַזְּקֵנִ֗ים וְאָֽמְרָה֙ מֵאֵ֨ן יְבָמִ֜י לְהָקִ֨ים לְאָחִ֥יו שֵׁם֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל לֹ֥א אָבָ֖ה יַבְּמִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But if the man will not want to marry his sister-in-law; his sister-in-law must go up to the portal, to the judges, and say, ‘‘My brother-in-law refuses to establish for his brother a name in Yisroel; he is unwilling to do perform levirate marriage with me.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And if the man like not to take his brother’s wife, then let his brother’s wife go up to the gate to the elders, and say, My husband’s brother refuses to raise up to his brother a name in Yisra᾽el, he will not perform the duty of a husband’s brother.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if the man does not desire to take his yevamah, then his yevamah shall go up to the gate [of beth-din] to the elders, and she shall say: My yavam does not desire to invest for his brother a name in Israel; he does not desire to have me in yibum.


###### Deuteronomy 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:8)


וְקָֽרְאוּ־ל֥וֹ זִקְנֵי־עִיר֖וֹ וְדִבְּר֣וּ אֵלָ֑יו וְעָמַ֣ד וְאָמַ֔ר לֹ֥א חָפַ֖צְתִּי לְקַחְתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The judges of his city will call him and converse with him. He shall stand and say, ‘‘I do not want to marry her.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then the elders of his city shall call him, and speak to him: and he shall stand, and say, I do not wish to take her;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the elders of his city shall call to him and they shall speak to him; and he shall stand up and say [in the holy tongue]: I did not desire to take her.


###### Deuteronomy 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:9)


וְנִגְּשָׁ֨ה יְבִמְתּ֣וֹ אֵלָיו֮ לְעֵינֵ֣י הַזְּקֵנִים֒ וְחָלְצָ֤ה נַעֲלוֹ֙ מֵעַ֣ל רַגְל֔וֹ וְיָרְקָ֖ה בְּפָנָ֑יו וְעָֽנְתָה֙ וְאָ֣מְרָ֔ה כָּ֚כָה יֵעָשֶׂ֣ה לָאִ֔ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יִבְנֶ֖ה אֶת־בֵּ֥ית אָחִֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And his sister-in-law will approach him in the sight of the judges, and she will remove his shoe from upon his foot, and spit before him; and she will say aloud, ‘‘This is done to the man who will not build his brother’s family.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then shall his brother’s wife approach him in the presence of the elders, and loose his shoe from off his foot, and spit in his face, and shall answer and say, Thus shall it be done to that man that will not build up his brother’s house.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then his yevamah shall draw near to him before the eyes of the elders, and she shall remove his shoe from his foot, and she shall spit before his face [on the ground], and she shall answer and say [in the holy tongue]: Thus shall it be done with the man who will not build [(he may not perform yibum at a later date)] the house of his brother.


###### Deuteronomy 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:10)


וְנִקְרָ֥א שְׁמ֖וֹ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל בֵּ֖ית חֲל֥וּץ הַנָּֽעַל׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And it will be entitled in Yisroel, the house of the divestiture of the shoe.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And his name shall be called in Yisra᾽el, The house of him that had his shoe loosed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And his name shall be called in Israel: "the house (of him) chalutz hana'al" ("whose shoe was removed"). [It is a mitzvah for all who are standing there to say: "chalutz hana'al."]


###### Deuteronomy 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:11)


כִּֽי־יִנָּצ֨וּ אֲנָשִׁ֤ים יַחְדָּו֙ אִ֣ישׁ וְאָחִ֔יו וְקָֽרְבָה֙ אֵ֣שֶׁת הָֽאֶחָ֔ד לְהַצִּ֥יל אֶת־אִישָׁ֖הּ מִיַּ֣ד מַכֵּ֑הוּ וְשָׁלְחָ֣ה יָדָ֔הּ וְהֶחֱזִ֖יקָה בִּמְבֻשָֽׁיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If men engage in an altercation—a man and his brother—and the wife of one approaches to save her husband from his assailant, and she puts out her hand and grasps his genitals,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When men strive together one with another, and the wife of the one draws near to deliver her husband out of the hand of him that smites him, and putting out her hand, she takes hold of his private parts:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If men strive together, a man and his brother, and the wife of one draw near to rescue her husband from his assailant [(They are bound to come to blows — peace does not result from strife)], and she send forth her hand and seize his privy parts,


###### Deuteronomy 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:12)


וְקַצֹּתָ֖ה אֶת־כַּפָּ֑הּ לֹ֥א תָח֖וֹס עֵינֶֽךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall sever her hand; you are not to have compassion.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then thou shalt cut off her hand, thy eye shall not pity her.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall "cut off her hand" [(monetary compensation (relative to the one shaming and the one shamed)]; you shall not have pity.


###### Deuteronomy 25:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:13)


לֹֽא־יִהְיֶ֥ה לְךָ֛ בְּכִֽיסְךָ֖ אֶ֣בֶן וָאָ֑בֶן גְּדוֹלָ֖ה וּקְטַנָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are not to have for yourself in your pouch varying weight-stones, large and small.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not have in thy bag divers weights, a great and a small.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not have [(If you have false weights, "you shall not have" anything!)] in your bag a stone and a stone [i.e., weights], a large and a small, [the large "contradicting" the small (buying with the large and selling with the small)].


###### Deuteronomy 25:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:14)


לֹא־יִהְיֶ֥ה לְךָ֛ בְּבֵיתְךָ֖ אֵיפָ֣ה וְאֵיפָ֑ה גְּדוֹלָ֖ה וּקְטַנָּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall not have in your house varying measures, large and small.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou shalt not have in thy house divers measures, a great and a small.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You shall not have in your house a measure and a measure, large and small.


###### Deuteronomy 25:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:15)


אֶ֣בֶן שְׁלֵמָ֤ה וָצֶ֙דֶק֙ יִֽהְיֶה־לָּ֔ךְ אֵיפָ֧ה שְׁלֵמָ֛ה וָצֶ֖דֶק יִֽהְיֶה־לָּ֑ךְ לְמַ֙עַן֙ יַאֲרִ֣יכוּ יָמֶ֔יךָ עַ֚ל הָֽאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
A fully accurate, just weight, you shall have, you are to have whole and honest measures; in order that you live long on the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But thou shalt have a perfect and just weight, a perfect and just measure shalt thou have: that thy days may be lengthened in the land which the Lord thy God gives thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[If you have] a whole and just stone, you shall have [much]; a whole and just measure, you shall have, so that your days be prolonged on the land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you.


###### Deuteronomy 25:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:16)


כִּ֧י תוֹעֲבַ֛ת יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ כׇּל־עֹ֣שֵׂה אֵ֑לֶּה כֹּ֖ל עֹ֥שֵׂה עָֽוֶל׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Because Adonoy, your God’s abomination, are all who do these [things]; all who do falsehood.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For all that do such things, and all that do unrighteously, are an abomination to the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the abomination of the L-rd your G-d are all who do these, all who do wrong.


###### Deuteronomy 25:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:17)


זָכ֕וֹר אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־עָשָׂ֥ה לְךָ֖ עֲמָלֵ֑ק בַּדֶּ֖רֶךְ בְּצֵאתְכֶ֥ם מִמִּצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Remember what Amalek perpetrated against you on the way when you were going out of Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remember what ῾Amaleq did to thee by the way, when you were come out of Miżrayim:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember what Amalek did to you on the way when you went out of Egypt [(If you falsify weights and measures, apprehend the onset of the foe.)]


###### Deuteronomy 25:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:18)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר קָֽרְךָ֜ בַּדֶּ֗רֶךְ וַיְזַנֵּ֤ב בְּךָ֙ כׇּל־הַנֶּחֱשָׁלִ֣ים אַֽחֲרֶ֔יךָ וְאַתָּ֖ה עָיֵ֣ף וְיָגֵ֑עַ וְלֹ֥א יָרֵ֖א אֱלֹהִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When they chanced upon you en route struck down your appendage—all the feeble ones behind you—and you were exhausted and wearied, and they had no fear of God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
how he met thee by the way, and smote the hindmost of thee, all that were feeble in thy rear, when thou wast faint and weary; and he feared not God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Who chanced upon you on the way and cut off [the membrums] from you [and cast them heavenwards], (of) all who were faint [from sin] behind you, [the clouds of glory having rejected them], and you were faint [from thirst] and weary [with traveling], and he [Amalek] did not fear G-d [to desist from attacking you].


###### Deuteronomy 25:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/25:19)


וְהָיָ֡ה בְּהָנִ֣יחַ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֣יךָ׀לְ֠ךָ֠ מִכׇּל־אֹ֨יְבֶ֜יךָ מִסָּבִ֗יב בָּאָ֙רֶץ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יְהֹוָה־אֱ֠לֹהֶ֠יךָ נֹתֵ֨ן לְךָ֤ נַחֲלָה֙ לְרִשְׁתָּ֔הּ תִּמְחֶה֙ אֶת־זֵ֣כֶר עֲמָלֵ֔ק מִתַּ֖חַת הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם לֹ֖א תִּשְׁכָּֽח׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy, your God, has given you repose from all your enemies around, in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory to inherit, you shall obliterate the memory of Amalek from beneath the sky; do not forget.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Therefore it shall be, when the Lord thy God has given thee rest from all thy enemies round about, in the land which the Lord thy God gives thee for an inheritance to possess it, that thou shalt blot out the remembrance of ῾Amaleq from under heaven; thou shalt not forget.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when the L-rd your G-d gives you repose from all your enemies roundabout in the land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you as an inheritance to inherit it, that you shall wipe out the remembrance of Amalek from under the heavens, [(so that not even a beast remain of them of which it might be said: "This belonged to Amalek.")] You shall not forget.



## Chapter 26

###### Deuteronomy 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:1)


וְהָיָה֙ כִּֽי־תָב֣וֹא אֶל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לְךָ֖ נַחֲלָ֑ה וִֽירִשְׁתָּ֖הּ וְיָשַׁ֥בְתָּ בָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When it happens that you come to the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you as territory, and you inherit it and settle it;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall be, when thou art come in to the land which the Lord thy God gives thee for an inheritance, and dost possess it and dost dwelt therein:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when you come to the land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you as an inheritance, and you inherit it and dwell in it [(The mitzvah of bikkurim did not obtain until they had conquered and apportioned the land)],


###### Deuteronomy 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:2)


וְלָקַחְתָּ֞ מֵרֵאשִׁ֣ית**׀** כׇּל־פְּרִ֣י הָאֲדָמָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר תָּבִ֧יא מֵֽאַרְצְךָ֛ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לָ֖ךְ וְשַׂמְתָּ֣ בַטֶּ֑נֶא וְהָֽלַכְתָּ֙ אֶל־הַמָּק֔וֹם אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִבְחַר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לְשַׁכֵּ֥ן שְׁמ֖וֹ שָֽׁם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to take of the first of all the fruits of the soil that you bring from your land, that Adonoy, your God, is giving you, and place [it] in a basket; and go to the place that Adonoy, your God, chooses to house His Presence there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou shalt take of the first of all the fruit of the earth, which thou shalt bring of thy land that the Lord thy God gives thee, and shalt put it in a basket, and shalt go to the place which the Lord thy God shall choose to place his name there.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall take of the first [(tying a band around the first fruit to ripen and designating it as bikkurim)] of all the fruits of the earth [the seven distinctive species of Eretz Yisrael] that you bring from your land that the L-rd your G-d gives unto you, and you shall put them in a basket and go to the place wherein the L-rd your G-d chooses to repose His name.


###### Deuteronomy 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:3)


וּבָאתָ֙ אֶל־הַכֹּהֵ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִהְיֶ֖ה בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֑ם וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֵלָ֗יו הִגַּ֤דְתִּי הַיּוֹם֙ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ כִּי־בָ֙אתִי֙ אֶל־הָאָ֔רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִשְׁבַּ֧ע יְהֹוָ֛ה לַאֲבֹתֵ֖ינוּ לָ֥תֶת לָֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to come to the *kohein* who will be [of service] during those days and you will say to him, ‘‘I ascertain today to Adonoy, your God, that I have arrived in the land that Adonoy swore to our forefathers to give us.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt go to the priest that shall be in those days, and say to him, I profess this day to the Lord thy God, that I am come to the country which the Lord swore to our fathers to give us.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall come to the Cohein who shall be in those days [(whoever he may be)], and you shall say to him [(that you are not remiss in gratitude)]: I have professed this day [(Bikkurim are offered once a year, not twice)] to the L-rd your G-d that I have come to the land which the L-rd swore to our forefathers to give us.


###### Deuteronomy 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:4)


וְלָקַ֧ח הַכֹּהֵ֛ן הַטֶּ֖נֶא מִיָּדֶ֑ךָ וְהִ֨נִּיח֔וֹ לִפְנֵ֕י מִזְבַּ֖ח יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The *kohein* shall then take the basket from your hand, and he will place it before the altar of Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the priest shall take the basket out of thy hand, and set it down before the altar of the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the Cohein shall take the basket from your hand [to wave it (The Cohein places his hand under that of the owner and waves it)], and he shall place it before the altar of the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:5)


וְעָנִ֨יתָ וְאָמַרְתָּ֜ לִפְנֵ֣י**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ אֲרַמִּי֙ אֹבֵ֣ד אָבִ֔י וַיֵּ֣רֶד מִצְרַ֔יְמָה וַיָּ֥גׇר שָׁ֖ם בִּמְתֵ֣י מְעָ֑ט וַֽיְהִי־שָׁ֕ם לְג֥וֹי גָּד֖וֹל עָצ֥וּם וָרָֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And you shall then proclaim and say before Adonoy, your God, ‘‘ the Aramite destroy[ed] my forefather, then he descended to Egypt and sojourned there with a tiny community; and there he became a great people, powerful and numerous.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt speak and say before the Lord thy God, An Arammian nomad was my father, and he went down to Miżrayim, and sojourned there with a few, and became there a nation, great, mighty, and populous:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall lift your voice and say before the L-rd your G-d: An Aramean [Lavan] destroyed my father [(Lavan intended to root out the entire nation in pursuing Jacob, and the evil intent of the gentiles is accounted as the act)], and, [after Lavan, others sought to destroy us; for after this] he went down to Egypt. And he lived there in scant number, [seventy souls], and he became there a great, mighty, and populous nation.


###### Deuteronomy 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:6)


וַיָּרֵ֧עוּ אֹתָ֛נוּ הַמִּצְרִ֖ים וַיְעַנּ֑וּנוּ וַיִּתְּנ֥וּ עָלֵ֖ינוּ עֲבֹדָ֥ה קָשָֽׁה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘The Egyptians treated us badly and oppressed us, and they imposed hard labor upon us.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Miżrim dealt ill with us, and afflicted us, and laid upon us hard bondage:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Egypt wrought evil unto us, and they afflicted us, and they placed us under hard labor.


###### Deuteronomy 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:7)


וַנִּצְעַ֕ק אֶל־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֹתֵ֑ינוּ וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ע יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־קֹלֵ֔נוּ וַיַּ֧רְא אֶת־עׇנְיֵ֛נוּ וְאֶת־עֲמָלֵ֖נוּ וְאֶֽת־לַחֲצֵֽנוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘We prayed to Adonoy, God of our forefathers, and Adonoy accepted our prayer, and perceived our oppression, and our labor, and the pressure upon us.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and when we cried to the Lord God of our fathers, the Lord heard our voice, and looked on our affliction, and our labour, and our oppression:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we cried out to the L-rd, the G-d of our fathers. And the L-rd heard our voices, and He saw our affliction, our toil, and our oppression.


###### Deuteronomy 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:8)


וַיּוֹצִאֵ֤נוּ יְהֹוָה֙ מִמִּצְרַ֔יִם בְּיָ֤ד חֲזָקָה֙ וּבִזְרֹ֣עַ נְטוּיָ֔ה וּבְמֹרָ֖א גָּדֹ֑ל וּבְאֹת֖וֹת וּבְמֹפְתִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘And Adonoy took us out of Egypt with a powerful hand and with an extended arm, and with great display, and with signs, and with wonders.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord brought us out of Miżrayim with a mighty hand, and with an outstretched arm, and with great terribleness, and with signs, and with wonders:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd took us out of Egypt with a strong hand and an outstretched arm, and with great fear, and with signs and with wonders.


###### Deuteronomy 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:9)


וַיְבִאֵ֖נוּ אֶל־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וַיִּתֶּן־לָ֙נוּ֙ אֶת־הָאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֔את אֶ֛רֶץ זָבַ֥ת חָלָ֖ב וּדְבָֽשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘And He brought us to this place, and He gave us this land, a land flowing milk and honey.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and he brought us to this place, and gave us this land, a land flowing with milk and honey.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He brought us to this place [the Temple], and He gave us this land, a land flowing with milk and honey.


###### Deuteronomy 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:10)


וְעַתָּ֗ה הִנֵּ֤ה הֵבֵ֙אתִי֙ אֶת־רֵאשִׁית֙ פְּרִ֣י הָאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־נָתַ֥תָּה לִּ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְהִנַּחְתּ֗וֹ לִפְנֵי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲוִ֔יתָ לִפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And now, see! I have brought the first of the fruits of the soil that You have given me Adonoy.’’ And you shall then place it before Adonoy, your God, and you will prostrate yourself in the presence of Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And now, behold, I have brought the firstfruits of the land, which thou, O Lord, hast given me. And thou shalt set it before the Lord thy God, and worship before the Lord thy God:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And now, behold, I have brought the first of the fruit of the land, which You, O L-rd, have given to me. Then you shall place it before the L-rd your G-d, [(the bearer having taken it after the Cohein's waving and having held it in his hand during his declaration, after which it was again waved)], and you shall bow down before the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:11)


וְשָׂמַחְתָּ֣ בְכׇל־הַטּ֗וֹב אֲשֶׁ֧ר נָֽתַן־לְךָ֛ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ וּלְבֵיתֶ֑ךָ אַתָּה֙ וְהַלֵּוִ֔י וְהַגֵּ֖ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּקִרְבֶּֽךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall rejoice with all the good that Adonoy, your God, gave you and your household; you, the levi, and the proselyte in your midst.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt rejoice in every good thing which the Lord thy God has given thee, and thy house, thou, and the Levite, and the stranger that is among you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall rejoice in all the good that the L-rd your G-d has given you and your household [(The bikkurim declaration is recited only in a time of rejoicing — from Shavuoth until Succoth — when one gathers his grain, his fruits, his wine and his oil. From Succoth on, he brings the bikkurim but does not recite the declaration)], you and the Levite [(from what was planted in their cities)], and the stranger in your midst. [He brings bikkurim, but does not recite the declaration, ("to our forefathers" (3), not applying in this instance.)]


###### Deuteronomy 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:12)


כִּ֣י תְכַלֶּ֞ה לַ֠עְשֵׂ֠ר אֶת־כׇּל־מַעְשַׂ֧ר תְּבוּאָתְךָ֛ בַּשָּׁנָ֥ה הַשְּׁלִישִׁ֖ת שְׁנַ֣ת הַֽמַּעֲשֵׂ֑ר וְנָתַתָּ֣ה לַלֵּוִ֗י לַגֵּר֙ לַיָּת֣וֹם וְלָֽאַלְמָנָ֔ה וְאָכְל֥וּ בִשְׁעָרֶ֖יךָ וְשָׂבֵֽעוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you finish tithing all the tithes of your produce in the third year, the year of the tithe; and you have given to the Levi, to the proselyte, to the orphan, and to the widow, so that they may eat within your portals to their satisfaction;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When thou hast made an end of tithing all the tithes of thy produce in the third year, which is the year of tithing, and hast given it the Levite, the stranger, the fatherless, and the widow, that they may eat within thy gates, and be replete;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When you finish tithing [(by Pesach of the fourth year)] all the tithe of your produce of the third year, the year of the tithe [i.e., when there obtains only one tithe (ma'aser rishon) of the two tithes (ma'aser rishon and ma'aser sheni) of the two preceding years, (ma'aser ani replacing ma'aser sheni in the third year)], and you have given [ma'aser rishon] to the Levite, and [ma'aser ani ("the poor tithe")] to the stranger, the orphan, and the widow, that they shall eat in your gates and be sated [(Give them enough for "satiety")],


###### Deuteronomy 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:13)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֡ לִפְנֵי֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ בִּעַ֧רְתִּי הַקֹּ֣דֶשׁ מִן־הַבַּ֗יִת וְגַ֨ם נְתַתִּ֤יו לַלֵּוִי֙ וְלַגֵּר֙ לַיָּת֣וֹם וְלָאַלְמָנָ֔ה כְּכׇל־מִצְוָתְךָ֖ אֲשֶׁ֣ר צִוִּיתָ֑נִי לֹֽא־עָבַ֥רְתִּי מִמִּצְוֺתֶ֖יךָ וְלֹ֥א שָׁכָֽחְתִּי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You shall declare before Adonoy, your God, ‘‘I have divested my estate of sacred material, and I have also presented it to the Levi and also to the proselyte, the orphan, and the widow, totally according to Your command that You commanded me; I did not transgress any of Your commandments nor did I forget.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then thou shalt say before the Lord thy God, I have removed the hallowed things out of my house, and also have given them to the Levite, and to the stranger, to the fatherless, and to the widow, according to all thy commandments which thou hast commanded me: I have not transgressed thy commandments, neither have I forgotten them:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then you shall say before the L-rd your G-d [(Declare that you have given your tithes)]: I have removed the holy thing [ma'aser sheni and neta revai (see Leviticus 27:30 and 19:24)] from the house [If he delayed bringing his tithes of the first two years to Jerusalem, he must do so now], and I have also [(Terumah and bikkurim are hereby included)] given it [ma'aser rishon] to the Levite, and [ma'aser ani] to the stranger, the orphan, and the widow, according to all of Your mitzvah that You commanded me [(I gave all of the tithes in their proper order)]. I did not depart from Your mitzvoth [(I did not tithe from one species for another or from the new for the old)], and I did not forget [to bless You at the tithing].


###### Deuteronomy 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:14)


לֹא־אָכַ֨לְתִּי בְאֹנִ֜י מִמֶּ֗נּוּ וְלֹא־בִעַ֤רְתִּי מִמֶּ֙נּוּ֙ בְּטָמֵ֔א וְלֹא־נָתַ֥תִּי מִמֶּ֖נּוּ לְמֵ֑ת שָׁמַ֗עְתִּי בְּקוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהָ֔י עָשִׂ֕יתִי כְּכֹ֖ל אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּיתָֽנִי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I did not eat of it when grieving, nor did I devour it when ritually defiled, nor did I make use of it for the dead; I have heeded the voice of Adonoy, my God; I have fulfilled everything that You commanded me.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I have not eaten of it in my mourning, neither have I consumed any part of it when unclean, nor given of it for the dead: but I have hearkened to the voice of the Lord my God, and have done according to all that thou hast commanded me.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I did not eat in my mourning of it, [it being forbidden to a mourner], and I did not remove of it in uncleanliness, [whether I were unclean, and it (the tithe) clean; or, I, clean, and it, unclean], and I did not give of it for the dead [for the making of casket and shrouds]. I have hearkened to the voice of the L-rd my G-d [(I have brought it to the Temple)]. I have done according to all that You commanded me [(I rejoiced, and I rejoiced others therewith)].


###### Deuteronomy 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:15)


הַשְׁקִ֩יפָה֩ מִמְּע֨וֹן קׇדְשְׁךָ֜ מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֗יִם וּבָרֵ֤ךְ אֶֽת־עַמְּךָ֙ אֶת־יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאֵת֙ הָאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תָּה לָ֑נוּ כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר נִשְׁבַּ֙עְתָּ֙ לַאֲבֹתֵ֔ינוּ אֶ֛רֶץ זָבַ֥ת חָלָ֖ב וּדְבָֽשׁ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
View, from Your sacred residence, from the heavens, and bless Your people, Yisroel and the soil which You have given us, as You swore to our forefathers, a land flowing with milk and honey.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Look down from thy holy habitation, from heaven, and bless thy people Yisra᾽el, and the land which thou hast given us, as thou didst swear to our fathers, a land flowing with milk and honey.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Look down from Your holy abode, from the heavens, and bless Your people, Israel, and the land which You have given us [We have done (with respect to the tithes) what You decreed upon us — You do what You have promised us (viz. Leviticus 26:3-4)] — just as You swore unto our fathers a land flowing with milk and honey [and You fulfilled what You promised them].


###### Deuteronomy 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:16)


הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֗ה יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ מְצַוְּךָ֧ לַעֲשׂ֛וֹת אֶת־הַחֻקִּ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה וְאֶת־הַמִּשְׁפָּטִ֑ים וְשָׁמַרְתָּ֤ וְעָשִׂ֙יתָ֙ אוֹתָ֔ם בְּכׇל־לְבָבְךָ֖ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
This very day, Adonoy, your God, commands you to perform these statutes and the laws; you shall observe and perform them wholeheartedly and with your whole being.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
This day the Lord thy God
				has commanded thee to do these statutes and judgments: thou shalt therefore keep and do them with all thy heart, and with all thy soul.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
This day the L-rd your G-d commands you to do these statutes and the judgments [(Let them always be new in your eyes, as if He is commanding you "this day.")] [A heavenly voice blesses the bringer of bikkurim, viz.]: And [as you have brought bikkurim this day], may you [merit to] keep and do them [the following year] with all your heart and with all your soul.


###### Deuteronomy 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:17)


אֶת־יְהֹוָ֥ה הֶאֱמַ֖רְתָּ הַיּ֑וֹם לִהְיוֹת֩ לְךָ֨ לֵֽאלֹהִ֜ים וְלָלֶ֣כֶת בִּדְרָכָ֗יו וְלִשְׁמֹ֨ר חֻקָּ֧יו וּמִצְוֺתָ֛יו וּמִשְׁפָּטָ֖יו וְלִשְׁמֹ֥עַ בְּקֹלֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, you have distinguised today to be a God for you and to go in His ways and to guard His statutes and His commandments and His laws, and to obey Him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Thou hast avouched the Lord this day to be thy God, and to walk in his ways, and to keep his statutes, and his commandments, and his judgments, and to hearken to his voice:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd did single you out this day to be to you as a G-d, and to walk in His ways, and to keep His statutes, and His mitzvoth, and His judgments, and to heed His voice.


###### Deuteronomy 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:18)


וַֽיהֹוָ֞ה הֶאֱמִֽירְךָ֣ הַיּ֗וֹם לִהְי֥וֹת לוֹ֙ לְעַ֣ם סְגֻלָּ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּר־לָ֑ךְ וְלִשְׁמֹ֖ר כׇּל־מִצְוֺתָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy has distinguished you today to be for Him a treasured people as He said to you, and to guard all His commandments.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord has avouched thee this day to be a people for his own possession, as he has promised thee, and that thou shouldst keep all his commandments;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd did single you out this day to be to Him as a chosen people, as He spoke to you [Exodus 19:5], and to keep all of His mitzvoth.


###### Deuteronomy 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/26:19)


וּֽלְתִתְּךָ֣ עֶלְי֗וֹן עַ֤ל כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֔ה לִתְהִלָּ֖ה וּלְשֵׁ֣ם וּלְתִפְאָ֑רֶת וְלִֽהְיֹתְךָ֧ עַם־קָדֹ֛שׁ לַיהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֵּֽר׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And to place you superior to all the nations whom He made, for praise and for renown and for glory, and so that you may be a people sanctified to Adonoy, your God, as He said.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and to make thee high above all nations which he has made, in praise, and in name, and in honour; and that thou mayst be a holy people to the Lord thy God, as he has spoken.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to exalt you above all of the nations that He made — for praise, name, and glory — and so that you be a holy people to the L-rd your G-d, as He spoke [Exodus 20:26].

אֵ֠לֶּה יַֽעַמְד֞וּ לְבָרֵ֤ךְ אֶת־הָעָם֙ עַל־הַ֣ר גְּרִזִ֔ים בְּעׇבְרְכֶ֖ם אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֑ן שִׁמְעוֹן֙ וְלֵוִ֣י וִֽיהוּדָ֔ה וְיִשָּׂשכָ֖ר וְיוֹסֵ֥ף וּבִנְיָמִֽן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘The following will stand to bless the people on Mount Gerizim, upon your crossing the Yardein: Shimon and Levi and Yehudah, and Yisachar and Yoseif and Binyomin.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These shall stand upon mount Gerizzim to bless the people, when you are come over the Yarden; Shim῾on, and Levi, and Yehuda, and Yissakhar, and Yosef, and Binyamin:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These shall stand for the blessing of the people on Mount Gerizim when you cross over the Jordan: Shimon, Levi, Yehudah, Yissachar, Yosef, and Binyamin [Six tribes went up to the top of Mount Gerizim and six to the top of Mount Eival, the Cohanim and the Levites standing with the ark, below, in the middle. The Levites turned their faces towards Mount Gerizim and opened with blessing, viz.: "Blessed is the man who does not make a graven image or a molten image," and all (on both mountains) answered Amen. Then they turned to Mount Eival and recited the corresponding curse, viz. (15): "Cursed is the man who makes a graven or a molten image, etc.", and so with all, until (26): "Cursed is he who does not fulfill, etc." (see 11:29)]

אָר֣וּר הָאִ֡ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יַעֲשֶׂה֩ פֶ֨סֶל וּמַסֵּכָ֜ה תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֗ה מַעֲשֵׂ֛ה יְדֵ֥י חָרָ֖שׁ וְשָׂ֣ם בַּסָּ֑תֶר וְעָנ֧וּ כׇל־הָעָ֛ם וְאָמְר֖וּ אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is the man who makes a statue or a molten image, Adonoy’s abomination, the handiwork of a craftsman, and places [it] in a secret [place];’ the entire people will respond and say, *Amein.*

###### Deuteronomy 27:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:16)


אָר֕וּר מַקְלֶ֥ה אָבִ֖יו וְאִמּ֑וֹ וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever reviles his father or his mother,’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that dishonours his father or his mother. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Deuteronomy 27:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:17)


אָר֕וּר מַסִּ֖יג גְּב֣וּל רֵעֵ֑הוּ וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever casts aside the boundary of his fellow;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Deuteronomy 27:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:18)


אָר֕וּר מַשְׁגֶּ֥ה עִוֵּ֖ר בַּדָּ֑רֶךְ וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever misleads the blind about the way;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that makes the blind to wander out of the way. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

footnotes:
¹ (ספק פרשה סתומה בכתר ארם צובה)


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever distorts justice for a convert, orphan, or widow;’ and entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that perverts the judgment of the stranger, fatherless, and widow. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who perverts the judgment of the stranger, the orphan, and the widow. And all the people shall say: Amen.


###### Deuteronomy 27:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:20)


אָר֗וּר שֹׁכֵב֙ עִם־אֵ֣שֶׁת אָבִ֔יו כִּ֥י גִלָּ֖ה כְּנַ֣ף אָבִ֑יו וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever has intercourse with his father’s wife, because he exposed the corner of his father[’s garment];’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that lies with his father’s wife; because he uncovers his father’s skirt. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who lies with his father's wife, for he has uncovered his father's lap. And all the people shall say: Amen.


###### Deuteronomy 27:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:21)


אָר֕וּר שֹׁכֵ֖ב עִם־כׇּל־בְּהֵמָ֑ה וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever has intercourse with any animal;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that lies with any manner of beast. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who lies with any beast. And all the people shall say: Amen.


###### Deuteronomy 27:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:22)


אָר֗וּר שֹׁכֵב֙ עִם־אֲחֹת֔וֹ בַּת־אָבִ֖יו א֣וֹ בַת־אִמּ֑וֹ וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever has intercourse with his sister, daughter of his father or daughter of his mother;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that lies with his sister, the daughter of his father, or the daughter of his mother. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who lies with his sister, the daughter of his father or the daughter of his mother. And all the people shall say: Amen.


###### Deuteronomy 27:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:23)


אָר֕וּר שֹׁכֵ֖ב עִם־חֹֽתַנְתּ֑וֹ וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever has intercourse with his father’s-in-law wife;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that lies with his mother in law. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who lies with his mother-in-law. And all the people shall say: Amen.


###### Deuteronomy 27:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:24)


אָר֕וּר מַכֵּ֥ה רֵעֵ֖הוּ בַּסָּ֑תֶר וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever strikes his fellow in secret;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that smites his neighbour secretly. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who smites his neighbor [i.e., who slanders him] in secret. And all the people shall say: Amen.


###### Deuteronomy 27:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:25)


אָרוּר֙ לֹקֵ֣חַ שֹׁ֔חַד לְהַכּ֥וֹת נֶ֖פֶשׁ דָּ֣ם נָקִ֑י וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever accepts a bribe to kill an innocent person;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that takes a bribe to slay an innocent person. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who takes graft to smite a soul of innocent blood. And all the people shall say: Amen.


###### Deuteronomy 27:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/27:26)


אָר֗וּר אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹא־יָקִ֛ים אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֥י הַתּוֹרָֽה־הַזֹּ֖את לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת אוֹתָ֑ם וְאָמַ֥ר כׇּל־הָעָ֖ם אָמֵֽן׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘Cursed is whoever does not uphold the statements of this Torah to perform them;’ and the entire people will say, *Amein.*


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Cursed be he that does not maintain all the words of this Tora to do them. And all the people shall say, Amen.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cursed is he who does not fulfill the words of this Torah to do them. And all the people shall say: Amen. [The entire Torah is herein included, and they accepted it under imprecation and oath.]



## Chapter 28

###### Deuteronomy 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:1)


וְהָיָ֗ה אִם־שָׁמ֤וֹעַ תִּשְׁמַע֙ בְּקוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לִשְׁמֹ֤ר לַעֲשׂוֹת֙ אֶת־כׇּל־מִצְוֺתָ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֛ר אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּ֑וֹם וּנְתָ֨נְךָ֜ יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ עֶלְי֔וֹן עַ֖ל כׇּל־גּוֹיֵ֥י הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you hearken and obey Adonoy, your God, to be sure to perform all of His commandments that I am commanding you today; Adonoy, your God, will place you superior to all the nations of the earth.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall come to pass, if thou shalt hearken diligently to the voice of the Lord thy God, to observe and to do all his commandments which I command thee this day, that the Lord thy God will set thee on high above all the nations of the earth:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, if you heed the voice of the L-rd your G-d to observe to do all of His mitzvoth that I command you today, then the L-rd your G-d shall exalt you above all the nations of the earth.


###### Deuteronomy 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:2)


וּבָ֧אוּ עָלֶ֛יךָ כׇּל־הַבְּרָכ֥וֹת הָאֵ֖לֶּה וְהִשִּׂיגֻ֑ךָ כִּ֣י תִשְׁמַ֔ע בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
All these blessings will come upon you and will reach you, when you obey Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and all these blessings shall come on thee, and overtake thee, if thou shalt hearken to the voice of the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there shall come upon you all of these blessings and they shall cleave unto you, if you heed the voice of the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:3)


בָּר֥וּךְ אַתָּ֖ה בָּעִ֑יר וּבָר֥וּךְ אַתָּ֖ה בַּשָּׂדֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Blessed are you in the city, and blessed are you in the field.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blessed shalt thou be in the city, and blessed shalt thou be in the field.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessed shall you be in the city, and blessed shall you be in the field.


###### Deuteronomy 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:4)


בָּר֧וּךְ פְּרִֽי־בִטְנְךָ֛ וּפְרִ֥י אַדְמָתְךָ֖ וּפְרִ֣י בְהֶמְתֶּ֑ךָ שְׁגַ֥ר אֲלָפֶ֖יךָ וְעַשְׁתְּר֥וֹת צֹאנֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Blessed is the fruit of your belly, and the produce of your soil, and the offspring of your animals, the issue of your cattle and the flocks of your sheep and goats.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blessed shall be the fruit of thy body, and the fruit of thy ground, and the fruit of thy beasts, the increase of thy cattle, and the flocks of thy sheep.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessed shall be the fruit of your womb, the fruit of your land, and the fruit of your beasts, the offspring of your cattle, and the flocks of your sheep.


###### Deuteronomy 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:5)


בָּר֥וּךְ טַנְאֲךָ֖ וּמִשְׁאַרְתֶּֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Blessed will be your [fruit] basket and your solid foods.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blessed shall be thy basket and thy store.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessed shall be your fruits and your remainder [i.e., what is dry and does not ooze out of the vessel].


###### Deuteronomy 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:6)


בָּר֥וּךְ אַתָּ֖ה בְּבֹאֶ֑ךָ וּבָר֥וּךְ אַתָּ֖ה בְּצֵאתֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will be blessed at your arrival, and blessed at your departure.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Blessed shalt thou be when thou comest in, and blessed shalt thou be when thou goest out.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Blessed shall you be in your coming in and blessed shall you be in your going out [You will leave the world without sin, as when you entered it.]


###### Deuteronomy 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:7)


יִתֵּ֨ן יְהֹוָ֤ה אֶת־אֹיְבֶ֙יךָ֙ הַקָּמִ֣ים עָלֶ֔יךָ נִגָּפִ֖ים לְפָנֶ֑יךָ בְּדֶ֤רֶךְ אֶחָד֙ יֵצְא֣וּ אֵלֶ֔יךָ וּבְשִׁבְעָ֥ה דְרָכִ֖ים יָנ֥וּסוּ לְפָנֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will set your enemies who rise against you smitten before you; by one road will they approach you, but by seven roads they will flee from you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord shall cause thy enemies that rise up against thee to be smitten before thy face: they shall come out against thee one way, and flee before thee seven ways.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd shall render your foes, who rise up against you, smitten before you. By one way shall they go forth against you, and by seven ways [in the manner of the panic-stricken] shall they flee before you.


###### Deuteronomy 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:8)


יְצַ֨ו יְהֹוָ֤ה אִתְּךָ֙ אֶת־הַבְּרָכָ֔ה בַּאֲסָמֶ֕יךָ וּבְכֹ֖ל מִשְׁלַ֣ח יָדֶ֑ךָ וּבֵ֣רַכְךָ֔ בָּאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ נֹתֵ֥ן לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will order upon you the blessing in your storehouses and in all your commerce; and He will bless you in the land that Adonoy, your God, is giving you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord shall command the blessing upon thee in thy barns, and in all that thou settest thy hand unto; and he shall bless thee in the land which the Lord thy God gives thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd shall command unto you the blessing in your store-houses and in all to which you put your hand, and He shall bless you in the land that the L-rd your G-d gives to you.


###### Deuteronomy 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:9)


יְקִֽימְךָ֨ יְהֹוָ֥ה לוֹ֙ לְעַ֣ם קָד֔וֹשׁ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר נִֽשְׁבַּֽע־לָ֑ךְ כִּ֣י תִשְׁמֹ֗ר אֶת־מִצְוֺת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וְהָלַכְתָּ֖ בִּדְרָכָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will establish you for Himself as a holy people as He swore to you; when you will guard the commandments of Adonoy, your God, and will go in His ways.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord shall establish thee a holy people 				to himself, as he has sworn to thee, if thou shalt keep the commandments of the Lord thy God, and walk in his ways.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd shall raise you as a holy people unto Him, as He swore to you, if you heed the mitzvoth of the L-rd your G-d and you walk in His ways.


###### Deuteronomy 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:10)


וְרָאוּ֙ כׇּל־עַמֵּ֣י הָאָ֔רֶץ כִּ֛י שֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה נִקְרָ֣א עָלֶ֑יךָ וְיָֽרְא֖וּ מִמֶּֽךָּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And all the peoples of the earth will see that you are called by Adonoy’s Name and they will fear you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And all people of the earth shall see that thou art called by the name of the Lord; and they shall be afraid of thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the peoples of the earth shall see that the name of the L-rd is called upon you, and they shall fear you.


###### Deuteronomy 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:11)


וְהוֹתִרְךָ֤ יְהֹוָה֙ לְטוֹבָ֔ה בִּפְרִ֧י בִטְנְךָ֛ וּבִפְרִ֥י בְהֶמְתְּךָ֖ וּבִפְרִ֣י אַדְמָתֶ֑ךָ עַ֚ל הָאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִשְׁבַּ֧ע יְהֹוָ֛ה לַאֲבֹתֶ֖יךָ לָ֥תֶת לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will give you surplus as benefit in the fruit of your belly, and the offspring of your animals, and the produce of your soil; upon the land that Adonoy swore to your forefathers to give you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord shall make thee plenteous in goods, in the fruit of thy body, and in the fruit of thy cattle, and in the fruit of thy ground, in the land which the Lord swore to thy fathers to give thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd shall cause you to remain for the good — in the fruit of your womb, and the fruit of your beasts, and the fruit of your land — on the land that the L-rd swore to your fathers to give to you.


###### Deuteronomy 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:12)


יִפְתַּ֣ח יְהֹוָ֣ה׀לְ֠ךָ֠ אֶת־אוֹצָר֨וֹ הַטּ֜וֹב אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֗יִם לָתֵ֤ת מְטַֽר־אַרְצְךָ֙ בְּעִתּ֔וֹ וּלְבָרֵ֕ךְ אֵ֖ת כׇּל־מַעֲשֵׂ֣ה יָדֶ֑ךָ וְהִלְוִ֙יתָ֙ גּוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֔ים וְאַתָּ֖ה לֹ֥א תִלְוֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will open for you His good storehouse, the sky, to give you your land’s rain on time and to bless all your endeavors; and you will lend to many nations, but you will not borrow.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord shall open to thee his good treasure, the heaven, to give the rain to thy land in its season, and to bless all the work of thy hand: and thou shalt lend to many nations, and thou shalt not borrow.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd shall open for you His goodly treasure trove, the heavens, to give to you the rain of your land in its time and to bless all to which you put your hand, and you shall lend many nations, and you shall not borrow.


###### Deuteronomy 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:13)


וּנְתָֽנְךָ֨ יְהֹוָ֤ה לְרֹאשׁ֙ וְלֹ֣א לְזָנָ֔ב וְהָיִ֙יתָ֙ רַ֣ק לְמַ֔עְלָה וְלֹ֥א תִהְיֶ֖ה לְמָ֑טָּה כִּֽי־תִשְׁמַ֞ע אֶל־מִצְוֺ֣ת**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֧י מְצַוְּךָ֛ הַיּ֖וֹם לִשְׁמֹ֥ר וְלַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will place you at the head, not at the tail; and you will be solely on top, you will not be on the bottom; when you heed the commandments of Adonoy, your God, that I am commanding you today to guard and to perform.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord shall make thee the head, and not the tail; and thou shalt be above only, and thou shalt not be beneath; if thou hearken to the commandments of the Lord thy God, which I command thee this day, to observe and to do them:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd shall make you a head and not a tail. And you will be only above, and you will not be below — if you heed the mitzvoth of the L-rd your G-d that I command you this day, to keep and to do.


###### Deuteronomy 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:14)


וְלֹ֣א תָס֗וּר מִכׇּל־הַדְּבָרִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֜י מְצַוֶּ֥ה אֶתְכֶ֛ם הַיּ֖וֹם יָמִ֣ין וּשְׂמֹ֑אול לָלֶ֗כֶת אַחֲרֵ֛י אֱלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִ֖ים לְעׇבְדָֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And you do not deviate from all the statements that I am commanding you today to the right or to the left, to follow other gods to serve them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thou shalt not go aside from any of the words which I command thee this day, to the right hand, or to the left, to go after other gods to serve them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall not deviate from all of the things that I command you this day, right and left, to go after other gods to serve them.


###### Deuteronomy 28:15

Should you not obey Adonoy, your God, to guard to perform all His commandments and His statutes that I am commanding you today; then all these curses will come upon you and will reach you.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

cursed shalt thou be in the city, and cursed shalt thou be in the field.

Cursed is your [fruit] basket and cursed are your solid foods.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will visit upon you ruin, panic, and frustration in all the endeavors you undertake, until you are destroyed and until you are swiftly removed because of the wickedness of your deeds [in] that you forsook Me.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

Adonoy will attach the plague to you until He exterminates you from upon the land that you are coming there to inherit.

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord shall smite thee with a consumption, and with a fever, and with an inflammation, and with an extreme burning, and with the sword, and with blasting, and with mildew; and they shall pursue thee until thou perish.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd will smite you with shachefeth [swelling and blowing up of the flesh], and with kadachath [a fever], and with daleketh [hotter than kadachath], and with charchur [internal fever and constant thirst], and with the sword, and with [field blights]: shidafon [the east wind] and yerakon [dryness (the grain turning pale)], and they shall pursue you until you go lost.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your skies over your head shall be as brass, and your earth under you, as iron [as opposed to (Leviticus 26:19): "And I shall make your skies as iron and your earth as brass," these being stated by the L-rd Himself, and being more severe, in that iron does not "sweat" (implying that the skies will provide no moisture whatsoever) and brass does "sweat" implying that the fruit will rot), whereas the curses here are stated by Moses, who attenuated them.]

A woman will you marry and another man will lie with her; a house will you build and will not live in it; a vineyard will you plant and will not redeem it[s fruit].

יַכְּכָ֨ה יְהֹוָ֜ה בִּשְׁחִ֣ין רָ֗ע עַל־הַבִּרְכַּ֙יִם֙ וְעַל־הַשֹּׁקַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־תוּכַ֖ל לְהֵרָפֵ֑א מִכַּ֥ף רַגְלְךָ֖ וְעַ֥ד קׇדְקֳדֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will plague you with bad boils on the knees and on the legs from which you will be unable to be healed; from the sole of your foot till your skull.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord shall smite thee in the knees, and in the legs, with a festering eruption that cannot be healed, from the sole of thy foot to the top of thy head.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd will smite you with sore boils on the knees and on the thighs, which you will not be able to heal, from the sole of your foot until your crown.
