###### Deuteronomy 28:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:55)


מִתֵּ֣ת**׀** לְאַחַ֣ד מֵהֶ֗ם מִבְּשַׂ֤ר בָּנָיו֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יֹאכֵ֔ל מִבְּלִ֥י הִשְׁאִֽיר־ל֖וֹ כֹּ֑ל בְּמָצוֹר֙ וּבְמָצ֔וֹק אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָצִ֥יק לְךָ֛ אֹיִבְךָ֖ בְּכׇל־שְׁעָרֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From giving to any of them of the flesh of his sons that he is eating for [fear of] not leaving everything for himself; in siege and in distress that your enemy will distress you in all your cities.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so that he will not give to any of them of the flesh of his children whom he shall eat, when he has nothing left him; because of the siege, and of the distress, with which thy enemies shall distress thee in all thy gates.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Not to give to one of them of the flesh of his sons that he will eat, nothing being left to him in the siege and in the extremity wherewith your foe will oppress you in all of your gates.


###### Deuteronomy 28:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:56)


הָרַכָּ֨ה בְךָ֜ וְהָעֲנֻגָּ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־נִסְּתָ֤ה כַף־רַגְלָהּ֙ הַצֵּ֣ג עַל־הָאָ֔רֶץ מֵהִתְעַנֵּ֖ג וּמֵרֹ֑ךְ תֵּרַ֤ע עֵינָהּ֙ בְּאִ֣ישׁ חֵיקָ֔הּ וּבִבְנָ֖הּ וּבְבִתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The soft female among you and the indulged one the sole of whose foot never experienced standing on the ground out of indulgence and out of softness; her eye will begrudge the husband of her bosom, and her son and daughter.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The tender and delicate woman among you, who would not venture to set the sole of her foot upon the ground for fastidiousness and delicateness, her eye shall be evil towards the husband of her bosom, and towards her son, and towards her daughter,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The woman of extreme delicacy among you, who would not venture to set the sole of her foot on the ground in the exquisiteness of her delicacy — her eye shall be grudging to the husband of her bosom, and to her [older] son and daughter,


###### Deuteronomy 28:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:57)


וּֽבְשִׁלְיָתָ֞הּ הַיּוֹצֵ֣ת**׀** מִבֵּ֣ין רַגְלֶ֗יהָ וּבְבָנֶ֙יהָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תֵּלֵ֔ד כִּֽי־תֹאכְלֵ֥ם בְּחֹסֶר־כֹּ֖ל בַּסָּ֑תֶר בְּמָצוֹר֙ וּבְמָצ֔וֹק אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָצִ֥יק לְךָ֛ אֹיִבְךָ֖ בִּשְׁעָרֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And her infant who emerges from between her legs, and her children whom she bears, when she eats them for lack of everything, secretly; in siege and in distress that your enemy will distress you in your cities.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and towards her afterbirth that comes out from her, and towards her children whom she shall bear: for she shall eat them for want of all things in secret; because of the siege and distress, wherewith thy enemy shall distress thee in thy gates.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And to her young who issue from between her legs, and to her children whom she will beget; for she will eat them in want of all, in secret, [and she will not share them with her remaining children], in the siege and in the extremity wherewith your foes will oppress you in your gates.


###### Deuteronomy 28:58

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:58](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:58)


אִם־לֹ֨א תִשְׁמֹ֜ר לַעֲשׂ֗וֹת אֶת־כׇּל־דִּבְרֵי֙ הַתּוֹרָ֣ה הַזֹּ֔את הַכְּתֻבִ֖ים בַּסֵּ֣פֶר הַזֶּ֑ה לְ֠יִרְאָ֠ה אֶת־הַשֵּׁ֞ם הַנִּכְבָּ֤ד וְהַנּוֹרָא֙ הַזֶּ֔ה אֵ֖ת יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If you will not guard to perform all the statements of this Torah that are written in this book to fear the respected and awesome Name, of Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thou wilt not observe to do all the words of this Tora that are written in this book, that thou mayst fear this glorious and fearful name, the Lord thy God;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you do not observe to do all the words of this Torah that are written in this scroll to fear this honored, awesome Name, the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 28:59

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:59](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:59)


וְהִפְלָ֤א יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־מַכֹּ֣תְךָ֔ וְאֵ֖ת מַכּ֣וֹת זַרְעֶ֑ךָ מַכּ֤וֹת גְּדֹלֹת֙ וְנֶ֣אֱמָנ֔וֹת וׇחֳלָיִ֖ם רָעִ֥ים וְנֶאֱמָנִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will strike you with prodigous blows and blows on your offspring, blows, great and dependable and illnesses harmful and dependable.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then the Lord will make thy plagues remarkable, and the plagues of thy offspring, even great plagues, and of long continuance, and severe sicknesses, and of long continuance.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the L-rd will make distinctive [from all other plagues] your plagues, and the plagues of your seed, plagues great and trusted ["to fulfill their embassy"], and sore, trusty illnesses.


###### Deuteronomy 28:60

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:60](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:60)


וְהֵשִׁ֣יב בְּךָ֗ אֵ֚ת כׇּל־מַדְוֵ֣ה מִצְרַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָגֹ֖רְתָּ מִפְּנֵיהֶ֑ם וְדָבְק֖וּ בָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He will bring back for you all the Egyptian diseases which you dreaded and they will cling to you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moreover he will bring upon thee all the diseases of Miżrayim, which thou wast afraid of; and they shall cleave to thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd will send against you all the afflictions [i.e., the plagues] of Egypt, which you feared [would come upon you], and they will cleave to you.


###### Deuteronomy 28:61

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:61](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:61)


גַּ֤ם כׇּל־חֳלִי֙ וְכׇל־מַכָּ֔ה אֲשֶׁר֙ לֹ֣א כָת֔וּב בְּסֵ֖פֶר הַתּוֹרָ֣ה הַזֹּ֑את יַעְלֵ֤ם יְהֹוָה֙ עָלֶ֔יךָ עַ֖ד הִשָּׁמְדָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Also every illness and every plague that is not written in the book of this Torah, will Adonoy raise up against you until you are destroyed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Also every sickness, and every plague, which is not written in the book of this Tora, them will the Lord bring upon thee, until thou art destroyed.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Also, every sickness and every plague which is not written in the scroll of this Torah, the L-rd will bring them up against you until you are destroyed.


###### Deuteronomy 28:62

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:62](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:62)


וְנִשְׁאַרְתֶּם֙ בִּמְתֵ֣י מְעָ֔ט תַּ֚חַת אֲשֶׁ֣ר הֱיִיתֶ֔ם כְּכוֹכְבֵ֥י הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם לָרֹ֑ב כִּֽי־לֹ֣א שָׁמַ֔עְתָּ בְּק֖וֹל יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will remain numerically few instead of your having been like the stars in the sky, multitudinous; for you have not obeyed Adonoy, your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And you shall be left few in number, whereas you were as the stars of heaven for multitude; because thou wouldst not obey the voice of the Lord thy God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will remain but few in number instead of being like the stars of heaven in multitude, for not having heeded the voice of the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 28:63

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:63](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:63)


וְ֠הָיָ֠ה כַּאֲשֶׁר־שָׂ֨שׂ יְהֹוָ֜ה עֲלֵיכֶ֗ם לְהֵיטִ֣יב אֶתְכֶם֮ וּלְהַרְבּ֣וֹת אֶתְכֶם֒ כֵּ֣ן יָשִׂ֤ישׂ יְהֹוָה֙ עֲלֵיכֶ֔ם לְהַאֲבִ֥יד אֶתְכֶ֖ם וּלְהַשְׁמִ֣יד אֶתְכֶ֑ם וְנִסַּחְתֶּם֙ מֵעַ֣ל הָאֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה בָא־שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It will happen that as Adonoy rejoiced over you to benefit you and to multiply you, so will Adonoy bring joy to others over you to remove you and to destroy you; and you will be uprooted from upon the land that you are coming there to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall come to pass, that as the Lord rejoiced over you to do you good, and to multiply you; so the Lord will rejoice over you to destroy you, and to annihilate you; and you shall be plucked from off the land into which thou goest to possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be that just as the L-rd rejoiced over you to do good to you and to increase you, so will the L-rd rejoice [others] over you to destroy you and to annihilate you, and you will be uprooted from the land whither you are coming to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 28:64

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:64](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:64)


וֶהֱפִֽיצְךָ֤ יְהֹוָה֙ בְּכׇל־הָ֣עַמִּ֔ים מִקְצֵ֥ה הָאָ֖רֶץ וְעַד־קְצֵ֣ה הָאָ֑רֶץ וְעָבַ֨דְתָּ שָּׁ֜ם אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֧ר לֹא־יָדַ֛עְתָּ אַתָּ֥ה וַאֲבֹתֶ֖יךָ עֵ֥ץ וָאָֽבֶן׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will disperse you among all the peoples from the end of the earth to the end of the earth; and you shall serve there other gods which neither you nor your ancestors knew, wood and stone.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord shall scatter thee among all peoples, from the one end of the earth to the other; and there thou shalt serve other gods, which neither thou nor thy fathers have known, wood and stone.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd will scatter you among all the peoples, from one end of the earth until the other, and you will serve there [(by paying tribute and poll-tax to the priests of idolatry)] other gods, that you did not know, you and your fathers, wood and stone.


###### Deuteronomy 28:65

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:65](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:65)


וּבַגּוֹיִ֤ם הָהֵם֙ לֹ֣א תַרְגִּ֔יעַ וְלֹא־יִהְיֶ֥ה מָנ֖וֹחַ לְכַף־רַגְלֶ֑ךָ וְנָתַן֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה לְךָ֥ שָׁם֙ לֵ֣ב רַגָּ֔ז וְכִלְי֥וֹן עֵינַ֖יִם וְדַאֲב֥וֹן נָֽפֶשׁ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And among those nations you will not be tranquil and there will be no rest for the sole of your foot; and Adonoy will give you there a fearful heart and pining eyes and disillusioned spirit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And among these nations shalt thou find no ease, neither shall the sole of thy foot have rest; but the Lord shall give thee there a trembling heart, and failing of eyes, and despair of mind:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And among those nations you shall not find ease, and there shall be no rest for the sole of your foot; and the L-rd shall give you there a quaking heart, and failing of eyes [aspiring to the salvation that does not come], and despair of soul.


###### Deuteronomy 28:66

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:66](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:66)


וְהָי֣וּ חַיֶּ֔יךָ תְּלֻאִ֥ים לְךָ֖ מִנֶּ֑גֶד וּפָֽחַדְתָּ֙ לַ֣יְלָה וְיוֹמָ֔ם וְלֹ֥א תַאֲמִ֖ין בְּחַיֶּֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Your life will dangle before you, and you will be in fear night and day, and will have no faith in your living.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and thy life shall hang in doubt before thee; and thou shalt fear day and night, and shalt have no assurance of thy life:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And your life shall be hanging [in doubt] before you [in your constant apprehension of death], and you will fear night and day, and you will not believe in your life.


###### Deuteronomy 28:67

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:67](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:67)


בַּבֹּ֤קֶר תֹּאמַר֙ מִֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן עֶ֔רֶב וּבָעֶ֥רֶב תֹּאמַ֖ר מִֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן בֹּ֑קֶר מִפַּ֤חַד לְבָֽבְךָ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּפְחָ֔ד וּמִמַּרְאֵ֥ה עֵינֶ֖יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תִּרְאֶֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In the morning you will say, ‘‘Would that it were evening,’’ and in the evening you will say, ‘‘Would that it were morning,’’ from the fear in your heart that you will fear and from the sights of your eyes that you will see.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
in the morning thou shalt say, Would it were evening! and at evening thou shalt say, Would it were morning! for the fear of thy heart with which thou shalt fear, and for the sight of thy eyes which thou shalt see.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In the morning you will say: Would that it were evening [of the preceding day], and in the evening you will say: Would that it were [the preceding] morning [(your suffering increasing progressively)], from the fear of your heart that you shall fear, and from the sight of your eyes that you shall see.


###### Deuteronomy 28:68

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:68](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:68)


וֶהֱשִֽׁיבְךָ֨ יְהֹוָ֥ה׀מִצְרַ֘יִם֮ בׇּאֳנִיּוֹת֒ בַּדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָמַ֣רְתִּֽי לְךָ֔ לֹא־תֹסִ֥יף ע֖וֹד לִרְאֹתָ֑הּ וְהִתְמַכַּרְתֶּ֨ם שָׁ֧ם לְאֹיְבֶ֛יךָ לַעֲבָדִ֥ים וְלִשְׁפָח֖וֹת וְאֵ֥ין קֹנֶֽה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will send you back to Egypt on ships, along the route that I told you, ‘‘You are not to see it again,’’ and there you will attempt to sell yourselves to your enemies as slaves and as maidservants but no one will buy.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord shall bring thee back into Miżrayim with ships, by that road of which I spoke to thee, Thou shalt see it no more again: and there you shall be sold to your enemies for bondmen and bondwomen, and no man shall buy you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd will return you to Egypt in boats [in captivity] by the way, of which I said to you: You shall not venture to see it again; and you shall [desire to] be sold there to your foes [as bondsmen and bondswomen] — but there shall be no buyer, [for death and destruction shall be decreed upon you.]


###### Deuteronomy 28:69

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:69](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/28:69)


אֵ֩לֶּה֩ דִבְרֵ֨י הַבְּרִ֜ית אֲֽשֶׁר־צִוָּ֧ה יְהֹוָ֣ה אֶת־מֹשֶׁ֗ה לִכְרֹ֛ת אֶת־בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מוֹאָ֑ב מִלְּבַ֣ד הַבְּרִ֔ית אֲשֶׁר־כָּרַ֥ת אִתָּ֖ם בְּחֹרֵֽב׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
These are the statements of the covenant that Adonoy commanded Moshe to ratify with Bnei Yisroel in the land of Moav, beside the covenant that he had made with them at Choreiv.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
These are the words of the covenant, which the Lord commanded Moshe to make with the children of Yisra᾽el in the land of Mo᾽av, besides the covenant which he made with them in Ḥorev.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
These are the words of the covenant that the L-rd commanded Moses to make with the children of Israel, [that they take the Torah upon themselves under imprecation and oath] in the land of Moav, aside from the covenant which He made with them in Chorev [i.e., aside from the curses in Leviticus stated at Sinai.]



## Chapter 29

###### Deuteronomy 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:1)


וַיִּקְרָ֥א מֹשֶׁ֛ה אֶל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֑ם אַתֶּ֣ם רְאִיתֶ֗ם אֵ֣ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁר֩ עָשָׂ֨ה יְהֹוָ֤ה לְעֵֽינֵיכֶם֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֔יִם לְפַרְעֹ֥ה וּלְכׇל־עֲבָדָ֖יו וּלְכׇל־אַרְצֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe called to all of Yisroel and said to them, ‘‘You have seen everything that Adonoy did as you watched, in the land of Egypt, to Pharoah and to all his attendants and to his entire land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe called to all Yisra᾽el, and said to them, You have seen all that the Lord did before your eyes in the land of Miżrayim to Par῾o, and to all his servants, and to all his land;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses called to all of Israel and he said to them: You saw all that the L-rd did before your eyes in the land of Egypt to Pharaoh and to all his servants and to all his land.


###### Deuteronomy 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:2)


הַמַּסּוֹת֙ הַגְּדֹלֹ֔ת אֲשֶׁ֥ר רָא֖וּ עֵינֶ֑יךָ הָאֹתֹ֧ת וְהַמֹּפְתִ֛ים הַגְּדֹלִ֖ים הָהֵֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The great miracles that you witnessed, those great signs and wonders.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the great trials which thy eyes have seen, the signs, and those great miracles:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The great trials which your eyes witnessed, the signs, and those great wonders [see 4:34].


###### Deuteronomy 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:3)


וְלֹא־נָתַן֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה לָכֶ֥ם לֵב֙ לָדַ֔עַת וְעֵינַ֥יִם לִרְא֖וֹת וְאׇזְנַ֣יִם לִשְׁמֹ֑עַ עַ֖ד הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Yet Adonoy did not grant you a knowledgeable mind and eyes to see, and ears to hear until this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
yet the Lord has not given you a heart to perceive, and eyes to see, and ears to hear, until this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd did not give you a heart to know [the lovingkindness of the L-rd and to cleave to Him], and eyes to see and ears to hear until this day. [On the day the L-rd gave the Torah scroll to the sons of Levi (31:9), the other tribes remonstrated at its not having been given to them, too. Moses was gratified at this and said (27:9): "This day you have become a people to the L-rd, etc."]


###### Deuteronomy 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:4)


וָאוֹלֵ֥ךְ אֶתְכֶ֛ם אַרְבָּעִ֥ים שָׁנָ֖ה בַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר לֹֽא־בָל֤וּ שַׂלְמֹֽתֵיכֶם֙ מֵעֲלֵיכֶ֔ם וְנַעַלְךָ֥ לֹֽא־בָלְתָ֖ה מֵעַ֥ל רַגְלֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I have led you forty years in the wilderness, your garments have not worn out on you and your shoes have not worn out on your feet.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And I have led you forty years in the wilderness: your clothes are not worn old upon you, and thy shoe is not worn old upon thy foot.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And I led you forty years in the desert. Your clothes have not worn from off you, and your shoes have not worn from off your feet.


###### Deuteronomy 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:5)


לֶ֚חֶם לֹ֣א אֲכַלְתֶּ֔ם וְיַ֥יִן וְשֵׁכָ֖ר לֹ֣א שְׁתִיתֶ֑ם לְמַ֙עַן֙ תֵּֽדְע֔וּ כִּ֛י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Bread have you not eaten, neither fresh nor aged wine have you drunk, in order that you know that I, Adonoy, am your God.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You have not eaten bread, neither have you drunk wine or strong drink: that you might know that I am the Lord your God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Bread you have not eaten, and wine and strong drink you have not drunk, so that you should know that I am the L-rd your G-d.


###### Deuteronomy 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:6)


וַתָּבֹ֖אוּ אֶל־הַמָּק֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה וַיֵּצֵ֣א סִיחֹ֣ן מֶֽלֶךְ־חֶ֠שְׁבּ֠וֹן וְע֨וֹג מֶלֶךְ־הַבָּשָׁ֧ן לִקְרָאתֵ֛נוּ לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה וַנַּכֵּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Then when you came to this place, Sichon, king of Cheshbon, came out—and Og, king of Boshon—toward us to wage war and we smote them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when you came to this place, Siĥon the king of Ḥeshbon, and ῾Og the king of Bashan, came out against us to battle, and we smote them:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you have come to this place [(Now you see yourselves in greatness and glory. Do not "kick" against the L-rd, and let your hearts not give way to pride, (8): "And you shall heed, etc.")], and Sichon the king of Cheshbon and Og the king of Bashan came out against us in war and we smote them.


###### Deuteronomy 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:7)


וַנִּקַּח֙ אֶת־אַרְצָ֔ם וַנִּתְּנָ֣הּ לְנַחֲלָ֔ה לָראוּבֵנִ֖י וְלַגָּדִ֑י וְלַחֲצִ֖י שֵׁ֥בֶט הַֽמְנַשִּֽׁי׃

And we took the their land and gave it as territory to the Reuvenites and to the Gaddites, and to half the tribe of the Menashians.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and we took their land, and gave it for an inheritance to the Re᾽uveni, and to the Gadi, and to the half tribe of the Menashshi.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And we took their land, and we gave it as an inheritance to the Reuveni and to the Gadi and to the half tribe of Menasheh.


###### Deuteronomy 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:8)


וּשְׁמַרְתֶּ֗ם אֶת־דִּבְרֵי֙ הַבְּרִ֣ית הַזֹּ֔את וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֖ם אֹתָ֑ם לְמַ֣עַן תַּשְׂכִּ֔ילוּ אֵ֖ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר תַּעֲשֽׂוּן׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You are to guard the tenets of this covenant and fulfill them, in order that you succeed in everything that you do.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Keep therefore the words of this covenant, and do them, that you may prosper in all that you do.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you shall heed the words of this covenant, and you shall do them, so that you may prosper in all that you do.


###### Deuteronomy 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:9)


אַתֶּ֨ם נִצָּבִ֤ים הַיּוֹם֙ כֻּלְּכֶ֔ם לִפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם רָאשֵׁיכֶ֣ם שִׁבְטֵיכֶ֗ם זִקְנֵיכֶם֙ וְשֹׁ֣טְרֵיכֶ֔ם כֹּ֖ל אִ֥ישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘You stand, this day all of you before Adonoy, your God; your tribal chieftains, your elders and your law officers, every man of Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
You stand this day all of you before the Lord your God; your captains of your tribes, your elders, and your officers, with all the men of Yisra᾽el,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
You are standing this day, all of you, before the L-rd your G-d [(Moses assembled them before the Holy One Blessed be He on the day of his death to bring them into the covenant)], your heads of your tribes, your elders and your officers, [in order of prominence, followed by] all the men of Israel.


###### Deuteronomy 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:10)


טַפְּכֶ֣ם נְשֵׁיכֶ֔ם וְגֵ֣רְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּקֶ֣רֶב מַחֲנֶ֑יךָ מֵחֹטֵ֣ב עֵצֶ֔יךָ עַ֖ד שֹׁאֵ֥ב מֵימֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Your young, your wives, and your convert who is within your camps; from your wood cutters to the water drawers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
your little ones, your wives, and thy stranger that is in thy camp, from the hewer of thy wood to the drawer of thy water:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Your little ones, your wives, and your stranger that is in the midst of your camp, from the hewer of your wood to the drawer of your water [(These were Canaanites who came by stratagem to convert in the days of Moses, as the Giveonites did in the days of Joshua (see Joshua 9:4)].


###### Deuteronomy 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:11)


לְעׇבְרְךָ֗ בִּבְרִ֛ית יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ וּבְאָלָת֑וֹ אֲשֶׁר֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ כֹּרֵ֥ת עִמְּךָ֖ הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For your passage into the covenant of Adonoy, your God, and His oath-curse, that Adonoy, your God, is making with you today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou shouldst enter into the covenant of the Lord thy God, and into his oath, which the Lord thy God makes with thee this day:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
So that you pass into the covenant [(The makers of a covenant would make a partition on both sides, through which they would pass)] of the L-rd your G-d and into His oath, which the L-rd your G-d makes with you this day.


###### Deuteronomy 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:12)


לְמַ֣עַן הָקִֽים־אֹתְךָ֩ הַיּ֨וֹם׀ל֜וֹ לְעָ֗ם וְה֤וּא יִֽהְיֶה־לְּךָ֙ לֵֽאלֹהִ֔ים כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּר־לָ֑ךְ וְכַאֲשֶׁ֤ר נִשְׁבַּע֙ לַאֲבֹתֶ֔יךָ לְאַבְרָהָ֥ם לְיִצְחָ֖ק וּֽלְיַעֲקֹֽב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
In order to sustain you today as His people, and He will be for you a God as He promished you and as He promised your forefathers Avrohom, Yitzchok, and Yaakov.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that he may establish thee today for a people to himself, and that he may be to thee a God, as he has said to thee, and as he has sworn to thy fathers, to Avraham, to Yiżĥaq, and to Ya῾aqov.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[He is doing all this] in order to confirm you this day unto Him as a people, and Himself to you as a G-d, as He spoke to you and as He swore to your fathers, to Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob [not to exchange you for another nation, for which reason He binds you with these oaths, so that you not provoke Him.]

וְלֹ֥א אִתְּכֶ֖ם לְבַדְּכֶ֑ם אָנֹכִ֗י כֹּרֵת֙ אֶת־הַבְּרִ֣ית הַזֹּ֔את וְאֶת־הָאָלָ֖ה הַזֹּֽאת׃

And not with you alone am I making this covenant and this oath-curse.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Neither with you only do I make this covenant and this oath;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And not with you alone do I contract this covenant and this oath,


###### Deuteronomy 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:14)


כִּי֩ אֶת־אֲשֶׁ֨ר יֶשְׁנ֜וֹ פֹּ֗ה עִמָּ֙נוּ֙ עֹמֵ֣ד הַיּ֔וֹם לִפְנֵ֖י יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ וְאֵ֨ת אֲשֶׁ֥ר אֵינֶ֛נּוּ פֹּ֖ה עִמָּ֥נוּ הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But with whoever is here with us standing today in the presence of Adonoy, our God, and with those who are not here with us today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
but with him that stands here with us this day before the Lord our God, and also with him that is not here with us this day:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
But with whoever is present here with us standing this day before the L-rd our G-d and with whoever is not here with us this day, [even the future generations].


###### Deuteronomy 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:15)


כִּֽי־אַתֶּ֣ם יְדַעְתֶּ֔ם אֵ֥ת אֲשֶׁר־יָשַׁ֖בְנוּ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וְאֵ֧ת אֲשֶׁר־עָבַ֛רְנוּ בְּקֶ֥רֶב הַגּוֹיִ֖ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר עֲבַרְתֶּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For you are aware how we lived in the land of Egypt, and how we passed through the nations whom you passed through.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
(for you know how we have dwelt in the land of Miżrayim; and how we came through the nations which you passed by;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For you have known our dwelling in the land of Egypt and our passing in the midst of the nations wherein you passed [and which some of you might be tempted to join],


###### Deuteronomy 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:16)


וַתִּרְאוּ֙ אֶת־שִׁקּ֣וּצֵיהֶ֔ם וְאֵ֖ת גִּלֻּלֵיהֶ֑ם עֵ֣ץ וָאֶ֔בֶן כֶּ֥סֶף וְזָהָ֖ב אֲשֶׁ֥ר עִמָּהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Where you observed their abominations and their loathsomeness [idols], wood and stone, silver and gold, that they owned.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and you have seen their abominations, and their idols, wood and stone, silver and gold, which were among them:)


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you saw their abominations and their offal, [idols of] wood and stone [which were open to view], and [idols of] silver and gold which [they kept] with them [in secret, fearing that they would be stolen],


###### Deuteronomy 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:17)


פֶּן־יֵ֣שׁ בָּ֠כֶ֠ם אִ֣ישׁ אֽוֹ־אִשָּׁ֞ה א֧וֹ מִשְׁפָּחָ֣ה אוֹ־שֵׁ֗בֶט אֲשֶׁר֩ לְבָב֨וֹ פֹנֶ֤ה הַיּוֹם֙ מֵעִם֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ לָלֶ֣כֶת לַעֲבֹ֔ד אֶת־אֱלֹהֵ֖י הַגּוֹיִ֣ם הָהֵ֑ם פֶּן־יֵ֣שׁ בָּכֶ֗ם שֹׁ֛רֶשׁ פֹּרֶ֥ה רֹ֖אשׁ וְלַעֲנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Perhaps there is among you a man or a woman or a family or a tribe whose thoughts stray today from [being] with Adonoy, our God, to go serve the gods of those nations; lest there is within you a root producing gall and bitter fruit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
lest there should be among you man, or woman, or family, or tribe, whose heart turns away this day from the Lord our God, to go and serve the gods of these nations; lest there should be among you a root that bears gall and wormwood;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Lest there be among you a man or a woman, a family or a tribe, whose heart turns this day from the L-rd our G-d to go and serve the gods of those nations — Lest there be among you a root sprouting gall and wormwood [i.e., generating evil in your midst],


###### Deuteronomy 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:18)


וְהָיָ֡ה בְּשׇׁמְעוֹ֩ אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֨י הָאָלָ֜ה הַזֹּ֗את וְהִתְבָּרֵ֨ךְ בִּלְבָב֤וֹ לֵאמֹר֙ שָׁל֣וֹם יִֽהְיֶה־לִּ֔י כִּ֛י בִּשְׁרִר֥וּת לִבִּ֖י אֵלֵ֑ךְ לְמַ֛עַן סְפ֥וֹת הָרָוָ֖ה אֶת־הַצְּמֵאָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When he hears the statements of this oath-curse, he will imagine self-blessings, saying, ‘‘Peace will be my lot when I shall follow what my thoughts envision,’’ so that the unintentional may be added to the sinful.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and it come to pass, when he hears the words of this curse, that he bless himself in his heart, saying, I shall have peace, though I walk in the stubborness of my heart, to add drunkenness to thirst:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it be, when he hears the words of this curse that he bless himself in his heart, saying: [These curses will not come upon me, but] "All will be well with me, for as my heart sees fit shall I do," so that the unwitting (sin) is added (for punishment) to the willful,


###### Deuteronomy 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:19)


לֹא־יֹאבֶ֣ה יְהֹוָה֮ סְלֹ֣חַֽ לוֹ֒ כִּ֣י אָ֠ז יֶעְשַׁ֨ן אַף־יְהֹוָ֤ה וְקִנְאָתוֹ֙ בָּאִ֣ישׁ הַה֔וּא וְרָ֤בְצָה בּוֹ֙ כׇּל־הָ֣אָלָ֔ה הַכְּתוּבָ֖ה בַּסֵּ֣פֶר הַזֶּ֑ה וּמָחָ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־שְׁמ֔וֹ מִתַּ֖חַת הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will be unwilling to forgive him, because then Adonoy’s nostrils will fume and His vengeful fury will enflame against that man, and there will cling to him the entire oath-curse written in this book; and Adonoy will eliminate his name from beneath the sky.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
the Lord will not spare him, but then the anger of the Lord and his jealousy shall smoke against that man, and all the curses that are written in this book shall lie upon him, and the Lord shall blot out his name from under heaven.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd will not wish to forgive him, for then the wrath of the L-rd and His vengeance will burn against that man, and there shall descend upon him the entire curse that is written in this scroll, and the L-rd will erase his name from under the heavens.


###### Deuteronomy 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:20)


וְהִבְדִּיל֤וֹ יְהֹוָה֙ לְרָעָ֔ה מִכֹּ֖ל שִׁבְטֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל כְּכֹל֙ אָל֣וֹת הַבְּרִ֔ית הַכְּתוּבָ֕ה בְּסֵ֥פֶר הַתּוֹרָ֖ה הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy will seperate him for harm from all the tribes of Yisroel, in accord with all the oath-curses of the covenant inscribed in this Torah scroll.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord shall mark him off for evil out of all the tribes of Yisra᾽el, according to all the curses of the covenant that are written in this book of the Tora:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd will set him aside for evil from all of the tribes of Israel, according to all the curses of the covenant that are written in this scroll of the Torah.


###### Deuteronomy 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:21)


וְאָמַ֞ר הַדּ֣וֹר הָאַחֲר֗וֹן בְּנֵיכֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר יָק֙וּמוּ֙ מֵאַ֣חֲרֵיכֶ֔ם וְהַ֨נׇּכְרִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָבֹ֖א מֵאֶ֣רֶץ רְחוֹקָ֑ה וְ֠רָא֠וּ אֶת־מַכּ֞וֹת הָאָ֤רֶץ הַהִוא֙ וְאֶת־תַּ֣חֲלֻאֶ֔יהָ אֲשֶׁר־חִלָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה בָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The latter generation will say—your children who will arise after you and the stranger who will come from a distant land; and they will see the plagues of that land and its illnesses that Adonoy has harmed it with.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so that the generation to come of your children that shall rise up after you, and the alien that shall come from a far land, shall say, when they see the plagues of that land, and the sicknesses which the Lord has laid upon it;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the latter generation will say, your children who arise after you, and the stranger who comes from a far-off land, and who see the plagues of that land and the ills which the L-rd has inflicted upon it,


###### Deuteronomy 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:22)


גׇּפְרִ֣ית וָמֶ֘לַח֮ שְׂרֵפָ֣ה כׇל־אַרְצָהּ֒ לֹ֤א תִזָּרַע֙ וְלֹ֣א תַצְמִ֔חַ וְלֹא־יַעֲלֶ֥ה בָ֖הּ כׇּל־עֵ֑שֶׂב כְּֽמַהְפֵּכַ֞ת סְדֹ֤ם וַעֲמֹרָה֙ אַדְמָ֣ה (וצביים) [וּצְבוֹיִ֔ם] אֲשֶׁר֙ הָפַ֣ךְ יְהֹוָ֔ה בְּאַפּ֖וֹ וּבַחֲמָתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Sulfur and salt, all its land, will be burnt, it will not be seeded, and it will not sprout, and no grass will grow there; like the overturning of Sodom and Amoroh, Admoh and Tzevoyim, which Adonoy overturned in His anger and His wrath.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and that the whole land is brimstone, and salt, and burning, that it is not sown, nor bears, nor does any grass grow on it, like the overthrow of Sedom, and ῾Amora, Adma, and Żevoyim, which the Lord overthrew in his anger, and in his wrath:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Brimstone and salt, a conflagration of the entire land — not being sown, and not sprouting, and not bringing forth any herb — like the overthrow of Sodom and Amorah, Admah and Tzvoyim, which the L-rd overturned in His wrath and His fury.


###### Deuteronomy 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:23)


וְאָֽמְרוּ֙ כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם עַל־מֶ֨ה עָשָׂ֧ה יְהֹוָ֛ה כָּ֖כָה לָאָ֣רֶץ הַזֹּ֑את מֶ֥ה חֳרִ֛י הָאַ֥ף הַגָּד֖וֹל הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And all the nations will say, ‘‘For what did Adonoy do so to this land? Why the vigor of this great anger?’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
then all the nations shall say, Why has the Lord done thus to this land? what means the heat of this great anger?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And all the nations will say: Why did the L-rd do thus to this land? What means the burning of this great wrath?


###### Deuteronomy 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:24)


וְאָ֣מְר֔וּ עַ֚ל אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָזְב֔וּ אֶת־בְּרִ֥ית יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י אֲבֹתָ֑ם אֲשֶׁר֙ כָּרַ֣ת עִמָּ֔ם בְּהוֹצִיא֥וֹ אֹתָ֖ם מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And they will reply, ‘‘Because they forsook the covenant of Adonoy, the God of their forefathers, which He made with them when He took them out of the land of Egypt.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Then men shall say, Because they have forsaken the covenant of the Lord God of their fathers, which he made with them when he brought them out of the land of Miżrayim:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then they will say: It is because they forsook the covenant of the L-rd, the G-d of their fathers, that He made with them when He brought them forth from the land of Egypt.


###### Deuteronomy 29:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:25)


וַיֵּלְכ֗וּ וַיַּֽעַבְדוּ֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים אֲחֵרִ֔ים וַיִּֽשְׁתַּחֲו֖וּ לָהֶ֑ם אֱלֹהִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹֽא־יְדָע֔וּם וְלֹ֥א חָלַ֖ק לָהֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And they went and served other gods and prostrated themselves to them; gods unknown to them and [which] He had not apportioned to them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
for they went and served other gods, and worshipped them, gods whom they knew not, and whom he had not given to them:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And they went and they served other gods and they bowed down to them, gods whom they did not know [to be potent] and whom He did not confer upon them.


###### Deuteronomy 29:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:26)


וַיִּחַר־אַ֥ף יְהֹוָ֖ה בָּאָ֣רֶץ הַהִ֑וא לְהָבִ֤יא עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־הַקְּלָלָ֔ה הַכְּתוּבָ֖ה בַּסֵּ֥פֶר הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy became furious with that land, [causing Him] to bring on it the entire curse written in this book.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the anger of the Lord burned against this land, to bring upon it all the curses that are written in this book:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the wrath of the L-rd burned against that land to bring upon it the entire curse written in this scroll.


###### Deuteronomy 29:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:27)


וַיִּתְּשֵׁ֤ם יְהֹוָה֙ מֵעַ֣ל אַדְמָתָ֔ם בְּאַ֥ף וּבְחֵמָ֖ה וּבְקֶ֣צֶף גָּד֑וֹל וַיַּשְׁלִכֵ֛ם אֶל־אֶ֥רֶץ אַחֶ֖רֶת כַּיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy forced them away from their land with anger and wrath and great fury; and He cast them into another land like this day.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord rooted them out of their land in anger, and in wrath, and in great indignation, and cast them into another land, as it is this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd drove them off from their land in wrath and fury and great rage, and He cast them into a different land as this day.


###### Deuteronomy 29:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/29:28)


הַנִּ֨סְתָּרֹ֔ת לַיהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ וְהַנִּגְלֹ֞ת לָ֤ׄנׄוּׄ וּׄלְׄבָׄנֵ֙ׄיׄנׄוּ֙ׄ עַׄד־עוֹלָ֔ם לַעֲשׂ֕וֹת אֶת־כׇּל־דִּבְרֵ֖י הַתּוֹרָ֥ה הַזֹּֽאת׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The hidden [matters] are Adonoy’s, our God’s, and the revealed [matters] are ours and our children’s forever, to perform all the statements of this Torah.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The secret things belong to the Lord our God: but those things which are revealed belong to us and to our children for ever, that we may do all the words of this Tora.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And if you ask: What can we do if we are punished for the hidden thoughts of one man" (see 17) — know that] the hidden things [of the individual] are for the L-rd our G-d [to punish], but the revealed ones are for us and our children [(who become subject to the oath after crossing the Jordan) to extirpate from our midst] forever, to do according to all the words of this Torah, [failing which, we are subject to punishment.]



## Chapter 30

###### Deuteronomy 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:1)


וְהָיָה֩ כִֽי־יָבֹ֨אוּ עָלֶ֜יךָ כׇּל־הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֗לֶּה הַבְּרָכָה֙ וְהַקְּלָלָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר נָתַ֖תִּי לְפָנֶ֑יךָ וַהֲשֵׁבֹתָ֙ אֶל־לְבָבֶ֔ךָ בְּכׇ֨ל־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֧ר הִדִּיחֲךָ֛ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ שָֽׁמָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When it happens that there come upon you all these statements, the blessing and the curse that I have set before you; and you will restore to your perception amid all the nations where Adonoy, your God, has exiled you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it shall come to pass, when all these things are come upon thee, the blessing and the curse, which I have set before thee, and thou shalt call them to mind among all the nations, into which the Lord thy God has driven thee,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it shall be, when there are come upon you all of these things, the blessing and the curse that I have placed before you, and you return unto your hearts among all the nations whereunto the L-rd your G-d has driven you,


###### Deuteronomy 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:2)


וְשַׁבְתָּ֞ עַד־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ וְשָׁמַעְתָּ֣ בְקֹל֔וֹ כְּכֹ֛ל אֲשֶׁר־אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּ֑וֹם אַתָּ֣ה וּבָנֶ֔יךָ בְּכׇל־לְבָבְךָ֖ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
You will return to Adonoy, your God, and obey Him exactly as I am commanding you today, you and your sons, wholeheartedly and with your whole being.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and shalt return to the Lord thy God, and shalt obey his voice according to all that I command thee this day, thou and thy children, with all thy heart, and with all thy soul;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you return to the L-rd your G-d and you heed His voice according to all that I command you this day, you and your children, with all your heart and with all your soul,


###### Deuteronomy 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:3)


וְשָׁ֨ב יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ אֶת־שְׁבוּתְךָ֖ וְרִחֲמֶ֑ךָ וְשָׁ֗ב וְקִבֶּצְךָ֙ מִכׇּל־הָ֣עַמִּ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֧ר הֱפִֽיצְךָ֛ יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ שָֽׁמָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, will bring back your returnees and will be merciful toward you; and He will return and gather you from all the peoples that Adonoy, your God, has dispersed you there.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that then the Lord thy God will turn thy captivity, and have compassion upon thee, and will return and gather thee from all the nations, amongst whom the Lord thy God has scattered thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then the L-rd your G-d will return with your captivity, [the Shechinah, as it were, having shared with Israel the suffering of the exile], and He shall have mercy upon you, and He shall return and gather you from all the peoples whereunto the L-rd your G-d has dispersed you.


###### Deuteronomy 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:4)


אִם־יִהְיֶ֥ה נִֽדַּחֲךָ֖ בִּקְצֵ֣ה הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם מִשָּׁ֗ם יְקַבֶּצְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וּמִשָּׁ֖ם יִקָּחֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
If your exiled one will be at the edge of the heavens, from there will Adonoy, your God, gather you and from there will He take you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
If thy outcasts be at the utmost parts of heaven, from there will the Lord thy God gather thee, and from there will he fetch thee:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If your cast-off ones be in the end of the heavens, from there the L-rd your G-d will gather you and from there He will take you.


###### Deuteronomy 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:5)


וֶהֱבִיאֲךָ֞ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ אֶל־הָאָ֛רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־יָרְשׁ֥וּ אֲבֹתֶ֖יךָ וִֽירִשְׁתָּ֑הּ וְהֵיטִֽבְךָ֥ וְהִרְבְּךָ֖ מֵאֲבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy, your God, will bring you to the land that your forefathers inherited and you will inherit it; and He will benefit you and multiply you more than your forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord thy God will bring thee into the land which thy fathers possessed, and thou shalt possess it; and he will do thee good, and multiply thee more than thy fathers.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d will bring you to the land which your forefathers inherited, and you will inherit it. And He shall do good unto you, and He shall increase you even more than your fathers.


###### Deuteronomy 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:6)


וּמָ֨ל יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ אֶת־לְבָבְךָ֖ וְאֶת־לְבַ֣ב זַרְעֶ֑ךָ לְאַהֲבָ֞ה אֶת־יְהֹוָ֧ה אֱלֹהֶ֛יךָ בְּכׇל־לְבָבְךָ֥ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשְׁךָ֖ לְמַ֥עַן חַיֶּֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy, your God, will circumcise your heart and the heart of your descendants, to love Adonoy, your God, wholeheartedly and with all your being in order that you live.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord thy God will circumcise thy heart, and the heart of thy seed, to love the Lord thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, that thou mayst live.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d will circumcise your heart and the heart of your seed to love the L-rd your G-d with all your heart and with all your soul, so that you live.


###### Deuteronomy 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:7)


וְנָתַן֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ אֵ֥ת כׇּל־הָאָל֖וֹת הָאֵ֑לֶּה עַל־אֹיְבֶ֥יךָ וְעַל־שֹׂנְאֶ֖יךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר רְדָפֽוּךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, will place all these oath-curses upon your enemies and upon your foes who chased you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord thy God will put all these curses upon thy enemies, and on them who hate thee, who persecuted thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d will place all these curses on your foes and on your haters, who pursued you.


###### Deuteronomy 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:8)


וְאַתָּ֣ה תָשׁ֔וּב וְשָׁמַעְתָּ֖ בְּק֣וֹל יְהֹוָ֑ה וְעָשִׂ֙יתָ֙ אֶת־כׇּל־מִצְוֺתָ֔יו אֲשֶׁ֛ר אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּֽוֹם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And you will turn back and obey Adonoy; and you will perform all His commandments that I am commanding you today.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And thou shalt return and obey the voice of the Lord, and do all his commandments which I command thee this day.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And you will return and you will hearken to the voice of the L-rd, and you will do all of His mitzvoth that I command you this day.


###### Deuteronomy 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:9)


וְהוֹתִֽירְךָ֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ בְּכֹ֣ל**׀** מַעֲשֵׂ֣ה יָדֶ֗ךָ בִּפְרִ֨י בִטְנְךָ֜ וּבִפְרִ֧י בְהֶמְתְּךָ֛ וּבִפְרִ֥י אַדְמָתְךָ֖ לְטֹבָ֑ה כִּ֣י**׀** יָשׁ֣וּב יְהֹוָ֗ה לָשׂ֤וּשׂ עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ לְט֔וֹב כַּאֲשֶׁר־שָׂ֖שׂ עַל־אֲבֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, will give you surplus in all your endeavors, in the fruit of your belly, and in the offspring of your animals, and in the produce of your soil—for benefit; for Adonoy will return to rejoice over you for benefit as He rejoiced over your forefathers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord thy God will make thee plenteous in every work of thy hand, in the fruit of thy body, and in the fruit of thy cattle, and in the fruit of thy land, for good: for the Lord will again rejoice over thee for good, as he rejoiced over thy fathers:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd your G-d will increase you in all the work of your hands, in the fruit of your womb, and in the fruit of your beasts, and in the fruit of your land for the good. For the L-rd will return to rejoice over you for the good as He rejoiced over your fathers.


###### Deuteronomy 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:10)


כִּ֣י תִשְׁמַ֗ע בְּקוֹל֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לִשְׁמֹ֤ר מִצְוֺתָיו֙ וְחֻקֹּתָ֔יו הַכְּתוּבָ֕ה בְּסֵ֥פֶר הַתּוֹרָ֖ה הַזֶּ֑ה כִּ֤י תָשׁוּב֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בְּכׇל־לְבָבְךָ֖ וּבְכׇל־נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When you obey Adonoy, your God, to guard His commandments and His statutes, written in this Torah scroll; when you turn back to Adonoy, your God, wholeheartedly and with all your being.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
if thou shalt hearken to the voice of the Lord thy God, to keep his commandments and his statutes which are written in this book of the Tora, and if thou turn to the Lord thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If you heed the voice of the L-rd your G-d to keep His mitzvoth and His statutes which are written in this Torah scroll, if you return to the L-rd your G-d with all your heart and with all your soul.


###### Deuteronomy 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:11)


כִּ֚י הַמִּצְוָ֣ה הַזֹּ֔את אֲשֶׁ֛ר אָנֹכִ֥י מְצַוְּךָ֖ הַיּ֑וֹם לֹא־נִפְלֵ֥את הִוא֙ מִמְּךָ֔ וְלֹ֥א רְחֹקָ֖ה הִֽוא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For this *mitzvah* that I am commanding you today; it is not abstruse to you nor is it distant.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For this commandment which I command thee this day, it is not hidden from thee, neither is it far off.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For this mitzvah which I command you this day is not concealed from you and it is not far off.


###### Deuteronomy 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:12)


לֹ֥א בַשָּׁמַ֖יִם הִ֑וא לֵאמֹ֗ר מִ֣י יַעֲלֶה־לָּ֤נוּ הַשָּׁמַ֙יְמָה֙ וְיִקָּחֶ֣הָ לָּ֔נוּ וְיַשְׁמִעֵ֥נוּ אֹתָ֖הּ וְנַעֲשֶֽׂנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
It is not in heaven, [for you] to say, ‘‘Who will go up to heaven for us, and acquire it for us, and inform us of it, and we will fulfill it?’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
It is not in heaven, that thou shouldst say, Who shall go up for us to heaven, and bring it to us, that we may hear it, and do it?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
It is not in the heavens [(If it were, you would have to go up after it to study it)], that one would say: Who will go up for us to heaven and take it for us and make us hear it that we might do it?


###### Deuteronomy 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:13)


וְלֹא־מֵעֵ֥בֶר לַיָּ֖ם הִ֑וא לֵאמֹ֗ר מִ֣י יַעֲבׇר־לָ֜נוּ אֶל־עֵ֤בֶר הַיָּם֙ וְיִקָּחֶ֣הָ לָּ֔נוּ וְיַשְׁמִעֵ֥נוּ אֹתָ֖הּ וְנַעֲשֶֽׂנָּה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Nor is it overseas, [for you] to say, ‘‘Who will travel overseas for us, and acquire it for us, and inform us of it, and we will fulfill it?’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Nor is it beyond the sea, that thou shouldst say, Who shall go over the sea for us, and bring it to us, that we may hear it, and do it?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it is not across the seas, that one would say: Who will cross the seas for us and take it for us and make us hear it that we might do it?


###### Deuteronomy 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:14)


כִּֽי־קָר֥וֹב אֵלֶ֛יךָ הַדָּבָ֖ר מְאֹ֑ד בְּפִ֥יךָ וּבִֽלְבָבְךָ֖ לַעֲשֹׂתֽוֹ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For the matter is extremely close to you; in your mouth and in your mind to fulfill it.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But the word is very near to thee, in thy mouth, and in thy heart, that thou mayst do it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For the thing is very near to you [(the Torah having been given in writing and by word of mouth)], in your mouth and in your heart to do it.


###### Deuteronomy 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:15)


רְאֵ֨ה נָתַ֤תִּי לְפָנֶ֙יךָ֙ הַיּ֔וֹם אֶת־הַֽחַיִּ֖ים וְאֶת־הַטּ֑וֹב וְאֶת־הַמָּ֖וֶת וְאֶת־הָרָֽע׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
See, I have placed before you today life and good, and death and bad.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
See, I have set before thee this day life and good, and death and evil;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
See, I have set before you this day, the life and the good [(The first is contingent upon the second)], and the death and the evil, [viz.:]


###### Deuteronomy 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:16)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָנֹכִ֣י מְצַוְּךָ֮ הַיּוֹם֒ לְאַהֲבָ֞ה אֶת־יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ לָלֶ֣כֶת בִּדְרָכָ֔יו וְלִשְׁמֹ֛ר מִצְוֺתָ֥יו וְחֻקֹּתָ֖יו וּמִשְׁפָּטָ֑יו וְחָיִ֣יתָ וְרָבִ֔יתָ וּבֵֽרַכְךָ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ בָּאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה בָא־שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For which I command you this day, to love Adonoy, your God, to go in His ways and to guard His commandments and His statutes and His laws; and you will live and you will flourish, and Adonoy, your God, will bless you in the land that you are coming there to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
in that I command thee this day to love the Lord thy God, to walk in his ways, and to keep his commandments and his statutes and his judgments: then thou shalt live and multiply: and the Lord thy God shall bless thee in the land into which thou goest to possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
What I command you this day, to love the L-rd your G-d, to walk in His ways, and to keep His mitzvoth, and His statutes, and His judgments [(This is "the good")], and you will live, and you will multiply [(This is "the life")], and the L-rd your G-d will bless you in the land whither you come to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:17)


וְאִם־יִפְנֶ֥ה לְבָבְךָ֖ וְלֹ֣א תִשְׁמָ֑ע וְנִדַּחְתָּ֗ וְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲוִ֛יתָ לֵאלֹהִ֥ים אֲחֵרִ֖ים וַעֲבַדְתָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
But if your heart strays and you do not listen, and you are misled, and you prostrate yourself to other gods and worship them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But if thy heart turn away, so that thou wilt not hear, but shalt be drawn away, and worship other gods, and serve them;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And if your heart turns, and you do not hearken, and you are drawn away, and you bow down to other gods and you serve them [(This is "the evil")],


###### Deuteronomy 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:18)


הִגַּ֤דְתִּי לָכֶם֙ הַיּ֔וֹם כִּ֥י אָבֹ֖ד תֹּאבֵד֑וּן לֹא־תַאֲרִיכֻ֤ן יָמִים֙ עַל־הָ֣אֲדָמָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר אַתָּ֤ה עֹבֵר֙ אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֔ן לָב֥וֹא שָׁ֖מָּה לְרִשְׁתָּֽהּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I tell you today that you shall certainly perish; you will not remain long on the land that you are crossing the Yardein to come there to inherit.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I announce to you this day, that you shall surely perish, and that you shall not prolong your days upon the land, whither thou passest over the Yarden to go to possess it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I tell you this day that you will go lost [(This is "the death")]; you will not prolong days on the land whither you cross over the Jordan to come there to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:19)


הַעִדֹ֨תִי בָכֶ֣ם הַיּוֹם֮ אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם וְאֶת־הָאָ֒רֶץ֒ הַחַיִּ֤ים וְהַמָּ֙וֶת֙ נָתַ֣תִּי לְפָנֶ֔יךָ הַבְּרָכָ֖ה וְהַקְּלָלָ֑ה וּבָֽחַרְתָּ֙ בַּחַיִּ֔ים לְמַ֥עַן תִּֽחְיֶ֖ה אַתָּ֥ה וְזַרְעֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I invoke, as witnesses against you this day, heaven and earth: Life and death have I placed before you, blessing and curse; you choose life in order that you live, you and your descendants,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I call heaven and earth to witness this day against you, that I have set before thee life and death, blessing and cursing: therefore choose life, that both thou and thy seed may live:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I call as witness upon you this day heaven and earth [(They endure forever, and if evil befall you, they will testify that I forewarned you of all this.)] The life and the death have I set before you, the blessing and the curse, and [I urge you to] choose the life, so that you live, you and your seed.


###### Deuteronomy 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/30:20)


לְאַֽהֲבָה֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ לִשְׁמֹ֥עַ בְּקֹל֖וֹ וּלְדׇבְקָה־ב֑וֹ כִּ֣י ה֤וּא חַיֶּ֙יךָ֙ וְאֹ֣רֶךְ יָמֶ֔יךָ לָשֶׁ֣בֶת עַל־הָאֲדָמָ֗ה אֲשֶׁר֩ נִשְׁבַּ֨ע יְהֹוָ֧ה לַאֲבֹתֶ֛יךָ לְאַבְרָהָ֛ם לְיִצְחָ֥ק וּֽלְיַעֲקֹ֖ב לָתֵ֥ת לָהֶֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
To love Adonoy, your God, to obey Him and to cling to Him; for He is your life and your longevity, to live on the soil that Adonoy swore to your forefathers—to Avrohom, to Yitzchok, and to Yaakov—to give them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that thou mayst love the Lord thy God, and that thou mayst obey his voice, and that thou mayst cleave to him: for he is thy life, and the length of thy days: that thou mayst dwell in the land which the Lord swore to thy fathers, to Avraham, to Yiżĥaq, and to Ya῾aqov, to give them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
To love the L-rd your G-d, to heed His voice, and to cleave to Him. For He is your life and the length of your days to dwell on the land that the L-rd swore to your forefathers — to Abraham to Isaac and to Jacob — to give to them.



## Chapter 31

###### Deuteronomy 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:1)


וַיֵּ֖לֶךְ מֹשֶׁ֑ה וַיְדַבֵּ֛ר אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֥ים הָאֵ֖לֶּה אֶל־כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe went and he addressed these statements to all of Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe went and spoke these words to all Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses went and he spoke these words to all of Israel.


###### Deuteronomy 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:2)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲלֵהֶ֗ם בֶּן־מֵאָה֩ וְעֶשְׂרִ֨ים שָׁנָ֤ה אָנֹכִי֙ הַיּ֔וֹם לֹא־אוּכַ֥ל ע֖וֹד לָצֵ֣את וְלָב֑וֹא וַֽיהֹוָה֙ אָמַ֣ר אֵלַ֔י לֹ֥א תַעֲבֹ֖ר אֶת־הַיַּרְדֵּ֥ן הַזֶּֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He said to them, ‘‘I am 120 years old today, I cannot any longer go forth and return; for Adonoy has said to me, ‘You will not cross this Yardein.’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said to them, I am a hundred and twenty years old this day; I can no more go out and come in: also the Lord has said to me, Thou shalt not go over this Yarden.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said to them: I am one hundred and twenty years old this day [(On this day I was born, and on this day I shall die)]. I shall no longer be able to go out and to come in, [(the warrant to do so having been taken from him and accorded Joshua)], for the L-rd has said to me: You shall not cross over this Jordan.


###### Deuteronomy 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:3)


יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ ה֣וּא**׀** עֹבֵ֣ר לְפָנֶ֗יךָ הֽוּא־יַשְׁמִ֞יד אֶת־הַגּוֹיִ֥ם הָאֵ֛לֶּה מִלְּפָנֶ֖יךָ וִירִשְׁתָּ֑ם יְהוֹשֻׁ֗עַ ה֚וּא עֹבֵ֣ר לְפָנֶ֔יךָ כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר דִּבֶּ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, your God, He is crossing before you, He will destroy these nations before you and you will inherit them. Yehoshua, he is crossing before you as Adonoy has stated.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The Lord thy God, he will go over before thee, and he will destroy these nations from before thee, and thou shalt dispossess them: and Yehoshua, he shall go over before thee, as the Lord has said.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd your G-d — He crosses over before you. He will destroy these nations before you and you will inherit them. Joshua — he crosses over before you, as the L-rd has spoken.


###### Deuteronomy 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:4)


וְעָשָׂ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ לָהֶ֔ם כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשָׂ֗ה לְסִיח֥וֹן וּלְע֛וֹג מַלְכֵ֥י הָאֱמֹרִ֖י וּלְאַרְצָ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר הִשְׁמִ֖יד אֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And Adonoy will do to them as He did to Sichon and to Og, kings of the Emorites, and to their land; whom He destroyed.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And the Lord will do to them as he did to Siĥon and to ῾Og, the kings of the Emori, and to their land; whom he destroyed,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd shall do to them as He did to Sichon and to Og, the kings of the Emori and to their land, having destroyed them.


###### Deuteronomy 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:5)


וּנְתָנָ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה לִפְנֵיכֶ֑ם וַעֲשִׂיתֶ֣ם לָהֶ֔ם כְּכׇ֨ל־הַמִּצְוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּ֖יתִי אֶתְכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy will defeat them before you, and you will do to them in accord with the *mitzvah* that I commanded you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and the Lord shall deliver them up before your face, that you may do to them according to all the commandments which I have commanded you.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd will deliver them before you, and you shall do to them according to all of the mitzvah that I have commanded you.


###### Deuteronomy 31:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:6)


חִזְק֣וּ וְאִמְצ֔וּ אַל־תִּֽירְא֥וּ וְאַל־תַּעַרְצ֖וּ מִפְּנֵיהֶ֑ם כִּ֣י**׀** יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֗יךָ ה֚וּא הַהֹלֵ֣ךְ עִמָּ֔ךְ לֹ֥א יַרְפְּךָ֖ וְלֹ֥א יַעַזְבֶֽךָּ׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Be courageous and bold, do not fear and do not quake before them; for Adonoy, your God, it is He Who is going with you, He will not enfeeble you nor will He abandon you.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Be strong and of a good courage, fear not, nor be afraid of them: for the Lord thy God, he it is that goes with thee; he will not fail thee, nor forsake thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Be strong and firm. Do not fear and do not break before them. For the L-rd your G-d — It is He that goes before you; He will not let go of you, and He will not forsake you.


###### Deuteronomy 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:7)


וַיִּקְרָ֨א מֹשֶׁ֜ה לִיהוֹשֻׁ֗עַ וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלָ֜יו לְעֵינֵ֣י כׇל־יִשְׂרָאֵל֮ חֲזַ֣ק וֶאֱמָץ֒ כִּ֣י אַתָּ֗ה תָּבוֹא֙ אֶת־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה אֶל־הָאָ֕רֶץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר נִשְׁבַּ֧ע יְהֹוָ֛ה לַאֲבֹתָ֖ם לָתֵ֣ת לָהֶ֑ם וְאַתָּ֖ה תַּנְחִילֶ֥נָּה אוֹתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe called Yehoshua and said to him as all of Yisroel watched, ‘‘Be courageous and bold, for you shall enter with this people into the land that Adonoy swore to their forefathers to give them, and you will apportion it to them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe called to Yehoshua, and said to him in the sight of all Yisra᾽el, Be strong and of a good courage: for thou must go with this people to the land which the Lord has sworn to their fathers to give them; and thou shalt cause them to inherit it.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses called to Joshua and he said to him before the eyes of all of Israel: Be strong and firm, for you will come with this people [(The elders of the generation shall be with you — all will be according to their judgment and counsel)] to the land that the L-rd swore to their fathers to give to them, and you shall cause them to inherit it.


###### Deuteronomy 31:8

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
