וַיְצַ֞ו אֶת־יְהוֹשֻׁ֣עַ בִּן־נ֗וּן וַיֹּ֘אמֶר֮ חֲזַ֣ק וֶאֱמָץ֒ כִּ֣י אַתָּ֗ה תָּבִיא֙ אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֶל־הָאָ֖רֶץ אֲשֶׁר־נִשְׁבַּ֣עְתִּי לָהֶ֑ם וְאָנֹכִ֖י אֶהְיֶ֥ה עִמָּֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He commanded Yehoshua, the son of Nun and said, ‘‘Be courageous and bold, for you will bring Bnei Yisroel to the land that I swore to them, and I will be with you.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he gave Yehoshua the son of Nun a charge, and said, Be strong and of a good courage: for thou shalt bring the children of Yisra᾽el into the land of which I swore to them: and I will be with thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He charged Joshua the son of Nun, saying: Be strong and firm, for you will bring the children of Israel to the land that I swore to them, and I shall be with you.


###### Deuteronomy 31:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:24)


וַיְהִ֣י**׀** כְּכַלּ֣וֹת מֹשֶׁ֗ה לִכְתֹּ֛ב אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֥י הַתּוֹרָֽה־הַזֹּ֖את עַל־סֵ֑פֶר עַ֖ד תֻּמָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Moshe finished writing the statements of this Torah in a book, till they were complete,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And it came to pass, when Moshe had made an end of writing the words of this Tora in a book, until they were finished,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And it was, when Moses had finished writing the words of this Torah on a scroll until their end,


###### Deuteronomy 31:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:25)


וַיְצַ֤ו מֹשֶׁה֙ אֶת־הַלְוִיִּ֔ם נֹ֥שְׂאֵ֛י אֲר֥וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֖ה לֵאמֹֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe commanded the Levites, bearers of the ark of Adonoy’s covenant, saying:


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
that Moshe commanded the Levites, who bore the ark of the covenant of the Lord, saying,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
That Moses commanded the Levites, the bearers of the ark of the covenant of the L-rd, saying:


###### Deuteronomy 31:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:26)


לָקֹ֗חַ אֵ֣ת סֵ֤פֶר הַתּוֹרָה֙ הַזֶּ֔ה וְשַׂמְתֶּ֣ם אֹת֔וֹ מִצַּ֛ד אֲר֥וֹן בְּרִית־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם וְהָיָה־שָׁ֥ם בְּךָ֖ לְעֵֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
‘‘Take this book of the Torah and place it on the side of the ark of Adonoy, your God’s covenant; and it will be there as witness against you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Take this book of the Tora, and put it in the side of the ark of the covenant of the Lord your God, that it may be there for a witness against thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Take the scroll of this Torah and place it at the side of the ark of the covenant of the L-rd your G-d [(according to one view, on a board projecting outside the ark; according to another, alongside the tablets within the ark)], and it shall be there for witness in you.


###### Deuteronomy 31:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:27)


כִּ֣י אָנֹכִ֤י יָדַ֙עְתִּי֙ אֶֽת־מֶרְיְךָ֔ וְאֶֽת־עׇרְפְּךָ֖ הַקָּשֶׁ֑ה הֵ֣ן בְּעוֹדֶ֩נִּי֩ חַ֨י עִמָּכֶ֜ם הַיּ֗וֹם מַמְרִ֤ים הֱיִתֶם֙ עִם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְאַ֖ף כִּי־אַחֲרֵ֥י מוֹתִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For I know your rebelliousness your stubborness. Look! even while I live with you today you have been defiers with Adonoy, surely after my death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I know thy rebellion, and thy stiff neck: behold, while I am yet alive with you this day, you have been rebellious against the Lord; and how much more after my death?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I have known your rebellion and your stiff neck. If until the time I have been living with you this day, you have been rebellious against the L-rd, how much more so after my death!


###### Deuteronomy 31:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:28)


הַקְהִ֧ילוּ אֵלַ֛י אֶת־כׇּל־זִקְנֵ֥י שִׁבְטֵיכֶ֖ם וְשֹׁטְרֵיכֶ֑ם וַאֲדַבְּרָ֣ה בְאׇזְנֵיהֶ֗ם אֵ֚ת הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֔לֶּה וְאָעִ֣ידָה בָּ֔ם אֶת־הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וְאֶת־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Assemble to me all the judges of your tribes and your enforcers, and I will declare in their hearing these statements, and invoke as witnesses against them heaven and earth,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Gather to me all the elders of your tribes, and your officers, that I may speak these words in their ears, and call heaven and earth to witness against them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Assemble unto me all the elders of your tribes and your officers and I will speak in their ears these words, and I will call upon heaven and earth to be witness in them [(In 30:19, he spoke to Israel; now he will speak to heaven and earth.)]


###### Deuteronomy 31:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:29)


כִּ֣י יָדַ֗עְתִּי אַחֲרֵ֤י מוֹתִי֙ כִּֽי־הַשְׁחֵ֣ת תַּשְׁחִת֔וּן וְסַרְתֶּ֣ם מִן־הַדֶּ֔רֶךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר צִוִּ֖יתִי אֶתְכֶ֑ם וְקָרָ֨את אֶתְכֶ֤ם הָרָעָה֙ בְּאַחֲרִ֣ית הַיָּמִ֔ים כִּֽי־תַעֲשׂ֤וּ אֶת־הָרַע֙ בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה לְהַכְעִיס֖וֹ בְּמַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יְדֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For I know after my death, that you will become depraved and you will veer from the way that I commanded you; and harm will happen to you at the end of time, when you do what is wicked in Adonoy’s eyes to spite Him with your actions.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For I know that after my death you will surely become corrupted, and turn aside from the way which I have commanded you; and evil will befall you in the latter days; because you will do evil in the sight of the Lord, to provoke him to anger through the work of your hands.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For I know that after my death [i.e., after the death of Joshua, whom Moses considered like himself] you will corrupt yourselves and you will turn from the way that I commanded unto you, and the evil will befall you in the end of days, for you will do the evil in the eyes of the L-rd to anger Him with the deeds of your hands.


###### Deuteronomy 31:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/31:30)


וַיְדַבֵּ֣ר מֹשֶׁ֗ה בְּאׇזְנֵי֙ כׇּל־קְהַ֣ל יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֥י הַשִּׁירָ֖ה הַזֹּ֑את עַ֖ד תֻּמָּֽם׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Moshe spoke in the hearing of all the assemblage of Yisroel the statements of this song, till they were complete.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And Moshe spoke in the ears of all the congregation of Yisra᾽el, the words of this poem, until they were ended.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses spoke in the ears of the entire assembly of Israel the words of this song until their end:



## Chapter 32

###### Deuteronomy 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:1)


הַאֲזִ֥ינוּ הַשָּׁמַ֖יִם וַאֲדַבֵּ֑רָהוְתִשְׁמַ֥ע הָאָ֖רֶץ אִמְרֵי־פִֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Hearken, O heaven as I declare, let the earth hear my mouth’s sayings.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Give ear, O heavens, and I will speak; and hear, O earth, the words of my mouth.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Listen, O heavens, and I shall speak, and hear, O earth, the words of my mouth [wherewith I am forewarning Israel, and be witnesses to the fact that I told them you would be my witnesses (see 30:19). (If they are meritorious, the heavens and the earth will give them their bounty; if not, they will withhold it.)]


###### Deuteronomy 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:2)


יַעֲרֹ֤ף כַּמָּטָר֙ לִקְחִ֔יתִּזַּ֥ל כַּטַּ֖ל אִמְרָתִ֑י כִּשְׂעִירִ֣ם עֲלֵי־דֶ֔שֶׁאוְכִרְבִיבִ֖ים עֲלֵי־עֵֽשֶׂב׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Let my instruction flow like rainfall, let my saying drip like dew; like storm winds on verdure, and like raindrops on grass.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
My doctrine shall drop as the rain, my speech shall distil as the dew, as the small rain upon the tender herb, and as the showers upon the grass:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[This is what you shall testify, that I say before you that the Torah that I gave to Israel is life to the world, just as rain is.] My teaching [i.e., Torah] shall drip as the rain; My word shall flow as the dew, [which is universally welcome], as winds upon the herbage, [which cause it to flourish (so Torah causes its disciples to flourish)], as showers upon the [(individual) blades] of grass.


###### Deuteronomy 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:3)


כִּ֛י שֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶקְרָ֑אהָב֥וּ גֹ֖דֶל לֵאלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When I pronounce the name of Adonoy, give greatness to our God,


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because I will call on the name of the Lord: ascribe greatness to our God.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When I call the name of the L-rd, ascribe greatness to our G-d [(whence it is derived that after a blessing in the Temple, the response is "Blessed is the name of the glory of His kingdom" (Baruch shem kevod malchutho)].


###### Deuteronomy 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:4)


הַצּוּר֙ תָּמִ֣ים פׇּֽעֳל֔וֹכִּ֥י כׇל־דְּרָכָ֖יו מִשְׁפָּ֑ט אֵ֤ל אֱמוּנָה֙ וְאֵ֣ין עָ֔וֶלצַדִּ֥יק וְיָשָׁ֖ר הֽוּא׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
The Almighty’s works are flawless, for all His ways are just; A God of faithfulness without injustice; righteous and upright is He.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He is the Rock, his work is perfect: for all his ways are justice: a God of truth and without iniquity, just and right is he.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Rock [Even when He is strong (as a rock), when He brings punishment upon the flouters of His will] — perfect, [in absolute accord with justice] is His work, for all of His ways are judgment [and not blind fury]. He is a G-d of trust [to repay the righteous in the world to come], without wrong [even to the wicked. He repays their deeds of righteousness in this world.] He is [pronounced] righteous [i.e., All vindicate His judgment], and it is fit [that they do so].


###### Deuteronomy 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:5)


שִׁחֵ֥ת ל֛וֹ לֹ֖א בָּנָ֣יו מוּמָ֑םדּ֥וֹר עִקֵּ֖שׁ וּפְתַלְתֹּֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Destructiveness does not affect Him, it is the defect of His children; a generation warped and twisted.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Not his the corruption, but the blemish of his sons: they are a perverse and crooked generation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They have corrupted [themselves], not Him. [They were] His sons, [and the corruption that they wrought is] their blemish [and not His] — a crooked and twisted generation!


###### Deuteronomy 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:6)


הַ לְיְהֹוָה֙¹ תִּגְמְלוּ־זֹ֔אתעַ֥ם נָבָ֖ל וְלֹ֣א חָכָ֑ם הֲלוֹא־הוּא֙ אָבִ֣יךָ קָּנֶ֔ךָה֥וּא עָשְׂךָ֖ וַֽיְכֹנְנֶֽךָ׃

footnotes:
¹ (בספרי תימן הַֽלְיהֹוָה֙ בתיבה אחת)


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Is it with Adonoy that you deal this way? base people lacking wisdom. Is He not your Father, who made you His? He made you and gave you a base.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Do you thus requite the Lord, O foolish people and unwise? is not he thy father that bought thee? has he not made thee, and established thee?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Would you thus requite the L-rd, [who has the power to exact punishment of you, and who accorded you all manner of good?] Ignorant people, [who forgot all that was done to them], and not wise [to foresee consequences, it being in His power to do good and ill]. Is He not your father, your provider? He made you [a nation among nations], and He established you [securely].


###### Deuteronomy 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:7)


זְכֹר֙ יְמ֣וֹת עוֹלָ֔ם בִּ֖ינוּ שְׁנ֣וֹת דֹּר־וָדֹ֑רשְׁאַ֤ל אָבִ֙יךָ֙ וְיַגֵּ֔דְךָ זְקֵנֶ֖יךָ וְיֹ֥אמְרוּ לָֽךְ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Remember world history, study the generational epochs. Ask your father and he will relate to you, your elders and they will tell you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Remember the days of old consider the years of many generations: ask thy father, and he will recount it to thee; thy elders, and they will tell thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Remember the days of yore [i.e., what He did to the first ones who angered Him]; reflect upon the years of [the] generation [of Enosh, which He flooded with Oceanus], and [the] generation [of the flood]. Ask your father [the prophets], and he shall tell you; your elders [the sages], and they will relate it [the early happenings] to you.


###### Deuteronomy 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:8)


בְּהַנְחֵ֤ל עֶלְיוֹן֙ גּוֹיִ֔םבְּהַפְרִיד֖וֹ בְּנֵ֣י אָדָ֑ם יַצֵּב֙ גְּבֻלֹ֣ת עַמִּ֔יםלְמִסְפַּ֖ר בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When the Exalted One bequeathed nations, when He set apart the sons of man; He established the boundaries of peoples according to the number of Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
When the most High divided to the nations their inheritance, when he separated the sons of Adam, he set the bounds of the people according to the number of the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the Most High caused [the] nations [who angered Him] to "inherit," [He inundated them]; when He separated the sons of man [i.e., when He scattered the generation of the Tower of Babel (dor haflagah), He could have removed them from the world; but He did not do so. Instead,] He set the bounds [(seventy tongues)] of the peoples [He established them and did not cause them to go lost], for the sake of the number of the children of Israel [that were destined to go forth from the children of Shem, and in accordance with the number of seventy souls of the children of Israel who went down to Egypt.]


###### Deuteronomy 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:9)


כִּ֛י חֵ֥לֶק יְהֹוָ֖ה עַמּ֑וֹיַעֲקֹ֖ב חֶ֥בֶל נַחֲלָתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Because [of] Adonoy’s portion, His people, Yaakov the cable of His heritage.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord’s portion is his people; Ya῾aqov is the lot of his inheritance.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[And why all this?] For the portion of the L-rd [was secreted among them and destined to come forth. And who is His "portion"?] — His people. [And who is His people?] Jacob, the cord of His inheritance, [the third cord of a three-fold cord (Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob), Jacob and his sons being His inheritance (not Ishmael, the son of Abraham, and not Esav, the son of Isaac.)]


###### Deuteronomy 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:10)


יִמְצָאֵ֙הוּ֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִדְבָּ֔רוּבְתֹ֖הוּ יְלֵ֣ל יְשִׁמֹ֑ן יְסֹבְבֶ֙נְהוּ֙ יְב֣וֹנְנֵ֔הוּיִצְּרֶ֖נְהוּ כְּאִישׁ֥וֹן עֵינֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He found them in a wilderness country, in arid, shrieking desolation. He encircled them around, He granted them understanding, He sheltered them like the pupil of His eye.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He found him in a desert land, and in the waste howling wilderness; he led him about, he instructed him, he kept him as the apple of his eye.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He found them [(Jacob's sons) faithful unto Him] in a desert land, [Israel accepting His Torah, His kingdom, and His yoke, as opposed to Ishmael and Esav (see 33:2)], and in a wasteland, howling [with jackals, etc.] and desolate. [(Even there they were steadfast in their faith.)] [There] He surrounded them [with clouds, and with standards on four sides, and with the base of the mountain (Sinai), arching it over them like a barrel], He invested them [with Torah and understanding]. He protected them [from fiery serpents, scorpions, and the nations] like the pupil of His eye.


###### Deuteronomy 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:11)


כְּנֶ֙שֶׁר֙ יָעִ֣יר קִנּ֔וֹ עַל־גּוֹזָלָ֖יו יְרַחֵ֑ףיִפְרֹ֤שׂ כְּנָפָיו֙ יִקָּחֵ֔הוּ יִשָּׂאֵ֖הוּ עַל־אֶבְרָתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Like an eagle who rouses his nest, fluttering over his young, He extends His wings, grasps them, He bears them on His wing.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
As an eagle stirs up her nest, broods over her young, spreads abroad her wings takes them, bears them on her pinions:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[He led them with mercy and compassion,] as an eagle [by soft motions] awakes its nest, hovering over its fledglings [and not bearing down upon them. (Likewise, the Holy One Blessed be He, in giving Israel the Torah, did not "bear down upon them" from one side, but "projected Himself" from four sides)], spreading its wings and taking them, bearing them on its pinion [from place to place, unlike other birds, who take their young in their talons, fearing the eagle who flies above them. Not so the eagle, which fears no other birds, but only projectiles, from which it shields its fledglings with its body.) The L-rd, likewise, "shielded" Israel at the Red Sea (See Exodus 19:4 and 14: 19-20)]


###### Deuteronomy 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:12)


יְהֹוָ֖ה בָּדָ֣ד יַנְחֶ֑נּוּוְאֵ֥ין עִמּ֖וֹ אֵ֥ל נֵכָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy conducted them alone, without [opposition by] alien gods.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
so the Lord alone did lead him, and there was no strange god with him.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The L-rd led them [in the desert], alone [and secure], and there was no power [in any of the] strange [i.e., the nations' gods] against them.


###### Deuteronomy 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:13)


יַרְכִּבֵ֙הוּ֙ עַל־[בָּ֣מֳתֵי] (במותי) אָ֔רֶץוַיֹּאכַ֖ל תְּנוּבֹ֣ת שָׂדָ֑י וַיֵּנִקֵ֤הֽוּ דְבַשׁ֙ מִסֶּ֔לַעוְשֶׁ֖מֶן מֵחַלְמִ֥ישׁ צֽוּר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He shall transport them over the summit of the earth where they will consume the produce of the fields; and He shall nurture them with honey of bedrock and oil of staunchest rock mass.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
He made him ride on the high places of the earth, and he ate the produce of the fields; and he made him suck honey out of the rock, and oil out of the flinty rock;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He made him ride on the high places of the earth [(Eretz Yisrael is the highest of all the lands)], and he ate the shoots of the field [(The fruits of Eretz Yisrael "shoot up" faster than all others)]. And He gave him to suck [fig-] honey from the rock, and oil from the flint of the rock [(These are the olives of Gush Chalav.)]


###### Deuteronomy 32:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:14)


חֶמְאַ֨ת בָּקָ֜ר וַחֲלֵ֣ב צֹ֗אןעִם־חֵ֨לֶב כָּרִ֜ים וְאֵילִ֤ים בְּנֵֽי־בָשָׁן֙ וְעַתּוּדִ֔יםעִם־חֵ֖לֶב כִּלְי֣וֹת חִטָּ֑ה וְדַם־עֵנָ֖ב תִּשְׁתֶּה־חָֽמֶר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Butter-fat of cattle and milk of sheep with the fat of lambs, and rams native to Bashan and goats, with the fat of wheat kidneys, and the wine-flavored blood of the grapes will you drink.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
butter of kine, and milk of sheep, with fat of lambs, and rams of the breed of Bashan, and goats, with the fat of kidneys of wheat; and thou didst drink wine of the pure blood of the grape.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Cream from the herd and milk from the flock [(as transpired in the days of Solomon (I Kings 5:2-3)], with fat of lambs [(as transpired in the days of the ten tribes (Amos 6:4)], and [fat] rams of the breed of Bashan, and he-goats, with wheat [as fat as] the fat of kidneys [(as transpired in the days of Solomon (I Kings 5:2)], and blood of the grape did you drink [as choice] wine [(as transpired in the days of the ten tribes (Amos 6:6)].


###### Deuteronomy 32:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:15)


וַיִּשְׁמַ֤ן יְשֻׁרוּן֙ וַיִּבְעָ֔ט שָׁמַ֖נְתָּ עָבִ֣יתָ כָּשִׂ֑יתָוַיִּטֹּשׁ֙ אֱל֣וֹהַּ עָשָׂ֔הוּ וַיְנַבֵּ֖ל צ֥וּר יְשֻׁעָתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Yeshurun grew fat and rebelled, you grew fat, you have become thick, and are covered with fat; he forsook the God who made him, he insulted the Rock of his salvation.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
But Yeshurun grew fat, and kicked: thou art grown fat, thou art become thick, thou art covered with fatness; then he forsook God who made him, and lightly esteemed the Rock of his salvation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Yeshurun grew fat and it kicked. You grew fat, you grew thick, you were covered [with fat], and he forsook the G-d who made him and abased the Rock of his salvation [see Ezekiel 8:16].


###### Deuteronomy 32:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:16)


יַקְנִאֻ֖הוּ בְּזָרִ֑ים בְּתוֹעֵבֹ֖ת יַכְעִיסֻֽהוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They incited His vengeance with strangers, with loathsomeness did they spite Him.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They provoked him to jealousy with strange gods, with abominations they provoked him to anger.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They provoked Him with strange [gods]; they angered Him with abominations [like sodomy and necromancy].


###### Deuteronomy 32:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:17)


יִזְבְּח֗וּ לַשֵּׁדִים֙ לֹ֣א אֱלֹ֔הַּ אֱלֹהִ֖ים לֹ֣א יְדָע֑וּםחֲדָשִׁים֙ מִקָּרֹ֣ב בָּ֔אוּ לֹ֥א שְׂעָר֖וּם אֲבֹתֵיכֶֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They slaughtered to ineffectual demons, gods they did not know; novelties, lately arrived—your forefathers were not awed by them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They sacrificed to powerless spirits; to gods whom they knew not, to new gods that came newly up, whom your fathers feared not.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They sacrificed to demons, which availed naught [(If there were some utility to them, His anger would not be "doubled" as it is now)], (to) gods they had not known, new ones, newly come, [who were unknown even to the heathens, (who thought them to be Jewish images)] — your fathers had not dreaded them.


###### Deuteronomy 32:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:18)


צ֥וּר יְלָדְךָ֖ תֶּ֑שִׁי וַתִּשְׁכַּ֖ח אֵ֥ל מְחֹלְלֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
the Rock Who bore you, you disregarded; you forsook the Almighty Who delivered you.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Of the Rock that begot thee thou art unmindful, and hast forgotten God that formed thee.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Rock of your birth you forgot; and you forgot the G-d who brought you forth [from the womb].


###### Deuteronomy 32:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:19)


וַיַּ֥רְא יְהֹוָ֖ה וַיִּנְאָ֑ץ מִכַּ֥עַס בָּנָ֖יו וּבְנֹתָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy saw and was angered, by the spite of His sons and His daughters.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And when the Lord saw it, he abhorred them, because of the provocation of his sons, and of his daughters.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And the L-rd saw and He thrust away in anger His sons and His daughters.


###### Deuteronomy 32:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:20)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר אַסְתִּ֤ירָה פָנַי֙ מֵהֶ֔ם אֶרְאֶ֖ה מָ֣ה אַחֲרִיתָ֑םכִּ֣י ד֤וֹר תַּהְפֻּכֹת֙ הֵ֔מָּה בָּנִ֖ים לֹא־אֵמֻ֥ן בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He said, ‘‘I will hide My face from them, I will see what their end will be; for they are an upsetting generation, sons who are devoid of breeding.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said, I will hide my face from them, I will see what their end shall be: for they are a very perverse generation, children in whom is no faith.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He said: I shall hide My face from them. I shall see what their end shall be. For they are a generation of conversion [They convert My favor to anger], sons in whom there is no rearing [i.e., in whom My rearing is not evident, I, having reared them to good, and they, having departed from it.]


###### Deuteronomy 32:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:21)


הֵ֚ם קִנְא֣וּנִי בְלֹא־אֵ֔ל כִּעֲס֖וּנִי בְּהַבְלֵיהֶ֑םוַֽאֲנִי֙ אַקְנִיאֵ֣ם בְּלֹא־עָ֔ם בְּג֥וֹי נָבָ֖ל אַכְעִיסֵֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They have incited Me to vengeance with a non-god—have spited Me with their idols; and I will incite them with a non-people, I shall anger them with a faithless nation.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They have moved me to jealousy with a no god; they have provoked me to anger with their vanities: and I will move them to jealousy with a no people; I will provoke them to anger with a vile nation.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They have incited Me with a no-god; they have angered Me with their vanities. And I will incite them with a no-people [i.e., a people of no repute (viz. Isaiah 23:13; Ovadiah 1:2)]. With a base nation [i.e., the heretics (viz. Psalms 14:1)] will I anger them.


###### Deuteronomy 32:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:22)


כִּי־אֵשׁ֙ קָדְחָ֣ה בְאַפִּ֔י וַתִּיקַ֖ד עַד־שְׁא֣וֹל תַּחְתִּ֑יתוַתֹּ֤אכַל אֶ֙רֶץ֙ וִֽיבֻלָ֔הּ וַתְּלַהֵ֖ט מוֹסְדֵ֥י הָרִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For a fire shall blaze in My anger, that will burn till the lowest grave; it shall consume the land and its produce, and enflame the bases of mountains.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For a fire is kindled in my anger, and shall burn to the nethermost parts of the earth, and shall consume the earth with its produce, and set on fire the foundations of the mountains.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For a fire has been kindled in My wrath, and it shall burn [in you] to the nethermost pit [the foundation], and it shall consume the land [i.e., your land] and its produce, and it shall set ablaze the foundations of the mountains [i.e., Jerusalem, which is founded on the mountains].


###### Deuteronomy 32:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:23)


אַסְפֶּ֥ה עָלֵ֖ימוֹ רָע֑וֹת חִצַּ֖י אֲכַלֶּה־בָּֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I shall amass iniquity against them, I shall deplete my arrows against them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will heap mischiefs upon them; I will spend my arrows on them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will join evils [i.e., evil upon evil] upon them. I will end [all of] My arrows in them [(— but they themselves will not end)].


###### Deuteronomy 32:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:24)


מְזֵ֥י רָעָ֛ב וּלְחֻ֥מֵי רֶ֖שֶׁף וְקֶ֣טֶב מְרִירִ֑יוְשֶׁן־בְּהֵמֹת֙ אֲשַׁלַּח־בָּ֔ם עִם־חֲמַ֖ת זֹחֲלֵ֥י עָפָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Bloated with hunger, and embattled with demons and slashed by Meriri; and the teeth of animals will I send against them along with the venom of dust-crawlers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall be sucked empty by hunger, and devoured with burning heat, and with bitter destruction: I will also send the teeth of beasts upon them, with the poison of crawling things of the dust.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
(They will be) hairy with hunger and embattled by reshef [demons] and cut down by Meriri [a demon]. And the tooth of beasts will I send against them [(as it, indeed, happened, that the bite of lambs caused death)], with the venom of the crawlers in the dust [i.e., the serpents].


###### Deuteronomy 32:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:25)


מִחוּץ֙ תְּשַׁכֶּל־חֶ֔רֶב וּמֵחֲדָרִ֖ים אֵימָ֑ה גַּם־בָּחוּר֙ גַּם־בְּתוּלָ֔ה יוֹנֵ֖ק עִם־אִ֥ישׁ שֵׂיבָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
From without, the sword shall decimate, and from within chambers, terror; both lad and lass, suckling and aged man.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The sword without, and terror within, shall destroy both the young man and the virgin, the suckling also with the man of grey hairs.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
On the outside [of the city] the sword [of legions] shall devour [them], and in the chambers [of their hearts, when they flee], fright [will pound, eventually killing them], both young man and virgin, suckling and the man of gray hairs.


###### Deuteronomy 32:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:26)


אָמַ֖רְתִּי אַפְאֵיהֶ֑ם אַשְׁבִּ֥יתָה מֵאֱנ֖וֹשׁ זִכְרָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
I thought, ‘I shall abandon them,’, I would eliminate from mankind mention of them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I said, I would scatter them into corners, I would make the remembrance of them to cease from among men:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I said: I would disclaim them; I would cut off their remembrance from men,


###### Deuteronomy 32:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:27)


לוּלֵ֗י כַּ֤עַס אוֹיֵב֙ אָג֔וּר פֶּֽן־יְנַכְּר֖וּ צָרֵ֑ימוֹפֶּן־יֹֽאמְרוּ֙ יָדֵ֣נוּ רָ֔מָה וְלֹ֥א יְהֹוָ֖ה פָּעַ֥ל כׇּל־זֹֽאת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Were it not for the enemy’s amassed rage, lest their oppressors estrange; lest they say, ‘Our armed might has prevailed, and not Adonoy has performed all this.’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
were it not for the heaped up wrath of the enemy, lest their adversaries should misdeem, and lest they should say, Our hand is high, and the Lord has not done all this.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Were it not that the anger of the foe were stored up [against them to destroy them. And if they did destroy them, they would attribute the victory to themselves and to their gods, and not to Me] — lest their oppressors estrange [i.e., lest they attribute their triumph to a stranger], lest they say: Our hand is exalted, and it is not the L-rd who has wrought all this.


###### Deuteronomy 32:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:28)


כִּי־ג֛וֹי אֹבַ֥ד עֵצ֖וֹת הֵ֑מָּה וְאֵ֥ין בָּהֶ֖ם תְּבוּנָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For those nations squander counsel, they lack intelligence.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For they are a nation void of counsel, neither is there any understanding in them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
For it [the foe] is a nation devoid of counsel, and there is no understanding in them, [for]


###### Deuteronomy 32:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:29)


ל֥וּ חָכְמ֖וּ יַשְׂכִּ֣ילוּ זֹ֑את יָבִ֖ינוּ לְאַחֲרִיתָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Were they wise they would grasp this, they would contemplate their ending.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
O that they were wise, that they understood this, that they would consider their latter end!


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
If they were wise, they would understand this, they would comprehend their [Israel's] end [i.e., their defeat (as the L-rd's doing and not as theirs). For they would say]


###### Deuteronomy 32:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:30)


אֵיכָ֞ה יִרְדֹּ֤ף אֶחָד֙ אֶ֔לֶף וּשְׁנַ֖יִם יָנִ֣יסוּ רְבָבָ֑האִם־לֹא֙ כִּֽי־צוּרָ֣ם מְכָרָ֔ם וַֽיהֹוָ֖ה הִסְגִּירָֽם׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
How can one pursue a thousand, and two make ten thousand flee? Unless their Almighty [lit. rock] has dealt them away, and Adonoy has delivered them?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
How should one man chase a thousand, and two put ten thousand to flight, unless their Rock had sold them, and the Lord had shut them up?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
How could one [of us] pursue a thousand, and two put ten thousand to flight, if their Rock had not sold them, and the L rd (had not) delivered them [into our hands]!


###### Deuteronomy 32:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:31)


כִּ֛י לֹ֥א כְצוּרֵ֖נוּ צוּרָ֑ם וְאֹיְבֵ֖ינוּ פְּלִילִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For their mighty one is not like our Almighty, yet our enemies sit in judgement.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For their rock is not as our Rock, even our enemies themselves being judges.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[They should understand this, knowing from past experience] that not as our Rock is their rock, ["their rock" having been entirely powerless against ours], and [now] our foes judge [us! It must be, then, that our Rock has sold us.]


###### Deuteronomy 32:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:32)


כִּֽי־מִגֶּ֤פֶן סְדֹם֙ גַּפְנָ֔ם וּמִשַּׁדְמֹ֖ת עֲמֹרָ֑העֲנָבֵ֙מוֹ֙ עִנְּבֵי־ר֔וֹשׁ אַשְׁכְּלֹ֥ת מְרֹרֹ֖ת לָֽמוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For their grapevine is of the grapevine of Sedom and from the fields of Amorah; their grapes are grapes of bitter herbs. clusters galling to them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For their vine is of the vine of Sedom, and of the fields of ῾Amora: their grapes are grapes of gall, their clusters are bitter:


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
["I said I would disclaim them, etc." (26)], for of the wine of Sodom is their wine, and of the fields of Amorah [i.e., their deeds are those of Sodom and Amorah.] Their grapes are grapes of gall. Bitter clusters are their desert [for their "bitter" deeds].


###### Deuteronomy 32:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:33)


חֲמַ֥ת תַּנִּינִ֖ם יֵינָ֑ם וְרֹ֥אשׁ פְּתָנִ֖ים אַכְזָֽר׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Like caustic serpents’ venom is their wine, and [like] the gall of adders, cruel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
their wine is the fierceness of crocodiles, and the cruel venom of asps.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The [bitter] venom of snakes is their wine [of retribution] and the cruel gall of asps [is their cup. A cruel foe will come and exact punishment of them].


###### Deuteronomy 32:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:34)


הֲלֹא־ה֖וּא כָּמֻ֣ס עִמָּדִ֑י חָת֖וּם בְּאוֹצְרֹתָֽי׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Is this not sequestered with Me, sealed in My treasuries.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Is not this laid up in store with me, and sealed up among my treasures?


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[They think I have forgotten their deeds, but] is it ["the fruit of their wine and the produce of their fields" (see 32)] not laid up with Me and sealed in My stores!


###### Deuteronomy 32:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:35)


לִ֤י נָקָם֙ וְשִׁלֵּ֔ם לְעֵ֖ת תָּמ֣וּט רַגְלָ֑םכִּ֤י קָרוֹב֙ י֣וֹם אֵידָ֔ם וְחָ֖שׁ עֲתִדֹ֥ת לָֽמוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Vengeance is with Me, and I shall administer retribution at the time that they shall stumble for the day of their calamity is close by, and destiny speeds towards them.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
To me belongs vengeance, and recompense, when their foot slides: for the day of their calamity is at hand, and the things that shall come upon them make haste.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
With Me is [readied] vengeance, and it shall pay them [in accordance with their deeds] at the time their foot gives way [i.e., when they exhaust the merit of their fathers, which is their support]. For readied is the day of their calamity, and what is destined [for them] shall quickly befall them. [(Thus far, Moses' words of chastisement, the song to serve as witness, when retribution comes upon them, that he had forewarned them. There follow his words of consolation, relating to what will befall them after their punishment has been exacted (see 30:1-2)]

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
When Adonoy administers justice against His people and He will reconsider about His servants; when He sees the power surging, and none are structured or fortified.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
For the Lord shall judge his people, and repent himself for his servants, when he sees that their power is gone, and there is none shut up, or left.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
When the L rd has judged His people [with the aforementioned afflictions], and He repents Himself of His servants, [turning to be merciful to them], seeing that the hand [of the foe] is becoming progressively stronger, and that they (Israel) are bereft of [saving] restraint or strengthening,

וְאָמַ֖ר אֵ֣י אֱלֹהֵ֑ימוֹ צ֖וּר חָסָ֥יוּ בֽוֹ׃

He shall say, ‘‘Where is their god the rock on which they relied for shelter?


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he shall say, Where are their gods, their rock in whom they trusted,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Then He will say [about them]: Where are their gods? The rock in whom they sought shelter,

those that did eat the fat of their sacrifices, and drank the wine of their drink offerings? let them rise up and help you, and be your protection.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who ate the fat of their sacrifices and drank the wine of their libations? Let them arise and aid you! Let him [the "rock"] be covert for you!


###### Deuteronomy 32:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:39)


רְא֣וּ**׀** עַתָּ֗ה כִּ֣י אֲנִ֤י אֲנִי֙ ה֔וּא וְאֵ֥ין אֱלֹהִ֖ים עִמָּדִ֑יאֲנִ֧י אָמִ֣ית וַאֲחַיֶּ֗ה מָחַ֙צְתִּי֙ וַאֲנִ֣י אֶרְפָּ֔אוְאֵ֥ין מִיָּדִ֖י מַצִּֽיל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Now observe! For it is I! I am the One! and no god matches Me; I put to death and make live, I smite and I heal and no one can rescue from My grasp.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
See now that I, even I, am he, and there is no god with me: I kill, and I make alive; I wound, and I heal: neither is there any that can deliver out of my hand.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
See, now, [from the retribution that I brought upon you and from which none could save you, and from the salvation that I shall bring you, from which none can deter Me], that it is I [for "lowering," and] I [for "lifting"], and there is no god against Me [to deter Me]. I put to death and I bring to life, I smite and I heal, and there is no saving [those who sin against Me] from My hand.

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Deuteronomy 32:41

If I whet My saber to a gleam, and hold judgement in My grasp. I shall wreak vengeance on My foes and My spiters will I pay.

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**

###### Deuteronomy 32:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:42)

**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
I will make my arrows drunk with blood, and my sword shall devour flesh; with the blood of the slain and of the captives; with the head of the wild bands of the enemy.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
I will make My arrows drunk with blood [of the nations], and My sword shall eat [their] flesh, [this, in retribution] for the blood of [Israel's] slain and [their] captivity, [in retribution] for [the sins of] the beginning of the breaches of the foe. [(When the Holy One Blessed be He exacts punishment of the nations, He visits upon them their sins and those of their forefathers, from the very beginning of the breach that they made in Israel.)]

[At that time] the nations will praise His people [for cleaving to Him in all of their vicissitudes and not forsaking Him, knowing His goodness and His glory]. For, the [shedding of the] blood of His servants shall He avenge, and vengeance shall He return to His adversaries [for their despoiling of Israel], and He will conciliate His land [and] His people [for the suffering they endured and for what the foe did to them. (When His people are consoled, His land is consoled.)]

**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And Moses came and he spoke all the words of this song in the ears of the people, he and Hoshea the son of Nun. וּמֻ֗ת בָּהָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר אַתָּה֙ עֹלֶ֣ה שָׁ֔מָּה וְהֵאָסֵ֖ף אֶל־עַמֶּ֑יךָ כַּֽאֲשֶׁר־מֵ֞ת אַהֲרֹ֤ן אָחִ֙יךָ֙ בְּהֹ֣ר הָהָ֔ר וַיֵּאָ֖סֶף אֶל־עַמָּֽיו׃

and die in the mount into which thou goest up, and be gathered to thy people; as Aharon thy brother died in mount Hor, and was gathered to his people:

###### Deuteronomy 32:51

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:51](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/32:51)


עַל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר מְעַלְתֶּ֜ם בִּ֗י בְּתוֹךְ֙ בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בְּמֵֽי־מְרִיבַ֥ת קָדֵ֖שׁ מִדְבַּר־צִ֑ן עַ֣ל אֲשֶׁ֤ר לֹֽא־קִדַּשְׁתֶּם֙ אוֹתִ֔י בְּת֖וֹךְ בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Because you were unfaithful to Me amid Bnei Yisroel at the waters of argument at Kodeish in the Tzin wilderness; because of [the fact] that you did not sanctify Me amid Bnei Yisroel.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
because you transgressed against me among the children of Yisra᾽el at the waters of Merivat-qadesh, in the wilderness of Żin; because you sanctified me not in the midst of the children of Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
("And die") because of your having trespassed against Me [i.e., because of your having led to a trespass against Me] in the midst of the children of Israel at the waters of Merivath Kadesh in the desert of Tzin, because of your not having sanctified Me [i.e., because of your having led to My not being sanctified] in the midst of the children of Israel [see Numbers 20:12].

For from afar shall you see the land, [for I know that it is beloved by you], but there you shall not come [(If you do not see it now, you will not see it at all — wherefore I tell you: "Go up … and see, etc."] — to the land that I give to the children of Israel.



## Chapter 33

###### Deuteronomy 33:1

וְזֹ֣את הַבְּרָכָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר בֵּרַ֥ךְ מֹשֶׁ֛ה אִ֥ישׁ הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים אֶת־בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל לִפְנֵ֖י מוֹתֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And this is the blessing that Moshe, the man of God, blessed Bnei Yisroel before his death.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
VEZOT
					HABBERAKHA 
				
				And this is the blessing, with which Moshe the man of God blessed the children of Yisra᾽el before his death.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this is the blessing wherewith Moses the man of G-d blessed the children of Israel [immediately] before his death ["If not now, when!"]


###### Deuteronomy 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:2)


וַיֹּאמַ֗ר יְהֹוָ֞ה מִסִּינַ֥י בָּא֙ וְזָרַ֤ח מִשֵּׂעִיר֙ לָ֔מוֹ הוֹפִ֙יעַ֙ מֵהַ֣ר פָּארָ֔ן וְאָתָ֖ה מֵרִבְבֹ֣ת קֹ֑דֶשׁ מִימִינ֕וֹ (אשדת) [אֵ֥שׁ דָּ֖ת] לָֽמוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He said: ‘‘Adonoy came from Sinai, radiated forth to them from Seir, appeared from Mount Paran He came with part of the sacred myriads; from His right [hand]— the flaming Torah law.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he said, The Lord came from Sinay, and rose up from Se῾ir to them; he shone forth from mount Paran, and he came from holy multitudes: from his right hand went a fiery law for them.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And he said [(Moses opens with praise of the L-rd and then with the requirements of Israel, Israel's merits inhering in the L-rd's praise)]: The L-rd came from Sinai [(He went out towards them when they came to stand at the foot of the mountain, as a groom goes out towards his bride)], and He shone forth from Seir to them, [after having first offered the Torah to the sons of Esav, (the inhabitants of Seir), who refused it]. He appeared from Mount Paran [after offering it to the children of Yishmael, who refused it]. And He came [to Israel, and with Him] some of the ten thousands of His holy [angels — and not even the greater part (as opposed to the ways of men of flesh and blood, who parade all of their wealth and glory on their nuptial day)]; from His right hand [i.e., In the tablets written by His right hand, He gave] them the law of fire [i.e., the Law which was written of yore before Him in black fire on white fire].


###### Deuteronomy 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:3)


אַ֚ף חֹבֵ֣ב עַמִּ֔ים כׇּל־קְדֹשָׁ֖יו בְּיָדֶ֑ךָ וְהֵם֙ תֻּכּ֣וּ לְרַגְלֶ֔ךָ יִשָּׂ֖א מִדַּבְּרֹתֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He also cherished the nations—all of their sacred ones are in Your keeping; for they were gathered in at Your feet and accepted the burden of Your word.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Truly he loves the peoples; all his holy ones are in thy hand: and they sat down at thy feet; every one shall receive of thy words.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
He also fondly loved the peoples [i.e., the tribes]; all of his holy ones are in Your hand [i.e., the souls of his righteous ones are secreted with You.], and [they are worthy of this, for at Mount Sinai] they placed themselves in the midst, at Your feet. They took upon themselves Your commandments.

תּוֹרָ֥ה צִוָּה־לָ֖נוּ מֹשֶׁ֑ה מוֹרָשָׁ֖ה קְהִלַּ֥ת יַעֲקֹֽב׃

The Torah which Moshe commanded us, is the heritage of the congregation of Yaakov.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Moshe commanded us a Tora, the inheritance of the congregation of Ya῾aqov.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The Torah which Moses commanded unto us is the inheritance of the congregation of Jacob [(We have possessed it and will not abandon it.)]

וַיְהִ֥י בִישֻׁר֖וּן מֶ֑לֶךְ בְּהִתְאַסֵּף֙ רָ֣אשֵׁי עָ֔ם יַ֖חַד שִׁבְטֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
There was a king in Yeshurun, at the gathering of the counting. the tribes of Yisroel together.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And he was king in Yeshurun, when the heads of the people and the tribes of Yisra᾽el were gathered together.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And He was a king in Yeshurun [i.e., the yoke of His kingdom was upon them] whenever their complement was assembled, the tribes of Israel together, [i.e., when there is peace (and not discord) among them].


###### Deuteronomy 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:6)


יְחִ֥י רְאוּבֵ֖ן וְאַל־יָמֹ֑ת וִיהִ֥י מְתָ֖יו מִסְפָּֽר׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
May Reuvein live and let him not die, and may his constituency be counted.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Let Re᾽uven live, and not die; and let not his men be few.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Reuven shall live [in this world] and he shall not die [in the world to come (The episode of Bilhah (Genesis 352) will not be "brought up" to him)], and his men will be counted [among those of his brothers].


###### Deuteronomy 33:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:7)


וְזֹ֣את לִיהוּדָה֮ וַיֹּאמַר֒ שְׁמַ֤ע יְהֹוָה֙ ק֣וֹל יְהוּדָ֔ה וְאֶל־עַמּ֖וֹ תְּבִיאֶ֑נּוּ יָדָיו֙ רָ֣ב ל֔וֹ וְעֵ֥זֶר מִצָּרָ֖יו תִּהְיֶֽה׃{פ}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Now this for Yehudah and he said, ‘‘Adonoy, hear the voice of Yehudah, and bring him to his people; May his hands fight his battles and may You provide assistance against his enemies.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And this is the blessing of Yehuda: and he said, Hear, Lord, the voice of Yehuda, and bring him to his people: let his hands be sufficient for him; and be thou a help to him from his enemies.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And this, [i.e., the same,] for Yehudah, [both Reuven and Yehudah having confessed for their misdeed (see Genesis 38:26)]. And he said: Hear, O L-rd, the voice of Yehudah [i.e., the prayers of Yehudah's descendants: those of David, Solomon, and Assa vis-à-vis the Kushites, those of Yehoshafat vis-à-vis the Ammonites, and those of Chizkiah vis-à-vis Sancheriv.], and to his people shall You bring him [in peace from battle]. His hands will fight [his battle and take vengeance] for him, and You shall be a help against his foes [(this, in respect to Yehoshafat in the war of Ramoth Gilead)].


###### Deuteronomy 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:8)


וּלְלֵוִ֣י אָמַ֔ר תֻּמֶּ֥יךָ וְאוּרֶ֖יךָ לְאִ֣ישׁ חֲסִידֶ֑ךָ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נִסִּיתוֹ֙ בְּמַסָּ֔ה תְּרִיבֵ֖הוּ עַל־מֵ֥י מְרִיבָֽה׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And regarding Levi he said, ‘‘Your *tumim* and Your *urim* to Your pious man; whom You tried with tests, You brought him strife at the waters of Merivah.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of Levi he said, Let thy Tummim and thy Urim be with thy pious one, whom thou didst prove at Massa, and with whom thou didst strive at the waters of Meriva;


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of Levi he said: Your Thumim and Your Urim to Your (acknowledged) pious one, whom You proved in Massah, [Levi not caviling like the others], whom You tested at the waters of Merivah,


###### Deuteronomy 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:9)


הָאֹמֵ֞ר לְאָבִ֤יו וּלְאִמּוֹ֙ לֹ֣א רְאִיתִ֔יו וְאֶת־אֶחָיו֙ לֹ֣א הִכִּ֔יר וְאֶת־בָּנָ֖ו לֹ֣א יָדָ֑ע כִּ֤י שָֽׁמְרוּ֙ אִמְרָתֶ֔ךָ וּבְרִיתְךָ֖ יִנְצֹֽרוּ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
He who said of his father and his mother, ‘I did not see them,‘ and his brothers he did not recognize and his sons he did not know; for they observed Your command and kept Your covenant.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
who said of his father and of his mother, I have not seen him; nor did he acknowledge his brothers, nor knew his own children: for they have observed thy word, and kept thy covenant.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
who says [(in the episode of the golden calf, when Levi was commanded to kill those who had served it)] of his father [i.e., his mother's father (the tribe of Levi not having sinned)] and of his mother: I have not seen them; and his brothers [i.e., his maternal brothers (by an Israelite)] he did not recognize, and his sons [i.e., his daughter's sons (by an Israelite)] he did not know. For they kept Your commandment ["You shall have no other gods"], and Your covenant [of circumcision] they kept [(the Levites, unlike the other tribes, having circumcised their sons in the desert)].


###### Deuteronomy 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:10)


יוֹר֤וּ מִשְׁפָּטֶ֙יךָ֙ לְיַֽעֲקֹ֔ב וְתוֹרָתְךָ֖ לְיִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל יָשִׂ֤ימוּ קְטוֹרָה֙ בְּאַפֶּ֔ךָ וְכָלִ֖יל עַֽל־מִזְבְּחֶֽךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
They shall teach Your law to Yaakov and Your Torah to Yisroel; let them place incense before You and totally consumed offerings on Your altar;


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall teach Ya῾aqov thy judgments, and Yisra᾽el thy Tora: they shall put incense before thee, and whole burnt sacrifice upon thy altar.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
They shall teach Your judgments to Jacob [(they are worthy of doing so)], and Your Torah to Israel. They shall place incense before You and a burnt-offering upon Your altar.


###### Deuteronomy 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:11)


בָּרֵ֤ךְ יְהֹוָה֙ חֵיל֔וֹ וּפֹ֥עַל יָדָ֖יו תִּרְצֶ֑ה מְחַ֨ץ מׇתְנַ֧יִם קָמָ֛יו וּמְשַׂנְאָ֖יו מִן־יְקוּמֽוּן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Adonoy, bless his wealth and accept his handiwork; shatter his adversaries at the loins and his enemies, so that they may not arise.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
Bless, Lord, his substance, and accept the work of his hands: smite through the loins those who rise against him, and those who hate him, that they rise not again.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Bless, O L-rd, his substance, and desire the work of his hands. Break the thighs of those who rise up against him, and of his foes, that they rise not.


###### Deuteronomy 33:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:12)


לְבִנְיָמִ֣ן אָמַ֔ר יְדִ֣יד יְהֹוָ֔ה יִשְׁכֹּ֥ן לָבֶ֖טַח עָלָ֑יו חֹפֵ֤ף עָלָיו֙ כׇּל־הַיּ֔וֹם וּבֵ֥ין כְּתֵפָ֖יו שָׁכֵֽן׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Regarding Binyomin he said, ‘‘Adonoy’s friend, let him live secure with Him; He hovers protectively over him throughout the day, and He resides between his shoulders.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of Binyamin he said, The beloved of the Lord; he shall dwell in safety by him; he shall cover him all the day long, and he shall dwell between his shoulders.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The rationale of the juxtaposition: The blessing of Levi is the sacrificial service and that of Binyamin, the building of the Temple in his portion. Joseph follows (13) in that the sanctuary of Shiloh was built in his portion (but Binyamin takes precedence in that the Temple was more beloved than the sanctuary.)] Of Binyamin he said: Beloved of the L-rd; he shall rest securely upon Him. He hovers over it [the Temple, protecting it] the entire day [i.e., forever (Once Jerusalem was selected, the Shechinah did not repose itself in a different place)]; and between his shoulders [(in a slight depression on the highest part of Binyamin's land)] does it (the Temple) rest.


###### Deuteronomy 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:13)


וּלְיוֹסֵ֣ף אָמַ֔ר מְבֹרֶ֥כֶת יְהֹוָ֖ה אַרְצ֑וֹ מִמֶּ֤גֶד שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ מִטָּ֔ל וּמִתְּה֖וֹם רֹבֶ֥צֶת תָּֽחַת׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And regarding Yoseif he said, ‘‘Blessed by Adonoy is his land; with the delight of heaven, from dew, and the deep waters that lie below.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of Yosef he said, Blessed of the Lord be his land, for the precious things of heaven, for the dew, and for the deep that couches beneath,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of Joseph he said: Blessed of the L-rd is his land, [his being the choicest of the land of all the tribes]: of the sweet things of the heaven, of the dew, and of the deep, lying below, [the deep rising and watering it from below (see Genesis 49:25). With all of the tribes, the blessing of Moses was reminiscent of that of Jacob].


###### Deuteronomy 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:14)


וּמִמֶּ֖גֶד תְּבוּאֹ֣ת שָׁ֑מֶשׁ וּמִמֶּ֖גֶד גֶּ֥רֶשׁ יְרָחִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
With the delight of the sun’s harvest; and with the delight of the moon’s ripening.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for the precious fruits brought forth by the sun, and for the precious things put forth by the moon,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of the sweet things brought forth by the sun, [Joseph's land being "open" to the sun, which sweetened the fruits], and of the sweet things [like melons] quickened by the moon,


###### Deuteronomy 33:15

וּמֵרֹ֖אשׁ הַרְרֵי־קֶ֑דֶם וּמִמֶּ֖גֶד גִּבְע֥וֹת עוֹלָֽם׃

With the first fruits of the early mountains and from the sweetness of the perennial hills.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for the chief things of the ancient mountains, and for the precious things of the primordial hills,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of the first [-ripened fruits] of the mountains of "yore" [(the mountains being earliest to ripen the fruits)], and of the sweet things of the eternal hills [i.e., hills which are "eternally" fruitful, never lacking rain],


###### Deuteronomy 33:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:16)


וּמִמֶּ֗גֶד אֶ֚רֶץ וּמְלֹאָ֔הּ וּרְצ֥וֹן שֹׁכְנִ֖י סְנֶ֑ה תָּב֙וֹאתָה֙ לְרֹ֣אשׁ יוֹסֵ֔ף וּלְקׇדְקֹ֖ד נְזִ֥יר אֶחָֽיו׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And from the sweetness of earth and its fullness, and the favor of the One who resides in the thornbush; let this be visited upon Yoseif’s head, and upon the head of the one set apart from his brothers.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and for the precious things of the earth and its fulness, and for the good will of him that dwelt in the bush: let the blessing come upon the head of Yosef, and upon the top of the head of him who was separated from his brothers.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of the sweet things of the earth and its fullness, and (of) the favor of the Dweller [who first appeared to me] in the bush (Exodus 3:2). May it [this blessing] come upon the head of Joseph, and upon the head of him sundered [from] his brothers.


###### Deuteronomy 33:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:17)


בְּכ֨וֹר שׁוֹר֜וֹ הָדָ֣ר ל֗וֹ וְקַרְנֵ֤י רְאֵם֙ קַרְנָ֔יו בָּהֶ֗ם עַמִּ֛ים יְנַגַּ֥ח יַחְדָּ֖ו אַפְסֵי־אָ֑רֶץ וְהֵם֙ רִבְב֣וֹת אֶפְרַ֔יִם וְהֵ֖ם אַלְפֵ֥י מְנַשֶּֽׁה׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
His firstborn ox, glory to him, and re’em’s horns are his horns, with which he will gore together peoples to the ends of the earth; and they are Ephraim’s myriads, and they are Menasheh’s thousands.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
The firstling of his herd, grandeur is his, and his horns are like the horns of a wild ox: with them he shall push the peoples altogether to the ends of the earth: and they are the ten thousands of Efrayim, and they are the thousands of Menashshe.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
The grandeur [and kingdom] of his "ox" [(Joshua, who comes from Joseph and who conquered many kings)] is accorded him [see Numbers 27:20], and the horns of a re'em are his horns [i.e., they are as strong as those of an ox and beautiful as those of a re'em]. With them he shall gore peoples as one, the ends of the earth [i.e., thirty-one kings from the ends of the earth, (Eretz Yisrael being so desirable that there was no king who did not establish a seat in it)]. And they [the "gored ones"] are the ten thousands of Ephraim [i.e., those slain by Joshua, who came from Ephraim], and they are the thousands of Menasheh [i.e., those slain by Gideon in Midian (Judges 8:10)].


###### Deuteronomy 33:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:18)


וְלִזְבוּלֻ֣ן אָמַ֔ר שְׂמַ֥ח זְבוּלֻ֖ן בְּצֵאתֶ֑ךָ וְיִשָּׂשכָ֖ר בְּאֹהָלֶֽיךָ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And regarding Zevulun he said, ‘‘Rejoice Zevulun at your departure; and Yissachar in your tents.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of Zevulun he said, Rejoice, Zevulun, in thy going out; and, Yissakhar, in thy tents.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
[The names of each of the last five tribes is "doubled" in the blessing in order to "strengthen" them, these being the weakest of all the tribes (which Joseph presented to Pharaoh. See Genesis 47:2)] And of Zevulun he said: Rejoice Zevulun in your going forth [in ships to trade], and Yissachar [learning] in your tents [of Torah, (Zevulun, through their support, enabling Yissachar to do so, for which reason Zevulun is mentioned first)].


###### Deuteronomy 33:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:19)


עַמִּים֙ הַר־יִקְרָ֔אוּ שָׁ֖ם יִזְבְּח֣וּ זִבְחֵי־צֶ֑דֶק כִּ֣י שֶׁ֤פַע יַמִּים֙ יִינָ֔קוּ וּשְׂפֻנֵ֖י טְמ֥וּנֵי חֽוֹל׃{ס}


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
Peoples shall assemble at the mountain—there will they slaughter righteous offerings; for by the streaming of the sea they shall be nurtured and the hidden treasures of sand.’’


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
They shall call the peoples to the mountain; there they shall offer sacrifices of righteousness: for they shall suck of the abundance of the seas, and of treasures hid in the sand.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
Peoples [of the tribes of Israel] at the mountain [Mount Moriah] they [Yissachar, by being the "decisors" of the festival times (see I Chronicles 12:33)] will assemble. There they will sacrifice offerings of righteousness. For the abundance of the seas will they [Yissachar and Zevulun] suck, [giving them leisure for Torah study], and the covered things hidden in the sand [(valuable things found in the sea and the sand in the territory of Yissachar and Zevulun)].


###### Deuteronomy 33:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/33:20)


וּלְגָ֣ד אָמַ֔ר בָּר֖וּךְ מַרְחִ֣יב גָּ֑ד כְּלָבִ֣יא שָׁכֵ֔ן וְטָרַ֥ף זְר֖וֹעַ אַף־קׇדְקֹֽד׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And regarding Gad he said, ‘‘Blessed is the One who broadens Gad; He lairs like a lion then rips the arm with the head.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And of Gad he said, Blessed is he that enlarges Gad: he dwells as a lion, and tears the arm with the crown of the head.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And of Gad he said: Blessed is He that broadens Gad, [the territory of Gad broadening out eastward]. As a lion he dwells, [(border dwellers requiring the "strength of a lion" to repel invaders)], and he tore the arm together with the crown [Gad's slain ones were conspicuous — they hacked off head and arm at one blow.]

**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**

[(Moses now says:) Why need I enumerate details? Everything is yours!] Happy are you, O Israel! Who is like you? A people helped by the L-rd, the Shield of your salvation and the Sword of your pride! Your foes will dissimulate to you [in fear of you, viz. the Giveonites (Joshua 9:9)], and you will tread on their high places [viz. Ibid. 10:24)].

###### Deuteronomy 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/34:10)


וְלֹא־קָ֨ם נָבִ֥יא ע֛וֹד בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל כְּמֹשֶׁ֑ה אֲשֶׁר֙ יְדָע֣וֹ יְהֹוָ֔ה פָּנִ֖ים אֶל־פָּנִֽים׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And there has not ever arisen a prophet within Yisroel like Moshe, whom Adonoy knew face-to-face.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
And there arose not a prophet since in Yisra᾽el like Moshe, whom the Lord knew face to face,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And there arose no other prophet in Israel like Moses, whom the L-rd knew face to face [i.e., Moses being "familiar" with the L-rd, speaking with Him whenever he desired]


###### Deuteronomy 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/34:11)


לְכׇל־הָ֨אֹתֹ֜ת וְהַמּוֹפְתִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר שְׁלָחוֹ֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה לַעֲשׂ֖וֹת בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם לְפַרְעֹ֥ה וּלְכׇל־עֲבָדָ֖יו וּלְכׇל־אַרְצֽוֹ׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
For all the signs and the wonders that Adonoy sent him to perform in the land of Egypt, to Pharoah and to all his slaves and to his entire land.


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
in all the signs and the wonders, which the Lord sent him to do in the land of Miżrayim to Par῾o, and to all his servants and to all his land,


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
In all the signs and the wonders which the L-rd sent him to do in the land of Egypt, to Pharaoh, and to all of his servants, and to all of his land,


###### Deuteronomy 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Deuteronomy/r/34:12)


וּלְכֹל֙ הַיָּ֣ד הַחֲזָקָ֔ה וּלְכֹ֖ל הַמּוֹרָ֣א הַגָּד֑וֹל אֲשֶׁר֙ עָשָׂ֣ה מֹשֶׁ֔ה לְעֵינֵ֖י כׇּל־יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


**Metsudah Chumash, Metsudah Publications, 2009:**
And for the entire strong hand and for the entire great display that Moshe performed in view of all of Yisroel. CHAZAK


**The Koren Jerusalem Bible:**
and in all that mighty hand, and in all the great terror which Moshe performed in the sight of all Yisra᾽el.


**The Rashi chumash by Rabbi Shraga Silverstein:**
And in all the strong hand [of Moses that received the Torah on the tablets], and in all the great awe, [the miracles and the mighty acts in the great, awesome desert] that Moses wrought before the eyes of all Israel, [his venturing to break the tablets before their eyes (viz. 9:17) — and the L-rd concurring in his resolution!]
